




|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

saṃyuttanikāyau


nidānavaggau


1. nidānasaṃyuttaṃ

1. buddhavaggau

1. paṭic‍casamuppādasuttaṃ

1. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘paṭic‍casamuppādaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi; taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, paṭic‍casamuppādau? avij‍jāpac‍cayā, bhikkhavai, saṅkhārā; saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ; viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ; nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatanaṃ; saḷāyatanapac‍cayā phassau; phassapac‍cayā vaidanā; vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā; taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ; upādānapac‍cayā bhavau; bhavapac‍cayā jāti; jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā sambhavanti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, paṭic‍casamuppādau|

‘‘avij‍jāya  tvaiva asaisavirāganiraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau; saṅkhāraniraudhā  viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhau; viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhā nāmarūpaniraudhau; nāmarūpaniraudhā saḷāyatananiraudhau; saḷāyatananiraudhā phassaniraudhau; phassaniraudhā vaidanāniraudhau; vaidanāniraudhā taṇhāniraudhau; taṇhāniraudhā upādānaniraudhau; upādānaniraudhā bhavaniraudhau; bhavaniraudhā jātiniraudhau; jātiniraudhā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā nirujjhanti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hautī’’ti| idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. vibhaṅgasuttaṃ

2. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘paṭic‍casamuppādaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi vibhajissāmi| taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, paṭic‍casamuppādau? avij‍jāpac‍cayā, bhikkhavai, saṅkhārā; saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ; viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ; nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatanaṃ; saḷāyatanapac‍cayā phassau; phassapac‍cayā vaidanā; vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā; taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ; upādānapac‍cayā bhavau; bhavapac‍cayā jāti; jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā sambhavanti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, jarāmaraṇaṃ? yā taisaṃ taisaṃ sattānaṃ tamhi tamhi sattanikāyai jarā jīraṇatā khaṇḍic‍caṃ pālic‍caṃ valittacatā āyunau saṃhāni indriyānaṃ paripākau; ayaṃ vuc‍cati  jarā| yā taisaṃ  taisaṃ sattānaṃ tamhā tamhā sattanikāyā cuti cavanatā bhaidau antaradhānaṃ mac‍cu maraṇaṃ kālakiriyā khandhānaṃ bhaidau kaḷaivarassa nikkhaipau ( ) (jīvitindriyassa upacchaidau) (syā॰ kaṃ॰) aivamuparipi, aṭṭhakathāyaṃ pana na dissati, idaṃ vuc‍cati maraṇaṃ| iti ayañ‍ca jarā, idañ‍ca maraṇaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, jarāmaraṇaṃ|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, jāti? yā taisaṃ taisaṃ sattānaṃ tamhi tamhi sattanikāyai jāti sañ‍jāti auk‍kanti nibbatti abhinibbatti khandhānaṃ pātubhāvau āyatanānaṃ paṭilābhau| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, jāti|

‘‘katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, bhavau? tayau mai, bhikkhavai, bhavā – kāmabhavau, rūpabhavau, arūpabhavau| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhavau|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, upādānaṃ? cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, upādānāni – kāmupādānaṃ, diṭṭhupādānaṃ, sīlabbatupādānaṃ, attavādupādānaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, upādānaṃ|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, taṇhā? chayimai, bhikkhavai, taṇhākāyā – rūpataṇhā, saddataṇhā, gandhataṇhā, rasataṇhā, phauṭṭhabbataṇhā, dhammataṇhā| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, taṇhā|

‘‘katamā  ca, bhikkhavai, vaidanā? chayimai, bhikkhavai, vaidanākāyā – cakkhusamphassajā vaidanā, sautasamphassajā vaidanā, ghānasamphassajā vaidanā, jivhāsamphassajā vaidanā, kāyasamphassajā vaidanā, manausamphassajā vaidanā| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, vaidanā|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, phassau? chayimai, bhikkhavai, phassakāyā – cakkhusamphassau, sautasamphassau, ghānasamphassau, jivhāsamphassau, kāyasamphassau, manausamphassau| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, phassau|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, saḷāyatanaṃ? cakkhāyatanaṃ, sautāyatanaṃ, ghānāyatanaṃ, jivhāyatanaṃ, kāyāyatanaṃ, manāyatanaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, saḷāyatanaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, nāmarūpaṃ? vaidanā, sañ‍ñā, caitanā, phassau, manasikārau – idaṃ vuc‍cati nāmaṃ| cattārau ca  mahābhūtā, catun‍nañ‍ca mahābhūtānaṃ upādāyarūpaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati rūpaṃ| iti idañ‍ca nāmaṃ, idañ‍ca rūpaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, nāmarūpaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ? chayimai, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñāṇakāyā – cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ|

‘‘katamai ca, bhikkhavai, saṅkhārā? tayaumai, bhikkhavai, saṅkhārā – kāyasaṅkhārau, vacīsaṅkhārau, cittasaṅkhārau| imai vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, saṅkhārā|

‘‘katamā  ca, bhikkhavai, avij‍jā? yaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, dukkhai añ‍ñāṇaṃ, dukkhasamudayai añ‍ñāṇaṃ, dukkhaniraudhai añ‍ñāṇaṃ, dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya añ‍ñāṇaṃ| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, avij‍jā|

‘‘iti  khau, bhikkhavai, avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā; saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti| avij‍jāya tvaiva asaisavirāganiraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau; saṅkhāraniraudhā viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhau…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hautī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. paṭipadāsuttaṃ

3. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘micchāpaṭipadañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi sammāpaṭipadañ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, micchāpaṭipadā? avij‍jāpac‍cayā, bhikkhavai, saṅkhārā; saṅkhārapac‍cayā  viñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, micchāpaṭipadā|

‘‘katamā  ca, bhikkhavai, sammāpaṭipadā? avij‍jāya tvaiva asaisavirāganiraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau; saṅkhāraniraudhā viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhau…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hauti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sammāpaṭipadā’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. vipassīsuttaṃ

4. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘vipassissa, bhikkhavai, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa  pubbaiva sambaudhā anabhisambuddhassa baudhisattassaiva satau aitadahausi – ‘kicchaṃ vatāyaṃ laukau āpan‍nau jāyati ca jīyati ca mīyati ca cavati ca upapaj‍jati ca| atha ca panimassa dukkhassa nissaraṇaṃ nappajānāti jarāmaraṇassa| kudāssu nāma imassa dukkhassa nissaraṇaṃ pañ‍ñāyissati jarāmaraṇassā’’’ti?

‘‘atha khau bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa aitadahausi – ‘kimhi nu khau sati jarāmaraṇaṃ hauti, kiṃpac‍cayā jarāmaraṇa’nti? atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa yaunisau manasikārā ahu pañ‍ñāya abhisamayau – ‘jātiyā khau sati jarāmaraṇaṃ hauti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇa’’’nti|

‘‘atha  khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa aitadahausi – ‘kimhi nu khau sati jāti hauti, kiṃpac‍cayā jātī’ti? atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa yaunisau manasikārā ahu pañ‍ñāya abhisamayau – ‘bhavai khau sati jāti hauti, bhavapac‍cayā jātī’’’ti|

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa aitadahausi – ‘kimhi nu khau sati bhavau hauti, kiṃpac‍cayā bhavau’ti? atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa yaunisau manasikārā  ahu pañ‍ñāya abhisamayau – ‘upādānai khau sati bhavau hauti, upādānapac‍cayā bhavau’’’ti|

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa aitadahausi – ‘kimhi nu khau sati upādānaṃ hauti, kiṃpac‍cayā upādāna’nti? atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa yaunisau manasikārā  ahu pañ‍ñāya abhisamayau – ‘taṇhāya khau sati upādānaṃ  hauti, taṇhāpac‍cayā upādāna’’’nti|

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa aitadahausi – ‘kimhi nu khau sati taṇhā hauti, kiṃpac‍cayā taṇhā’ti? atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa yaunisau manasikārā ahu pañ‍ñāya abhisamayau – ‘vaidanāya khau sati taṇhā hauti, vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā’’’ti|

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa aitadahausi – ‘kimhi nu khau sati vaidanā hauti, kiṃpac‍cayā vaidanā’ti? atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa yaunisau manasikārā ahu pañ‍ñāya abhisamayau – ‘phassai khau sati vaidanā hauti, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā’’’ti|

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa aitadahausi – ‘kimhi nu khau sati phassau hauti, kiṃpac‍cayā phassau’ti? atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa yaunisau manasikārā ahu pañ‍ñāya abhisamayau – ‘saḷāyatanai khau sati phassau hauti, saḷāyatanapac‍cayā phassau’’’ti|

‘‘atha  khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa aitadahausi – ‘kimhi nu khau sati saḷāyatanaṃ hauti, kiṃpac‍cayā saḷāyatana’nti? atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa yaunisau manasikārā ahu pañ‍ñāya abhisamayau – ‘nāmarūpai khau sati saḷāyatanaṃ hauti, nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatana’’’nti|

‘‘atha  khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa aitadahausi – ‘kimhi nu khau sati nāmarūpaṃ hauti, kiṃpac‍cayā nāmarūpa’nti? atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa yaunisau manasikārā ahu pañ‍ñāya abhisamayau – ‘viñ‍ñāṇai khau sati nāmarūpaṃ hauti, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpa’’’nti|

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa aitadahausi – ‘kimhi nu khau sati viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hauti, kiṃpac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇa’nti? atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa yaunisau manasikārā ahu pañ‍ñāya abhisamayau – ‘saṅkhāraisu khau sati viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hauti, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇa’’’nti|

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa aitadahausi  – ‘kimhi nu khau sati saṅkhārā haunti, kiṃpac‍cayā saṅkhārā’ti? atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa yaunisau manasikārā ahu pañ‍ñāya abhisamayau – ‘avij‍jāya khau sati saṅkhārā haunti, avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā’’’ti|

‘‘iti hidaṃ avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā; saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti| ‘samudayau, samudayau’ti khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādi|

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa aitadahausi – ‘kimhi nu khau asati jarāmaraṇaṃ na hauti, kissa niraudhā jarāmaraṇaniraudhau’ti? atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa yaunisau manasikārā ahu pañ‍ñāya abhisamayau – ‘jātiyā khau asati jarāmaraṇaṃ na hauti, jātiniraudhā jarāmaraṇaniraudhau’’’ti|

‘‘atha  khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa aitadahausi – ‘kimhi nu khau asati jāti na hauti, kissa niraudhā jātiniraudhau’ti? atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa  yaunisau manasikārā ahu pañ‍ñāya abhisamayau – ‘bhavai khau asati jāti na hauti, bhavaniraudhā jātiniraudhau’’’ti|

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa aitadahausi – ‘kimhi nu khau asati bhavau na hauti, kissa niraudhā bhavaniraudhau’ti? atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa yaunisau manasikārā ahu pañ‍ñāya abhisamayau – ‘upādānai khau asati bhavau na hauti, upādānaniraudhā bhavaniraudhau’’’ti|

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa aitadahausi – ‘kimhi nu khau asati upādānaṃ na hauti, kissa niraudhā upādānaniraudhau’ti? atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa yaunisau manasikārā ahu pañ‍ñāya abhisamayau – ‘taṇhāya khau asati upādānaṃ na hauti, taṇhāniraudhā upādānaniraudhau’’’ti|

‘‘atha  khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa aitadahausi – ‘kimhi nu khau asati taṇhā na hauti, kissa niraudhā taṇhāniraudhau’ti? atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa yaunisau manasikārā ahu pañ‍ñāya abhisamayau – ‘vaidanāya khau asati taṇhā na hauti, vaidanāniraudhā taṇhāniraudhau’’’ti|

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa aitadahausi – ‘kimhi nu khau asati vaidanā na hauti, kissa niraudhā vaidanāniraudhau’ti? atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa yaunisau manasikārā ahu pañ‍ñāya abhisamayau – ‘phassai khau asati vaidanā na hauti, phassaniraudhā  vaidanāniraudhau’’’ti|

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa aitadahausi – ‘kimhi nu khau asati phassau na hauti, kissa niraudhā phassaniraudhau’ti? atha khau bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa yaunisau manasikārā ahu  pañ‍ñāya abhisamayau – ‘saḷāyatanai khau asati phassau na hauti, saḷāyatananiraudhā phassaniraudhau’’’ti|

‘‘atha  khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa aitadahausi – ‘kimhi nu khau asati saḷāyatanaṃ na hauti, kissa niraudhā saḷāyatananiraudhau’ti? atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa yaunisau manasikārā ahu pañ‍ñāya abhisamayau – ‘nāmarūpai khau asati saḷāyatanaṃ na hauti, nāmarūpaniraudhā saḷāyatananiraudhau’’’ti|

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa aitadahausi – ‘kimhi nu khau asati nāmarūpaṃ na hauti, kissa niraudhā nāmarūpaniraudhau’ti? atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa yaunisau manasikārā ahu pañ‍ñāya abhisamayau – ‘viñ‍ñāṇai khau asati nāmarūpaṃ na hauti, viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhā nāmarūpaniraudhau’’’ti|

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa aitadahausi – ‘kimhi nu khau asati viñ‍ñāṇaṃ na hauti, kissa niraudhā viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhau’ti? atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa yaunisau manasikārā ahu pañ‍ñāya abhisamayau – ‘saṅkhāraisu khau asati viñ‍ñāṇaṃ na hauti, saṅkhāraniraudhā viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhau’’’ti|

‘‘atha  khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa aitadahausi – ‘kimhi nu khau asati saṅkhārā na haunti, kissa niraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau’ti? atha khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa yaunisau manasikārā ahu pañ‍ñāya abhisamayau – ‘avij‍jāya khau asati saṅkhārā na haunti, avij‍jāniraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau’’’ti|

‘‘iti hidaṃ avij‍jāniraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau; saṅkhāraniraudhā viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhau…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hautīti| ‘niraudhau, niraudhau’ti khau, bhikkhavai, vipassissa baudhisattassa pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādi’’| catutthaṃ|

(sattan‍nampi buddhānaṃ aivaṃ vitthāraitabbau)|

5. sikhīsuttaṃ

5. sikhissa, bhikkhavai, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa…pai॰…|

6. vaissabhūsuttaṃ

6. vaissabhussa, bhikkhavai, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa…pai॰…|

7. kakusandhasuttaṃ

7. kakusandhassa, bhikkhavai, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa…pai॰…|

8. kauṇāgamanasuttaṃ

8. kauṇāgamanassa, bhikkhavai, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa…pai॰…|

9. kassapasuttaṃ

9. kassapassa, bhikkhavai, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa…pai॰…|

10. gautamasuttaṃ

10. ‘‘pubbaiva  mai, bhikkhavai, sambaudhā anabhisambuddhassa baudhisattassaiva satau aitadahausi – ‘kicchaṃ vatāyaṃ laukau āpan‍nau jāyati ca jīyati ca mīyati ca cavati ca upapaj‍jati ca| atha ca panimassa dukkhassa nissaraṇaṃ nappajānāti jarāmaraṇassa| kudāssu nāma imassa dukkhassa nissaraṇaṃ pañ‍ñāyissati jarāmaraṇassā’’’ti?

‘‘tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi – ‘kimhi nu khau sati jarāmaraṇaṃ hauti, kiṃpac‍cayā  jarāmaraṇa’nti? tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, yaunisau manasikārā ahu pañ‍ñāya abhisamayau – ‘jātiyā khau sati jarāmaraṇaṃ hauti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇa’’’nti|

‘‘tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi – ‘kimhi nu khau sati jāti hauti…pai॰… bhavau… upādānaṃ… taṇhā… vaidanā… phassau… saḷāyatanaṃ… nāmarūpaṃ… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ… saṅkhārā haunti, kiṃpac‍cayā saṅkhārā’ti? tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, yaunisau manasikārā ahu pañ‍ñāya abhisamayau – ‘avij‍jāya khau sati saṅkhārā haunti, avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā’’’ti|

‘‘iti hidaṃ avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā; saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti| ‘samudayau, samudayau’ti khau mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādi|

‘‘tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi – ‘kimhi nu khau asati jarāmaraṇaṃ na hauti, kissa niraudhā jarāmaraṇaniraudhau’ti? tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, yaunisau manasikārā ahu pañ‍ñāya abhisamayau – ‘jātiyā khau asati jarāmaraṇaṃ na hauti, jātiniraudhā jarāmaraṇaniraudhau’’’ti|

‘‘tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi – ‘kimhi nu khau  asati jāti na hauti…pai॰… bhavau… upādānaṃ… taṇhā… vaidanā… phassau… saḷāyatanaṃ… nāmarūpaṃ… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ… saṅkhārā na haunti, kissa niraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau’ti? tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, yaunisau manasikārā ahu  pañ‍ñāya abhisamayau – ‘avij‍jāya khau asati saṅkhārā na haunti, avij‍jāniraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau’’’ti|

‘‘iti hidaṃ avij‍jāniraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau; saṅkhāraniraudhā viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhau…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hauti| ‘niraudhau, niraudhau’ti  khau mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādī’’ti| dasamau|

buddhavaggau paṭhamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

daisanā vibhaṅgapaṭipadā ca,

vipassī sikhī ca vaissabhū|

kakusandhau kauṇāgamanau kassapau,

mahāsakyamuni ca gautamauti||

2. āhāravaggau

1. āhārasuttaṃ

11. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai…pai॰… aitadavauca – ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, āhārā bhūtānaṃ vā sattānaṃ ṭhitiyā sambhavaisīnaṃ vā anuggahāya| katamai cattārau? kabaḷīkārau kabaḷiṃkārau (sī॰ pī॰), kavaḷīkārau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) āhārau – auḷārikau vā sukhumau vā, phassau dutiyau, manausañ‍caitanā tatiyā, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ catutthaṃ| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau āhārā bhūtānaṃ vā sattānaṃ ṭhitiyā sambhavaisīnaṃ vā anuggahāya’’|

‘‘imai, bhikkhavai, cattārau āhārā kiṃnidānā kiṃsamudayā  kiṃjātikā kiṃpabhavā? imai cattārau āhārā taṇhānidānā taṇhāsamudayā taṇhājātikā taṇhāpabhavā| taṇhā cāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, kiṃnidānā kiṃsamudayā kiṃjātikā kiṃpabhavā? taṇhā vaidanānidānā vaidanāsamudayā vaidanājātikā  vaidanāpabhavā| vaidanā cāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, kiṃnidānā kiṃsamudayā kiṃjātikā kiṃpabhavā? vaidanā phassanidānā phassasamudayā phassajātikā phassapabhavā| phassau cāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, kiṃnidānau kiṃsamudayau kiṃjātikau kiṃpabhavau? phassau saḷāyatananidānau saḷāyatanasamudayau saḷāyatanajātikau saḷāyatanapabhavau| saḷāyatanañ‍cidaṃ, bhikkhavai, kiṃnidānaṃ  kiṃsamudayaṃ kiṃjātikaṃ kiṃpabhavaṃ? saḷāyatanaṃ nāmarūpanidānaṃ nāmarūpasamudayaṃ nāmarūpajātikaṃ nāmarūpapabhavaṃ| nāmarūpañ‍cidaṃ, bhikkhavai, kiṃnidānaṃ kiṃsamudayaṃ kiṃjātikaṃ kiṃpabhavaṃ? nāmarūpaṃ viñ‍ñāṇanidānaṃ viñ‍ñāṇasamudayaṃ viñ‍ñāṇajātikaṃ viñ‍ñāṇapabhavaṃ| viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cidaṃ, bhikkhavai, kiṃnidānaṃ kiṃsamudayaṃ kiṃjātikaṃ kiṃpabhavaṃ? viñ‍ñāṇaṃ saṅkhāranidānaṃ saṅkhārasamudayaṃ saṅkhārajātikaṃ saṅkhārapabhavaṃ| saṅkhārā cimai, bhikkhavai, kiṃnidānā kiṃsamudayā kiṃjātikā kiṃpabhavā? saṅkhārā avij‍jānidānā avij‍jāsamudayā avij‍jājātikā avij‍jāpabhavā|

‘‘iti khau, bhikkhavai, avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā; saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti| avij‍jāya tvaiva asaisavirāganiraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau; saṅkhāraniraudhā viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhau  …pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hautī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. mauḷiyaphaggunasuttaṃ

12. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, āhārā bhūtānaṃ vā sattānaṃ ṭhitiyā sambhavaisīnaṃ vā anuggahāya| katamai cattārau? kabaḷīkārau āhārau – auḷārikau vā sukhumau vā, phassau dutiyau, manausañ‍caitanā tatiyā, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ catutthaṃ| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau āhārā bhūtānaṃ vā sattānaṃ ṭhitiyā sambhavaisīnaṃ vā anuggahāyā’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai, āyasmā mauḷiyaphaggunau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, viñ‍ñāṇāhāraṃ āhāraitī’’ti? ‘‘nau kal‍lau pañhau’’ti bhagavā avauca – ‘‘‘āhāraitī’ti ahaṃ na vadāmi| ‘āhāraitī’ti cāhaṃ  vadaiyyaṃ, tatrassa kal‍lau pañhau – ‘kau nu khau, bhantai, āhāraitī’ti? aivaṃ cāhaṃ na vadāmi| aivaṃ maṃ avadantaṃ yau aivaṃ pucchaiyya – ‘kissa nu khau, bhantai, viñ‍ñāṇāhārau’ti, aisa kal‍lau pañhau| tatra kal‍laṃ vaiyyākaraṇaṃ – ‘viñ‍ñāṇāhārau āyatiṃ punabbhavābhinibbattiyā pac‍cayau, tasmiṃ bhūtai sati saḷāyatanaṃ, saḷāyatanapac‍cayā phassau’’’ti|

‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, phusatī’’ti? ‘‘nau kal‍lau pañhau’’ti bhagavā avauca – ‘‘‘phusatī’ti ahaṃ na vadāmi| ‘phusatī’ti cāhaṃ vadaiyyaṃ, tatrassa kal‍lau pañhau – ‘kau nu khau, bhantai, phusatī’ti? aivaṃ cāhaṃ na vadāmi| aivaṃ maṃ avadantaṃ yau aivaṃ pucchaiyya – ‘kiṃpac‍cayā nu khau, bhantai, phassau’ti, aisa kal‍lau pañhau| tatra kal‍laṃ vaiyyākaraṇaṃ – ‘saḷāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā’’’ti|

‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, vaidayatī’’ti vaidiyatīti (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰)? ‘‘nau kal‍lau pañhau’’ti bhagavā avauca – ‘‘‘vaidayatī’ti ahaṃ na vadāmi| ‘vaidayatī’ti cāhaṃ vadaiyyaṃ, tatrassa kal‍lau pañhau – ‘kau nu khau, bhantai, vaidayatī’ti? aivaṃ cāhaṃ na vadāmi| aivaṃ maṃ avadantaṃ yau aivaṃ pucchaiyya – ‘kiṃpac‍cayā nu khau, bhantai, vaidanā’ti, aisa kal‍lau pañhau| tatra kal‍laṃ vaiyyākaraṇaṃ – ‘phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā’’’ti|

‘‘kau  nu khau, bhantai, tasatī’’ti taṇhīyatīti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰)? ‘‘nau kal‍lau pañhau’’ti bhagavā avauca – ‘‘‘tasatī’ti ahaṃ na vadāmi| ‘tasatī’ti cāhaṃ  vadaiyyaṃ, tatrassa kal‍lau pañhau – ‘kau nu khau, bhantai, tasatī’ti? aivaṃ cāhaṃ na vadāmi| aivaṃ maṃ avadantaṃ yau aivaṃ pucchaiyya – ‘kiṃpac‍cayā nu khau, bhantai, taṇhā’ti, aisa kal‍lau pañhau| tatra kal‍laṃ vaiyyākaraṇaṃ – ‘vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā, taṇhāpac‍cayā upādāna’’’nti|

‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, upādiyatī’’ti? ‘‘nau kal‍lau pañhau’’ti bhagavā avauca – ‘‘‘upādiyatī’ti ahaṃ na vadāmi| ‘upādiyatī’ti cāhaṃ vadaiyyaṃ, tatrassa kal‍lau pañhau – ‘kau nu khau, bhantai, upādiyatī’ti? aivaṃ cāhaṃ na vadāmi| aivaṃ maṃ avadantaṃ yau aivaṃ pucchaiyya – ‘kiṃpac‍cayā nu khau, bhantai, upādāna’nti, aisa kal‍lau pañhau| tatra kal‍laṃ vaiyyākaraṇaṃ  – ‘taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ; upādānapac‍cayā bhavau’ti…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

‘‘chan‍naṃ tvaiva, phagguna, phassāyatanānaṃ asaisavirāganiraudhā phassaniraudhau; phassaniraudhā vaidanāniraudhau; vaidanāniraudhā taṇhāniraudhau; taṇhāniraudhā upādānaniraudhau; upādānaniraudhā bhavaniraudhau; bhavaniraudhā jātiniraudhau; jātiniraudhā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā nirujjhanti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hautī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. samaṇabrāhmaṇasuttaṃ

13. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā  vā jarāmaraṇaṃ nappajānanti, jarāmaraṇasamudayaṃ nappajānanti, jarāmaraṇaniraudhaṃ nappajānanti, jarāmaraṇaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ nappajānanti; jātiṃ…pai॰… bhavaṃ… upādānaṃ… taṇhaṃ… vaidanaṃ… phassaṃ… saḷāyatanaṃ… nāmarūpaṃ… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ… saṅkhārai nappajānanti, saṅkhārasamudayaṃ nappajānanti, saṅkhāraniraudhaṃ nappajānanti, saṅkhāraniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ nappajānanti, na mai tai, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā samaṇaisu vā samaṇasammatā brāhmaṇaisu vā brāhmaṇasammatā; na ca pana tai āyasmantau sāmañ‍ñatthaṃ vā brahmañ‍ñatthaṃ  brāhmañ‍ñatthaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰) mauggal‍lānabyākaraṇaṃ aulaukaitabbaṃ vā diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharanti|

‘‘yai ca khau kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā jarāmaraṇaṃ pajānanti, jarāmaraṇasamudayaṃ pajānanti, jarāmaraṇaniraudhaṃ pajānanti, jarāmaraṇaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānanti; jātiṃ…pai॰… bhavaṃ… upādānaṃ… taṇhaṃ… vaidanaṃ… phassaṃ… saḷāyatanaṃ… nāmarūpaṃ… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ… saṅkhārai pajānanti, saṅkhārasamudayaṃ pajānanti, saṅkhāraniraudhaṃ pajānanti, saṅkhāraniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānanti, tai khau mai, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā samaṇaisu caiva samaṇasammatā brāhmaṇaisu ca brāhmaṇasammatā; tai ca panāyasmantau sāmañ‍ñatthañ‍ca brahmañ‍ñatthañ‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharantī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. dutiyasamaṇabrāhmaṇasuttaṃ

14. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati…pai॰… ‘‘yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā imai dhammai nappajānanti, imaisaṃ dhammānaṃ samudayaṃ nappajānanti, imaisaṃ dhammānaṃ niraudhaṃ nappajānanti, imaisaṃ dhammānaṃ niraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ nappajānanti, katamai dhammai nappajānanti, katamaisaṃ dhammānaṃ samudayaṃ nappajānanti, katamaisaṃ dhammānaṃ niraudhaṃ nappajānanti, katamaisaṃ dhammānaṃ niraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ nappajānanti’’?

‘‘jarāmaraṇaṃ nappajānanti, jarāmaraṇasamudayaṃ nappajānanti, jarāmaraṇaniraudhaṃ nappajānanti, jarāmaraṇaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ nappajānanti; jātiṃ…pai॰… bhavaṃ… upādānaṃ… taṇhaṃ… vaidanaṃ… phassaṃ… saḷāyatanaṃ… nāmarūpaṃ… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ… saṅkhārai nappajānanti, saṅkhārasamudayaṃ nappajānanti, saṅkhāraniraudhaṃ nappajānanti, saṅkhāraniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ nappajānanti| imai dhammai nappajānanti, imaisaṃ dhammānaṃ samudayaṃ nappajānanti, imaisaṃ dhammānaṃ  niraudhaṃ nappajānanti, imaisaṃ dhammānaṃ niraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ nappajānanti| na mai tai, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā samaṇaisu vā samaṇasammatā brāhmaṇaisu vā brāhmaṇasammatā, na ca pana tai āyasmantau sāmañ‍ñatthaṃ vā brahmañ‍ñatthaṃ vā diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharanti|

‘‘yai  ca khau kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā imai dhammai pajānanti, imaisaṃ dhammānaṃ samudayaṃ pajānanti, imaisaṃ dhammānaṃ  niraudhaṃ pajānanti, imaisaṃ dhammānaṃ niraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānanti, katamai dhammai pajānanti, katamaisaṃ dhammānaṃ samudayaṃ pajānanti, katamaisaṃ dhammānaṃ niraudhaṃ pajānanti, katamaisaṃ dhammānaṃ niraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānanti?

‘‘jarāmaraṇaṃ pajānanti, jarāmaraṇasamudayaṃ pajānanti, jarāmaraṇaniraudhaṃ pajānanti, jarāmaraṇaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānanti; jātiṃ…pai॰… bhavaṃ… upādānaṃ… taṇhaṃ… vaidanaṃ… phassaṃ… saḷāyatanaṃ… nāmarūpaṃ… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ… saṅkhārai pajānanti, saṅkhārasamudayaṃ pajānanti, saṅkhāraniraudhaṃ pajānanti, saṅkhāraniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānanti| imai dhammai pajānanti, imaisaṃ dhammānaṃ samudayaṃ pajānanti, imaisaṃ dhammānaṃ niraudhaṃ pajānanti, imaisaṃ dhammānaṃ niraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānanti| tai khau mai, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā samaṇaisu caiva samaṇasammatā, brāhmaṇaisu ca brāhmaṇasammatā| tai ca panāyasmantau sāmañ‍ñatthañ‍ca brahmañ‍ñatthañ‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharantī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. kac‍cānagauttasuttaṃ

15. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati| atha  khau āyasmā kac‍cānagauttau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā kac‍cānagauttau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘sammādiṭṭhi sammādiṭṭhī’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, sammādiṭṭhi hautī’’ti?

‘‘dvayanissitau  khvāyaṃ, kac‍cāna, laukau yaibhuyyaina – atthitañ‍caiva natthitañ‍ca| laukasamudayaṃ khau, kac‍cāna, yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passatau yā laukai natthitā sā na hauti| laukaniraudhaṃ khau, kac‍cāna, yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passatau yā laukai atthitā sā na hauti| upayupādānābhinivaisavinibandhau upāyupādānābhinivaisavinibandhau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) khvāyaṃ, kac‍cāna, laukau yaibhuyyaina| tañ‍cāyaṃ upayupādānaṃ caitasau adhiṭṭhānaṃ abhinivaisānusayaṃ na upaiti na upādiyati nādhiṭṭhāti – ‘attā mai’ti| ‘dukkhamaiva uppaj‍jamānaṃ  uppaj‍jati, dukkhaṃ nirujjhamānaṃ nirujjhatī’ti na kaṅkhati na vicikicchati aparapac‍cayā ñāṇamaivassa aittha hauti| aittāvatā khau, kac‍cāna, sammādiṭṭhi hauti|

‘‘‘sabbaṃ atthī’ti khau, kac‍cāna, ayamaikau antau| ‘sabbaṃ natthī’ti ayaṃ dutiyau antau| aitai tai, kac‍cāna, ubhau antai anupagamma majjhaina tathāgatau dhammaṃ daisaiti – ‘avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā; saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti| avij‍jāya tvaiva asaisavirāganiraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau; saṅkhāraniraudhā viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhau…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hautī’’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dhammakathikasuttaṃ

16. sāvatthiyaṃ  …pai॰… atha khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘dhammakathikau  dhammakathikau’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, dhammakathikau hautī’’ti?

‘‘jarāmaraṇassa cai bhikkhu nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, ‘dhammakathikau bhikkhū’ti alaṃ vacanāya| jarāmaraṇassa cai bhikkhu nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, ‘dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau bhikkhū’ti alaṃ vacanāya| jarāmaraṇassa cai bhikkhu nibbidā virāgā niraudhā anupādāvimuttau hauti, ‘diṭṭhadhammanibbānappattau bhikkhū’ti alaṃ vacanāya|

‘‘jātiyā cai bhikkhu…pai॰… bhavassa cai bhikkhu… upādānassa cai bhikkhu… taṇhāya cai bhikkhu… vaidanāya cai bhikkhu… phassassa cai bhikkhu… saḷāyatanassa cai bhikkhu… nāmarūpassa cai bhikkhu… viñ‍ñāṇassa cai bhikkhu… saṅkhārānaṃ cai bhikkhu… avij‍jāya cai bhikkhu nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, ‘dhammakathikau bhikkhū’ti alaṃ vacanāya| avij‍jāya cai bhikkhu nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, ‘dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau bhikkhū’ti alaṃ vacanāya| avij‍jāya cai bhikkhu nibbidā virāgā niraudhā anupādāvimuttau hauti, ‘diṭṭhadhammanibbānappattau bhikkhū’ti alaṃ vacanāyā’’ti|

7. acailakassapasuttaṃ

17. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| atha  khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya rājagahaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| addasā  khau acailau kassapau  bhagavantaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau acailau kassapau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘pucchaiyyāma mayaṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ kañ‍cidaiva kiñ‍cidaiva (ka॰) daisaṃ, sacai nau bhavaṃ gautamau aukāsaṃ karauti pañhassa vaiyyākaraṇāyā’’ti|

‘‘akālau khau tāva, kassapa, pañhassa; antaragharaṃ paviṭṭhamhā’’ti| dutiyampi khau acailau kassapau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca ‘‘pucchaiyyāma mayaṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ kañ‍cidaiva daisaṃ, sacai nau bhavaṃ gautamau aukāsaṃ karauti pañhassa vaiyyākaraṇāyā’’ti| ‘‘akālau khau tāva, kassapa, pañhassa; antaragharaṃ paviṭṭhamhā’’ti| tatiyampi khau acailau kassapau…pai॰… antaragharaṃ paviṭṭhamhāti| aivaṃ vuttai, acailau kassapau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘na khau pana mayaṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ bahudaiva pucchitukāmā’’ti| ‘‘puccha, kassapa, yadākaṅkhasī’’ti|

‘‘kiṃ nu khau, bhau gautama, ‘sayaṃkataṃ dukkha’nti? ‘mā haivaṃ, kassapā’ti bhagavā avauca| ‘kiṃ pana, bhau gautama, paraṃkataṃ dukkha’nti? ‘mā haivaṃ, kassapā’ti bhagavā avauca| ‘kiṃ nu khau, bhau gautama, sayaṃkatañ‍ca paraṃkatañ‍ca dukkha’nti? ‘mā haivaṃ, kassapā’ti bhagavā avauca| ‘kiṃ  pana bhau gautama, asayaṃkāraṃ aparaṃkāraṃ adhic‍casamuppan‍naṃ dukkha’nti? ‘mā haivaṃ, kassapā’ti bhagavā avauca| ‘kiṃ nu  khau, bhau gautama, natthi dukkha’nti? ‘na khau, kassapa, natthi dukkhaṃ| atthi khau, kassapa, dukkha’nti| ‘taina hi bhavaṃ gautamau dukkhaṃ na jānāti, na passatī’ti| ‘na khvāhaṃ, kassapa, dukkhaṃ na jānāmi, na passāmi| jānāmi khvāhaṃ, kassapa, dukkhaṃ; passāmi khvāhaṃ, kassapa, dukkha’’’nti|

‘‘ki nu khau, bhau gautama, ‘sayaṃkataṃ dukkha’nti iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘mā haivaṃ, kassapā’ti vadaisi| ‘kiṃ pana, bhau gautama, paraṃkataṃ dukkha’nti iti  puṭṭhau samānau ‘mā haivaṃ, kassapā’ti vadaisi| ‘kiṃ nu khau, bhau gautama, sayaṃkatañ‍ca paraṃkatañ‍ca dukkha’nti iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘mā haivaṃ, kassapā’ti vadaisi| ‘kiṃ pana, bhau gautama, asayaṃkāraṃ aparaṃkāraṃ adhic‍casamuppan‍naṃ dukkha’nti iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘mā haivaṃ, kassapā’ti vadaisi| ‘kiṃ nu khau, bhau gautama, natthi dukkha’nti iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘na khau, kassapa, natthi dukkhaṃ, atthi khau, kassapa, dukkha’nti vadaisi| ‘taina hi bhavaṃ gautamau dukkhaṃ na jānāti na passatī’ti iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘na khvāhaṃ, kassapa, dukkhaṃ na jānāmi na passāmi| jānāmi khvāhaṃ, kassapa, dukkhaṃ; passāmi khvāhaṃ, kassapa, dukkha’nti vadaisi| ācikkhatu ca ayaṃ cakārau sī॰ pautthakai natthi mai, bhantai, bhagavā dukkhaṃ| daisaitu ca ayaṃ cakārau sī॰ pautthakai natthi mai, bhantai, bhagavā dukkha’’nti|

‘‘‘sau karauti sau paṭisaṃvaidayatī’ti paṭisaṃvaidiyatīti (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) khau, kassapa, āditau satau ‘sayaṃkataṃ dukkha’nti iti vadaṃ sassataṃ aitaṃ paraiti| ‘añ‍ñau karauti añ‍ñau paṭisaṃvaidayatī’ti khau, kassapa, vaidanābhitun‍nassa satau ‘paraṃkataṃ dukkha’nti  iti vadaṃ ucchaidaṃ aitaṃ paraiti| aitai tai, kassapa, ubhau antai anupagamma majjhaina tathāgatau dhammaṃ daisaiti – ‘avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā; saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti| avij‍jāya tvaiva asaisavirāganiraudhā  saṅkhāraniraudhau; saṅkhāraniraudhā viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhau…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hautī’’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai, acailau kassapau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai! saiyyathāpi, bhantai, nik‍kuj‍jitaṃ vā uk‍kuj‍jaiyya…pai॰… cakkhumantau rūpāni dakkhantīti; aivamaivaṃ bhagavatā anaikapariyāyaina dhammau pakāsitau| aisāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi dhammañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| labhaiyyāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ, labhaiyyaṃ upasampada’’nti|

‘‘yau khau, kassapa, añ‍ñatitthiyapubbau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai ākaṅkhati pabbaj‍jaṃ, ākaṅkhati upasampadaṃ, sau cattārau māsai parivasati| catun‍naṃ māsānaṃ ac‍cayaina ac‍cayaina parivuṭṭhaparivāsaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) (parivutthaparivāsaṃ) āraddhacittā bhikkhū bhikkhū ākaṅkhamānā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) pabbājainti upasampādainti bhikkhubhāvāya| api ca mayā puggalavaimattatā viditā’’ti|

‘‘sacai, bhantai, añ‍ñatitthiyapubbau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai ākaṅkhati pabbaj‍jaṃ, ākaṅkhati upasampadaṃ, cattārau māsai parivasati| catun‍naṃ māsānaṃ ac‍cayaina ac‍cayaina parivuṭṭhaparivāsaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) (parivutthaparivāsaṃ) āraddhacittā bhikkhū bhikkhū ākaṅkhamānā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) pabbājainti upasampādainti bhikkhubhāvāya| ahaṃ cattāri vassāni parivasissāmi, catun‍naṃ vassānaṃ ac‍cayaina ac‍cayaina parivuṭṭhaparivāsaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) (parivutthaparivāsaṃ) āraddhacittā bhikkhū pabbājaintu upasampādaintu bhikkhubhāvāyā’’ti|

alattha khau acailau kassapau bhagavatau santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ, alattha upasampadaṃ| acirūpasampan‍nau ca panāyasmā kassapau aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau nacirassaiva – yassatthāya  kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti tadanuttaraṃ – brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja vihāsi| ‘‘khīṇā jāti vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’’ti abbhañ‍ñāsi| añ‍ñatarau ca panāyasmā kassapau arahataṃ ahausīti| sattamaṃ|

8. timbarukasuttaṃ

18. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati| atha khau timbarukau paribbājakau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau timbarukau paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘‘kiṃ nu khau, bhau gautama, sayaṃkataṃ sukhadukkha’nti? ‘mā haivaṃ, timbarukā’ti bhagavā avauca| ‘kiṃ pana, bhau gautama, paraṃkataṃ sukhadukkha’nti? ‘mā haivaṃ, timbarukā’ti bhagavā avauca| ‘kiṃ nu khau, bhau gautama, sayaṃkatañ‍ca paraṃkatañ‍ca sukhadukkha’nti? ‘mā haivaṃ, timbarukā’ti bhagavā avauca| ‘kiṃ pana, bhau gautama, asayaṃkāraṃ aparaṃkāraṃ adhic‍casamuppan‍naṃ sukhadukkha’nti? ‘mā haivaṃ, timbarukā’ti bhagavā avauca| ‘kiṃ nu khau, bhau gautama, natthi sukhadukkha’nti? ‘na khau, timbaruka, natthi sukhadukkhaṃ; atthi khau, timbaruka, sukhadukkha’nti| ‘taina hi bhavaṃ gautamau sukhadukkhaṃ na jānāti, na passatī’ti? ‘na khvāhaṃ, timbaruka, sukhadukkhaṃ na jānāmi, na passāmi| jānāmi khvāhaṃ, timbaruka, sukhadukkhaṃ; passāmi khvāhaṃ, timbaruka, sukhadukkha’’’nti|

‘‘‘kiṃ  nu khau, bhau gautama, sayaṃkataṃ sukhadukkha’nti iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘mā haivaṃ, timbarukā’ti vadaisi| ‘kiṃ  pana, bhau gautama, paraṃkataṃ sukhadukkha’nti iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘mā  haivaṃ, timbarukā’ti vadaisi| ‘kiṃ nu khau, bhau gautama, sayaṃkatañ‍ca paraṃkatañ‍ca sukhadukkha’nti iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘mā haivaṃ, timbarukā’ti vadaisi| ‘kiṃ pana, bhau gautama, asayaṃkāraṃ aparaṃkāraṃ adhic‍casamuppan‍naṃ sukhadukkha’nti iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘mā haivaṃ, timbarukā’ti vadaisi| ‘kiṃ nu khau, bhau gautama, natthi sukhadukkha’nti iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘na khau, timbaruka, natthi sukhadukkhaṃ; atthi khau, timbaruka, sukhadukkha’nti vadaisi| ‘taina hi bhavaṃ gautamau sukhadukkhaṃ na jānāti, na passatī’ti iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘na khvāhaṃ, timbaruka, sukhadukkhaṃ na jānāmi, na passāmi| jānāmi khvāhaṃ, timbaruka, sukhadukkhaṃ; passāmi khvāhaṃ, timbaruka, sukhadukkha’nti vadaisi| ācikkhatu ca mai bhavaṃ gautamau sukhadukkhaṃ| daisaitu ca mai bhavaṃ gautamau sukhadukkha’’nti|

‘‘‘sā vaidanā, sau vaidayatī’ti khau, timbaruka, āditau satau ‘sayaṃkataṃ sukhadukkha’nti aivampāhaṃ na vadāmi| ‘añ‍ñā vaidanā, añ‍ñau vaidayatī’ti  khau, timbaruka, vaidanābhitun‍nassa satau ‘paraṃkataṃ sukhadukkha’nti aivampāhaṃ na vadāmi| aitai tai, timbaruka, ubhau antai anupagamma majjhaina tathāgatau dhammaṃ daisaiti – ‘avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā; saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti| avij‍jāya tvaiva asaisavirāganiraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau; saṅkhāraniraudhā viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhau…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hautī’’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai, timbarukau paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama…pai॰… aisāhaṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi dhammañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. bālapaṇḍitasuttaṃ

19. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘avij‍jānīvaraṇassa, bhikkhavai, bālassa taṇhāya sampayuttassa  aivamayaṃ kāyau samudāgatau| iti ayañ‍caiva kāyau bahiddhā ca nāmarūpaṃ, itthaitaṃ dvayaṃ, dvayaṃ paṭic‍ca phassau saḷaivāyatanāni  saḷāyatanāni (ka॰), yaihi phuṭṭhau bālau sukhadukkhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidayati aitaisaṃ vā añ‍ñataraina’’|

‘‘avij‍jānīvaraṇassa, bhikkhavai, paṇḍitassa taṇhāya sampayuttassa aivamayaṃ kāyau samudāgatau| iti ayañ‍caiva kāyau bahiddhā ca nāmarūpaṃ, itthaitaṃ dvayaṃ, dvayaṃ paṭic‍ca phassau saḷaivāyatanāni, yaihi phuṭṭhau paṇḍitau sukhadukkhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidayati aitaisaṃ vā añ‍ñataraina’’|

‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, kau visaisau kau adhippayāsau adhippāyau (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰), adhippāyasau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) adhi + pa + yasu + ṇa + sī = adhippayāsau kiṃ nānākaraṇaṃ paṇḍitassa bālainā’’ti? ‘‘bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā, bhagavaṃnaittikā, bhagavaṃpaṭisaraṇā| sādhu vata, bhantai, bhagavantaṃyaiva paṭibhātu aitassa bhāsitassa atthau| bhagavatau sutvā bhikkhū dhāraissantī’’ti|

‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha, bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘yāya ca, bhikkhavai, avij‍jāya nivutassa bālassa yāya ca taṇhāya sampayuttassa ayaṃ kāyau samudāgatau, sā caiva avij‍jā bālassa appahīnā sā ca taṇhā aparikkhīṇā| taṃ kissa haitu? na, bhikkhavai, bālau acari brahmacariyaṃ sammā dukkhakkhayāya| tasmā bālau kāyassa bhaidā kāyūpagau hauti, sau kāyūpagau samānau na parimuc‍cati jātiyā jarāmaraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi| na parimuc‍cati dukkhasmāti vadāmi|

‘‘yāya ca, bhikkhavai, avij‍jāya nivutassa paṇḍitassa yāya ca taṇhāya sampayuttassa ayaṃ kāyau samudāgatau, sā caiva avij‍jā paṇḍitassa pahīnā, sā ca taṇhā parikkhīṇā| taṃ kissa haitu? acari, bhikkhavai, paṇḍitau brahmacariyaṃ  sammā dukkhakkhayāya| tasmā paṇḍitau kāyassa bhaidā na kāyūpagau hauti| sau akāyūpagau samānau parimuc‍cati jātiyā jarāmaraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi| parimuc‍cati dukkhasmāti vadāmi| ayaṃ  khau, bhikkhavai, visaisau, ayaṃ adhippayāsau, idaṃ nānākaraṇaṃ paṇḍitassa bālaina yadidaṃ brahmacariyavāsau’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. pac‍cayasuttaṃ

20. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘paṭic‍casamuppādañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nai ca dhammai| taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha, bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, paṭic‍casamuppādau? jātipac‍cayā, bhikkhavai, jarāmaraṇaṃ| uppādā vā tathāgatānaṃ anuppādā vā tathāgatānaṃ, ṭhitāva sā dhātu dhammaṭṭhitatā dhammaniyāmatā idappac‍cayatā| taṃ tathāgatau abhisambujjhati abhisamaiti| abhisambujjhitvā abhisamaitvā ācikkhati daisaiti pañ‍ñāpaiti paṭṭhapaiti vivarati vibhajati uttānīkarauti| ‘passathā’ti cāha – ‘jātipac‍cayā, bhikkhavai, jarāmaraṇaṃ’’’|

‘‘bhavapac‍cayā, bhikkhavai, jāti…pai॰… upādānapac‍cayā, bhikkhavai, bhavau… taṇhāpac‍cayā, bhikkhavai, upādānaṃ… vaidanāpac‍cayā, bhikkhavai, taṇhā… phassapac‍cayā, bhikkhavai, vaidanā… saḷāyatanapac‍cayā, bhikkhavai, phassau… nāmarūpapac‍cayā, bhikkhavai, saḷāyatanaṃ… viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā, bhikkhavai, nāmarūpaṃ… saṅkhārapac‍cayā, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ… avij‍jāpac‍cayā, bhikkhavai, saṅkhārā uppādā vā tathāgatānaṃ anuppādā vā tathāgatānaṃ, ṭhitāva sā dhātu dhammaṭṭhitatā dhammaniyāmatā idappac‍cayatā| taṃ tathāgatau abhisambujjhati abhisamaiti| abhisambujjhitvā abhisamaitvā ācikkhati  daisaiti pañ‍ñāpaiti paṭṭhapaiti vivarati vibhajati uttānīkarauti| ‘passathā’ti cāha ‘avij‍jāpac‍cayā, bhikkhavai, saṅkhārā’| iti khau, bhikkhavai, yā tatra tathatā avitathatā anañ‍ñathatā idappac‍cayatā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, paṭic‍casamuppādau|

‘‘katamai ca, bhikkhavai, paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā dhammā? jarāmaraṇaṃ, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ saṅkhataṃ paṭic‍casamuppan‍naṃ khayadhammaṃ vayadhammaṃ virāgadhammaṃ niraudhadhammaṃ| jāti, bhikkhavai, anic‍cā saṅkhatā paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā  khayadhammā vayadhammā virāgadhammā niraudhadhammā| bhavau, bhikkhavai, anic‍cau saṅkhatau paṭic‍casamuppan‍nau khayadhammau vayadhammau virāgadhammau niraudhadhammau| upādānaṃ bhikkhavai…pai॰… taṇhā, bhikkhavai… vaidanā, bhikkhavai… phassau, bhikkhavai… saḷāyatanaṃ, bhikkhavai… nāmarūpaṃ, bhikkhavai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, bhikkhavai… saṅkhārā, bhikkhavai… avij‍jā, bhikkhavai, anic‍cā saṅkhatā paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā khayadhammā vayadhammā virāgadhammā niraudhadhammā| imai vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā dhammā|

‘‘yatau khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakassa ‘ayañ‍ca paṭic‍casamuppādau, imai ca paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā dhammā’ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhā haunti, sau vata pubbantaṃ vā paṭidhāvissati – ‘ahausiṃ nu khau ahaṃ nu khvāhaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) atītamaddhānaṃ, nanu khau ahausiṃ atītamaddhānaṃ, kiṃ nu khau ahausiṃ atītamaddhānaṃ, kathaṃ nu khau ahausiṃ atītamaddhānaṃ, kiṃ hutvā kiṃ ahausiṃ  nu khau ahaṃ atītamaddhāna’nti; aparantaṃ vā upadhāvissati apadhāvissati (ka॰) – ‘bhavissāmi nu khau ahaṃ anāgatamaddhānaṃ, nanu khau bhavissāmi anāgatamaddhānaṃ, kiṃ nu khau bhavissāmi anāgatamaddhānaṃ, kathaṃ nu khau bhavissāmi anāgatamaddhānaṃ, kiṃ hutvā kiṃ bhavissāmi nu khau ahaṃ anāgatamaddhāna’nti; aitarahi vā pac‍cuppan‍naṃ addhānaṃ ajjhattaṃ kathaṃkathī bhavissati – ‘ahaṃ nu khausmi, nau nu khausmi, kiṃ nu khausmi, kathaṃ nu khausmi, ayaṃ nu khau sattau kutau āgatau, sau kuhiṃ gamissatī’ti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| taṃ kissa haitu? tathāhi, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakassa ayañ‍ca paṭic‍casamuppādau imai ca paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā dhammā yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhā’’ti| dasamaṃ|

āhāravaggau dutiyau|

tassuddānaṃ –

āhāraṃ phaggunau caiva, dvai ca samaṇabrāhmaṇā|

kac‍cānagauttau dhammakathikaṃ, acailaṃ timbarukaina ca|

bālapaṇḍitatau caiva, dasamau pac‍cayaina cāti||

3. dasabalavaggau

1. dasabalasuttaṃ

21. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati…pai॰… ‘‘dasabalasaman‍nāgatau, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau catūhi ca vaisāraj‍jaihi saman‍nāgatau āsabhaṃ ṭhānaṃ paṭijānāti, parisāsu  sīhanādaṃ nadati, brahmacak‍kaṃ pavattaiti – iti  rūpaṃ iti rūpassa samudayau iti rūpassa atthaṅgamau, iti vaidanā iti vaidanāya samudayau iti vaidanāya atthaṅgamau, iti sañ‍ñā iti sañ‍ñāya samudayau iti sañ‍ñāya atthaṅgamau, iti saṅkhārā iti saṅkhārānaṃ samudayau iti saṅkhārānaṃ atthaṅgamau, iti viñ‍ñāṇaṃ iti viñ‍ñāṇassa samudayau iti viñ‍ñāṇassa atthaṅgamau| iti imasmiṃ sati idaṃ hauti, imassuppādā idaṃ uppaj‍jati| imasmiṃ asati idaṃ na hauti, imassa niraudhā idaṃ nirujjhati| yadidaṃ avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā; saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti| avij‍jāya tvaiva asaisavirāganiraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau; saṅkhāraniraudhā viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhau…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hautī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. dutiyadasabalasuttaṃ

22. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘dasabalasaman‍nāgatau, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau catūhi ca vaisāraj‍jaihi saman‍nāgatau āsabhaṃ ṭhānaṃ paṭijānāti, parisāsu sīhanādaṃ nadati, brahmacak‍kaṃ pavattaitti – ‘iti rūpaṃ iti rūpassa samudayau iti rūpassa atthaṅgamau, iti vaidanā iti vaidanāya samudayau iti vaidanāya  atthaṅgamau, iti sañ‍ñā iti sañ‍ñāya samudayau iti sañ‍ñāya atthaṅgamau, iti saṅkhārā iti saṅkhārānaṃ samudayau iti saṅkhārānaṃ atthaṅgamau, iti viñ‍ñāṇaṃ iti viñ‍ñāṇassa samudayau iti viñ‍ñāṇassa atthaṅgamau| iti imasmiṃ sati idaṃ hauti, imassuppādā idaṃ uppaj‍jati; imasmiṃ asati idaṃ na hauti imassa niraudhā idaṃ nirujjhati| yadidaṃ avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā; saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰…  aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti| avij‍jāya tvaiva asaisavirāganiraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau; saṅkhāraniraudhā viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhau…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hauti’’’|

‘‘aivaṃ svākkhātau, bhikkhavai, mayā dhammau uttānau vivaṭau pakāsitau chin‍napilautikau| aivaṃ svākkhātai khau, bhikkhavai, mayā dhammai uttānai vivaṭai pakāsitai chin‍napilautikai alamaiva saddhāpabbajitaina kulaputtaina vīriyaṃ ārabhituṃ – ‘kāmaṃ tacau ca nhāru nahāru (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) ca aṭṭhi  ca avasissatu, sarīrai upasussatu avasussatu ma॰ ni॰ 2.184 maṃsalauhitaṃ| yaṃ taṃ purisathāmaina purisavīriyaina purisaparak‍kamaina pattabbaṃ, na taṃ apāpuṇitvā vīriyassa saṇṭhānaṃ bhavissatī’’’ti|

‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhikkhavai, kusītau viharati vaukiṇṇau pāpakaihi akusalaihi dhammaihi, mahantañ‍ca sadatthaṃ parihāpaiti| āraddhavīriyau ca khau, bhikkhavai, sukhaṃ viharati pavivittau pāpakaihi akusalaihi dhammaihi, mahantañ‍ca sadatthaṃ paripūraiti| na, bhikkhavai, hīnaina  aggassa patti hauti| aggaina ca khau, bhikkhavai, aggassa patti hauti| maṇḍapaiyyamidaṃ, bhikkhavai, brahmacariyaṃ, satthā sammukhībhūtau| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, vīriyaṃ ārabhatha appattassa pattiyā, anadhigatassa adhigamāya, asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya| ‘aivaṃ nau ayaṃ amhākaṃ pabbaj‍jā avañjhā bhavissati saphalā saudrayā| yaisañ‍ca yaisaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰), yaisaṃ hi (pī॰ ka॰) mayaṃ paribhuñ‍jāma cīvara-piṇḍapātasaināsana-gilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraṃ taisaṃ tai kārā amhaisu mahapphalā bhavissanti mahānisaṃsā’ti – aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabbaṃ| attatthaṃ vā hi, bhikkhavai, sampassamānaina alamaiva appamādaina sampādaituṃ; paratthaṃ vā hi, bhikkhavai, sampassamānaina alamaiva appamādaina sampādaituṃ; ubhayatthaṃ vā hi, bhikkhavai, sampassamānaina alamaiva appamādaina sampādaitu’’nti| dutiyaṃ|

3. upanisasuttaṃ

23. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘jānatau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, passatau āsavānaṃ khayaṃ vadāmi, nau ajānatau nau apassatau| kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, jānatau kiṃ passatau āsavānaṃ khayau hauti? iti rūpaṃ iti rūpassa samudayau iti rūpassa atthaṅgamau, iti vaidanā…pai॰… iti sañ‍ñā… iti saṅkhārā… iti viñ‍ñāṇaṃ iti viñ‍ñāṇassa samudayau iti viñ‍ñāṇassa atthaṅgamauti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, jānatau aivaṃ passatau āsavānaṃ khayau hauti’’|

‘‘yampissa  taṃ, bhikkhavai, khayasmiṃ khayaiñ‍ñāṇaṃ, tampi saupanisaṃ vadāmi, nau anupanisaṃ| kā ca, bhikkhavai, khayaiñāṇassa upanisā? ‘vimuttī’tissa  vacanīyaṃ| vimuttimpāhaṃ vimuttimpahaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰), bhikkhavai, saupanisaṃ vadāmi, nau anupanisaṃ| kā ca, bhikkhavai, vimuttiyā  upanisā? ‘virāgau’tissa vacanīyaṃ| virāgampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, saupanisaṃ vadāmi, nau anupanisaṃ| kā ca, bhikkhavai, virāgassa upanisā? ‘nibbidā’tissa vacanīyaṃ| nibbidampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, saupanisaṃ vadāmi, nau anupanisaṃ| kā ca, bhikkhavai, nibbidāya upanisā? ‘yathābhūtañāṇadassana’ntissa vacanīyaṃ| yathābhūtañāṇadassanampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, saupanisaṃ vadāmi, nau anupanisaṃ| kā ca, bhikkhavai, yathābhūtañāṇadassanassa upanisā? ‘samādhī’tissa vacanīyaṃ| samādhimpāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, saupanisaṃ vadāmi, nau anupanisaṃ|

‘‘kā ca, bhikkhavai, samādhissa upanisā? ‘sukha’ntissa vacanīyaṃ| sukhampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, saupanisaṃ vadāmi, nau anupanisaṃ| kā ca, bhikkhavai, sukhassa upanisā? ‘passaddhī’tissa vacanīyaṃ| passaddhimpāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, saupanisaṃ vadāmi, nau anupanisaṃ| kā ca, bhikkhavai, passaddhiyā upanisā? ‘pītī’tissa vacanīyaṃ| pītimpāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, saupanisaṃ vadāmi, nau anupanisaṃ| kā ca, bhikkhavai, pītiyā upanisā? ‘pāmauj‍ja’ntissa vacanīyaṃ| pāmauj‍jampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, saupanisaṃ vadāmi, nau anupanisaṃ| kā ca, bhikkhavai, pāmauj‍jassa upanisā? ‘saddhā’tissa vacanīyaṃ| saddhampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, saupanisaṃ vadāmi, nau anupanisaṃ|

‘‘kā  ca, bhikkhavai, saddhāya upanisā? ‘dukkha’ntissa vacanīyaṃ| dukkhampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, saupanisaṃ vadāmi, nau anupanisaṃ| kā ca, bhikkhavai, dukkhassa upanisā? ‘jātī’tissa vacanīyaṃ| jātimpāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, saupanisaṃ vadāmi, nau anupanisaṃ| kā ca, bhikkhavai, jātiyā upanisā? ‘bhavau’tissa vacanīyaṃ| bhavampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, saupanisaṃ vadāmi, nau anupanisaṃ| kā ca, bhikkhavai, bhavassa upanisā? ‘upādāna’ntissa vacanīyaṃ| upādānampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, saupanisaṃ vadāmi, nau anupanisaṃ| kā ca, bhikkhavai, upādānassa upanisā? ‘taṇhā’tissa vacanīyaṃ| taṇhampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, saupanisaṃ vadāmi, nau anupanisaṃ|

‘‘kā ca, bhikkhavai, taṇhāya upanisā? ‘vaidanā’tissa vacanīyaṃ…pai॰… ‘phassau’tissa vacanīyaṃ… ‘saḷāyatana’ntissa vacanīyaṃ… ‘nāmarūpa’ntissa vacanīyaṃ… ‘viñ‍ñāṇa’ntissa vacanīyaṃ… ‘saṅkhārā’tissa vacanīyaṃ| saṅkhāraipāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, saupanisai vadāmi, nau anupanisai| kā ca, bhikkhavai, saṅkhārānaṃ upanisā? ‘avij‍jā’tissa vacanīyaṃ|

‘‘iti  khau, bhikkhavai, avij‍jūpanisā saṅkhārā, saṅkhārūpanisaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇūpanisaṃ nāmarūpaṃ, nāmarūpūpanisaṃ saḷāyatanaṃ, saḷāyatanūpanisau phassau, phassūpanisā vaidanā, vaidanūpanisā taṇhā, taṇhūpanisaṃ upādānaṃ, upādānūpanisau bhavau, bhavūpanisā jāti, jātūpanisaṃ dukkhaṃ, dukkhūpanisā saddhā, saddhūpanisaṃ pāmauj‍jaṃ, pāmauj‍jūpanisā pīti, pītūpanisā passaddhi, passaddhūpanisaṃ sukhaṃ, sukhūpanisau samādhi, samādhūpanisaṃ yathābhūtañāṇadassanaṃ, yathābhūtañāṇadassanūpanisā  nibbidā, nibbidūpanisau virāgau, virāgūpanisā vimutti, vimuttūpanisaṃ khayaiñāṇaṃ|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, uparipabbatai thul‍laphusitakai daivai vassantai  taṃ udakaṃ yathānin‍naṃ pavattamānaṃ pabbatakandarapadarasākhā paripūraiti| pabbatakandarapadarasākhāparipūrā kusaubbhai kussubbhai (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰), kusubbhai (pī॰) ṇvādi 129 suttaṃ aulaukaitabbaṃ paripūrainti| kusaubbhā paripūrā mahāsaubbhai paripūrainti| mahāsaubbhā paripūrā kun‍nadiyau paripūrainti| kun‍nadiyau paripūrā mahānadiyau paripūrainti| mahānadiyau paripūrā mahāsamuddaṃ paripūrainti|

‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, avij‍jūpanisā saṅkhārā, saṅkhārūpanisaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇūpanisaṃ nāmarūpaṃ, nāmarūpūpanisaṃ saḷāyatanaṃ, saḷāyatanūpanisau phassau, phassūpanisā vaidanā, vaidanūpanisā taṇhā, taṇhūpanisaṃ upādānaṃ, upādānūpanisau bhavau, bhavūpanisā jāti, jātūpanisaṃ  dukkhaṃ, dukkhūpanisā saddhā, saddhūpanisaṃ pāmauj‍jaṃ, pāmauj‍jūpanisā pīti, pītūpanisā passaddhi, passaddhūpanisaṃ sukhaṃ, sukhūpanisau samādhi, samādhūpanisaṃ yathābhūtañāṇadassanaṃ, yathābhūtañāṇadassanūpanisā nibbidā, nibbidūpanisau virāgau, virāgūpanisā vimutti, vimuttūpanisaṃ khayaiñāṇa’’nti| tatiyaṃ|

4. añ‍ñatitthiyasuttaṃ

24. rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai| atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya rājagahaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| atha khau āyasmatau sāriputtassa aitadahausi – ‘‘atippagau khau tāva rājagahai piṇḍāya carituṃ| yaṃnūnāhaṃ yaina añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ ārāmau tainupasaṅkamaiyya’’nti|

atha  khau āyasmā sāriputtau yaina añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ  ārāmau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā  taihi añ‍ñatitthiyaihi paribbājakaihi saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ tai añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aitadavaucuṃ –

‘‘santāvusau, sāriputta, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā kammavādā sayaṃkataṃ dukkhaṃ pañ‍ñapainti| santi panāvusau sāriputta, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā kammavādā paraṃkataṃ dukkhaṃ pañ‍ñapainti| santāvusau sāriputta, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā kammavādā sayaṃkatañ‍ca paraṃkatañ‍ca dukkhaṃ pañ‍ñapainti| santi panāvusau sāriputta, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā kammavādā asayaṃkāraṃ aparaṃkāraṃ adhic‍casamuppan‍naṃ dukkhaṃ pañ‍ñapainti| idha, panāvusau sāriputta, samaṇau gautamau kiṃvādī kimakkhāyī? kathaṃ byākaramānā ca mayaṃ vuttavādinau caiva samaṇassa gautamassa assāma, na ca samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ abhūtaina abbhācikkhaiyyāma, dhammassa cānudhammaṃ byākaraiyyāma, na ca kauci sahadhammikau vādānupātau vādānuvādau (ka॰) dī॰ ni॰ 1.381 gārayhaṃ ṭhānaṃ āgacchaiyyā’’ti?

‘‘paṭic‍casamuppan‍naṃ khau, āvusau, dukkhaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā| kiṃ paṭic‍ca? phassaṃ paṭic‍ca| iti  vadaṃ vuttavādī caiva bhagavatau assa, na ca bhagavantaṃ abhūtaina abbhācikkhaiyya, dhammassa cānudhammaṃ byākaraiyya, na ca kauci sahadhammikau vādānupātau gārayhaṃ ṭhānaṃ āgacchaiyya|

‘‘tatrāvusau, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā kammavādā sayaṃkataṃ dukkhaṃ pañ‍ñapainti tadapi phassapac‍cayā| yaipi tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā kammavādā paraṃkataṃ dukkhaṃ pañ‍ñapainti tadapi phassapac‍cayā| yaipi tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā kammavādā sayaṃkatañ‍ca paraṃkatañ‍ca dukkhaṃ pañ‍ñapainti tadapi phassapac‍cayā| yaipi  tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā kammavādā asayaṃkāraṃ aparaṃkāraṃ adhic‍casamuppan‍naṃ dukkhaṃ pañ‍ñapainti tadapi phassapac‍cayā|

‘‘tatrāvusau, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā kammavādā sayaṃkataṃ dukkhaṃ pañ‍ñapainti, tai vata añ‍ñatra phassā paṭisaṃvaidissantīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| yaipi tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā kammavādā paraṃkataṃ dukkhaṃ pañ‍ñapainti, tai vata añ‍ñatra phassā paṭisaṃvaidissantīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| yaipi tai  samaṇabrāhmaṇā kammavādā sayaṃkatañ‍ca paraṃkatañ‍ca dukkhaṃ pañ‍ñapainti, tai vata añ‍ñatra phassā paṭisaṃvaidissantīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| yaipi tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā kammavādā asayaṃkāraṃ aparaṃkāraṃ adhic‍casamuppan‍naṃ dukkhaṃ pañ‍ñapainti, tai vata añ‍ñatra phassā paṭisaṃvaidissantīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jatī’’ti|

assausi khau āyasmā ānandau āyasmatau sāriputtassa taihi añ‍ñatitthiyaihi paribbājakaihi saddhiṃ imaṃ kathāsal‍lāpaṃ| atha khau āyasmā ānandau rājagahai piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau yāvatakau āyasmatau sāriputtassa taihi añ‍ñatitthiyaihi paribbājakaihi saddhiṃ ahausi kathāsal‍lāpau taṃ sabbaṃ bhagavatau āraucaisi|

‘‘sādhu sādhu, ānanda, yathā taṃ sāriputtau sammā byākaramānau  byākaraiyya| paṭic‍casamuppan‍naṃ khau, ānanda, dukkhaṃ vuttaṃ mayā| kiṃ paṭic‍ca? phassaṃ paṭic‍ca| iti vadaṃ vuttavādī caiva mai assa, na ca maṃ abhūtaina abbhācikkhaiyya, dhammassa cānudhammaṃ byākaraiyya, na ca kauci sahadhammikau vādānupātau gārayhaṃ ṭhānaṃ āgacchaiyya|

‘‘tatrānanda, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā kammavādā sayaṃkataṃ dukkhaṃ pañ‍ñapainti tadapi phassapac‍cayā| yaipi  tai…pai॰… yaipi tai…pai॰… yaipi tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā kammavādā asayaṃkāraṃ aparaṃkāraṃ adhic‍casamuppan‍naṃ dukkhaṃ pañ‍ñapainti tadapi phassapac‍cayā|

‘‘tatrānanda, yaipi tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā kammavādā sayaṃkataṃ dukkhaṃ pañ‍ñapainti, tai vata añ‍ñatra phassā paṭisaṃvaidissantīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| yaipi tai…pai॰… yaipi tai…pai॰… yaipi tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā kammavādā asayaṃkāraṃ aparaṃkāraṃ adhic‍casamuppan‍naṃ dukkhaṃ pañ‍ñapainti, tai vata añ‍ñatra phassā paṭisaṃvaidissantīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘aikamidāhaṃ, ānanda, samayaṃ idhaiva rājagahai viharāmi vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| atha khvāhaṃ, ānanda, pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya rājagahaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisiṃ| tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, aitadahausi – ‘atippagau  khau tāva rājagahai piṇḍāya carituṃ| yaṃnūnāhaṃ yaina añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ ārāmau tainupasaṅkamaiyya’’’nti|

‘‘atha khvāhaṃ, ānanda, yaina añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ ārāmau tainupasaṅkamiṃ; upasaṅkamitvā taihi añ‍ñatitthiyaihi paribbājakaihi  saddhiṃ sammaudiṃ| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau maṃ, ānanda, tai añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aitadavaucuṃ –

‘santāvusau gautama, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā kammavādā sayaṃkataṃ dukkhaṃ pañ‍ñapainti| santi panāvusau gautama, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā kammavādā paraṃkataṃ dukkhaṃ pañ‍ñapainti| santāvusau gautama, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā kammavādā sayaṃkatañ‍ca paraṃkatañ‍ca dukkhaṃ pañ‍ñapainti| santi panāvusau gautama, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā kammavādā asayaṃkāraṃ aparaṃkāraṃ adhic‍casamuppan‍naṃ dukkhaṃ pañ‍ñapainti| idha nau āyasmā gautamau kiṃvādī kimakkhāyī? kathaṃ byākaramānā ca mayaṃ vuttavādinau caiva āyasmatau gautamassa assāma, na ca āyasmantaṃ gautamaṃ abhūtaina abbhācikkhaiyyāma, dhammassa cānudhammaṃ byākaraiyyāma, na ca kauci sahadhammikau vādānupātau gārayhaṃ ṭhānaṃ āgacchaiyyā’’’ti?

‘‘aivaṃ  vuttāhaṃ, ānanda, tai añ‍ñatitthiyai paribbājakai aitadavaucaṃ – ‘paṭic‍casamuppan‍naṃ khau, āvusau, dukkhaṃ vuttaṃ mayā| kiṃ paṭic‍ca? phassaṃ paṭic‍ca| iti vadaṃ vuttavādī caiva mai assa, na ca maṃ abhūtaina abbhācikkhaiyya, dhammassa cānudhammaṃ byākaraiyya, na ca kauci sahadhammikau vādānupātau gārayhaṃ ṭhānaṃ āgacchaiyyā’’’ti|

‘‘tatrāvusau, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā kammavādā sayaṃkataṃ dukkhaṃ pañ‍ñapainti tadapi phassapac‍cayā| yaipi tai…pai॰… yaipi tai…pai॰…  yaipi tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā kammavādā asayaṃkāraṃ aparaṃkāraṃ adhic‍casamuppan‍naṃ dukkhaṃ pañ‍ñapainti tadapi phassapac‍cayā|

‘‘tatrāvusau, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā kammavādā sayaṃkataṃ dukkhaṃ pañ‍ñapainti, tai vata añ‍ñatra phassā paṭisaṃvaidissantīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| yaipi tai…pai॰… yaipi tai…pai॰… yaipi tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā kammavādā asayaṃkāraṃ aparaṃkāraṃ adhic‍casamuppan‍naṃ dukkhaṃ pañ‍ñapainti, tai vata añ‍ñatra phassā paṭisaṃvaidissantīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jatī’’ti| ‘‘acchariyaṃ bhantai, abbhutaṃ bhantai! yatra hi nāma aikaina padaina sabbau atthau vuttau bhavissati| siyā nu khau, bhantai, aisaivatthau vitthāraina vuc‍camānau gambhīrau caiva assa gambhīrāvabhāsau cā’’ti?

‘‘taina hānanda, tañ‍ñaivaittha paṭibhātū’’ti| ‘‘sacai maṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘jarāmaraṇaṃ, āvusau ānanda, kiṃnidānaṃ kiṃsamudayaṃ kiṃjātikaṃ kiṃpabhava’nti? aivaṃ puṭṭhauhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ byākaraiyyaṃ – ‘jarāmaraṇaṃ khau, āvusau, jātinidānaṃ jātisamudayaṃ jātijātikaṃ jātipabhava’nti| aivaṃ puṭṭhauhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ byākaraiyyaṃ|

‘‘sacai  maṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘jāti panāvusau ānanda, kiṃnidānā kiṃsamudayā kiṃjātikā kiṃpabhavā’ti? aivaṃ puṭṭhauhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ byākaraiyyaṃ – ‘jāti khau, āvusau, bhavanidānā bhavasamudayā bhavajātikā bhavappabhavā’ti| aivaṃ puṭṭhauhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ byākaraiyyaṃ|

‘‘sacai maṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘bhavau panāvusau ānanda, kiṃnidānau kiṃsamudayau kiṃjātikau kiṃpabhavau’ti? aivaṃ puṭṭhauhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ byākaraiyyaṃ – ‘bhavau khau, āvusau, upādānanidānau  upādānasamudayau upādānajātikau upādānappabhavau’ti| aivaṃ puṭṭhauhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ byākaraiyyaṃ|

‘‘sacai maṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – upādānaṃ panāvusau…pai॰… taṇhā panāvusau…pai॰… vaidanā panāvusau…pai॰… sacai maṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘phassau panāvusau ānanda, kiṃnidānau kiṃsamudayau kiṃjātikau kiṃpabhavau’ti? aivaṃ puṭṭhauhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ byākaraiyyaṃ – ‘phassau khau, āvusau, saḷāyatananidānau saḷāyatanasamudayau saḷāyatanajātikau saḷāyatanappabhavau’ti| ‘chan‍naṃtvaiva, āvusau, phassāyatanānaṃ asaisavirāganiraudhā phassaniraudhau; phassaniraudhā vaidanāniraudhau; vaidanāniraudhā taṇhāniraudhau; taṇhāniraudhā upādānaniraudhau; upādānaniraudhā bhavaniraudhau; bhavaniraudhā jātiniraudhau; jātiniraudhā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā nirujjhanti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hautī’ti| aivaṃ puṭṭhauhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ byākaraiyya’’nti| catutthaṃ|

5. bhūmijasuttaṃ

25. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati| atha khau āyasmā bhūmijau sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaināyasmā sāriputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā  āyasmatā sāriputtaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau  āyasmā bhūmijau āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘santāvusau sāriputta, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā kammavādā sayaṃkataṃ sukhadukkhaṃ pañ‍ñapainti| santi panāvusau sāriputta, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā kammavādā paraṃkataṃ sukhadukkhaṃ pañ‍ñapainti| santāvusau sāriputta, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā kammavādā sayaṃkatañ‍ca paraṃkatañ‍ca sukhadukkhaṃ pañ‍ñapainti| santi panāvusau sāriputta, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā kammavādā asayaṃkāraṃ aparaṃkāraṃ adhic‍casamuppan‍naṃ sukhadukkhaṃ pañ‍ñapainti| idha nau, āvusau sāriputta, bhagavā kiṃvādī kimakkhāyī, kathaṃ byākaramānā ca mayaṃ vuttavādinau caiva bhagavatau assāma, na ca bhagavantaṃ abhūtaina abbhācikkhaiyyāma, dhammassa cānudhammaṃ byākaraiyyāma, na ca kauci sahadhammikau vādānupātau gārayhaṃ ṭhānaṃ āgacchaiyyā’’ti?

‘‘paṭic‍casamuppan‍naṃ khau, āvusau, sukhadukkhaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā| kiṃ paṭic‍ca? phassaṃ paṭic‍ca| iti vadaṃ vuttavādī caiva bhagavatau assa, na ca bhagavantaṃ abhūtaina abbhācikkhaiyya, dhammassa cānudhammaṃ byākaraiyya, na ca kauci sahadhammikau vādānupātau gārayhaṃ ṭhānaṃ āgacchaiyya|

‘‘tatrāvusau, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā kammavādā sayaṃkataṃ sukhadukkhaṃ pañ‍ñapainti, tadapi phassapac‍cayā| yaipi tai…pai॰… yaipi tai…pai॰… yaipi tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā kammavādā asayaṃkāraṃ aparaṃkāraṃ adhic‍casamuppan‍naṃ sukhadukkhaṃ pañ‍ñapainti, tadapi phassapac‍cayā|

‘‘tatrāvusau, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā kammavādā sayaṃkataṃ sukhadukkhaṃ  pañ‍ñapainti, tai vata añ‍ñatra phassā paṭisaṃvaidissantīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| yaipi tai…pai॰ … yaipi tai…pai॰… yaipi tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā kammavādā asayaṃkāraṃ aparaṃkāraṃ adhic‍casamuppan‍naṃ sukhadukkhaṃ pañ‍ñapainti, tai vata añ‍ñatra phassā paṭisaṃvaidissantīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jatī’’ti|

assausi  khau āyasmā ānandau āyasmatau sāriputtassa āyasmatā bhūmijaina saddhiṃ imaṃ kathāsal‍lāpaṃ| atha khau āyasmā ānandau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau yāvatakau āyasmatau sāriputtassa āyasmatā bhūmijaina saddhiṃ ahausi kathāsal‍lāpau taṃ sabbaṃ bhagavatau āraucaisi|

‘‘sādhu sādhu, ānanda, yathā taṃ sāriputtau sammā byākaramānau byākaraiyya| paṭic‍casamuppan‍naṃ khau, ānanda, sukhadukkhaṃ vuttaṃ mayā| kiṃ paṭic‍ca? phassaṃ paṭic‍ca| iti vadaṃ vuttavādī caiva mai assa, na ca maṃ abhūtaina abbhācikkhaiyya, dhammassa cānudhammaṃ byākaraiyya, na ca kauci sahadhammikau vādānupātau gārayhaṃ ṭhānaṃ āgacchaiyya|

‘‘tatrānanda, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā kammavādā sayaṃkataṃ sukhadukkhaṃ pañ‍ñapainti tadapi phassapac‍cayā| yaipi tai…pai॰… yaipi tai…pai॰… yaipi tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā kammavādā asayaṃkāraṃ aparaṃkāraṃ adhic‍casamuppan‍naṃ sukhadukkhaṃ pañ‍ñapainti tadapi phassapac‍cayā|

‘‘tatrānanda, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā kammavādā sayaṃkataṃ sukhadukkhaṃ pañ‍ñapainti, tai vata añ‍ñatra phassā paṭisaṃvaidissantīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| yaipi tai…pai॰… yaipi tai…pai॰… yaipi tai samaṇabrāhmaṇākammavādā asayaṃkāraṃ aparaṃkāraṃ adhic‍casamuppan‍naṃ sukhadukkhaṃ pañ‍ñapainti, tai vata añ‍ñatra phassā paṭisaṃvaidissantīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘kāyai vā hānanda, sati kāyasañ‍caitanāhaitu uppaj‍jati ajjhattaṃ  sukhadukkhaṃ| vācāya vā hānanda, sati vacīsañ‍caitanāhaitu uppaj‍jati ajjhattaṃ sukhadukkhaṃ| manai vā hānanda, sati manausañ‍caitanāhaitu uppaj‍jati ajjhattaṃ sukhadukkhaṃ avij‍jāpac‍cayā ca|

‘‘sāmaṃ vā taṃ, ānanda, kāyasaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti, yaṃpac‍cayāssa yaṃpac‍cayāya (syā॰ kaṃ॰), yaṃpac‍cayā yaṃ (ka॰) taṃ uppaj‍jati ajjhattaṃ sukhadukkhaṃ| parai vā taṃ parai vāssa taṃ (sī॰ pī॰), parai vāyataṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰), ānanda, kāyasaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharaunti, yaṃpac‍cayāssa taṃ uppaj‍jati ajjhattaṃ sukhadukkhaṃ| sampajānau vā taṃ, ānanda, kāyasaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti yaṃpac‍cayāssa taṃ uppaj‍jati ajjhattaṃ sukhadukkhaṃ| asampajānau  vā taṃ, ānanda, kāyasaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti yaṃpac‍cayāssa taṃ uppaj‍jati ajjhattaṃ sukhadukkhaṃ|

‘‘sāmaṃ vā taṃ, ānanda, vacīsaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti yaṃpac‍cayāssa taṃ uppaj‍jati ajjhattaṃ sukhadukkhaṃ| parai  vā taṃ, ānanda, vacīsaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharaunti yaṃpac‍cayāssa taṃ uppaj‍jati ajjhattaṃ sukhadukkhaṃ| sampajānau vā taṃ, ānanda…pai॰… asampajānau vā taṃ, ānanda, vacīsaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti yaṃpac‍cayāssa taṃ uppaj‍jati ajjhattaṃ sukhadukkhaṃ|

‘‘sāmaṃ vā taṃ, ānanda, manausaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti yaṃpac‍cayāssa taṃ uppaj‍jati ajjhattaṃ sukhadukkhaṃ| parai vā taṃ, ānanda, manausaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharaunti yaṃpac‍cayāssa taṃ uppaj‍jati ajjhattaṃ sukhadukkhaṃ| sampajānau vā taṃ, ānanda…pai॰… asampajānau vā taṃ, ānanda, manausaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti yaṃpac‍cayāssa taṃ uppaj‍jati ajjhattaṃ sukhadukkhaṃ|

‘‘imaisu, ānanda, dhammaisu avij‍jā anupatitā| avij‍jāya tvaiva, ānanda, asaisavirāganiraudhā sau kāyau na hauti yaṃpac‍cayāssa taṃ uppaj‍jati ajjhattaṃ sukhadukkhaṃ| sā vācā na hauti yaṃpac‍cayāssa taṃ uppaj‍jati ajjhattaṃ sukhadukkhaṃ| sau manau na hauti yaṃpac‍cayāssa taṃ uppaj‍jati  ajjhattaṃ sukhadukkhaṃ| khaittaṃ taṃ na hauti…pai॰… vatthu taṃ na hauti…pai॰… āyatanaṃ taṃ na hauti…pai॰… adhikaraṇaṃ taṃ na hauti yaṃpac‍cayāssa taṃ uppaj‍jati ajjhattaṃ sukhadukkha’’nti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. upavāṇasuttaṃ

26. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati| atha khau āyasmā upavāṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā upavāṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca  –

‘‘santi, bhantai, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā sayaṃkataṃ dukkhaṃ pañ‍ñapainti| santi pana, bhantai, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā paraṃkataṃ dukkhaṃ pañ‍ñapainti| santi pana, bhantai, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā sayaṃkatañ‍ca paraṃkatañ‍ca dukkhaṃ pañ‍ñapainti| santi pana, bhantai, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā asayaṃkāraṃ aparaṃkāraṃ adhic‍casamuppan‍naṃ dukkhaṃ pañ‍ñapainti| idha nau, bhantai, bhagavā kiṃvādī kimakkhāyī kathaṃ byākaramānā ca mayaṃ vuttavādinau caiva bhagavatau assāma, na ca bhagavantaṃ abhūtaina  abbhācikkhaiyyāma, dhammassa cānudhammaṃ byākaraiyyāma, na ca kauci sahadhammikau vādānupātau gārayhaṃ ṭhānaṃ āgacchaiyyā’’ti?

‘‘paṭic‍casamuppan‍naṃ khau, upavāṇa, dukkhaṃ vuttaṃ mayā| kiṃ paṭic‍ca? phassaṃ paṭic‍ca| iti vadaṃ vuttavādī caiva mai assa, na ca maṃ abhūtaina abbhācikkhaiyya, dhammassa cānudhammaṃ byākaraiyya, na ca kauci sahadhammikau vādānupātau gārayhaṃ ṭhānaṃ āgacchaiyya|

‘‘tatra, upavāṇa, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā sayaṃkataṃ dukkhaṃ pañ‍ñapainti, tadapi phassapac‍cayā| yaipi tai…pai॰… yaipi tai…pai॰… yaipi tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā asayaṃkāraṃ aparaṃkāraṃ adhic‍casamuppan‍naṃ dukkhaṃ pañ‍ñapainti tadapi phassapac‍cayā|

‘‘tatra, upavāṇa, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā sayaṃkataṃ dukkhaṃ pañ‍ñapainti, tai vata añ‍ña phassā paṭisaṃvaidissantīti  naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| yaipi tai…pai॰… yaipi tai…pai॰… yaipi tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā asayaṃkāraṃ aparaṃkāraṃ adhic‍casamuppan‍naṃ dukkhaṃ pañ‍ñapainti, tai vata añ‍ñatra phassā paṭisaṃvaidissantīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jatī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. pac‍cayasuttaṃ

27. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘avij‍jāpac‍cayā, bhikkhavai, saṅkhārā; saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, jarāmaraṇaṃ? yā taisaṃ taisaṃ sattānaṃ tamhi tamhi sattanikāyai jarā jīraṇatā khaṇḍic‍caṃ pālic‍caṃ valittacatā āyunau saṃhāni indriyānaṃ paripākau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati jarā| yā taisaṃ taisaṃ sattānaṃ tamhā tamhā sattanikāyā cuti cavanatā bhaidau antaradhānaṃ mac‍cu maraṇaṃ kālakiriyā khandhānaṃ bhaidau kaḷaivarassa nikkhaipau; idaṃ vuc‍cati maraṇaṃ| iti ayañ‍ca jarā idañ‍ca maraṇaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, jarāmaraṇaṃ| jātisamudayā jarāmaraṇasamudayau; jātiniraudhā jarāmaraṇaniraudhau| ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau jarāmaraṇaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā| saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi, sammāsaṅkappau, sammāvācā, sammākammantau, sammāājīvau, sammāvāyāmau, sammāsati, sammāsamādhi|

‘‘katamā  ca, bhikkhavai, jāti…pai॰… katamau ca, bhikkhavai, bhavau… katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, upādānaṃ… katamā  ca, bhikkhavai, taṇhā… katamā ca, bhikkhavai, vaidanā… katamau ca, bhikkhavai, phassau… katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, saḷāyatanaṃ… katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, nāmarūpaṃ… katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ…?

‘‘katamai  ca, bhikkhavai, saṅkhārā? tayaumai, bhikkhavai, saṅkhārā – kāyasaṅkhārau, vacīsaṅkhārau, cittasaṅkhārau| imai vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, saṅkhārā| avij‍jāsamudayā saṅkhārasamudayau; avij‍jāniraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau| ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau saṅkhāraniraudhagāminī paṭipadā| saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi|

‘‘yatau khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau aivaṃ pac‍cayaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ pac‍cayasamudayaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ pac‍cayaniraudhaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ pac‍cayaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānāti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau diṭṭhisampan‍nau itipi, dassanasampan‍nau itipi, āgatau imaṃ saddhammaṃ itipi, passati imaṃ saddhammaṃ itipi, saikkhaina ñāṇaina saman‍nāgatau itipi, saikkhāya vij‍jāya saman‍nāgatau itipi, dhammasautaṃ samāpan‍nau itipi, ariyau nibbaidhikapañ‍ñau itipi, amatadvāraṃ āhac‍ca tiṭṭhati itipī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. bhikkhusuttaṃ

28. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘tatra khau…pai॰… idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu jarāmaraṇaṃ pajānāti, jarāmaraṇasamudayaṃ pajānāti, jarāmaraṇaniraudhaṃ pajānāti, jarāmaraṇaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānāti, jātiṃ pajānāti…pai॰… bhavaṃ pajānāti… upādānaṃ pajānāti… taṇhaṃ  pajānāti… vaidanaṃ pajānāti… phassaṃ pajānāti… saḷāyatanaṃ pajānāti… nāmarūpaṃ pajānāti… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ pajānāti… saṅkhārai pajānāti, saṅkhārasamudayaṃ pajānāti, saṅkhāraniraudhaṃ pajānāti, saṅkhāraniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānāti|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, jarāmaraṇaṃ? yā taisaṃ taisaṃ sattānaṃ tamhi tamhi sattanikāyai jarā jīraṇatā khaṇḍic‍caṃ pālic‍caṃ valittacatā āyunau saṃhāni indriyānaṃ paripākau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati jarā| yā taisaṃ taisaṃ sattānaṃ tamhā tamhā sattanikāyā cuti cavanatā bhaidau antaradhānaṃ mac‍cu maraṇaṃ kālakiriyā khandhānaṃ bhaidau kaḷaivarassa nikkhaipau; idaṃ vuc‍cati  maraṇaṃ| iti ayaṃ ca jarā idañ‍ca maraṇaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, jarāmaraṇaṃ| jātisamudayā  jarāmaraṇasamudayau; jātiniraudhā jarāmaraṇaniraudhau| ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau jarāmaraṇaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā| saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, jāti…pai॰… katamau ca, bhikkhavai, bhavau… katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, upādānaṃ… vaidanā… phassau… saḷāyatanaṃ… nāmarūpaṃ… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ…|

‘‘katamai ca, bhikkhavai, saṅkhārā? tayaumai, bhikkhavai, saṅkhārā – kāyasaṅkhārau, vacīsaṅkhārau, cittasaṅkhārau| imai vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, saṅkhārā| avij‍jāsamudayā saṅkhārasamudayau; avij‍jāniraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau| ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau saṅkhāraniraudhagāminī paṭipadā| saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi|

‘‘yatau khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ jarāmaraṇaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ jarāmaraṇasamudayaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ jarāmaraṇaniraudhaṃ  pajānāti, aivaṃ jarāmaraṇaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ jātiṃ pajānāti…pai॰… bhavaṃ… upādānaṃ… taṇhaṃ… vaidanaṃ… phassaṃ… saḷāyatanaṃ… nāmarūpaṃ… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ … saṅkhārai… saṅkhārasamudayaṃ… saṅkhāraniraudhaṃ… aivaṃ saṅkhāraniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānāti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu diṭṭhisampan‍nau itipi, dassanasampan‍nau itipi, āgatau imaṃ saddhammaṃ itipi, passati imaṃ saddhammaṃ itipi, saikkhaina ñāṇaina saman‍nāgatau itipi, saikkhāya vij‍jāya saman‍nāgatau itipi, dhammasautaṃ samāpan‍nau itipi, ariyau nibbaidhikapañ‍ñau itipi, amatadvāraṃ āhac‍ca tiṭṭhati itipī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. samaṇabrāhmaṇasuttaṃ

29. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘tatra khau…pai॰… yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā  vā jarāmaraṇaṃ na parijānanti, jarāmaraṇasamudayaṃ na parijānanti, jarāmaraṇaniraudhaṃ na parijānanti, jarāmaraṇaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ na parijānanti, jātiṃ na parijānanti…pai॰… bhavaṃ… upādānaṃ… taṇhaṃ… vaidanaṃ… phassaṃ… saḷāyatanaṃ… nāmarūpaṃ… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ… saṅkhārai… saṅkhārasamudayaṃ… saṅkhāraniraudhaṃ… saṅkhāraniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ na parijānanti| na maitai, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā samaṇaisu vā samaṇasammatā brāhmaṇaisu vā brāhmaṇasammatā| na ca panaitai  āyasmantau sāmañ‍ñatthaṃ vā brahmañ‍ñatthaṃ vā diṭṭhaiva dhammai  sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharanti’’|

‘‘yai ca khau kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā jarāmaraṇaṃ parijānanti, jarāmaraṇasamudayaṃ parijānanti, jarāmaraṇaniraudhaṃ parijānanti, jarāmaraṇaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ parijānanti, jātiṃ parijānanti…pai॰… bhavaṃ… upādānaṃ… taṇhaṃ… vaidanaṃ… phassaṃ… saḷāyatanaṃ… nāmarūpaṃ… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ… saṅkhārai parijānanti, saṅkhārasamudayaṃ parijānanti, saṅkhāraniraudhaṃ parijānanti, saṅkhāraniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ parijānanti| tai khau mai, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā samaṇaisu caiva samaṇasammatā brāhmaṇaisu ca brāhmaṇasammatā| tai ca panāyasmantau sāmañ‍ñatthañ‍ca brahmañ‍ñatthañ‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharantī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. dutiyasamaṇabrāhmaṇasuttaṃ

30. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘tatra khau…pai॰… yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā jarāmaraṇaṃ nappajānanti, jarāmaraṇasamudayaṃ nappajānanti, jarāmaraṇaniraudhaṃ nappajānanti, jarāmaraṇaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ nappajānanti tai vata jarāmaraṇaṃ samatik‍kamma ṭhassantīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| jātiṃ nappajānanti…pai॰… bhavaṃ… upādānaṃ… taṇhaṃ… vaidanaṃ… phassaṃ… saḷāyatanaṃ… nāmarūpaṃ… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ… saṅkhārai nappajānanti, saṅkhārasamudayaṃ nappajānanti, saṅkhāraniraudhaṃ nappajānanti, saṅkhāraniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ nappajānanti tai vata saṅkhārai samatik‍kamma ṭhassantīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati’’|

‘‘yai  ca khau kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā jarāmaraṇaṃ pajānanti, jarāmaraṇasamudayaṃ pajānanti, jarāmaraṇaniraudhaṃ pajānanti, jarāmaraṇaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānanti tai vata jarāmaraṇaṃ samatik‍kamma ṭhassantīti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| jātiṃ pajānanti…pai॰… bhavaṃ… upādānaṃ… taṇhaṃ… vaidanaṃ… phassaṃ… saḷāyatanaṃ… nāmarūpaṃ… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ… saṅkhārai pajānanti, saṅkhārasamudayaṃ pajānanti, saṅkhāraniraudhaṃ pajānanti, saṅkhāraniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānanti| tai vata saṅkhārai samatik‍kamma ṭhassantīti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jatī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

dasabalavaggau  tatiyau|

tassuddānaṃ –

dvai  dasabalā upanisā ca, añ‍ñatitthiyabhūmijau|

upavāṇau pac‍cayau bhikkhu, dvai ca samaṇabrāhmaṇāti||

4. kaḷārakhattiyavaggau

1. bhūtasuttaṃ

31. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati| tatra khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘vuttamidaṃ, sāriputta, pārāyanai pārāyaṇai (sī॰) ajitapañhai –

‘‘yai ca saṅkhātadhammāsai, yai ca saikkhā puthū idha|

taisaṃ mai nipakau iriyaṃ, puṭṭhau pabrūhi mārisā’’ti||

‘‘imassa nu khau, sāriputta, saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa kathaṃ vitthāraina atthau daṭṭhabbau’’ti? aivaṃ  vuttai, āyasmā sāriputtau tuṇhī ahausi| dutiyampi khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ āmantaisi…pai॰… dutiyampi khau āyasmā sāriputtau tuṇhī ahausi| tatiyampi khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘vuttamidaṃ, sāriputta, pārāyanai ajitapañhai –

‘‘yai ca saṅkhātadhammāsai, yai ca saikkhā puthū idha|

taisaṃ mai nipakau iriyaṃ, puṭṭhau pabrūhi mārisā’’ti||

‘‘imassa  nu khau, sāriputta, saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa kathaṃ vitthāraina atthau daṭṭhabbau’’ti? tatiyampi khau āyasmā sāriputtau tuṇhī ahausi|

‘‘bhūtamidanti, sāriputta, passasī’’ti? bhūtamidanti, bhantai, yathābhūtaṃ  sammappañ‍ñāya passati| bhūtamidanti yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya disvā bhūtassa nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hauti| tadāhārasambhavanti yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passati| tadāhārasambhavanti yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya disvā āhārasambhavassa nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau  hauti| tadāhāraniraudhā yaṃ bhūtaṃ taṃ niraudhadhammanti yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passati| tadāhāraniraudhā yaṃ bhūtaṃ taṃ niraudhadhammanti yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya disvā niraudhadhammassa nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hauti| aivaṃ khau, bhantai, saikkhau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhantai, saṅkhātadhammau hauti? bhūtamidanti, bhantai, yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passati| bhūtamidanti yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya disvā bhūtassa nibbidā virāgā niraudhā anupādā vimuttau hauti| tadāhārasambhavanti yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passati| tadāhārasambhavanti yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya disvā āhārasambhavassa nibbidā virāgā niraudhā anupādā vimuttau hauti| tadāhāraniraudhā yaṃ bhūtaṃ taṃ niraudhadhammanti yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passati| tadāhāraniraudhā yaṃ bhūtaṃ taṃ niraudhadhammanti yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya disvā niraudhadhammassa nibbidā virāgā niraudhā  anupādā vimuttau hauti| aivaṃ khau, bhantai, saṅkhātadhammau hauti| iti khau, bhantai, yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ pārāyanai ajitapañhai –

‘‘yai  ca saṅkhātadhammāsai, yai ca saikkhā puthū idha|

taisaṃ mai nipakau iriyaṃ, puṭṭhau pabrūhi mārisā’’ti||

‘‘imassa khvāhaṃ, bhantai, saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāmī’’ti|

‘‘sādhu sādhu, sāriputta, bhūtamidanti, sāriputta, yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passati| bhūtamidanti yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya disvā bhūtassa nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭippan‍nau hauti| tadāhārasambhavanti yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passati| tadāhārasambhavanti yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya disvā āhārasambhavassa nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hauti| tadāhāraniraudhā yaṃ bhūtaṃ niraudhadhammanti yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passati| tadāhāraniraudhā yaṃ bhūtaṃ taṃ niraudhadhammanti yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya disvā niraudhadhammassa nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hauti| aivaṃ khau, sāriputta, saikkhau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, sāriputta, saṅkhātadhammau hauti? bhūtamidanti, sāriputta, yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passati| bhūtamidanti yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya disvā  bhūtassa nibbidā virāgā niraudhā anupādā vimuttau hauti| tadāhārasambhavanti yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passati| tadāhārasambhavanti yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya disvā āhārasambhavassa nibbidā virāgā niraudhā anupādā vimuttau hauti| tadāhāraniraudhā yaṃ bhūtaṃ taṃ niraudhadhammanti yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passati| tadāhāraniraudhā  yaṃ bhūtaṃ taṃ niraudhadhammanti yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñā  disvā niraudhadhammassa nibbidā virāgā niraudhā anupādā vimuttau hauti| aivaṃ khau, sāriputta, saṅkhātadhammau hauti| iti khau, sāriputta, yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ pārāyanai ajitapañhai –

‘‘yai ca saṅkhātadhammāsai, yai ca saikkhā puthū idha|

taisaṃ mai nipakau iriyaṃ, puṭṭhau pabrūhi mārisā’’ti||

‘‘imassa khau sāriputta saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthau daṭṭhabbau’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. kaḷārasuttaṃ

32. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati| atha khau kaḷārakhattiyau bhikkhu yaināyasmā sāriputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā sāriputtaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau kaḷārakhattiyau bhikkhu āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘mauḷiyaphaggunau, āvusau sāriputta, bhikkhu sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattauti| na hi nūna sau āyasmā imasmiṃ dhammavinayai assāsamalatthāti| taina hāyasmā sāriputtau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai assāsaṃ pattau’’ti?

‘‘na khvāhaṃ, āvusau, kaṅkhāmī’’ti| ‘‘āyatiṃ, panāvusau’’ti?

‘‘na khvāhaṃ, āvusau, vicikicchāmī’’ti|

atha khau kaḷārakhattiyau bhikkhu uṭṭhāyāsanā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau kaḷārakhattiyau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ  aitadavauca – ‘‘āyasmatā, bhantai, sāriputtaina añ‍ñā byākatā – ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāmī’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘aihi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, mama vacanaina sāriputtaṃ āmantaihi – ‘satthā taṃ, āvusau sāriputta, āmantaitī’’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau sau bhikkhu bhagavatau paṭissutvā yaināyasmā sāriputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘satthā taṃ, āvusau sāriputta, āmantaitī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, āvusau’’ti khau āyasmā sāriputtau tassa bhikkhunau paṭissutvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tayā, sāriputta, añ‍ñā byākatā – ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāmī’’ti? ‘‘na khau, bhantai, aitaihi padaihi aitaihi byañ‍janaihi atthau atthau ca (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) vuttau’’ti| ‘‘yaina kainacipi, sāriputta, pariyāyaina kulaputtau añ‍ñaṃ byākarauti, atha khau byākataṃ byākatatau daṭṭhabba’’nti| ‘‘nanu ahampi, bhantai, aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘na khau, bhantai, aitaihi padaihi aitaihi byañ‍janaihi atthau vuttau’’’ti|

‘‘sacai taṃ, sāriputta, aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘kathaṃ jānatā pana tayā, āvusau sāriputta, kathaṃ passatā añ‍ñā  byākatā – khīṇā jāti vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānāmī’ti| aivaṃ puṭṭhau tvaṃ, sāriputta, kinti byākaraiyyāsī’’ti?
