‘‘sacai maṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘kathaṃ jānatā pana tayā, āvusau sāriputta, kathaṃ passatā añ‍ñā byākatā – khīṇā  jāti vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānāmī’ti; aivaṃ puṭṭhauhaṃ puṭṭhau ahaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰), puṭṭhāhaṃ (pī॰ ka॰), bhantai, aivaṃ byākaraiyyaṃ – ‘yaṃnidānā, āvusau, jāti, tassa nidānassa khayā khīṇasmiṃ khīṇāmhīti viditaṃ| khīṇāmhīti viditvā – khīṇājāti vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānāmī’ti| aivaṃ puṭṭhauhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ byākaraiyya’’nti|

‘‘sacai pana taṃ, sāriputta, aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘jāti panāvusau sāriputta, kiṃnidānā kiṃsamudayā kiṃjātikā kiṃpabhavā’ti? aivaṃ puṭṭhau taṃ, sāriputta, kinti byākaraiyyāsī’’ti? ‘‘sacai maṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘jāti panāvusau sāriputta, kiṃnidānā kiṃsamudayā kiṃjātikā kiṃpabhavā’ti? aivaṃ puṭṭhauhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ byākaraiyyaṃ – ‘jāti khau, āvusau, bhavanidānā bhavasamudayā  bhavajātikā bhavappabhavā’ti| aivaṃ puṭṭhauhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ byākaraiyya’’nti|

‘‘sacai pana taṃ, sāriputta, aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘bhavau panāvusau sāriputta, kiṃnidānau kiṃsamudayau kiṃjātikau kiṃpabhavau’ti? aivaṃ puṭṭhau tvaṃ, sāriputta, kinti byākaraiyyāsī’’ti? ‘‘sacai maṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘bhavau panāvusau sāriputta, kiṃnidānau kiṃsamudayau kiṃjātikau kiṃpabhavau’ti? aivaṃ puṭṭhauhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ byākaraiyyaṃ – ‘bhavau khau, āvusau, upādānanidānau upādānasamudayau upādānajātikau upādānappabhavau’ti| aivaṃ puṭṭhauhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ byākaraiyya’’nti|

‘‘sacai  pana taṃ, sāriputta, aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘upādānaṃ panāvusau…pai॰… sacai pana taṃ, sāriputta, aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – taṇhā panāvusau sāriputta, kiṃnidānā kiṃsamudayā kiṃjātikā kiṃpabhavā’ti? aivaṃ puṭṭhau tvaṃ, sāriputta, kinti byākaraiyyāsī’’ti? ‘‘sacai  maṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – taṇhā panāvusau sāriputta, kiṃnidānā kiṃsamudayā kiṃjātikā kiṃpabhavā’ti? aivaṃ puṭṭhauhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ byākaraiyyaṃ – ‘taṇhā khau, āvusau, vaidanānidānā vaidanāsamudayā vaidanājātikā vaidanāpabhavā’ti| aivaṃ puṭṭhauhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ byākaraiyya’’nti|

‘‘sacai  pana taṃ, sāriputta, aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘kathaṃ jānatau pana tai, āvusau sāriputta, kathaṃ passatau yā vaidanāsu nandī sā na upaṭṭhāsī’ti| aivaṃ puṭṭhau tvaṃ, sāriputta, kinti byākaraiyyāsī’’ti? ‘‘sacai maṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘kathaṃ jānatau pana tai, āvusau sāriputta, kathaṃ passatau yā vaidanāsu nandī sā na upaṭṭhāsī’ti aivaṃ puṭṭhauhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ byākaraiyyaṃ – ‘tissau khau imā, āvusau, vaidanā| katamā tissau? sukhā vaidanā, dukkhā vaidanā, adukkhamasukhā vaidanā| imā khau, āvusau, tissau vaidanā anic‍cā| yadanic‍caṃ taṃ dukkhanti viditaṃ viditā (ṭīkā), yā vaidanāsu nandī sā na upaṭṭhāsī’ti| aivaṃ, puṭṭhauhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ byākaraiyya’’nti|

‘‘sādhu sādhu, sāriputta| ayampi khau, sāriputta, pariyāyau, aitassaiva atthassa saṃkhittaina vaiyyākaraṇāya – ‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci vaidayitaṃ taṃ dukkhasmi’’’nti|

‘‘sacai  pana taṃ, sāriputta, aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘kathaṃ vimaukkhā pana tayā, āvusau sāriputta, añ‍ñā byākatā – khīṇā jāti vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānāmī’ti? aivaṃ puṭṭhau tvaṃ, sāriputta, kinti byākaraiyyāsī’’ti? ‘‘sacai maṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘kathaṃ vimaukkhā pana tayā, āvusau sāriputta, añ‍ñā byākatā  – khīṇā jāti vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānāmī’ti| aivaṃ puṭṭhauhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ byākaraiyyaṃ – ‘ajjhattaṃ  vimaukkhā khvāhaṃ, āvusau, sabbupādānakkhayā tathā satau viharāmi yathā sataṃ viharantaṃ āsavā nānussavanti, attānañ‍ca nāvajānāmī’ti| aivaṃ puṭṭhauhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ byākaraiyya’’nti|

‘‘sādhu  sādhu, sāriputta| ayampi khau sāriputta, pariyāyau aitassaiva atthassa saṃkhittaina vaiyyākaraṇāya – yai āsavā samaṇaina vuttā taisvāhaṃ na kaṅkhāmi, tai mai pahīnāti na vicikicchāmī’’ti| idamavauca bhagavā| idaṃ vatvā sugatau uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ pāvisi|

tatra khau āyasmā sāriputtau acirapak‍kantassa bhagavatau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘pubbai appaṭisaṃviditaṃ maṃ, āvusau, bhagavā paṭhamaṃ pañhaṃ apucchi, tassa mai ahausi dandhāyitattaṃ| yatau ca khau mai, āvusau, bhagavā paṭhamaṃ pañhaṃ anumaudi, tassa mayhaṃ, āvusau, aitadahausi – divasaṃ caipi maṃ bhagavā aitamatthaṃ pucchaiyya añ‍ñamañ‍ñaihi padaihi añ‍ñamañ‍ñaihi pariyāyaihi, divasampāhaṃ bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ byākaraiyyaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaihi padaihi añ‍ñamañ‍ñaihi pariyāyaihi| rattiṃ caipi maṃ bhagavā aitamatthaṃ pucchaiyya añ‍ñamañ‍ñaihi padaihi añ‍ñamañ‍ñaihi pariyāyaihi, rattimpāhaṃ bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ byākaraiyyaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaihi padaihi añ‍ñamañ‍ñaihi pariyāyaihi| rattindivaṃ rattidivaṃ (ka॰) caipi maṃ bhagavā aitamatthaṃ pucchaiyya añ‍ñamañ‍ñaihi padaihi añ‍ñamañ‍ñaihi pariyāyaihi, rattindivampāhaṃ bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ byākaraiyyaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaihi padaihi añ‍ñamañ‍ñaihi pariyāyaihi| dvai  rattindivāni caipi maṃ bhagavā aitamatthaṃ pucchaiyya…pai॰… dvai rattindivānipāhaṃ bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ byākaraiyyaṃ…pai॰… tīṇi rattindivāni caipi maṃ bhagavā aitamatthaṃ pucchaiyya…pai॰… tīṇi rattindivānipāhaṃ bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ byākaraiyyaṃ…pai॰… cattāri rattindivāni caipi maṃ bhagavā aitamatthaṃ pucchaiyya…pai॰… cattāri rattindivānipāhaṃ  bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ byākaraiyyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ca rattindivāni caipi maṃ bhagavā aitamatthaṃ pucchaiyya…pai॰… pañ‍ca rattindivānipāhaṃ bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ byākaraiyyaṃ…pai॰… cha rattindivāni caipi maṃ bhagavā aitamatthaṃ pucchaiyya…pai॰… cha rattindivānipāhaṃ bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ byākaraiyyaṃ…pai॰… satta rattindivāni caipi maṃ bhagavā aitamatthaṃ pucchaiyya añ‍ñamañ‍ñaihi padaihi añ‍ñamañ‍ñaihi pariyāyaihi, satta rattindivānipāhaṃ bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ byākaraiyyaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaihi padaihi añ‍ñamañ‍ñaihi pariyāyaihī’’ti|

atha khau kaḷārakhattiyau bhikkhu uṭṭhāyāsanā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau kaḷārakhattiyau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘āyasmatā, bhantai, sāriputtaina sīhanādau naditau – pubbai appaṭisaṃviditaṃ maṃ, āvusau, bhagavā paṭhamaṃ pañhaṃ apucchi, tassa mai ahausi dandhāyitattaṃ| yatau  ca khau mai, āvusau, bhagavā paṭhamaṃ pañhaṃ anumaudi, tassa mayhaṃ, āvusau, aitadahausi – divasaṃ caipi maṃ bhagavā aitamatthaṃ pucchaiyya añ‍ñamañ‍ñaihi padaihi añ‍ñamañ‍ñaihi pariyāyaihi, divasampāhaṃ bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ byākaraiyyaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaihi padaihi añ‍ñamañ‍ñaihi pariyāyaihi; rattiṃ caipi…pai॰… rattindivaṃ caipi maṃ bhagavā…pai॰… dvai rattindivāni caipi maṃ bhagavā…pai॰… tīṇi… cattāri… pañ‍ca… cha… satta rattindivāni caipi maṃ bhagavā aitamatthaṃ pucchaiyya  añ‍ñamañ‍ñaihi padaihi añ‍ñamañ‍ñaihi pariyāyaihi, satta rattindivānipāhaṃ bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ byākaraiyyaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaihi padaihi añ‍ñamañ‍ñaihi pariyāyaihī’’ti|

‘‘sā hi, bhikkhu, sāriputtassa dhammadhātu suppaṭividdhā, yassā dhammadhātuyā suppaṭividdhattā divasaṃ caipāhaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitamatthaṃ pucchaiyyaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaihi padaihi añ‍ñamañ‍ñaihi pariyāyaihi, divasampi mai sāriputtau aitamatthaṃ byākaraiyya añ‍ñamañ‍ñaihi padaihi añ‍ñamañ‍ñaihi pariyāyaihi| rattiṃ caipāhaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitamatthaṃ pucchaiyyaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaihi padaihi añ‍ñamañ‍ñaihi pariyāyaihi, rattimpi mai sāriputtau aitamatthaṃ byākaraiyya…pai॰… rattindivaṃ caipāhaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitamatthaṃ pucchaiyyaṃ, rattindivampi mai sāriputtau aitamatthaṃ byākaraiyya… dvai rattindivāni caipāhaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitamatthaṃ pucchaiyyaṃ, dvai rattindivānipi mai sāriputtau aitamatthaṃ byākaraiyya… tīṇi rattindivāni caipāhaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitamatthaṃ pucchaiyyaṃ, tīṇi rattindivānipi  mai sāriputtau aitamatthaṃ  byākaraiyya… cattāri rattindivāni caipāhaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitamatthaṃ pucchaiyyaṃ, cattāri rattindivānipi mai sāriputtau aitamatthaṃ byākaraiyya… pañ‍ca rattindivāni caipāhaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitamatthaṃ pucchaiyyaṃ, pañ‍ca rattindivānipi mai sāriputtau aitamatthaṃ byākaraiyya… cha rattindivāni caipāhaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitamatthaṃ pucchaiyyaṃ, cha rattindivānipi mai sāriputtau aitamatthaṃ byākaraiyya… satta rattindivāni caipāhaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitamatthaṃ pucchaiyyaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaihi padaihi añ‍ñamañ‍ñaihi pariyāyaihi, satta rattindivānipi mai sāriputtau aitamatthaṃ byākaraiyya añ‍ñamañ‍ñaihi padaihi añ‍ñamañ‍ñaihi pariyāyaihī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. ñāṇavatthusuttaṃ

33. sāvatthiyaṃ…pai॰… ‘‘catucattārīsaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, ñāṇavatthūni daisaissāmi, taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ  manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘katamāni katamāni ca (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰), bhikkhavai, catucattārīsaṃ ñāṇavatthūni? jarāmaraṇai  ñāṇaṃ, jarāmaraṇasamudayai ñāṇaṃ, jarāmaraṇaniraudhai ñāṇaṃ, jarāmaraṇaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya ñāṇaṃ; jātiyā ñāṇaṃ, jātisamudayai ñāṇaṃ, jātiniraudhai ñāṇaṃ, jātiniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya ñāṇaṃ; bhavai ñāṇaṃ, bhavasamudayai ñāṇaṃ, bhavaniraudhai ñāṇaṃ, bhavaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya ñāṇaṃ; upādānai ñāṇaṃ, upādānasamudayai ñāṇaṃ, upādānaniraudhai ñāṇaṃ, upādānaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya  ñāṇaṃ; taṇhāya ñāṇaṃ, taṇhāsamudayai ñāṇaṃ, taṇhāniraudhai ñāṇaṃ, taṇhāniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya ñāṇaṃ; vaidanāya ñāṇaṃ, vaidanāsamudayai ñāṇaṃ, vaidanāniraudhai ñāṇaṃ, vaidanāniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya ñāṇaṃ; phassai ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… saḷāyatanai ñāṇaṃ… nāmarūpai ñāṇaṃ… viñ‍ñāṇai ñāṇaṃ… saṅkhāraisu ñāṇaṃ, saṅkhārasamudayai ñāṇaṃ, saṅkhāraniraudhai ñāṇaṃ, saṅkhāraniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya ñāṇaṃ| imāni vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, catucattārīsaṃ ñāṇavatthūni|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, jarāmaraṇaṃ? yā taisaṃ taisaṃ sattānaṃ tamhi tamhi sattanikāyai jarā jīraṇatā khaṇḍic‍caṃ pālic‍caṃ valittacatā āyunau saṃhāni  indriyānaṃ paripākau, ayaṃ vuc‍cati jarā| yā taisaṃ taisaṃ sattānaṃ tamhā tamhā sattanikāyā cuti cavanatā bhaidau antaradhānaṃ mac‍cu maraṇaṃ kālakiriyā khandhānaṃ bhaidau kaḷaivarassa nikkhaipau| idaṃ vuc‍cati maraṇaṃ| iti ayañ‍ca jarā, idañ‍ca maraṇaṃ; idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, jarāmaraṇaṃ|

‘‘jātisamudayā jarāmaraṇasamudayau; jātiniraudhā jarāmaraṇaniraudhau; ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau jarāmaraṇaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi|

‘‘yatau khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau aivaṃ jarāmaraṇaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ jarāmaraṇasamudayaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ jarāmaraṇaniraudhaṃ  pajānāti, aivaṃ jarāmaraṇaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānāti, idamassa dhammai ñāṇaṃ| sau iminā dhammaina diṭṭhaina viditaina akālikaina pattaina pariyaugāḷhaina atītānāgataina yaṃ naiti|

‘‘yai  khau kaici atītamaddhānaṃ samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā jarāmaraṇaṃ abbhañ‍ñaṃsu, jarāmaraṇasamudayaṃ abbhañ‍ñaṃsu, jarāmaraṇaniraudhaṃ abbhañ‍ñaṃsu, jarāmaraṇaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ abbhañ‍ñaṃsu, sabbai tai aivamaiva abbhañ‍ñaṃsu, saiyyathāpāhaṃ aitarahi|

‘‘yaipi hi kaici anāgatamaddhānaṃ samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā jarāmaraṇaṃ abhijānissanti, jarāmaraṇasamudayaṃ abhijānissanti, jarāmaraṇaniraudhaṃ abhijānissanti, jarāmaraṇaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ abhijānissanti, sabbai tai aivamaiva abhijānissanti, saiyyathāpāhaṃ aitarahīti| idamassa anvayai ñāṇaṃ|

‘‘yatau khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakassa imāni dvai ñāṇāni parisuddhāni haunti pariyaudātāni – dhammai ñāṇañ‍ca anvayai ñāṇañ‍ca| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau diṭṭhisampan‍nau itipi, dassanasampan‍nau itipi, āgatau imaṃ saddhammaṃ itipi, passati imaṃ saddhammaṃ itipi, saikkhaina ñāṇaina saman‍nāgatau itipi, saikkhāya vij‍jāya saman‍nāgatau itipi, dhammasautaṃ samāpan‍nau itipi, ariyau nibbaidhikapañ‍ñau itipi, amatadvāraṃ āhac‍ca tiṭṭhati itipīti|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, jāti…pai॰… katamau ca, bhikkhavai, bhavau… katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, upādānaṃ… katamā ca, bhikkhavai taṇhā… katamā ca, bhikkhavai, vaidanā… katamau ca, bhikkhavai, phassau… katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, saḷāyatanaṃ… katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, nāmarūpaṃ … katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ… katamai ca, bhikkhavai, saṅkhārā? tayaumai, bhikkhavai, saṅkhārā – kāyasaṅkhārau, vacīsaṅkhārau, cittasaṅkhārauti| imai vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, saṅkhārā|

‘‘avij‍jāsamudayā saṅkhārasamudayau; avij‍jāniraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau; ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau saṅkhāraniraudhagāminī paṭipadā, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi|

‘‘yatau khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau aivaṃ saṅkhārai pajānāti, aivaṃ saṅkhārasamudayaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ saṅkhāraniraudhaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ saṅkhāraniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānāti, idamassa dhammai ñāṇaṃ| sau iminā dhammaina diṭṭhaina viditaina akālikaina pattaina pariyaugāḷhaina atītānāgataina yaṃ naiti|

‘‘yai khau kaici atītamaddhānaṃ samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā saṅkhārai abbhañ‍ñaṃsu, saṅkhārasamudayaṃ abbhañ‍ñaṃsu, saṅkhāraniraudhaṃ abbhañ‍ñaṃsu, saṅkhāraniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ abbhañ‍ñaṃsu, sabbai tai aivamaiva abbhañ‍ñaṃsu, saiyyathāpāhaṃ aitarahi|

‘‘yaipi hi kaici anāgatamaddhānaṃ samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā saṅkhārai abhijānissanti, saṅkhārasamudayaṃ abhijānissanti, saṅkhāraniraudhaṃ abhijānissanti, saṅkhāraniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ abhijānissanti, sabbai tai aivamaiva abhijānissanti, saiyyathāpāhaṃ aitarahi| idamassa anvayai ñāṇaṃ|

‘‘yatau khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakassa imāni dvai ñāṇāni parisuddhāni  haunti pariyaudātāni – dhammai ñāṇañ‍ca anvayai ñāṇañ‍ca| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau diṭṭhisampan‍nau itipi, dassanasampan‍nau itipi, āgatau imaṃ saddhammaṃ itipi, passati imaṃ saddhammaṃ itipi, saikkhaina ñāṇaina saman‍nāgatau itipi, saikkhāya vij‍jāya saman‍nāgatau itipi, dhammasautaṃ samāpan‍nau itipi, ariyau nibbaidhikapañ‍ñau itipi, amatadvāraṃ āhac‍ca tiṭṭhati itipī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. dutiyañāṇavatthusuttaṃ

34. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘sattasattari vau, bhikkhavai, ñāṇavatthūni daisaissāmi| taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘katamāni, bhikkhavai, sattasattari ñāṇavatthūni? jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇanti ñāṇaṃ; asati jātiyā natthi jarāmaraṇanti ñāṇaṃ; atītampi addhānaṃ jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇanti ñāṇaṃ, asati jātiyā natthi jarāmaraṇanti ñāṇaṃ; anāgatampi addhānaṃ jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇanti ñāṇaṃ, asati jātiyā natthi jarāmaraṇanti ñāṇaṃ; yampissa taṃ dhammaṭṭhitiñāṇaṃ tampi khayadhammaṃ vayadhammaṃ virāgadhammaṃ niraudhadhammanti ñāṇaṃ|

‘‘bhavapac‍cayā jātīti ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… upādānapac‍cayā bhavauti ñāṇaṃ… taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānanti ñāṇaṃ… vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhāti ñāṇaṃ… phassapac‍cayā vaidanāti ñāṇaṃ… saḷāyatanapac‍cayā phassauti ñāṇaṃ… nāmarūpapac‍cayā  saḷāyatananti ñāṇaṃ… viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpanti ñāṇaṃ… saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇanti ñāṇaṃ; avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārāti ñāṇaṃ, asati avij‍jāya natthi saṅkhārāti ñāṇaṃ; atītampi addhānaṃ avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārāti ñāṇaṃ, asati avij‍jāya natthi saṅkhārāti ñāṇaṃ; anāgatampi addhānaṃ avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārāti ñāṇaṃ, asati avij‍jāya natthi saṅkhārāti ñāṇaṃ; yampissa taṃ dhammaṭṭhitiñāṇaṃ tampi khayadhammaṃ vayadhammaṃ virāgadhammaṃ niraudhadhammanti ñāṇaṃ| imāni vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, sattasattari ñāṇavatthūnī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. avij‍jāpac‍cayasuttaṃ

35. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘avij‍jāpac‍cayā, bhikkhavai, saṅkhārā; saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hautī’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘katamaṃ nu khau, bhantai, jarāmaraṇaṃ, kassa ca panidaṃ jarāmaraṇa’nti? ‘nau kal‍lau pañhau’ti bhagavā avauca, ‘katamaṃ jarāmaraṇaṃ, kassa ca panidaṃ jarāmaraṇa’nti iti vā, bhikkhu, yau vadaiyya, ‘añ‍ñaṃ jarāmaraṇaṃ añ‍ñassa ca panidaṃ jarāmaraṇa’nti, iti vā, bhikkhu, yau vadaiyya, ubhayamaitaṃ aikatthaṃ byañ‍janamaiva nānaṃ| taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīranti vā, bhikkhu, diṭṭhiyā sati brahmacariyavāsau na hauti| añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīranti vā, bhikkhu, diṭṭhiyā sati brahmacariyavāsau na hauti| aitai  tai, bhikkhu, ubhau antai anupagamma majjhaina tathāgatau dhammaṃ daisaiti – ‘jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇa’’’nti|

‘‘katamā nu khau, bhantai, jāti, kassa ca panāyaṃ jātī’’ti? ‘‘nau kal‍lau pañhau’’ti bhagavā avauca, ‘‘‘katamā jāti, kassa ca panāyaṃ jātī’ti iti  vā, bhikkhu, yau vadaiyya, ‘añ‍ñā jāti añ‍ñassa ca panāyaṃ jātī’ti iti vā, bhikkhu, yau vadaiyya, ubhayamaitaṃ aikatthaṃ byañ‍janamaiva nānaṃ| taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīranti vā, bhikkhu, diṭṭhiyā sati brahmacariyavāsau na hauti| añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīranti vā, bhikkhu, diṭṭhiyā sati brahmacariyavāsau na hauti| aitai tai, bhikkhu, ubhau antai anupagamma majjhaina tathāgatau dhammaṃ daisaiti – ‘bhavapac‍cayā jātī’’’ti|

‘‘katamau nu khau, bhantai, bhavau, kassa ca panāyaṃ bhavau’’ti? ‘‘nau kal‍lau pañhau’’ti bhagavā avauca, ‘‘‘katamau bhavau, kassa ca panāyaṃ bhavau’ti iti vā, bhikkhu, yau vadaiyya, ‘añ‍ñau bhavau añ‍ñassa ca panāyaṃ bhavau’ti iti vā, bhikkhu, yau vadaiyya, ubhayamaitaṃ aikatthaṃ byañ‍janamaiva nānaṃ| taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīranti vā, bhikkhu, diṭṭhiyā sati brahmacariyavāsau na hauti; añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīranti vā, bhikkhu, diṭṭhiyā sati brahmacariyavāsau na hauti| aitai tai, bhikkhu, ubhau antai anupagamma majjhaina tathāgatau dhammaṃ daisaiti – ‘upādānapac‍cayā bhavau’ti…pai॰… ‘taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānanti… vaidanāpac‍cayā  taṇhāti… phassapac‍cayā vaidanāti… saḷāyatanapac‍cayā phassauti… nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatananti… viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā  nāmarūpanti… saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇa’’’nti|

‘‘katamai nu khau, bhantai, saṅkhārā, kassa ca panimai saṅkhārā’’ti? ‘‘nau kal‍lau pañhau’’ti bhagavā avauca, ‘‘‘katamai saṅkhārā kassa ca panimai saṅkhārā’ti iti vā, bhikkhu, yau vadaiyya, ‘añ‍ñai saṅkhārā añ‍ñassa ca panimai saṅkhārā’ti iti vā, bhikkhu, yau vadaiyya, ubhayamaitaṃ aikatthaṃ byañ‍janamaiva nānaṃ| taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīranti vā, bhikkhu, diṭṭhiyā sati brahmacariyavāsau na hauti; añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīranti vā, bhikkhu, diṭṭhiyā sati brahmacariyavāsau na hauti| aitai tai, bhikkhu, ubhau antai anupagamma majjhaina tathāgatau dhammaṃ daisaiti – ‘avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā’’’ti|

‘‘avij‍jāya  tvaiva, bhikkhu, asaisavirāganiraudhā yānissa tāni visūkāyikāni visaivitāni vipphanditāni kānici kānici| ‘katamaṃ jarāmaraṇaṃ, kassa ca panidaṃ jarāmaraṇaṃ’ iti vā, ‘añ‍ñaṃ jarāmaraṇaṃ, añ‍ñassa ca panidaṃ jarāmaraṇaṃ’ iti vā, ‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīraṃ’ iti vā, ‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīraṃ’ iti vā| sabbānissa tāni pahīnāni bhavanti ucchin‍namūlāni tālāvatthukatāni anabhāvaṅkatāni āyatiṃ anuppādadhammāni|

‘‘avij‍jāya  tvaiva, bhikkhu, asaisavirāganiraudhā yānissa tāni visūkāyikāni visaivitāni vipphanditāni kānici kānici| ‘katamā jāti, kassa  ca panāyaṃ jāti’ iti vā, ‘añ‍ñā jāti, añ‍ñassa ca panāyaṃ jāti’ iti vā, ‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīraṃ’ iti vā, ‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīraṃ’ iti vā| sabbānissa tāni pahīnāni bhavanti ucchin‍namūlāni tālāvatthukatāni anabhāvaṅkatāni āyatiṃ anuppādadhammāni|

‘‘avij‍jāya tvaiva, bhikkhu, asaisavirāganiraudhā yānissa tāni visūkāyikāni visaivitāni vipphanditāni kānici kānici| katamau bhavau…pai॰… katamaṃ upādānaṃ… katamā taṇhā… katamā  vaidanā… katamau phassau… katamaṃ saḷāyatanaṃ… katamaṃ nāmarūpaṃ… katamaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰…|

‘‘avij‍jāya tvaiva, bhikkhu, asaisavirāganiraudhā yānissa tāni visūkāyikāni visaivitāni vipphanditāni kānici kānici| ‘katamai saṅkhārā, kassa ca panimai saṅkhārā’ iti vā, ‘añ‍ñai saṅkhārā, añ‍ñassa ca panimai saṅkhārā’ iti vā, ‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīraṃ’ iti vā, ‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ, añ‍ñaṃ sarīraṃ’ iti vā| sabbānissa tāni pahīnāni bhavanti ucchin‍namūlāni tālāvatthukatāni anabhāvaṅkatāni āyatiṃ anuppādadhammānī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dutiyaavij‍jāpac‍cayasuttaṃ

36. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati…pai॰… ‘‘avij‍jāpac‍cayā, bhikkhavai, saṅkhārā; saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

‘‘‘katamaṃ jarāmaraṇaṃ, kassa ca panidaṃ jarāmaraṇa’nti iti vā, bhikkhavai, yau vadaiyya, ‘añ‍ñaṃ jarāmaraṇaṃ, añ‍ñassa ca panidaṃ jarāmaraṇa’nti iti vā, bhikkhavai, yau vadaiyya, ubhayamaitaṃ aikatthaṃ byañ‍janamaiva nānaṃ| ‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīraṃ’ iti vā, bhikkhavai, diṭṭhiyā sati brahmacariyavāsau na hauti| ‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīraṃ’ iti vā, bhikkhavai, diṭṭhiyā sati brahmacariyavāsau na hauti| aitai tai, bhikkhavai, ubhau antai anupagamma majjhaina tathāgatau dhammaṃ daisaiti – ‘jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇa’’’nti|

‘‘katamā  jāti…pai॰… katamau bhavau… katamaṃ upādānaṃ… katamā taṇhā… katamā vaidanā… katamau phassau… katamaṃ  saḷāyatanaṃ… katamaṃ nāmarūpaṃ… katamaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ… katamai saṅkhārā, kassa ca panimai saṅkhārāti iti vā, bhikkhavai, yau vadaiyya, ‘añ‍ñai saṅkhārā añ‍ñassa ca panimai saṅkhārā’ti iti vā, bhikkhavai, yau vadaiyya, ubhayamaitaṃ aikatthaṃ byañ‍janamaiva nānaṃ| ‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīraṃ’ iti vā, bhikkhavai, diṭṭhiyā sati brahmacariyavāsau na hauti| ‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīraṃ’ iti vā, bhikkhavai, diṭṭhiyā sati brahmacariyavāsau na hauti| aitai tai, bhikkhavai, ubhau antai anupagamma majjhaina tathāgatau dhammaṃ daisaiti – ‘avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā’’’ti|

‘‘avij‍jāya tvaiva, bhikkhavai, asaisavirāganiraudhā yānissa tāni visūkāyikāni visaivitāni vipphanditāni kānici kānici| ‘katamaṃ jarāmaraṇaṃ, kassa ca panidaṃ jarāmaraṇaṃ’ iti vā, ‘añ‍ñaṃ jarāmaraṇaṃ, añ‍ñassa ca panidaṃ  jarāmaraṇaṃ’ iti vā, ‘taṃ jīvaṃ  taṃ sarīraṃ’ iti vā, ‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ, añ‍ñaṃ sarīraṃ’ iti vā| sabbānissa tāni pahīnāni bhavanti ucchin‍namūlāni tālāvatthukatāni anabhāvaṅkatāni āyatiṃ anuppādadhammāni|

‘‘avij‍jāya tvaiva, bhikkhavai, asaisavirāganiraudhā yānissa tāni visūkāyikāni visaivitāni vipphanditāni kānici kānici| katamā jāti…pai॰… katamau bhavau… katamaṃ upādānaṃ… katamā taṇhā… katamā vaidanā… katamau phassau… katamaṃ saḷāyatanaṃ… katamaṃ nāmarūpaṃ… katamaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ… ‘katamai saṅkhārā, kassa ca panimai saṅkhārā’ iti vā, ‘añ‍ñai saṅkhārā, añ‍ñassa ca panimai saṅkhārā’ iti vā; ‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīraṃ’ iti vā, ‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīraṃ’ iti vā| sabbānissa tāni pahīnāni bhavanti ucchin‍namūlāni tālāvatthukatāni anabhāvaṅkatāni āyatiṃ anuppādadhammānī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. natumhasuttaṃ

37. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘nāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, kāyau tumhākaṃ napi añ‍ñaisaṃ| purāṇamidaṃ, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ abhisaṅkhataṃ abhisañ‍caitayitaṃ vaidaniyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ’’|

‘‘tatra  khau, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau paṭic‍casamuppādañ‍ñaiva sādhukaṃ yaunisau manasi karauti – ‘iti imasmiṃ sati idaṃ hauti, imassuppādā  idaṃ uppaj‍jati; imasmiṃ asati idaṃ na hauti, imassa niraudhā idaṃ nirujjhati, yadidaṃ – avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā; saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti| avij‍jāya tvaiva asaisavirāganiraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau; saṅkhāraniraudhā viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhau…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hautī’’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. caitanāsuttaṃ

38. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘yañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, caitaiti yañ‍ca pakappaiti yañ‍ca anusaiti, ārammaṇamaitaṃ āramaṇamaitaṃ (?) hauti viñ‍ñāṇassa ṭhitiyā| ārammaṇai sati patiṭṭhā viñ‍ñāṇassa hauti| tasmiṃ patiṭṭhitai viñ‍ñāṇai virūḷhai āyatiṃ punabbhavābhinibbatti hauti| āyatiṃ punabbhavābhinibbattiyā sati āyatiṃ jāti jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā sambhavanti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti’’|

‘‘nau cai, bhikkhavai, caitaiti nau cai pakappaiti, atha cai anusaiti, ārammaṇamaitaṃ hauti viñ‍ñāṇassa ṭhitiyā| ārammaṇai sati patiṭṭhā viñ‍ñāṇassa hauti| tasmiṃ patiṭṭhitai viñ‍ñāṇai virūḷhai āyatiṃ punabbhavābhinibbatti hauti| āyatiṃ punabbhavābhinibbattiyā sati āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā sambhavanti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

‘‘yatau ca khau, bhikkhavai, nau caiva caitaiti nau ca pakappaiti nau ca anusaiti, ārammaṇamaitaṃ na hauti viñ‍ñāṇassa ṭhitiyā| ārammaṇai asati  patiṭṭhā viñ‍ñāṇassa na hauti| tadappatiṭṭhitai viñ‍ñāṇai avirūḷhai āyatiṃ punabbhavābhinibbatti na hauti| āyatiṃ punabbhavābhinibbattiyā asati āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā nirujjhanti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hautī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. dutiyacaitanāsuttaṃ

39. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati…pai॰… ‘‘yañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, caitaiti yañ‍ca pakappaiti yañ‍ca anusaiti, ārammaṇamaitaṃ hauti viñ‍ñāṇassa ṭhitiyā| ārammaṇai sati patiṭṭhā viñ‍ñāṇassa hauti| tasmiṃ patiṭṭhitai viñ‍ñāṇai virūḷhai nāmarūpassa avak‍kanti hauti| nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatanaṃ; saḷāyatanapac‍cayā phassau; phassapac‍cayā vaidanā…pai॰… taṇhā… upādānaṃ… bhavau… jāti… jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā sambhavanti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti’’|

‘‘nau  cai, bhikkhavai, caitaiti nau cai pakappaiti, atha cai anusaiti, ārammaṇamaitaṃ hauti viñ‍ñāṇassa ṭhitiyā| ārammaṇai sati patiṭṭhā viñ‍ñāṇassa hauti| tasmiṃ patiṭṭhitai viñ‍ñāṇai virūḷhai nāmarūpassa avak‍kanti hauti| nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatanaṃ…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

‘‘yatau ca khau, bhikkhavai, nau caiva caitaiti nau ca pakappaiti nau ca anusaiti, ārammaṇamaitaṃ na hauti viñ‍ñāṇassa ṭhitiyā| ārammaṇai  asati patiṭṭhā viñ‍ñāṇassa na hauti| tadappatiṭṭhitai viñ‍ñāṇai avirūḷhai nāmarūpassa avak‍kanti na hauti| nāmarūpaniraudhā saḷāyatananiraudhau…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hautī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. tatiyacaitanāsuttaṃ

40. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘yañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, caitaiti yañ‍ca pakappaiti yañ‍ca anusaiti ārammaṇamaitaṃ hauti viñ‍ñāṇassa ṭhitiyā| ārammaṇai sati patiṭṭhā viñ‍ñāṇassa hauti| tasmiṃ patiṭṭhitai viñ‍ñāṇai virūḷhai nati hauti| natiyā sati āgatigati hauti| āgatigatiyā sati cutūpapātau hauti| cutūpapātai sati āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā sambhavanti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti’’|

‘‘nau cai, bhikkhavai, caitaiti nau cai pakappaiti atha cai anusaiti, ārammaṇamaitaṃ hauti viñ‍ñāṇassa ṭhitiyā| ārammaṇai sati patiṭṭhā viñ‍ñāṇassa hauti| tasmiṃ patiṭṭhitai viñ‍ñāṇai virūḷhai nati hauti| natiyā  sati āgatigati hauti| āgatigatiyā sati cutūpapātau hauti| cutūpapātai sati āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā sambhavanti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

‘‘yatau ca khau, bhikkhavai, nau caiva caitaiti nau ca pakappaiti nau ca anusaiti, ārammaṇamaitaṃ  na hauti viñ‍ñāṇassa ṭhitiyā| ārammaṇai  asati patiṭṭhā viñ‍ñāṇassa na hauti| tadappatiṭṭhitai viñ‍ñāṇai avirūḷhai nati na hauti| natiyā asati āgatigati na hauti| āgatigatiyā asati cutūpapātau na hauti| cutūpapātai asati āyatiṃ jāti jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā nirujjhanti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hautī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

kaḷārakhattiyavaggau catutthau|

tassuddānaṃ –

bhūtamidaṃ kaḷārañ‍ca, duvai ca ñāṇavatthūni|

avij‍jāpac‍cayā  ca dvai, natumhā caitanā tayauti||

5. gahapativaggau

1. pañ‍cavairabhayasuttaṃ

41. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati| atha khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘yatau khau, gahapati, ariyasāvakassa pañ‍ca bhayāni vairāni vūpasantāni haunti, catūhi ca sautāpattiyaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti, ariyau cassa ñāyau pañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhau hauti suppaṭividdhau, sau ākaṅkhamānau attanāva attānaṃ byākaraiyya – ‘khīṇanirayaumhi khīṇatiracchānayauni khīṇapaittivisayau khīṇāpāyaduggativinipātau, sautāpan‍nauhamasmi avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyanau’’’ti|

‘‘katamāni  pañ‍ca bhayāni vairāni vūpasantāni haunti? yaṃ, gahapati, pāṇātipātī pāṇātipātapac‍cayā diṭṭhadhammikampi bhayaṃ vairaṃ pasavati, samparāyikampi bhayaṃ vairaṃ pasavati, caitasikampi dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidayati, pāṇātipātā paṭiviratassa aivaṃ taṃ bhayaṃ vairaṃ vūpasantaṃ hauti|

‘‘yaṃ, gahapati, adin‍nādāyī adin‍nādānapac‍cayā diṭṭhadhammikampi bhayaṃ vairaṃ pasavati, samparāyikampi bhayaṃ vairaṃ pasavati, caitasikampi dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidayati, adin‍nādānā paṭiviratassa aivaṃ taṃ bhayaṃ vairaṃ vūpasantaṃ hauti|

‘‘yaṃ, gahapati, kāmaisumicchācārī kāmaisumicchācārapac‍cayā  diṭṭhadhammikampi bhayaṃ vairaṃ pasavati, samparāyikampi bhayaṃ vairaṃ pasavati, caitasikampi dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidayati, kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratassa aivaṃ taṃ bhayaṃ vairaṃ vūpasantaṃ hauti|

‘‘yaṃ, gahapati, musāvādī musāvādapac‍cayā diṭṭhadhammikampi bhayaṃ vairaṃ pasavati, samparāyikampi bhayaṃ vairaṃ pasavati, caitasikampi dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidayati, musāvādā paṭiviratassa aivaṃ taṃ bhayaṃ vairaṃ vūpasantaṃ hauti|

‘‘yaṃ, gahapati, surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhāyī surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānapac‍cayā diṭṭhadhammikampi bhayaṃ vairaṃ pasavati, samparāyikampi bhayaṃ vairaṃ pasavati, caitasikampi dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidayati, surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratassa aivaṃ taṃ bhayaṃ vairaṃ vūpasantaṃ hauti| imāni pañ‍ca bhayāni vairāni vūpasantāni haunti|

‘‘katamaihi catūhi sautāpattiyaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti? idha, gahapati, ariyasāvakau buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’’’ti|

‘‘dhammai  avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – ‘svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau sandiṭṭhikau akālikau aihipassikau aupanaiyyikau pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhī’’’ti|

‘‘saṅghai  avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – ‘suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau, ujuppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau, ñāyappaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau, sāmīcippaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau, yadidaṃ cattāri purisayugāni aṭṭha purisapuggalā, aisa bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau  āhunaiyyau pāhunaiyyau dakkhiṇaiyyau añ‍jalikaraṇīyau anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’’’ti|

‘‘ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti akhaṇḍaihi acchiddaihi asabalaihi akammāsaihi bhujissaihi viñ‍ñuppasatthaihi aparāmaṭṭhaihi samādhisaṃvattanikaihi| imaihi catūhi sautāpattiyaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti|

‘‘katamau cassa ariyau ñāyau pañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhau hauti suppaṭividdhau? idha, gahapati, ariyasāvakau paṭic‍casamuppādañ‍ñaiva sādhukaṃ yaunisau manasi karauti – ‘iti imasmiṃ sati idaṃ hauti, imasmiṃ asati idaṃ na hauti; imassuppādā idaṃ uppaj‍jati, imassa niraudhā idaṃ nirujjhati| yadidaṃ avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā; saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti| avij‍jāya tvaiva asaisavirāganiraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau; saṅkhāraniraudhā viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhau…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hautī’’’ti| ayamassa ariyau ñāyau pañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhau hauti suppaṭividdhau|

‘‘yatau khau, gahapati, ariyasāvakassa imāni pañ‍ca bhayāni vairāni vūpasantāni haunti, imaihi catūhi sautāpattiyaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti, ayañ‍cassa ariyau ñāyau pañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhau hauti suppaṭividdhau, sau ākaṅkhamānau attanāva attānaṃ byākaraiyya – ‘khīṇanirayaumhi  khīṇatiracchānayauni khīṇapaittivisayau khīṇāpāyaduggativinipātau, sautāpan‍nauhamasmi avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyanau’’’ti|

2. dutiyapañ‍cavairabhayasuttaṃ

42. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati…pai॰… ‘‘yatau  khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakassa pañ‍ca bhayāni vairāni vūpasantāni haunti, catūhi ca sautāpattiyaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti, ariyau cassa ñāyau pañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhau hauti  suppaṭividdhau, sau ākaṅkhamānau attanāva attānaṃ byākaraiyya – ‘khīṇanirayaumhi khīṇatiracchānayauni khīṇapaittivisayau khīṇāpāyaduggativinipātau, sautāpan‍nauhamasmi avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyanau’’’ti|

‘‘katamāni pañ‍ca bhayāni vairāni vūpasantāni haunti? yaṃ, bhikkhavai, pāṇātipātī …pai॰… yaṃ, bhikkhavai, adin‍nādāyī…pai॰… yaṃ, bhikkhavai, kāmaisumicchācārī… yaṃ, bhikkhavai, musāvādī… yaṃ, bhikkhavai, surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhāyī…pai॰… imāni pañ‍ca bhayāni vairāni vūpasantāni haunti|

‘‘katamaihi catūhi sautāpattiyaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau buddhai…pai॰… dhammai… saṅghai… ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti| imaihi catūhi sautāpattiyaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti|

‘‘katamau cassa ariyau ñāyau pañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhau hauti suppaṭividdhau? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau paṭic‍casamuppādañ‍ñaiva sādhukaṃ yaunisau manasi karauti…pai॰… ayamassa ariyau ñāyau pañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhau hauti suppaṭividdhau|

‘‘yatau khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakassa imāni pañ‍ca bhayāni vairāni vūpasantāni haunti, imaihi ca catūhi sautāpattiyaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti, ayañ‍cassa ariyau ñāyau pañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhau hauti suppaṭividdhau, sau ākaṅkhamānau attanāva attānaṃ byākaraiyya – ‘khīṇanirayaumhi khīṇatiracchānayauni khīṇapaittivisayau khīṇāpāyaduggativinipātau, sautāpan‍nauhamasmi avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyanau’’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. dukkhasuttaṃ

43. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati…pai॰… ‘‘dukkhassa, bhikkhavai, samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca daisaissāmi| taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, dukkhassa samudayau? cakkhuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca rūpai ca uppaj‍jati cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ| tiṇṇaṃ saṅgati phassau| phassapac‍cayā vaidanā; vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, dukkhassa samudayau|

‘‘sautañ‍ca  paṭic‍ca saddai ca uppaj‍jati sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… ghānañ‍ca paṭic‍ca gandhai ca…pai॰… jivhañ‍ca paṭic‍ca rasai ca…pai॰… kāyañ‍ca paṭic‍ca phauṭṭhabbai ca…pai॰… manañ‍ca paṭic‍ca dhammai ca uppaj‍jati manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ| tiṇṇaṃ saṅgati phassau| phassapac‍cayā vaidanā; vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā| ayaṃ  khau, bhikkhavai, dukkhassa samudayau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, dukkhassa atthaṅgamau? cakkhuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca rūpai ca uppaj‍jati cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ| tiṇṇaṃ saṅgati phassau| phassapac‍cayā vaidanā; vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā| tassāyaiva taṇhāya asaisavirāganiraudhā upādānaniraudhau; upādānaniraudhā bhavaniraudhau; bhavaniraudhā jātiniraudhau; jātiniraudhā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā nirujjhanti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hauti| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, dukkhassa atthaṅgamau|

‘‘sautañ‍ca paṭic‍ca saddai ca uppaj‍jati sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… ghānañ‍ca paṭic‍ca gandhai ca…pai॰… jivhañ‍ca paṭic‍ca rasai ca…pai॰… kāyañ‍ca paṭic‍ca phauṭṭhabbai ca…pai॰… manañ‍ca paṭic‍ca dhammai ca uppaj‍jati manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ| tiṇṇaṃ saṅgati phassau| phassapac‍cayā vaidanā; vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā| tassāyaiva taṇhāya asaisavirāganiraudhā upādānaniraudhau; upādānaniraudhā bhavaniraudhau; bhavaniraudhā jātiniraudhau; jātiniraudhā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā  nirujjhanti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hauti| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, dukkhassa atthaṅgamau’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. laukasuttaṃ

44. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati…pai॰… ‘‘laukassa, bhikkhavai, samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca daisaissāmi| taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, laukassa samudayau? cakkhuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca rūpai ca uppaj‍jati cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ| tiṇṇaṃ  saṅgati phassau| phassapac‍cayā vaidanā; vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā; taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ; upādānapac‍cayā bhavau; bhavapac‍cayā jāti; jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā sambhavanti| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, laukassa samudayau|

‘‘sautañ‍ca  paṭic‍ca saddai ca…pai॰… ghānañ‍ca paṭic‍ca gandhai ca… jivhañ‍ca paṭic‍ca rasai ca… kāyañ‍ca paṭic‍ca phauṭṭhabbai ca… manañ‍ca paṭic‍ca dhammai ca uppaj‍jati manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ| tiṇṇaṃ saṅgati phassau| phassapac‍cayā vaidanā…pai॰… jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā sambhavanti| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, laukassa samudayau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, laukassa atthaṅgamau? cakkhuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca rūpai ca uppaj‍jati cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ| tiṇṇaṃ saṅgati phassau| phassapac‍cayā vaidanā; vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā| tassāyaiva taṇhāya asaisavirāganiraudhā upādānaniraudhau; upādānaniraudhā bhavaniraudhau…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hauti| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, laukassa atthaṅgamau|

‘‘sautañ‍ca paṭic‍ca saddai ca…pai॰… ghānañ‍ca  paṭic‍ca gandhai ca… jivhañ‍ca paṭic‍ca rasai ca… kāyañ‍ca paṭic‍ca phauṭṭhabbai ca… manañ‍ca paṭic‍ca dhammai ca uppaj‍jati manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ| tiṇṇaṃ saṅgati phassau| phassapac‍cayā vaidanā; vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā| tassāyaiva taṇhāya asaisavirāganiraudhā upādānaniraudhau; upādānaniraudhā bhavaniraudhau…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa  dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hauti| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, laukassa atthaṅgamau’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. ñātikasuttaṃ

45. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā ñātikai viharati giñ‍jakāvasathai| atha khau bhagavā rahaugatau paṭisal‍lānau imaṃ dhammapariyāyaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘cakkhuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca rūpai ca uppaj‍jati cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ| tiṇṇaṃ saṅgati phassau| phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā; taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

‘‘sautañ‍ca paṭic‍ca saddai ca…pai॰… ghānañ‍ca paṭic‍ca gandhai ca… jivhañ‍ca paṭic‍ca rasai ca… kāyañ‍ca paṭic‍ca phauṭṭhabbai ca… manañ‍ca paṭic‍ca dhammai ca uppaj‍jati manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ| tiṇṇaṃ saṅgati phassau| phassapac‍cayā vaidanā; vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā; taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

‘‘cakkhuñ‍ca  paṭic‍ca rūpai ca uppaj‍jati cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ| tiṇṇaṃ saṅgati phassau| phassapac‍cayā vaidanā; vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā| tassāyaiva taṇhāya asaisavirāganiraudhā upādānaniraudhau; upādānaniraudhā bhavaniraudhau…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hauti|

‘‘sautañ‍ca  paṭic‍ca saddai ca…pai॰… manañ‍ca paṭic‍ca dhammai ca uppaj‍jati manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ| tiṇṇaṃ saṅgati phassau| phassapac‍cayā vaidanā; vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā| tassāyaiva taṇhāya asaisavirāganiraudhā  upādānaniraudhau; upādānaniraudhā bhavaniraudhau…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hautī’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu bhagavatau upassuti upassutiṃ (sī॰ pī॰) ṭhitau hauti| addasā khau bhagavā taṃ bhikkhuṃ upassuti ṭhitaṃ| disvāna taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘assausi nau tvaṃ, bhikkhu, imaṃ dhammapariyāya’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti| ‘‘uggaṇhāhi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, imaṃ dhammapariyāyaṃ; pariyāpuṇāhi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, imaṃ dhammapariyāyaṃ; dhāraihi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, imaṃ dhammapariyāyaṃ| atthasaṃhitau ayaṃ atthasaṃhitauyaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰), atthasaṃhitāyaṃ (pī॰ ka॰), bhikkhu, dhammapariyāyau ādibrahmacariyakau’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. añ‍ñatarabrāhmaṇasuttaṃ

46. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati| atha khau añ‍ñatarau brāhmaṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘kiṃ nu khau, bhau gautama, sau karauti sau paṭisaṃvaidayatī’’ti? ‘‘‘sau karauti sau paṭisaṃvaidayatī’ti khau, brāhmaṇa, ayamaikau antau’’|

‘‘kiṃ  pana, bhau gautama, añ‍ñau karauti, añ‍ñau paṭisaṃvaidayatī’’ti? ‘‘‘añ‍ñau karauti, añ‍ñau paṭisaṃvaidayatī’ti khau, brāhmaṇa, ayaṃ dutiyau antau| aitai tai, brāhmaṇa, ubhau antai anupagamma majjhaina tathāgatau dhammaṃ daisaiti – ‘avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā; saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti| avij‍jāya  tvaiva asaisavirāganiraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau; saṅkhāraniraudhā viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhau  …pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hautī’’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai, sau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama,…pai॰… upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. jāṇussauṇisuttaṃ

47. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati| atha khau jāṇussauṇi brāhmaṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau jāṇussauṇi brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘kiṃ  nu khau, bhau, gautama, sabbamatthī’’ti? ‘‘‘sabbamatthī’ti khau, brāhmaṇa, ayamaikau antau’’|

‘‘kiṃ pana, bhau gautama, sabbaṃ natthī’’ti? ‘‘‘sabbaṃ natthī’ti khau, brāhmaṇa, ayaṃ dutiyau antau| aitai tai, brāhmaṇa, ubhau antai anupagamma majjhaina tathāgatau dhammaṃ daisaiti – ‘avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā; saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti| avij‍jāya tvaiva asaisavirāganiraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau; saṅkhāraniraudhā viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhau…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hautī’’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai, jāṇussauṇi brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca  – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ bhau gautama…pai॰… pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti| sattamaṃ|

8. laukāyatikasuttaṃ

48. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati| atha khau laukāyatikau brāhmaṇau yaina  bhagavā…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau laukāyatikau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘kiṃ nu khau, bhau gautama, sabbamatthī’’ti? ‘‘‘sabbamatthī’ti khau, brāhmaṇa, jaiṭṭhamaitaṃ laukāyataṃ’’|

‘‘kiṃ  pana, bhau gautama, sabbaṃ natthī’’ti? ‘‘‘sabbaṃ natthī’ti khau, brāhmaṇa, dutiyamaitaṃ laukāyataṃ’’|

‘‘kiṃ nu khau, bhau gautama, sabbamaikatta’’nti? ‘‘‘sabbamaikatta’nti khau, brāhmaṇa, tatiyamaitaṃ laukāyataṃ’’|

‘‘kiṃ  pana, bhau gautama, sabbaṃ puthutta’’nti? ‘‘‘sabbaṃ puthutta’nti khau, brāhmaṇa, catutthamaitaṃ laukāyataṃ’’|

‘‘aitai tai, brāhmaṇa, ubhau antai anupagamma majjhaina tathāgatau dhammaṃ daisaiti – ‘avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā; saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti| avij‍jāya tvaiva asaisavirāganiraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau; saṅkhāraniraudhā viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhau…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hautī’’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai, laukāyatikau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama…pai॰… aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. ariyasāvakasuttaṃ

49. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, sutavatau ariyasāvakassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘kiṃ nu khau kismiṃ sati kiṃ hauti, kissuppādā kiṃ uppaj‍jati? (kismiṃ sati saṅkhārā haunti, kismiṃ sati viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hauti,) ( ) aitthantarai pāṭhā kaisuci pautthakaisu na dissantīti sī॰ pī॰ pautthakaisu dassitā| tathā sati anantarasuttaṭīkāya samaiti kismiṃ sati nāmarūpaṃ hauti, kismiṃ sati saḷāyatanaṃ hauti, kismiṃ sati phassau hauti, kismiṃ sati vaidanā  hauti, kismiṃ sati taṇhā hauti, kismiṃ sati upādānaṃ hauti, kismiṃ sati bhavau hauti, kismiṃ sati jāti hauti, kismiṃ sati jarāmaraṇaṃ hautī’’’ti?

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, sutavatau ariyasāvakassa aparappac‍cayā ñāṇamaivaittha hauti – ‘imasmiṃ sati idaṃ hauti, imassuppādā idaṃ uppaj‍jati| (avij‍jāya sati saṅkhārā haunti; saṅkhāraisu sati viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hauti;) ( ) aitthakaisu pāṭhā kaisuci pautthakaisu na dissantīti sī॰ pī॰ pautthakaisu dassitā| tathā sati anantarasuttaṭīkāya samaiti viñ‍ñāṇai sati nāmarūpaṃ hauti; nāmarūpai sati saḷāyatanaṃ hauti; saḷāyatanai sati phassau hauti; phassai sati vaidanā hauti; vaidanāya sati taṇhā hauti; taṇhāya sati upādānaṃ hauti; upādānai sati bhavau hauti; bhavai sati jāti hauti; jātiyā sati jarāmaraṇaṃ hautī’ti| sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘aivamayaṃ laukau samudayatī’’’ti|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, sutavatau ariyasāvakassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘kiṃ nu khau kismiṃ asati kiṃ na hauti, kissa niraudhā kiṃ nirujjhati? (kismiṃ asati saṅkhārā na haunti, kismiṃ asati viñ‍ñāṇaṃ na hauti,) ( ) aitthantarai pāṭhāpi tattha tathaiva dassitā kismiṃ asati nāmarūpaṃ na hauti, kismiṃ asati saḷāyatanaṃ na hauti, kismiṃ asati phassau na hauti, kismiṃ asati vaidanā na hauti, kismiṃ asati taṇhā na hauti, kismiṃ asati upādānaṃ na hauti, kismiṃ asati bhavau na hauti, kismiṃ asati jāti na hauti, kismiṃ asati jarāmaraṇaṃ na hautī’’’ti?

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, sutavatau ariyasāvakassa aparappac‍cayā ñāṇamaivaittha hauti – ‘imasmiṃ asati idaṃ na hauti, imassa niraudhā idaṃ nirujjhati| (avij‍jāya asati saṅkhārā na haunti; saṅkhāraisu asati viñ‍ñāṇaṃ na hauti;) ( ) aitthantarai pāṭhāpi tattha tathaiva dassitā viñ‍ñāṇai asati nāmarūpaṃ na hauti; nāmarūpai asati saḷāyatanaṃ  na hauti…pai॰… bhavau na hauti… jāti na hauti… jātiyā asati jarāmaraṇaṃ na hautī’ti| sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘aivamayaṃ laukau nirujjhatī’’’ti|

‘‘yatau  khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau aivaṃ laukassa samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau diṭṭhisampan‍nau itipi…pai॰… amatadvāraṃ āhac‍ca tiṭṭhati itipī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. dutiyaariyasāvakasuttaṃ

50. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, sutavatau ariyasāvakassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘kiṃ nu khau kismiṃ sati kiṃ hauti, kissuppādā kiṃ uppaj‍jati? kismiṃ sati saṅkhārā haunti, kismiṃ sati viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hauti, kismiṃ sati nāmarūpaṃ hauti, kismiṃ sati saḷāyatanaṃ hauti, kismiṃ sati  phassau hauti, kismiṃ sati vaidanā hauti, kismiṃ sati taṇhā hauti, kismiṃ sati upādānaṃ hauti, kismiṃ sati bhavau hauti, kismiṃ sati jāti hauti, kismiṃ sati jarāmaraṇaṃ hautī’’’ti?

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, sutavatau ariyasāvakassa aparappac‍cayā ñāṇamaivaittha hauti – ‘imasmiṃ  sati idaṃ hauti, imassuppādā idaṃ uppaj‍jati| avij‍jāya sati saṅkhārā haunti; saṅkhāraisu sati viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hauti; viñ‍ñāṇai sati nāmarūpaṃ hauti; nāmarūpai sati saḷāyatanaṃ hauti; saḷāyatanai sati phassau hauti; phassai sati vaidanā hauti; vaidanāya sati taṇhā hauti; taṇhāya sati upādānaṃ hauti; upādānai sati bhavau hauti; bhavai sati jāti hauti; jātiyā sati jarāmaraṇaṃ hautī’ti| sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘aivamayaṃ laukau samudayatī’’’ti|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, sutavatau ariyasāvakassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘kiṃ nu khau kismiṃ asati kiṃ na hauti, kissa niraudhā kiṃ nirujjhati? kismiṃ asati saṅkhārā na haunti, kismiṃ asati viñ‍ñāṇaṃ na hauti, kismiṃ asati nāmarūpaṃ na hauti, kismiṃ asati saḷāyatanaṃ na hauti, kismiṃ asati phassau na hauti, kismiṃ asati vaidanā na hauti, kismiṃ asati taṇhā na hauti…pai॰… upādānaṃ… bhavau… jāti… kismiṃ asati jarāmaraṇaṃ na hautī’’’ti?

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, sutavatau ariyasāvakassa aparappac‍cayā ñāṇamaivaittha hauti – ‘imasmiṃ asati idaṃ na hauti, imassa niraudhā idaṃ nirujjhati| avij‍jāya asati saṅkhārā  na haunti; saṅkhāraisu asati viñ‍ñāṇaṃ na hauti; viñ‍ñāṇai asati nāmarūpaṃ na hauti; nāmarūpai asati saḷāyatanaṃ na hauti…pai॰… jātiyā asati jarāmaraṇaṃ na hautī’ti| sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘aivamayaṃ laukau nirujjhatī’’’ti|

‘‘yatau khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau aivaṃ laukassa samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau diṭṭhisampan‍nau itipi, dassanasampan‍nau itipi, āgatau imaṃ saddhammaṃ itipi, passati imaṃ saddhammaṃ itipi, saikkhaina ñāṇaina saman‍nāgatau itipi, saikkhāya  vij‍jāya saman‍nāgatau itipi, dhammasautaṃ samāpan‍nau itipi, ariyau nibbaidhikapañ‍ñau itipi, amatadvāraṃ āhac‍ca tiṭṭhati itipī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

gahapativaggau pañ‍camau|

tassuddānaṃ –

dvai  pañ‍cavairabhayā vuttā, dukkhaṃ laukau ca ñātikaṃ|

añ‍ñataraṃ jāṇussauṇi ca, laukāyatikaina aṭṭhamaṃ|

dvai ariyasāvakā vuttā, vaggau taina pavuc‍catīti||

6. dukkhavaggau

1. parivīmaṃsanasuttaṃ

51. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘kittāvatā nu khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū parivīmaṃsamānau parivīmaṃsaiyya sabbasau sammā dukkhakkhayāyā’’ti? ‘‘bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā bhagavaṃnaittikā bhagavaṃpaṭisaraṇā| sādhu vata, bhantai, bhagavantaṃyaiva paṭibhātu aitassa bhāsitassa atthau| bhagavatau sutvā bhikkhū dhāraissantī’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha, bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu parivīmaṃsamānau parivīmaṃsati – ‘yaṃ khau idaṃ anaikavidhaṃ nānappakārakaṃ dukkhaṃ laukai uppaj‍jati jarāmaraṇaṃ; idaṃ nu khau dukkhaṃ kiṃnidānaṃ kiṃsamudayaṃ kiṃjātikaṃ kiṃpabhavaṃ? kismiṃ sati jarāmaraṇaṃ hauti, kismiṃ asati jarāmaraṇaṃ na hautī’ti? sau parivīmaṃsamānau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘yaṃ khau idaṃ anaikavidhaṃ nānappakārakaṃ dukkhaṃ laukai uppaj‍jati  jarāmaraṇaṃ, idaṃ khau dukkhaṃ jātinidānaṃ jātisamudayaṃ jātijātikaṃ jātippabhavaṃ| jātiyā sati jarāmaraṇaṃ hauti, jātiyā asati jarāmaraṇaṃ na hautī’’’ti|

‘‘sau  jarāmaraṇañ‍ca pajānāti, jarāmaraṇasamudayañ‍ca pajānāti, jarāmaraṇaniraudhañ‍ca  pajānāti, yā ca jarāmaraṇaniraudhasāruppagāminī paṭipadā tañ‍ca pajānāti, tathā paṭipan‍nau ca hauti anudhammacārī; ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sabbasau sammā dukkhakkhayāya paṭipan‍nau jarāmaraṇaniraudhāya|

‘‘athāparaṃ parivīmaṃsamānau parivīmaṃsati – ‘jāti panāyaṃ kiṃnidānā kiṃsamudayā kiṃjātikā kiṃpabhavā, kismiṃ sati jāti hauti, kismiṃ asati jāti na hautī’ti? sau parivīmaṃsamānau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘jāti bhavanidānā bhavasamudayā bhavajātikā bhavappabhavā; bhavai sati jāti hauti, bhavai asati jāti na hautī’’’ti|

‘‘sau jātiñ‍ca pajānāti, jātisamudayañ‍ca pajānāti, jātiniraudhañ‍ca pajānāti, yā ca jātiniraudhasāruppagāminī paṭipadā tañ‍ca pajānāti, tathā paṭipan‍nau ca hauti anudhammacārī; ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sabbasau sammā dukkhakkhayāya paṭipan‍nau jātiniraudhāya|

‘‘athāparaṃ parivīmaṃsamānau parivīmaṃsati – ‘bhavau panāyaṃ kiṃnidānau…pai॰… upādānaṃ panidaṃ kiṃnidānaṃ… taṇhā panāyaṃ kiṃnidānā… vaidanā… phassau… saḷāyatanaṃ panidaṃ kiṃnidānaṃ… nāmarūpaṃ panidaṃ… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ panidaṃ… saṅkhārā panimai kiṃnidānā kiṃsamudayā kiṃjātikā kiṃpabhavā; kismiṃ sati saṅkhārā haunti, kismiṃ asati saṅkhārā na hauntī’ti? sau parivīmaṃsamānau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘saṅkhārā avij‍jānidānā  avij‍jāsamudayā avij‍jājātikā avij‍jāpabhavā; avij‍jāya  sati saṅkhārā haunti, avij‍jāya asati saṅkhārā na hauntī’’’ti|

‘‘sau saṅkhārai ca pajānāti, saṅkhārasamudayañ‍ca pajānāti, saṅkhāraniraudhañ‍ca pajānāti, yā ca saṅkhāraniraudhasāruppagāminī paṭipadā tañ‍ca pajānāti, tathā paṭipan‍nau ca hauti anudhammacārī; ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sabbasau sammā dukkhakkhayāya paṭipan‍nau saṅkhāraniraudhāya|

‘‘avij‍jāgatau  yaṃ, bhikkhavai, purisapuggalau puñ‍ñaṃ cai saṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti, puñ‍ñūpagaṃ hauti viñ‍ñāṇaṃ| apuñ‍ñaṃ cai saṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti, apuñ‍ñūpagaṃ hauti viñ‍ñāṇaṃ| ānaiñ‍jaṃ cai saṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti ānaiñ‍jūpagaṃ hauti viñ‍ñāṇaṃ| yatau khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau avij‍jā pahīnā hauti vij‍jā uppan‍nā, sau avij‍jāvirāgā vij‍juppādā naiva puñ‍ñābhisaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti na apuñ‍ñābhisaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti na ānaiñ‍jābhisaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti| anabhisaṅkharauntau anabhisañ‍caitayantau na kiñ‍ci laukai upādiyati; anupādiyaṃ na paritassati, aparitassaṃ pac‍cattañ‍ñaiva parinibbāyati| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāti|

‘‘sau sukhaṃ cai vaidanaṃ vaidayati, sā anic‍cāti pajānāti, anajjhausitāti pajānāti, anabhinanditāti pajānāti| dukkhaṃ cai vaidanaṃ vaidayati, sā anic‍cāti pajānāti, anajjhausitāti pajānāti, anabhinanditāti pajānāti| adukkhamasukhaṃ cai vaidanaṃ vaidayati, sā anic‍cāti  pajānāti, anajjhausitāti pajānāti, anabhinanditāti pajānāti| sau sukhaṃ cai vaidanaṃ vaidayati, visaṃyuttau naṃ vaidayati| dukkhaṃ cai vaidanaṃ vaidayati, visaṃyuttau naṃ taṃ vaidanaṃ (sī॰ pī॰), vaidanaṃ (ka॰) vaidayati| adukkhamasukhaṃ cai vaidanaṃ vaidayati, visaṃyuttau naṃ vaidayati|

‘‘sau  kāyapariyantikaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayamānau kāyapariyantikaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayāmīti pajānāti, jīvitapariyantikaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayamānau jīvitapariyantikaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayāmīti pajānāti| kāyassa bhaidā uddhaṃ jīvitapariyādānā idhaiva sabbavaidayitāni anabhinanditāni sītībhavissanti, sarīrāni avasissantīti pajānāti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau kumbhakārapākā uṇhaṃ kumbhaṃ uddharitvā samai bhūmibhāgai paṭisissaiyya paṭivisaiyya (sī॰), patiṭṭhapaiyya (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), paṭisaivaiyya (ṭīkā)| tatra yāyaṃ usmā sā tatthaiva vūpasamaiyya, kapal‍lāni avasissaiyyuṃ| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyapariyantikaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayamānau kāyapariyantikaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayāmīti pajānāti, jīvitapariyantikaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayamānau jīvitapariyantikaṃ  vaidanaṃ vaidayāmīti pajānāti| kāyassa  bhaidā uddhaṃ jīvitapariyādānā idhaiva sabbavaidayitāni anabhinanditāni sītībhavissanti, sarīrāni avasissantīti pajānāti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, api nu khau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu puñ‍ñābhisaṅkhāraṃ vā abhisaṅkharaiyya apuñ‍ñābhisaṅkhāraṃ vā abhisaṅkharaiyya ānaiñ‍jābhisaṅkhāraṃ vā abhisaṅkharaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘sabbasau vā  pana saṅkhāraisu asati, saṅkhāraniraudhā api nu khau viñ‍ñāṇaṃ pañ‍ñāyaithā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘sabbasau vā pana viñ‍ñāṇai asati, viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhā api nu khau nāmarūpaṃ pañ‍ñāyaithā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘sabbasau vā pana nāmarūpai asati, nāmarūpaniraudhā api nu khau saḷāyatanaṃ pañ‍ñāyaithā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘sabbasau vā pana saḷāyatanai asati, saḷāyatananiraudhā api nu khau phassau pañ‍ñāyaithā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘sabbasau  vā pana phassai asati, phassaniraudhā api nu khau vaidanā pañ‍ñāyaithā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘sabbasau vā pana vaidanāya asati, vaidanāniraudhā api nu khau taṇhā pañ‍ñāyaithā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘sabbasau vā pana taṇhāya asati, taṇhāniraudhā api nu khau upādānaṃ pañ‍ñāyaithā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘sabbasau vā pana upādānai asati, upādānaniraudhā api nu khau bhavau pañ‍ñāyaithā’’ti| ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘sabbasau vā pana bhavai asati, bhavaniraudhā api nu khau jāti pañ‍ñāyaithā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘sabbasau vā pana jātiyā asati, jātiniraudhā api nu khau jarāmaraṇaṃ pañ‍ñāyaithā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘sādhu sādhu, bhikkhavai, aivamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, naitaṃ añ‍ñathā| saddahatha mai taṃ, bhikkhavai, adhimuc‍catha, nik‍kaṅkhā aittha hautha nibbicikicchā| aisaivantau dukkhassā’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. upādānasuttaṃ

52. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati…pai॰… ‘‘upādāniyaisu, bhikkhavai, dhammaisu assādānupassinau viharatau taṇhā pavaḍḍhati| taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ; upādānapac‍cayā bhavau; bhavapac‍cayā jāti; jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā sambhavanti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti’’|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, dasan‍naṃ vā kaṭṭhavāhānaṃ vīsāya  vā kaṭṭhavāhānaṃ tiṃsāya vā kaṭṭhavāhānaṃ cattārīsāya vā kaṭṭhavāhānaṃ mahāaggikkhandhau jalaiyya| tatra purisau kālaina kālaṃ sukkhāni caiva tiṇāni pakkhipaiyya, sukkhāni ca gaumayāni pakkhipaiyya, sukkhāni ca kaṭṭhāni pakkhipaiyya| aivañhi sau, bhikkhavai, mahāaggikkhandhau tadāhārau tadupādānau ciraṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ jalaiyya| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, upādāniyaisu dhammaisu assādānupassinau viharatau taṇhā pavaḍḍhati| taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

‘‘upādāniyaisu, bhikkhavai, dhammaisu ādīnavānupassinau viharatau taṇhā nirujjhati| taṇhāniraudhā upādānaniraudhau; upādānaniraudhā bhavaniraudhau; bhavaniraudhā jātiniraudhau; jātiniraudhā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā nirujjhanti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hauti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, dasan‍naṃ vā kaṭṭhavāhānaṃ vīsāya vā tiṃsāya  vā cattārīsāya vā kaṭṭhavāhānaṃ mahāaggikkhandhau jalaiyya; tatra purisau na kālaina kālaṃ sukkhāni caiva tiṇāni pakkhipaiyya, na sukkhāni ca gaumayāni pakkhipaiyya, na sukkhāni ca kaṭṭhāni pakkhipaiyya| aivañhi sau, bhikkhavai, mahāaggikkhandhau purimassa ca upādānassa pariyādānā añ‍ñassa ca anupahārā anupāhārā (pī॰) anāhārau nibbāyaiyya| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, upādāniyaisu dhammaisu ādīnavānupassinau viharatau taṇhā nirujjhati, taṇhāniraudhā upādānaniraudhau…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hautī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. saṃyaujanasuttaṃ

53. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati  …pai॰… ‘‘saṃyaujaniyaisu, bhikkhavai, dhammaisu assādānupassinau viharatau taṇhā pavaḍḍhati| taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ; upādānapac‍cayā bhavau; bhavapac‍cayā jāti; jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā sambhavanti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti’’|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, tailañ‍ca paṭic‍ca vaṭṭiñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tailappadīpau jhāyaiyya| tatra purisau kālaina kālaṃ tailaṃ āsiñ‍caiyya vaṭṭiṃ upasaṃharaiyya| aivañhi sau, bhikkhavai, tailappadīpau tadāhārau tadupādānau ciraṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ jalaiyya| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, saṃyaujaniyaisu dhammaisu assādānupassinau viharatau taṇhā pavaḍḍhati| taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ; upādānapac‍cayā bhavau; bhavapac‍cayā jāti; jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā sambhavanti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

‘‘saṃyaujaniyaisu, bhikkhavai, dhammaisu ādīnavānupassinau viharatau taṇhā nirujjhati| taṇhāniraudhā upādānaniraudhau; upādānaniraudhā bhavaniraudhau; bhavaniraudhā jātiniraudhau; jātiniraudhā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā nirujjhanti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hauti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, tailañ‍ca paṭic‍ca vaṭṭiñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tailappadīpau jhāyaiyya| tatra purisau na kālaina kālaṃ tailaṃ āsiñ‍caiyya na vaṭṭiṃ upasaṃharaiyya| aivañhi sau, bhikkhavai, tailappadīpau purimassa ca upādānassa pariyādānā añ‍ñassa ca anupahārā anāhārau nibbāyaiyya| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, saṃyaujaniyaisu dhammaisu ādīnavānupassinau viharatau taṇhā nirujjhati| taṇhāniraudhā upādānaniraudhau…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hautī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. dutiyasaṃyaujanasuttaṃ

54. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati  …pai॰… ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, tailañ‍ca paṭic‍ca vaṭṭiñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tailappadīpau jhāyaiyya| tatra purisau kālaina kālaṃ tailaṃ āsiñ‍caiyya vaṭṭiṃ upasaṃharaiyya| aivañhi sau, bhikkhavai, tailappadīpau tadāhārau tadupādānau ciraṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ jalaiyya| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, saṃyaujaniyaisu dhammaisu assādānupassinau  viharatau taṇhā pavaḍḍhati| taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti’’|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, tailañ‍ca paṭic‍ca vaṭṭiñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tailappadīpau jhāyaiyya| tatra purisau na kālaina kālaṃ tailaṃ āsiñ‍caiyya na vaṭṭiṃ upasaṃharaiyya| aivañhi sau, bhikkhavai, tailappadīpau purimassa ca upādānassa pariyādānā añ‍ñassa ca anupahārā anāhārau nibbāyaiyya| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, saṃyaujaniyaisu dhammaisu ādīnavānupassinau viharatau taṇhā nirujjhati| taṇhāniraudhā upādānaniraudhau…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hautī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. mahārukkhasuttaṃ

55. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘upādāniyaisu, bhikkhavai, dhammaisu assādānupassinau viharatau taṇhā pavaḍḍhati| taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ; upādānapac‍cayā bhavau…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti’’|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, mahārukkhau| tassa yāni caiva mūlāni adhaugamāni, yāni ca tiriyaṅgamāni, sabbāni tāni uddhaṃ aujaṃ abhiharanti| aivañhi sau, bhikkhavai, mahārukkhau tadāhārau tadupādānau ciraṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ tiṭṭhaiyya| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, upādāniyaisu dhammaisu assādānupassinau viharatau taṇhā pavaḍḍhati| taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ…pai॰… aivamaitassa  kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

‘‘upādāniyaisu, bhikkhavai, dhammaisu ādīnavānupassinau viharatau taṇhā nirujjhati| taṇhāniraudhā upādānaniraudhau; upādānaniraudhā bhavaniraudhau…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hauti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, mahārukkhau| atha purisau āgacchaiyya kuddālapiṭakaṃ kudālapiṭakaṃ (añ‍ñattha) ādāya| sau taṃ rukkhaṃ mūlai chindaiyya, mūlaṃ chinditvā palikhaṇaiyya paliṃkhaṇaiyya (pī॰ ka॰), palikhaṇitvā mūlāni uddharaiyya antamasau usīranāḷimattānipi| sau taṃ rukkhaṃ khaṇḍākhaṇḍikaṃ chindaiyya, khaṇḍākhaṇḍikaṃ chinditvā phālaiyya, phālaitvā sakalikaṃ sakalikaṃ karaiyya, sakalikaṃ sakalikaṃ karitvā vātātapai visausaiyya; vātātapai visausaitvā agginā ḍahaiyya, agginā ḍahaitvā masiṃ karaiyya, masiṃ  karitvā mahāvātai vā auphuṇaiyya aupunaiyya (sī॰ pī॰), auphunaiyya (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) nadiyā vā sīghasautāya pavāhaiyya| aivañhi sau, bhikkhavai, mahārukkhau ucchin‍namūlau  assa tālāvatthukatau anabhāvaṃkatau anabhāvakatau (sī॰), anabhāvaṅgatau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) āyatiṃ anuppādadhammau| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, upādāniyaisu dhammaisu ādīnavānupassinau viharatau taṇhā nirujjhati| taṇhāniraudhā upādānaniraudhau; upādānaniraudhā bhavaniraudhau…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hautī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dutiyamahārukkhasuttaṃ

56. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, mahārukkhau| tassa yāni caiva mūlāni adhaugamāni, yāni ca tiriyaṅgamāni, sabbāni tāni uddhaṃ aujaṃ abhiharanti| aivañhi sau, bhikkhavai, mahārukkhau tadāhārau tadupādānau ciraṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ tiṭṭhaiyya| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, upādāniyaisu dhammaisu assādānupassinau viharatau taṇhā pavaḍḍhati| taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ …pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti’’|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, mahārukkhau| atha purisau āgacchaiyya kuddālapiṭakaṃ ādāya| sau taṃ rukkhaṃ mūlai chindaiyya, mūlai chaitvā palikhaṇaiyya, palikhaṇitvā mūlāni uddharaiyya…pai॰… nadiyā vā sīghasautāya pavāhaiyya| aivañhi sau, bhikkhavai, mahārukkhau ucchin‍namūlau assa tālāvatthukatau anabhāvaṅkatau āyatiṃ anuppādadhammau| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, upādāniyaisu dhammaisu ādīnavānupassinau viharatau taṇhā nirujjhati| taṇhāniraudhā upādānaniraudhau…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hautī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. taruṇarukkhasuttaṃ

57. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘saṃyaujaniyaisu, bhikkhavai, dhammaisu assādānupassinau viharatau taṇhā pavaḍḍhati| taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti’’|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, taruṇau rukkhau| tassa purisau kālaina kālaṃ mūlāni palimaj‍jaiyya palisan‍naiyya (sī॰), palisaj‍jaiyya (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), palipaṭṭhaiyya (ka॰), palisandaiyya, palibandhaiyya (ṭīkānurūpaṃ) kālaina kālaṃ paṃsuṃ dadaiyya, kālaina  kālaṃ  udakaṃ dadaiyya| aivañhi sau, bhikkhavai, taruṇau rukkhau tadāhārau tadupādānau vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jaiyya| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, saṃyaujaniyaisu dhammaisu assādānupassinau viharatau taṇhā pavaḍḍhati| taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

‘‘saṃyaujaniyaisu, bhikkhavai, dhammaisu ādīnavānupassinau viharatau taṇhā nirujjhati| taṇhāniraudhā upādānaniraudhau…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hauti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, taruṇau rukkhau| atha purisau āgacchaiyya kuddālapiṭakaṃ ādāya…pai॰… nadiyā vā sīghasautāya pavāhaiyya| aivañhi sau, bhikkhavai, taruṇau rukkhau ucchin‍namūlau assa tālāvatthukatau anabhāvaṅkatau āyatiṃ anuppādadhammau| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, saṃyaujaniyaisu dhammaisu ādīnavānupassinau viharatau taṇhā nirujjhati| taṇhāniraudhā upādānaniraudhau…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hautī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. nāmarūpasuttaṃ

58. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘saṃyaujaniyaisu, bhikkhavai, dhammaisu assādānupassinau viharatau nāmarūpassa avak‍kanti hauti| nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatanaṃ…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti’’|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, mahārukkhau| tassa yāni caiva mūlāni  adhaugamāni, yāni ca tiriyaṅgamāni, sabbāni tāni uddhaṃ aujaṃ abhiharanti| aivañhi sau, bhikkhavai, mahārukkhau tadāhārau tadupādānau ciraṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ tiṭṭhaiyya| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, saṃyaujaniyaisu dhammaisu assādānupassinau viharatau nāmarūpassa avak‍kanti hauti…pai॰…|

‘‘saṃyaujaniyaisu, bhikkhavai, dhammaisu ādīnavānupassinau viharatau nāmarūpassa avak‍kanti na hauti| nāmarūpaniraudhā  saḷāyatananiraudhau…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hauti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, mahārukkhau| atha purisau āgacchaiyya kuddālapiṭakaṃ ādāya…pai॰… āyatiṃ anuppādadhammau| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, saṃyaujaniyaisu dhammaisu ādīnavānupassinau viharatau nāmarūpassa avak‍kanti na hauti| nāmarūpaniraudhā saḷāyatananiraudhau…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hautī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. viñ‍ñāṇasuttaṃ

59. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘saṃyaujaniyaisu, bhikkhavai, dhammaisu assādānupassinau viharatau viñ‍ñāṇassa avak‍kanti hauti| viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti’’|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, mahārukkhau| tassa yāni caiva mūlāni  …pai॰… aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, saṃyaujaniyaisu dhammaisu assādānupassinau viharatau viñ‍ñāṇassa avak‍kanti hauti…pai॰…|

‘‘saṃyaujaniyaisu, bhikkhavai, dhammaisu ādīnavānupassinau viharatau viñ‍ñāṇassa avak‍kanti na hauti| viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhā nāmarūpaniraudhau…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hauti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, mahārukkhau| atha purisau āgacchaiyya kuddālapiṭakaṃ ādāya…pai॰… āyatiṃ anuppādadhammau| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, saṃyaujaniyaisu dhammaisu ādīnavānupassinau viharatau viñ‍ñāṇassa avak‍kanti na hauti| viñ‍ñāṇassa niraudhā nāmarūpaniraudhau…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hautī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. nidānasuttaṃ

60. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā kurūsu viharati kammāsadhammaṃ nāma kurūnaṃ nigamau| atha khau āyasmā ānandau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhantai, abbhutaṃ, bhantai! yāva gambhīrau cāyaṃ, bhantai, paṭic‍casamuppādau gambhīrāvabhāsau  ca, atha ca pana mai uttānakuttānakau viya khāyatī’’ti|

‘‘mā  haivaṃ, ānanda, mā haivaṃ, ānanda mā haivaṃ ānanda avaca mā haivaṃ ānanda avaca (dī॰ ni॰ 2 mahānidānasuttai)! gambhīrau cāyaṃ, ānanda, paṭic‍casamuppādau gambhīrāvabhāsau ca| aitassa, ānanda, dhammassa ananubaudhā appaṭivaidhā aivamayaṃ pajā tantākulakajātā kulagaṇṭhikajātā guḷāguṇṭhikajātā (sī॰), guḷīguṇṭhikajātā (syā॰ kaṃ॰) muñ‍japabbajabhūtā muñ‍jababbajabhūtā (sī॰) apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ saṃsāraṃ nātivattati|

‘‘upādāniyaisu, ānanda, dhammaisu assādānupassinau viharatau taṇhā pavaḍḍhati| taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ; upādānapac‍cayā bhavau; bhavapac‍cayā jāti; jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā sambhavanti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, ānanda, mahārukkhau| tassa yāni caiva mūlāni adhaugamāni, yāni ca tiriyaṅgamāni, sabbāni tāni uddhaṃ aujaṃ abhiharanti| aivañhi sau, ānanda, mahārukkhau tadāhārau tadupādānau ciraṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ tiṭṭhaiyya| aivamaiva khau, ānanda, upādāniyaisu dhammaisu assādānupassinau viharatau taṇhā pavaḍḍhati| taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ; upādānapac‍cayā bhavau…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

‘‘upādāniyaisu, ānanda, dhammaisu ādīnavānupassinau viharatau taṇhā  nirujjhati| taṇhāniraudhā upādānaniraudhau; upādānaniraudhā bhavaniraudhau…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hauti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, ānanda, mahārukkhau| atha purisau āgacchaiyya kuddālapiṭakaṃ ādāya| sau taṃ rukkhaṃ mūlai chindaiyya, mūlai chaitvā palikhaṇaiyya, palikhaṇitvā mūlāni uddharaiyya antamasau usīranāḷimattānipi| sau taṃ rukkhaṃ khaṇḍākhaṇḍikaṃ chindaiyya| khaṇḍākhaṇḍikaṃ chinditvā phālaiyya; phālaitvā sakalikaṃ sakalikaṃ karaiyya, sakalikaṃ sakalikaṃ karitvā vātātapai visausaiyya, vātātapai visausaitvā agginā ḍahaiyya, agginā ḍahaitvā masiṃ karaiyya, masiṃ karitvā mahāvātai vā auphuṇaiyya, nadiyā vā sīghasautāya pavāhaiyya| aivañhi sau, ānanda, mahārukkhau ucchin‍namūlau assa tālāvatthukatau anabhāvaṅkatau āyatiṃ anuppādadhammau| aivamaiva khau, ānanda, upādāniyaisu dhammaisu ādīnavānupassinau viharatau taṇhā nirujjhati| taṇhāniraudhā upādānaniraudhau; upādānaniraudhā bhavaniraudhau; bhavaniraudhā jātiniraudhau; jātiniraudhā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā nirujjhanti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hautī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

dukkhavaggau chaṭṭhau|

tassuddānaṃ –

parivīmaṃsanupādānaṃ,  dvai ca saṃyaujanāni ca|

mahārukkhaina dvai vuttā, taruṇaina ca sattamaṃ|

nāmarūpañ‍ca viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, nidānaina ca tai dasāti||

7. mahāvaggau

1. assutavāsuttaṃ

61. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai…pai॰… ‘‘assutavā, bhikkhavai, puthuj‍janau imasmiṃ cātumahābhūtikasmiṃ kāyasmiṃ nibbindaiyyapi viraj‍jaiyyapi vimuc‍caiyyapi| taṃ kissa haitu? cātummahābhūtikasmiṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) dissati, bhikkhavai dissati hi bhikkhavai (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰), imassa  cātumahābhūtikassa kāyassa ācayaupi apacayaupi ādānampi nikkhaipanampi| tasmā tatrāssutavā puthuj‍janau nibbindaiyyapi viraj‍jaiyyapi vimuc‍caiyyapi’’|

‘‘yañ‍ca khau aitaṃ, bhikkhavai, vuc‍cati cittaṃ itipi, manau itipi, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ itipi, tatrāssutavā puthuj‍janau nālaṃ nibbindituṃ nālaṃ viraj‍jituṃ nālaṃ vimuc‍cituṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? dīgharattañhaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, assutavatau puthuj‍janassa ajjhausitaṃ mamāyitaṃ parāmaṭṭhaṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’ti| tasmā tatrāssutavā puthuj‍janau nālaṃ nibbindituṃ nālaṃ viraj‍jituṃ nālaṃ vimuc‍cituṃ|

‘‘varaṃ, bhikkhavai, assutavā puthuj‍janau imaṃ cātumahābhūtikaṃ kāyaṃ attatau upagacchaiyya, na tvaiva cittaṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? dissatāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, cātumahābhūtikau kāyau aikampi vassaṃ tiṭṭhamānau dvaipi vassāni tiṭṭhamānau tīṇipi vassāni tiṭṭhamānau cattāripi vassāni tiṭṭhamānau pañ‍capi vassāni tiṭṭhamānau dasapi vassāni tiṭṭhamānau vīsatipi vassāni tiṭṭhamānau tiṃsampi vassāni tiṭṭhamānau cattārīsampi  vassāni tiṭṭhamānau pañ‍ñāsampi vassāni tiṭṭhamānau vassasatampi tiṭṭhamānau, bhiyyaupi  tiṭṭhamānau|

‘‘yañ‍ca khau aitaṃ, bhikkhavai, vuc‍cati cittaṃ itipi, manau itipi, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ itipi, taṃ rattiyā ca divasassa ca añ‍ñadaiva uppaj‍jati añ‍ñaṃ nirujjhati| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, mak‍kaṭau arañ‍ñai pavanai caramānau sākhaṃ gaṇhati, taṃ muñ‍citvā añ‍ñaṃ gaṇhati, taṃ muñ‍citvā añ‍ñaṃ gaṇhati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yamidaṃ vuc‍cati cittaṃ itipi, manau itipi, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ itipi, taṃ rattiyā ca divasassa ca añ‍ñadaiva uppaj‍jati añ‍ñaṃ nirujjhati|

‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau paṭic‍casamuppādaṃyaiva sādhukaṃ yaunisau manasi karauti – ‘iti imasmiṃ sati idaṃ hauti, imassuppādā idaṃ uppaj‍jati; imasmiṃ asati idaṃ na hauti, imassa niraudhā idaṃ nirujjhati – yadidaṃ avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā; saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti| avij‍jāya tvaiva asaisavirāganiraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau; saṅkhāraniraudhā  viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhau…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hautī’’’ti|

‘‘aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau rūpasmimpi nibbindati, vaidanāyapi nibbindati, sañ‍ñāyapi nibbindati, saṅkhāraisupi  nibbindati, viñ‍ñāṇasmimpi nibbindati; nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati, virāgā vimuc‍cati, vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānātī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. dutiyaassutavāsuttaṃ

62. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘assutavā, bhikkhavai, puthuj‍janau imasmiṃ cātumahābhūtikasmiṃ kāyasmiṃ nibbindaiyyapi viraj‍jaiyyapi vimuc‍caiyyapi| taṃ kissa haitu? dissati, bhikkhavai, imassa cātumahābhūtikassa kāyassa ācayaupi  apacayaupi ādānampi nikkhaipanampi| tasmā tatrāssutavā puthuj‍janau nibbindaiyyapi viraj‍jaiyyapi vimuc‍caiyyapi| yañ‍ca khau aitaṃ, bhikkhavai, vuc‍cati cittaṃ itipi, manau itipi, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ itipi, tatrāssutavā puthuj‍janau nālaṃ nibbindituṃ nālaṃ viraj‍jituṃ nālaṃ vimuc‍cituṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? dīgharattañhaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, assutavatau puthuj‍janassa ajjhausitaṃ mamāyitaṃ parāmaṭṭhaṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’ti| tasmā tatrāssutavā puthuj‍janau nālaṃ nibbindituṃ nālaṃ viraj‍jituṃ nālaṃ vimuc‍cituṃ’’|

‘‘varaṃ, bhikkhavai, assutavā puthuj‍janau imaṃ cātumahābhūtikaṃ kāyaṃ attatau upagacchaiyya, na tvaiva cittaṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? dissatāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, cātumahābhūtikau kāyau aikampi vassaṃ tiṭṭhamānau dvaipi vassāni tiṭṭhamānau tīṇipi vassāni tiṭṭhamānau cattāripi vassāni tiṭṭhamānau pañ‍capi vassāni tiṭṭhamānau dasapi vassāni tiṭṭhamānau  vīsatipi vassāni tiṭṭhamānau tiṃsampi vassāni tiṭṭhamānau cattārīsampi vassāni tiṭṭhamānau pañ‍ñāsampi vassāni tiṭṭhamānau vassasatampi tiṭṭhamānau, bhiyyaupi tiṭṭhamānau| yañ‍ca khau aitaṃ, bhikkhavai, vuc‍cati cittaṃ itipi, manau itipi, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ itipi, taṃ rattiyā ca divasassa ca añ‍ñadaiva uppaj‍jati añ‍ñaṃ nirujjhati|

‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau paṭic‍casamuppādaṃyaiva sādhukaṃ yaunisau manasi karauti – ‘iti imasmiṃ sati idaṃ hauti, imassuppādā idaṃ uppaj‍jati; imasmiṃ asati idaṃ na hauti, imassa niraudhā idaṃ nirujjhatī’ti| sukhavaidaniyaṃ, bhikkhavai, phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhavaidanā| tassaiva sukhavaidaniyassa phassassa niraudhā yaṃ taj‍jaṃ vaidayitaṃ sukhavaidaniyaṃ phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppan‍nā sukhavaidanā sā nirujjhati sā vūpasammati| dukkhavaidaniyaṃ, bhikkhavai, phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dukkhavaidanā| tassaiva dukkhavaidaniyassa phassassa niraudhā yaṃ taj‍jaṃ vaidayitaṃ dukkhavaidaniyaṃ  phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppan‍nā dukkhavaidanā sā nirujjhati sā vūpasammati| adukkhamasukhavaidaniyaṃ, bhikkhavai, phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati adukkhamasukhavaidanā| tassaiva adukkhamasukhavaidaniyassa phassassa niraudhā yaṃ taj‍jaṃ vaidayitaṃ adukkhamasukhavaidaniyaṃ phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppan‍nā adukkhamasukhavaidanā sā nirujjhati sā vūpasammati|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, dvin‍naṃ kaṭṭhānaṃ saṅghaṭṭanasamaudhānā usmā jāyati taijau abhinibbattati| taisaṃyaiva dvin‍naṃ kaṭṭhānaṃ nānākatavinibbhaugā  nānābhāvāvinikkhaipā (sī॰ pī॰) ma॰ ni॰ 3.357 yā taj‍jā usmā sā nirujjhati sā vūpasammati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, sukhavaidaniyaṃ phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhavaidanā| tassaiva sukhavaidaniyassa phassassa niraudhā yaṃ taj‍jaṃ vaidayitaṃ sukhavaidaniyaṃ phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppan‍nā sukhavaidanā sā nirujjhati sā vūpasammati…pai॰… adukkhamasukhavaidaniyaṃ phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati adukkhamasukhavaidanā| tassaiva adukkhamasukhavaidaniyassa phassassa niraudhā yaṃ taj‍jaṃ vaidayitaṃ adukkhamasukhavaidaniyaṃ phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppan‍nā adukkhamasukhavaidanā sā nirujjhati sā vūpasammati|

‘‘aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau phassaipi nibbindati, vaidanāyapi nibbindati, sañ‍ñāyapi nibbindati, saṅkhāraisupi nibbindati, viñ‍ñāṇasmimpi nibbindati; nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati, virāgā vimuc‍cati, vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānātī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. puttamaṃsūpamasuttaṃ

63. sāvatthiyaṃ  …pai॰… ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, āhārā bhūtānaṃ vā sattānaṃ ṭhitiyā sambhavaisīnaṃ vā anuggahāya| katamai cattārau? kabaḷīkārau āhārau auḷārikau vā sukhumau vā, phassau dutiyau, manausañ‍caitanā tatiyā, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ catutthaṃ| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau āhārā bhūtānaṃ vā sattānaṃ ṭhitiyā sambhavaisīnaṃ vā anuggahāya’’|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, kabaḷīkārau āhārau daṭṭhabbau? saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, dvai jāyampatikā jayampatikā (sī॰ pī॰) ṭīkā aulaukaitabbā parittaṃ sambalaṃ ādāya kantāramaggaṃ paṭipaj‍jaiyyuṃ| taisamassa aikaputtakau piyau manāpau| atha khau taisaṃ, bhikkhavai, dvin‍naṃ jāyampatikānaṃ kantāragatānaṃ yā parittā sambalamattā, sā parikkhayaṃ pariyādānaṃ gacchaiyya| siyā ca naisaṃ kantārāvasaisau anatiṇṇau| atha khau taisaṃ, bhikkhavai, dvin‍naṃ jāyampatikānaṃ aivamassa – ‘amhākaṃ khau yā parittā sambalamattā sā parikkhīṇā pariyādiṇṇā pariyādin‍nā (syā॰ kaṃ॰)| atthi  cāyaṃ kantārāvasaisau anittiṇṇau anitthiṇṇau (syā॰ kaṃ॰), anatiṇṇau (ka॰)| yaṃnūna mayaṃ imaṃ aikaputtakaṃ piyaṃ manāpaṃ vadhitvā val‍lūrañ‍ca sauṇḍikañ‍ca karitvā puttamaṃsāni khādantā aivaṃ taṃ kantārāvasaisaṃ nittharaiyyāma, mā sabbaiva tayau vinassimhā’ti| atha khau tai, bhikkhavai, dvai jāyampatikā taṃ aikaputtakaṃ piyaṃ manāpaṃ vadhitvā val‍lūrañ‍ca sauṇḍikañ‍ca karitvā puttamaṃsāni khādantā aivaṃ taṃ kantārāvasaisaṃ nittharaiyyuṃ| tai puttamaṃsāni caiva khādaiyyuṃ, urai ca paṭipisaiyyuṃ – ‘kahaṃ, aikaputtaka, kahaṃ, aikaputtakā’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, api nu tai davāya vā āhāraṃ āhāraiyyuṃ, madāya vā āhāraṃ  āhāraiyyuṃ, maṇḍanāya  vā āhāraṃ āhāraiyyuṃ, vibhūsanāya vā āhāraṃ āhāraiyyu’’nti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘nanu tai, bhikkhavai, yāvadaiva kantārassa nittharaṇatthāya āhāraṃ āhāraiyyu’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aivamaiva khvāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, kabaḷīkārau āhārau daṭṭhabbau’’ti vadāmi| kabaḷīkārai, bhikkhavai, āhārai pariñ‍ñātai pañ‍cakāmaguṇikau rāgau pariñ‍ñātau hauti| pañ‍cakāmaguṇikai rāgai pariñ‍ñātai natthi taṃ saṃyaujanaṃ yaina saṃyaujanaina saṃyuttau ariyasāvakau puna imaṃ laukaṃ āgacchaiyya|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, phassāhārau daṭṭhabbau? saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, gāvī nic‍cammā kuṭṭaṃ cai kuḍḍañ‍cai (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)  nissāya tiṭṭhaiyya| yai kuṭṭanissitā pāṇā tai naṃ khādaiyyuṃ| rukkhaṃ cai nissāya tiṭṭhaiyya, yai rukkhanissitā pāṇā tai naṃ khādaiyyuṃ| udakaṃ cai nissāya tiṭṭhaiyya, yai udakanissitā pāṇā tai naṃ khādaiyyuṃ| ākāsaṃ cai nissāya tiṭṭhaiyya, yai ākāsanissitā pāṇā tai naṃ khādaiyyuṃ| yaṃ yadaiva hi sā, bhikkhavai, gāvī nic‍cammā nissāya tiṭṭhaiyya, yai tan‍nissitā yai tan‍nissitā tan‍nissitā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) pāṇā tai naṃ khādaiyyuṃ| aivamaiva khvāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, ‘‘phassāhārau daṭṭhabbau’’ti vadāmi| phassai, bhikkhavai, āhārai pariñ‍ñātai tissau vaidanā pariñ‍ñātā haunti| tīsu vaidanāsu pariñ‍ñātāsu ariyasāvakassa natthi kiñ‍ci uttarikaraṇīyanti uttariṃkaraṇīyanti (sī॰ pī॰) vadāmi|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, manausañ‍caitanāhārau daṭṭhabbau? saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, aṅgārakāsu sādhikapaurisā puṇṇā aṅgārānaṃ vītac‍cikānaṃ vītadhūmānaṃ| atha purisau āgacchaiyya jīvitukāmau amaritukāmau sukhakāmau dukkhappaṭikūlau| tamainaṃ dvai balavantau purisā nānābāhāsu gahaitvā taṃ aṅgārakāsuṃ upakaḍḍhaiyyuṃ| atha khau, bhikkhavai, tassa purisassa ārakāvassa caitanā ārakā patthanā ārakā paṇidhi| taṃ  kissa haitu? aivañhi, bhikkhavai, tassa purisassa hauti – ‘imaṃ cāhaṃ aṅgārakāsuṃ papatissāmi, tataunidānaṃ maraṇaṃ vā nigacchāmi maraṇamattaṃ vā dukkha’nti| aivamaiva khvāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, ‘manausañ‍caitanāhārau daṭṭhabbau’ti vadāmi| manausañ‍caitanāya, bhikkhavai, āhārai pariñ‍ñātai tissau taṇhā pariñ‍ñātā haunti| tīsu taṇhāsu pariñ‍ñātāsu ariyasāvakassa natthi kiñ‍ci uttarikaraṇīyanti vadāmi|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñāṇāhārau daṭṭhabbau? saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, cauraṃ āgucāriṃ gahaitvā rañ‍ñau dassaiyyuṃ – ‘ayaṃ tai, daiva, caurau āgucārī, imassa yaṃ icchasi taṃ daṇḍaṃ paṇaihī’ti| tamainaṃ rājā aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘gacchatha, bhau, imaṃ purisaṃ pubbaṇhasamayaṃ sattisataina hanathā’ti| tamainaṃ pubbaṇhasamayaṃ sattisataina hanaiyyuṃ| atha rājā majjhanhikasamayaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘ambhau, kathaṃ sau purisau’ti? ‘tathaiva, daiva, jīvatī’ti| tamainaṃ rājā aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘gacchatha, bhau, taṃ purisaṃ majjhanhikasamayaṃ sattisataina hanathā’ti| tamainaṃ majjhanhikasamayaṃ sattisataina hanaiyyuṃ| atha rājā sāyanhasamayaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘ambhau, kathaṃ  sau purisau’ti? ‘tathaiva, daiva, jīvatī’ti| tamainaṃ rājā aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘gacchatha, bhau, taṃ purisaṃ sāyanhasamayaṃ sattisataina hanathā’ti| tamainaṃ sāyanhasamayaṃ sattisataina hanaiyyuṃ| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, api nu sau purisau divasaṃ tīhi sattisataihi hañ‍ñamānau tataunidānaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidiyaithā’’ti? ‘‘aikissāpi, bhantai, sattiyā hañ‍ñamānau tataunidānaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidiyaitha; kau pana vādau tīhi sattisataihi hañ‍ñamānau’’ti! ‘‘aivamaiva khvāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñāṇāhārau daṭṭhabbauti vadāmi| viñ‍ñāṇai, bhikkhavai, āhārai pariñ‍ñātai nāmarūpaṃ pariñ‍ñātaṃ hauti, nāmarūpai pariñ‍ñātai ariyasāvakassa natthi kiñ‍ci uttarikaraṇīyanti vadāmī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. atthirāgasuttaṃ

64. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati…pai॰… ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, āhārā bhūtānaṃ vā sattānaṃ ṭhitiyā sambhavaisīnaṃ vā anuggahāya| katamai cattārau? kabaḷīkārau āhārau auḷārikau vā sukhumau vā, phassau dutiyau, manausañ‍caitanā tatiyā, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ catutthaṃ| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau āhārā bhūtānaṃ vā sattānaṃ ṭhitiyā sambhavaisīnaṃ vā anuggahāya’’|

‘‘kabaḷīkārai cai, bhikkhavai, āhārai atthi rāgau atthi nandī atthi taṇhā, patiṭṭhitaṃ tattha viñ‍ñāṇaṃ virūḷhaṃ| yattha patiṭṭhitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ virūḷhaṃ, atthi tattha nāmarūpassa avak‍kanti| yattha atthi nāmarūpassa avak‍kanti, atthi tattha saṅkhārānaṃ vuddhi| yattha  atthi saṅkhārānaṃ vuddhi, atthi tattha āyatiṃ punabbhavābhinibbatti| yattha atthi āyatiṃ punabbhavābhinibbatti, atthi tattha āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇaṃ| yattha atthi āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇaṃ, sasaukaṃ taṃ, bhikkhavai, sadaraṃ saupāyāsanti vadāmi|

‘‘phassai cai, bhikkhavai, āhārai…pai॰… manausañ‍caitanāya cai, bhikkhavai, āhārai… viñ‍ñāṇai cai, bhikkhavai, āhārai atthi rāgau atthi nandī atthi taṇhā, patiṭṭhitaṃ tattha viñ‍ñāṇaṃ virūḷhaṃ| yattha patiṭṭhitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ virūḷhaṃ, atthi tattha nāmarūpassa avak‍kanti| yattha atthi nāmarūpassa avak‍kanti, atthi tattha saṅkhārānaṃ vuddhi| yattha atthi saṅkhārānaṃ vuddhi, atthi tattha āyatiṃ punabbhavābhinibbatti| yattha atthi āyatiṃ punabbhavābhinibbatti, atthi tattha āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇaṃ| yattha atthi āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇaṃ, sasaukaṃ taṃ, bhikkhavai, sadaraṃ saupāyāsanti vadāmi|
