‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, rajakau vā cittakārakau vā sati rajanāya vā lākhāya vā haliddiyā vā nīliyā vā mañ‍jiṭṭhāya  vā suparimaṭṭhai vā phalakai bhittiyā vā dussapaṭṭai vā itthirūpaṃ vā purisarūpaṃ vā abhinimminaiyya sabbaṅgapac‍caṅgaṃ; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, kabaḷīkārai cai āhārai atthi rāgau atthi nandī atthi taṇhā, patiṭṭhitaṃ tattha viñ‍ñāṇaṃ virūḷhaṃ| yattha patiṭṭhitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ virūḷhaṃ, atthi tattha nāmarūpassa avak‍kanti| yattha atthi nāmarūpassa avak‍kanti, atthi tattha saṅkhārānaṃ vuddhi| yattha atthi saṅkhārānaṃ vuddhi, atthi tattha  āyatiṃ punabbhavābhinibbatti| yattha atthi āyatiṃ punabbhavābhinibbatti, atthi tattha  āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇaṃ| yattha atthi āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇaṃ, sasaukaṃ taṃ, bhikkhavai, sadaraṃ saupāyāsanti vadāmi|

‘‘phassai cai, bhikkhavai, āhārai…pai॰… manausañ‍caitanāya cai, bhikkhavai, āhārai… viñ‍ñāṇai cai, bhikkhavai, āhārai atthi rāgau atthi nandī atthi taṇhā, patiṭṭhitaṃ tattha viñ‍ñāṇaṃ virūḷhaṃ| yattha patiṭṭhitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ virūḷhaṃ, atthi tattha nāmarūpassa avak‍kanti| yattha atthi nāmarūpassa avak‍kanti, atthi tattha saṅkhārānaṃ vuddhi| yattha atthi saṅkhārānaṃ vuddhi, atthi tattha āyatiṃ punabbhavābhinibbatti| yattha atthi āyatiṃ punabbhavābhinibbatti, atthi tattha āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇaṃ| yattha atthi āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇaṃ, sasaukaṃ taṃ, bhikkhavai, sadaraṃ saupāyāsanti vadāmi|

‘‘kabaḷīkārai cai, bhikkhavai, āhārai natthi rāgau natthi nandī natthi taṇhā, appatiṭṭhitaṃ tattha viñ‍ñāṇaṃ avirūḷhaṃ| yattha appatiṭṭhitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ avirūḷhaṃ, natthi tattha nāmarūpassa avak‍kanti| yattha natthi nāmarūpassa avak‍kanti, natthi tattha saṅkhārānaṃ vuddhi| yattha natthi saṅkhārānaṃ vuddhi, natthi tattha āyatiṃ punabbhavābhinibbatti| yattha natthi āyatiṃ punabbhavābhinibbatti, natthi tattha āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇaṃ| yattha natthi āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇaṃ, asaukaṃ taṃ, bhikkhavai, adaraṃ anupāyāsanti vadāmi|

‘‘phassai  cai, bhikkhavai, āhārai…pai॰… manausañ‍caitanāya cai, bhikkhavai, āhārai… viñ‍ñāṇai cai, bhikkhavai, āhārai  natthi rāgau natthi nandī natthi taṇhā, appatiṭṭhitaṃ tattha viñ‍ñāṇaṃ avirūḷhaṃ| yattha appatiṭṭhitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ avirūḷhaṃ, natthi tattha nāmarūpassa avak‍kanti| yattha natthi nāmarūpassa avak‍kanti, natthi tattha saṅkhārānaṃ vuddhi| yattha natthi saṅkhārānaṃ vuddhi, natthi tattha āyatiṃ punabbhavābhinibbatti| yattha natthi āyatiṃ punabbhavābhinibbatti, natthi tattha āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇaṃ| yattha natthi āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇaṃ, asaukaṃ taṃ, bhikkhavai, adaraṃ anupāyāsanti vadāmi|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, kūṭāgāraṃ vā kūṭāgārasālaṃ vā uttarāya vā dakkhiṇāya vā pācīnāya vā vātapānā sūriyai uggacchantai vātapānaina rasmi pavisitvā k‍vāssa patiṭṭhitā’’ kattha patiṭṭhitā (ka॰) ti? ‘‘pacchimāyaṃ, bhantai, bhittiya’’nti| ‘‘pacchimā cai, bhikkhavai, bhitti nāssa k‍vāssa patiṭṭhitā’’ti? ‘‘pathaviyaṃ, bhantai’’ti| ‘‘pathavī cai, bhikkhavai, nāssa k‍vāssa patiṭṭhitā’’ti? ‘‘āpasmiṃ, bhantai’’ti| ‘‘āpau cai, bhikkhavai, nāssa k‍vāssa patiṭṭhitā’’ti? ‘‘appatiṭṭhitā, bhantai’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, kabaḷīkārai cai āhārai natthi rāgau natthi nandī natthi taṇhā…pai॰…|

‘‘phassai cai, bhikkhavai, āhārai… manausañ‍caitanāya cai, bhikkhavai, āhārai… viñ‍ñāṇai cai, bhikkhavai, āhārai natthi rāgau natthi nandī natthi taṇhā, appatiṭṭhitaṃ tattha viñ‍ñāṇaṃ avirūḷhaṃ| yattha appatiṭṭhitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ avirūḷhaṃ, natthi tattha nāmarūpassa avak‍kanti| yattha natthi nāmarūpassa avak‍kanti, natthi tattha saṅkhārānaṃ vuddhi| yattha natthi saṅkhārānaṃ  vuddhi, natthi tattha āyatiṃ punabbhavābhinibbatti| yattha natthi āyatiṃ punabbhavābhinibbatti, natthi tattha āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇaṃ| yattha natthi āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇaṃ asaukaṃ taṃ, bhikkhavai, adaraṃ anupāyāsanti vadāmī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. nagarasuttaṃ

65. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘pubbai mai, bhikkhavai, sambaudhā anabhisambuddhassa baudhisattassaiva satau aitadahausi – ‘kicchā vatāyaṃ laukau āpan‍nau jāyati ca jīyati ca mīyati ca cavati ca upapaj‍jati ca| atha ca panimassa dukkhassa nissaraṇaṃ nappajānāti jarāmaraṇassa| kudāssu nāma imassa dukkhassa nissaraṇaṃ pañ‍ñāyissati jarāmaraṇassā’ti? tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi – ‘kimhi nu khau sati jarāmaraṇaṃ hauti, kiṃpac‍cayā jarāmaraṇa’nti? tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, yaunisau manasikārā ahu pañ‍ñāya abhisamayau – ‘jātiyā khau sati jarāmaraṇaṃ hauti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇa’’’nti|

‘‘tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi – ‘kimhi nu khau sati jāti hauti…pai॰… bhavau hauti… upādānaṃ hauti… taṇhā hauti… vaidanā hauti… phassau hauti… saḷāyatanaṃ hauti… nāmarūpaṃ hauti… kiṃpac‍cayā nāmarūpa’nti? tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, yaunisau manasikārā ahu pañ‍ñāya abhisamayau – ‘viñ‍ñāṇai khau sati nāmarūpaṃ hauti, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpa’nti| tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi – ‘kimhi nu khau sati viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hauti, kiṃpac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇa’nti? tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, yaunisau manasikārā ahu pañ‍ñāya abhisamayau – ‘nāmarūpai khau sati viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hauti, nāmarūpapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇa’’’nti|

‘‘tassa  mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi – pac‍cudāvattati khau idaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nāmarūpamhā na paraṃ gacchati| aittāvatā jāyaitha vā jīyaitha vā mīyaitha vā cavaitha vā upapaj‍jaitha vā, yadidaṃ nāmarūpapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ; viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ; nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatanaṃ; saḷāyatanapac‍cayā phassau…pai॰… aivamaitassa  kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti| ‘samudayau, samudayau’ti khau mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi ñāṇaṃ udapādi pañ‍ñā udapādi vij‍jā udapādi ālaukau udapādi|

‘‘tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi – ‘kimhi nu khau asati, jarāmaraṇaṃ na hauti; kissa niraudhā jarāmaraṇaniraudhau’ti? tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, yaunisau manasikārā ahu pañ‍ñāya abhisamayau – ‘jātiyā khau asati, jarāmaraṇaṃ na hauti; jātiniraudhā jarāmaraṇaniraudhau’ti| tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi – ‘kimhi nu khau asati jāti na hauti…pai॰… bhavau na hauti… upādānaṃ na hauti… taṇhā na hauti… vaidanā na hauti… phassau na hauti… saḷāyatanaṃ na hauti… nāmarūpaṃ na hauti| kissa niraudhā nāmarūpaniraudhau’ti? tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, yaunisau manasikārā ahu pañ‍ñāya abhisamayau  – ‘viñ‍ñāṇai khau asati, nāmarūpaṃ na hauti; viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhā nāmarūpaniraudhau’’’ti|

‘‘tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi – ‘kimhi nu khau asati viñ‍ñāṇaṃ na hauti; kissa niraudhā viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhau’ti? tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, yaunisau manasikārā ahu pañ‍ñāya abhisamayau – ‘nāmarūpai khau asati, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ na hauti; nāmarūpaniraudhā viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhau’’’ti|

‘‘tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi – adhigatau khau myāyaṃ maggau baudhāya yadidaṃ – nāmarūpaniraudhā viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhau; viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhā nāmarūpaniraudhau; nāmarūpaniraudhā saḷāyatananiraudhau; saḷāyatananiraudhā phassaniraudhau…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hauti| ‘niraudhau, niraudhau’ti khau mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi ñāṇaṃ udapādi pañ‍ñā udapādi vij‍jā udapādi ālaukau udapādi|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau arañ‍ñai pavanai caramānau passaiyya purāṇaṃ maggaṃ purāṇañ‍jasaṃ pubbakaihi manussaihi anuyātaṃ| sau tamanugacchaiyya| tamanugacchantau passaiyya purāṇaṃ nagaraṃ purāṇaṃ rājadhāniṃ pubbakaihi  manussaihi ajjhāvuṭṭhaṃ ajjhāvutthaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) ārāmasampan‍naṃ vanasampan‍naṃ paukkharaṇīsampan‍naṃ uddhāpavantaṃ uddāpavantaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) ramaṇīyaṃ| atha khau sau, bhikkhavai, purisau rañ‍ñau vā rājamahāmattassa vā āraucaiyya – ‘yagghai, bhantai, jānaiyyāsi – ahaṃ addasaṃ arañ‍ñai pavanai caramānau purāṇaṃ maggaṃ purāṇañ‍jasaṃ pubbakaihi manussaihi anuyātaṃ tamanugacchiṃ| tamanugacchantau addasaṃ purāṇaṃ nagaraṃ purāṇaṃ rājadhāniṃ pubbakaihi manussaihi ajjhāvuṭṭhaṃ  ārāmasampan‍naṃ vanasampan‍naṃ paukkharaṇīsampan‍naṃ uddhāpavantaṃ ramaṇīyaṃ| taṃ, bhantai, nagaraṃ māpaihī’ti| atha khau sau, bhikkhavai, rājā vā rājamahāmattau vā taṃ nagaraṃ māpaiyya| tadassa nagaraṃ aparaina samayaina iddhañ‍caiva phītañ‍ca bāhujañ‍ñaṃ ākiṇṇamanussaṃ vuddhivaipul‍lappattaṃ| aivamaiva khvāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, addasaṃ purāṇaṃ maggaṃ purāṇañ‍jasaṃ pubbakaihi sammāsambuddhaihi anuyātaṃ|

‘‘katamau ca sau, bhikkhavai, purāṇamaggau purāṇañ‍jasau pubbakaihi sammāsambuddhaihi anuyātau? ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi| ayaṃ khau sau, bhikkhavai, purāṇamaggau purāṇañ‍jasau pubbakaihi sammāsambuddhaihi anuyātau, tamanugacchiṃ; tamanugacchantau jarāmaraṇaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ; jarāmaraṇasamudayaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ; jarāmaraṇaniraudhaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ; jarāmaraṇaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ| tamanugacchiṃ; tamanugacchantau jātiṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ…pai॰… bhavaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ… upādānaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ… taṇhaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ… vaidanaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ… phassaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ… saḷāyatanaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ… nāmarūpaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ| tamanugacchiṃ; tamanugacchantau saṅkhārai abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ; saṅkhārasamudayaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ; saṅkhāraniraudhaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ; saṅkhāraniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ| tadabhiñ‍ñā  ācikkhiṃ bhikkhūnaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ upāsakānaṃ upāsikānaṃ| tayidaṃ, bhikkhavai, brahmacariyaṃ iddhañ‍caiva phītañ‍ca vitthārikaṃ bāhujañ‍ñaṃ puthubhūtaṃ yāva daivamanussaihi suppakāsita’’nti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. sammasasuttaṃ

66. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kurūsu viharati kammāsadhammaṃ nāma kurūnaṃ nigamau| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti  tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘sammasatha nau tumhai, bhikkhavai, antaraṃ sammasa’’nti antarā sammasananti (sī॰)| aivaṃ vuttai, añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ahaṃ khau, bhantai, sammasāmi antaraṃ sammasa’’nti| ‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana tvaṃ, bhikkhu, sammasasi antaraṃ sammasa’’nti? atha khau sau bhikkhu byākāsi| yathā sau bhikkhu byākāsi na sau bhikkhu bhagavatau cittaṃ ārādhaisi|

aivaṃ vuttai, āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aitassa, bhagavā, kālau; aitassa, sugata, kālau; yaṃ bhagavā antaraṃ sammasaṃ bhāsaiyya| bhagavatau sutvā bhikkhū dhāraissantī’’ti| ‘‘tainahānanda, suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammasamānau sammasati antaraṃ sammasaṃ sammasanaṃ (sī॰) – ‘yaṃ khau idaṃ anaikavidhaṃ nānappakārakaṃ dukkhaṃ laukai uppaj‍jati jarāmaraṇaṃ| idaṃ khau dukkhaṃ kiṃnidānaṃ kiṃsamudayaṃ kiṃjātikaṃ  kiṃpabhavaṃ, kismiṃ sati jarāmaraṇaṃ hauti, kismiṃ asati jarāmaraṇaṃ na hautī’ti? sau sammasamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘yaṃ khau idaṃ anaikavidhaṃ nānappakārakaṃ dukkhaṃ  laukai uppaj‍jati jarāmaraṇaṃ| idaṃ khau dukkhaṃ upadhinidānaṃ upadhisamudayaṃ upadhijātikaṃ upadhipabhavaṃ, upadhismiṃ sati jarāmaraṇaṃ hauti, upadhismiṃ asati jarāmaraṇaṃ na hautī’ti| sau jarāmaraṇañ‍ca pajānāti jarāmaraṇasamudayañ‍ca pajānāti jarāmaraṇaniraudhañ‍ca pajānāti yā ca jarāmaraṇaniraudhasāruppagāminī paṭipadā tañ‍ca pajānāti| tathāpaṭipan‍nau ca hauti anudhammacārī| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sabbasau sammā dukkhakkhayāya paṭipan‍nau jarāmaraṇaniraudhāya|

‘‘athāparaṃ sammasamānau sammasati antaraṃ sammasaṃ – ‘upadhi panāyaṃ kiṃnidānau kiṃsamudayau kiṃjātikau kiṃpabhavau, kismiṃ sati upadhi hauti, kismiṃ asati upadhi na hautī’ti? sau sammasamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘upadhi taṇhānidānau taṇhāsamudayau taṇhājātikau taṇhāpabhavau, taṇhāya sati upadhi hauti, taṇhāya asati upadhi na hautī’ti| sau upadhiñ‍ca pajānāti upadhisamudayañ‍ca pajānāti upadhiniraudhañ‍ca  pajānāti yā ca upadhiniraudhasāruppagāminī paṭipadā tañ‍ca pajānāti| tathā paṭipan‍nau ca hauti anudhammacārī| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sabbasau sammā dukkhakkhayāya paṭipan‍nau upadhiniraudhāya|

‘‘athāparaṃ sammasamānau sammasati antaraṃ sammasaṃ – ‘taṇhā panāyaṃ kattha uppaj‍jamānā uppaj‍jati, kattha nivisamānā nivisatī’ti? sau sammasamānau aivaṃ jānāti – yaṃ khau laukai piyarūpaṃ sātarūpaṃ aitthaisā taṇhā uppaj‍jamānā uppaj‍jati, aittha nivisamānā nivisati| kiñ‍ca laukai piyarūpaṃ sātarūpaṃ? cakkhuṃ laukai piyarūpaṃ, sātarūpaṃ| aitthaisā taṇhā uppaj‍jamānā uppaj‍jati, aittha nivisamānā nivisati| sautaṃ laukai piyarūpaṃ sātarūpaṃ…pai॰… ghānaṃ  laukai piyarūpaṃ sātarūpaṃ… jivhā laukai piyarūpaṃ sātarūpaṃ… kāyau laukai piyarūpaṃ sātarūpaṃ… manau laukai piyarūpaṃ sātarūpaṃ aitthaisā taṇhā uppaj‍jamānā uppaj‍jati aittha nivisamānā nivisati|

‘‘yai  hi kaici, bhikkhavai, atītamaddhānaṃ samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā yaṃ laukai piyarūpaṃ sātarūpaṃ taṃ nic‍catau addakkhuṃ sukhatau addakkhuṃ attatau addakkhuṃ āraugyatau addakkhuṃ khaimatau addakkhuṃ| tai taṇhaṃ vaḍḍhaisuṃ| yai taṇhaṃ vaḍḍhaisuṃ tai upadhiṃ vaḍḍhaisuṃ| yai upadhiṃ vaḍḍhaisuṃ tai dukkhaṃ vaḍḍhaisuṃ| yai dukkhaṃ vaḍḍhaisuṃ tai na parimuc‍ciṃsu jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi, na parimuc‍ciṃsu dukkhasmāti vadāmi|

‘‘yaipi hi kaici, bhikkhavai, anāgatamaddhānaṃ samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā yaṃ laukai piyarūpaṃ sātarūpaṃ taṃ nic‍catau dakkhissanti dakkhinti (sī॰) sukhatau dakkhissanti attatau dakkhissanti āraugyatau dakkhissanti khaimatau dakkhissanti| tai taṇhaṃ vaḍḍhissanti| yai taṇhaṃ vaḍḍhissanti tai upadhiṃ vaḍḍhissanti| yai upadhiṃ vaḍḍhissanti tai dukkhaṃ vaḍḍhissanti| yai dukkhaṃ vaḍḍhissanti tai na parimuc‍cissanti jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi, na parimuc‍cissanti dukkhasmāti vadāmi|

‘‘yaipi hi kaici, bhikkhavai, aitarahi samaṇā  vā brāhmaṇā vā yaṃ laukai piyarūpaṃ sātarūpaṃ taṃ nic‍catau passanti sukhatau passanti attatau passanti āraugyatau passanti khaimatau passanti| tai taṇhaṃ vaḍḍhainti| yai taṇhaṃ vaḍḍhainti tai upadhiṃ vaḍḍhainti| yai upadhiṃ vaḍḍhainti tai dukkhaṃ vaḍḍhainti| yai dukkhaṃ vaḍḍhainti tai na parimuc‍canti jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi, na parimuc‍canti dukkhasmāti vadāmi|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, āpānīyakaṃsau vaṇṇasampan‍nau gandhasampan‍nau rasasampan‍nau| sau ca khau visaina saṃsaṭṭhau| atha purisau āgacchaiyya ghammābhitattau ghammaparaitau kilantau tasitau pipāsitau| tamainaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘ayaṃ tai, ambhau purisa, āpānīyakaṃsau vaṇṇasampan‍nau gandhasampan‍nau rasasampan‍nau; sau ca khau visaina saṃsaṭṭhau| sacai ākaṅkhasi piva| pivatau hi khau taṃ chādaissati vaṇṇainapi gandhainapi rasainapi, pivitvā ca pana tataunidānaṃ maraṇaṃ vā nigacchasi maraṇamattaṃ vā dukkha’nti| sau taṃ āpānīyakaṃsaṃ sahasā appaṭisaṅkhā pivaiyya, nappaṭinissaj‍jaiyya| sau tataunidānaṃ maraṇaṃ vā nigacchaiyya maraṇamattaṃ vā dukkhaṃ| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yai hi kaici atītamaddhānaṃ samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā yaṃ laukai piyarūpaṃ…pai॰… anāgatamaddhānaṃ…pai॰… aitarahi samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā yaṃ laukai piyarūpaṃ  sātarūpaṃ taṃ nic‍catau passanti sukhatau  passanti attatau passanti āraugyatau passanti khaimatau passanti, tai taṇhaṃ vaḍḍhainti| yai taṇhaṃ vaḍḍhainti tai upadhiṃ vaḍḍhainti| yai upadhiṃ vaḍḍhainti tai dukkhaṃ vaḍḍhainti| yai dukkhaṃ vaḍḍhainti tai na parimuc‍canti jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi, na parimuc‍canti dukkhasmāti vadāmi|

‘‘yai ca khau kaici, bhikkhavai, atītamaddhānaṃ samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā yaṃ laukai piyarūpaṃ sātarūpaṃ taṃ anic‍catau addakkhuṃ dukkhatau addakkhuṃ anattatau addakkhuṃ raugatau addakkhuṃ bhayatau addakkhuṃ, tai taṇhaṃ pajahiṃsu| yai taṇhaṃ pajahiṃsu tai upadhiṃ pajahiṃsu| yai upadhiṃ pajahiṃsu tai dukkhaṃ pajahiṃsu| yai dukkhaṃ pajahiṃsu tai parimuc‍ciṃsu jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi, parimuc‍ciṃsu dukkhasmāti vadāmi|

‘‘yaipi hi kaici, bhikkhavai, anāgatamaddhānaṃ samaṇā vā  brāhmaṇā vā yaṃ laukai piyarūpaṃ sātarūpaṃ taṃ anic‍catau dakkhissanti dukkhatau dakkhissanti anattatau  dakkhissanti raugatau dakkhissanti bhayatau dakkhissanti, tai taṇhaṃ pajahissanti| yai taṇhaṃ pajahissanti…pai॰… parimuc‍cissanti dukkhasmāti vadāmi|

‘‘yaipi hi kaici, bhikkhavai, aitarahi samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā yaṃ laukai piyarūpaṃ sātarūpaṃ taṃ anic‍catau passanti dukkhatau passanti anattatau passanti raugatau passanti bhayatau  passanti, tai taṇhaṃ pajahanti| yai taṇhaṃ pajahanti tai upadhiṃ pajahanti| yai upadhiṃ pajahanti tai dukkhaṃ pajahanti| yai dukkhaṃ pajahanti tai parimuc‍canti jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi, parimuc‍canti dukkhasmāti vadāmi|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, āpānīyakaṃsau vaṇṇasampan‍nau gandhasampan‍nau rasasampan‍nau| sau ca khau visaina saṃsaṭṭhau| atha purisau āgacchaiyya ghammābhitattau ghammaparaitau kilantau tasitau pipāsitau| tamainaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘ayaṃ tai, ambhau purisa, āpānīyakaṃsau vaṇṇasampan‍nau gandhasampan‍nau rasasampan‍nau sau ca khau visaina saṃsaṭṭhau| sacai ākaṅkhasi piva| pivatau hi khau taṃ chādaissati vaṇṇainapi gandhainapi rasainapi; pivitvā ca pana tataunidānaṃ maraṇaṃ vā nigacchasi  maraṇamattaṃ vā dukkha’nti| atha khau, bhikkhavai, tassa purisassa aivamassa – ‘sak‍kā khau mai ayaṃ surāpipāsitā surāpipāsā (?) pānīyaina vā vinaituṃ dadhimaṇḍakaina vā vinaituṃ bhaṭṭhalauṇikāya maṭṭhalauṇikāya (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) vā vinaituṃ lauṇasauvīrakaina vā vinaituṃ, na tvaivāhaṃ taṃ pivaiyyaṃ, yaṃ mama assa dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāyā’ti| sau taṃ āpānīyakaṃsaṃ paṭisaṅkhā na pivaiyya, paṭinissaj‍jaiyya| sau tataunidānaṃ na maraṇaṃ vā nigacchaiyya maraṇamattaṃ vā dukkhaṃ| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yai hi kaici atītamaddhānaṃ samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā yaṃ laukai piyarūpaṃ sātarūpaṃ taṃ  anic‍catau addakkhuṃ dukkhatau addakkhuṃ anattatau addakkhuṃ raugatau addakkhuṃ bhayatau addakkhuṃ, tai taṇhaṃ pajahiṃsu| yai taṇhaṃ pajahiṃsu tai upadhiṃ pajahiṃsu| yai upadhiṃ pajahiṃsu tai dukkhaṃ pajahiṃsu| yai dukkhaṃ pajahiṃsu tai parimuc‍ciṃsu jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi, parimuc‍ciṃsu dukkhasmāti vadāmi|

‘‘yaipi  hi kaici, bhikkhavai, anāgatamaddhānaṃ…pai॰… aitarahi samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā yaṃ laukai piyarūpaṃ sātarūpaṃ taṃ anic‍catau passanti dukkhatau passanti anattatau passanti raugatau passanti bhayatau passanti, tai taṇhaṃ pajahanti| yai taṇhaṃ pajahanti tai upadhiṃ pajahanti| yai upadhiṃ pajahanti tai dukkhaṃ pajahanti| yai dukkhaṃ pajahanti tai parimuc‍canti jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi, parimuc‍canti dukkhasmāti vadāmī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. naḷakalāpīsuttaṃ

67. aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā ca sāriputtau āyasmā ca mahākauṭṭhikau mahākauṭṭhitau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) bārāṇasiyaṃ viharanti isipatanai migadāyai| atha khau āyasmā mahākauṭṭhikau sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaināyasmā sāriputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā sāriputtaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā mahākauṭṭhikau āyasmantaṃ  sāriputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau, āvusau sāriputta, sayaṃkataṃ jarāmaraṇaṃ, paraṃkataṃ jarāmaraṇaṃ, sayaṃkatañ‍ca  paraṃkatañ‍ca jarāmaraṇaṃ, udāhu asayaṃkāraṃ aparaṃkāraṃ adhic‍casamuppan‍naṃ jarāmaraṇa’’nti? ‘‘na khau, āvusau kauṭṭhika, sayaṃkataṃ  jarāmaraṇaṃ, na paraṃkataṃ jarāmaraṇaṃ, na sayaṃkatañ‍ca paraṃkatañ‍ca jarāmaraṇaṃ, nāpi asayaṃkāraṃ aparaṃkāraṃ adhic‍casamuppan‍naṃ jarāmaraṇaṃ| api ca, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇa’’nti|

‘‘kiṃ nu khau, āvusau sāriputta, sayaṃkatā jāti, paraṃkatā jāti, sayaṃkatā ca paraṃkatā ca jāti, udāhu asayaṃkārā aparaṃkārā adhic‍casamuppan‍nā jātī’’ti? ‘‘na khau, āvusau kauṭṭhika, sayaṃkatā jāti, na paraṃkatā jāti, na sayaṃkatā ca paraṃkatā ca jāti, nāpi asayaṃkārā aparaṃkārā adhic‍casamuppan‍nā jāti| api ca, bhavapac‍cayā jātī’’ti|

‘‘kiṃ nu khau, āvusau sāriputta, sayaṃkatau bhavau…pai॰… sayaṃkataṃ upādānaṃ… sayaṃkatā taṇhā… sayaṃkatā vaidanā… sayaṃkatau phassau… sayaṃkataṃ saḷāyatanaṃ… sayaṃkataṃ nāmarūpaṃ, paraṃkataṃ nāmarūpaṃ, sayaṃkatañ‍ca paraṃkatañ‍ca nāmarūpaṃ, udāhu asayaṃkāraṃ aparaṃkāraṃ adhic‍casamuppan‍naṃ nāmarūpa’’nti? ‘‘na khau, āvusau kauṭṭhika, sayaṃkataṃ nāmarūpaṃ, na paraṃkataṃ nāmarūpaṃ, na sayaṃkatañ‍ca paraṃkatañ‍ca nāmarūpaṃ, nāpi asayaṃkāraṃ aparaṃkāraṃ, adhic‍casamuppan‍naṃ nāmarūpaṃ| api ca, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpa’’nti|

‘‘kiṃ nu khau, āvusau sāriputta, sayaṅkataṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, paraṅkataṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, sayaṃkatañ‍ca paraṃkatañ‍ca viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, udāhu  asayaṃkāraṃ aparaṃkāraṃ adhic‍casamuppan‍naṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’’nti? ‘‘na khau, āvusau kauṭṭhika, sayaṃkataṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, na paraṃkataṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ na sayaṃkatañ‍ca paraṃkatañ‍ca viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, nāpi asayaṃkāraṃ aparaṃkāraṃ adhic‍casamuppan‍naṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ| api ca, nāmarūpapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇa’’nti|

‘‘idānaiva  khau mayaṃ āyasmatau sāriputtassa bhāsitaṃ aivaṃ ājānāma – ‘na khvāvusau kauṭṭhika, sayaṃkataṃ nāmarūpaṃ, na paraṃkataṃ nāmarūpaṃ, na sayaṃkatañ‍ca paraṃkatañ‍ca nāmarūpaṃ, nāpi asayaṃkāraṃ aparaṃkāraṃ adhic‍casamuppan‍naṃ nāmarūpaṃ| api ca, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpa’’’nti|

‘‘idānaiva ca pana mayaṃ āyasmatau sāriputtassa bhāsitaṃ aivaṃ ājānāma – ‘na khvāvusau kauṭṭhika, sayaṃkataṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, na paraṃkataṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, na sayaṃkatañ‍ca paraṃkatañ‍ca viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, nāpi asayaṃkāraṃ aparaṃkāraṃ adhic‍casamuppan‍naṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ| api ca, nāmarūpapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇa’’’nti|

‘‘yathā kathaṃ panāvusau sāriputta, imassa bhāsitassa atthau daṭṭhabbau’’ti? ‘‘tainahāvusau, upamaṃ tai karissāmi| upamāyapidhaikac‍cai viñ‍ñū purisā bhāsitassa atthaṃ jānanti| saiyyathāpi, āvusau, dvai naḷakalāpiyau añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nissāya tiṭṭhaiyyuṃ| aivamaiva khau, āvusau, nāmarūpapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ; viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ; nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatanaṃ; saḷāyatanapac‍cayā phassau…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti| tāsaṃ cai, āvusau, naḷakalāpīnaṃ aikaṃ ākaḍḍhaiyya, aikā papataiyya; aparaṃ cai ākaḍḍhaiyya, aparā papataiyya| aivamaiva khau, āvusau, nāmarūpaniraudhā viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhau; viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhā nāmarūpaniraudhau; nāmarūpaniraudhā saḷāyatananiraudhau; saḷāyatananiraudhā phassaniraudhau…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hautī’’ti| ‘‘acchariyaṃ, āvusau sāriputta; abbhutaṃ, āvusau sāriputta! yāvasubhāsitaṃ cidaṃ āyasmatā sāriputtaina| idañ‍ca pana mayaṃ āyasmatau sāriputtassa bhāsitaṃ imaihi chattiṃsāya vatthūhi anumaudāma – ‘jarāmaraṇassa cai, āvusau, bhikkhu nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, dhammakathikau bhikkhūti alaṃ  vacanāya| jarāmaraṇassa cai, āvusau, bhikkhu nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau bhikkhūti alaṃ vacanāya| jarāmaraṇassa cai, āvusau, bhikkhu nibbidā virāgā niraudhā anupādā vimuttau hauti, diṭṭhadhammanibbānappattau bhikkhūti alaṃ vacanāya| jātiyā cai… bhavassa cai… upādānassa cai… taṇhāya cai… vaidanāya cai… phassassa cai… saḷāyatanassa cai… nāmarūpassa cai… viñ‍ñāṇassa cai… saṅkhārānaṃ cai… avij‍jāya cai, āvusau, bhikkhu nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, dhammakathikau bhikkhūti alaṃ vacanāya| avij‍jāya cai, āvusau, bhikkhu nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau bhikkhūti alaṃ vacanāya| avij‍jāya cai, āvusau, bhikkhu nibbidā virāgā niraudhā anupādā vimuttau hauti, diṭṭhadhammanibbānappattau bhikkhūti alaṃ vacanāyā’’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. kausambisuttaṃ

68. aikaṃ  samayaṃ āyasmā ca musilau mūsilau (sī॰), musīlau (pī॰) āyasmā ca paviṭṭhau saviṭṭhau (sī॰ pī॰) āyasmā ca nāradau āyasmā ca ānandau kausambiyaṃ viharanti ghausitārāmai| atha khau āyasmā paviṭṭhau āyasmantaṃ musilaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘añ‍ñatraiva, āvusau musila, saddhāya añ‍ñatra ruciyā añ‍ñatra anussavā añ‍ñatra ākāraparivitak‍kā añ‍ñatra diṭṭhinijjhānakkhantiyā atthāyasmatau musilassa pac‍cattamaiva ñāṇaṃ – ‘jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇa’’’nti? ‘‘añ‍ñatraiva, āvusau paviṭṭha, saddhāya añ‍ñatra ruciyā añ‍ñatra anussavā añ‍ñatra ākāraparivitak‍kā añ‍ñatra diṭṭhinijjhānakkhantiyā ahamaitaṃ jānāmi ahamaitaṃ passāmi – ‘jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇa’’’nti|

‘‘añ‍ñatraiva, āvusau musila, saddhāya añ‍ñatra ruciyā añ‍ñatra anussavā añ‍ñatra ākāraparivitak‍kā añ‍ñatra diṭṭhinijjhānakkhantiyā atthāyasmatau musilassa  pac‍cattamaiva ñāṇaṃ – ‘bhavapac‍cayā jātīti…pai॰… upādānapac‍cayā bhavauti… taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānanti… vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhāti… phassapac‍cayā vaidanāti… saḷāyatanapac‍cayā phassauti… nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatananti… viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpanti… saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇanti… avij‍jāpac‍cayā  saṅkhārā’’’ti? ‘‘añ‍ñatraiva, āvusau paviṭṭha, saddhāya añ‍ñatra ruciyā añ‍ñatra anussavā añ‍ñatra ākāraparivitak‍kā añ‍ñatra diṭṭhinijjhānakkhantiyā ahamaitaṃ jānāmi ahamaitaṃ passāmi – ‘avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā’’’ti|

‘‘añ‍ñatraiva, āvusau musila, saddhāya añ‍ñatra ruciyā añ‍ñatra anussavā añ‍ñatra ākāraparivitak‍kā añ‍ñatra diṭṭhinijjhānakkhantiyā atthāyasmatau musilassa pac‍cattamaiva ñāṇaṃ – ‘jātiniraudhā jarāmaraṇaniraudhau’’’ti? ‘‘añ‍ñatraiva, āvusau paviṭṭha, saddhāya añ‍ñatra ruciyā añ‍ñatra anussavā añ‍ñatra ākāraparivitak‍kā añ‍ñatra diṭṭhinijjhānakkhantiyā ahamaitaṃ jānāmi ahamaitaṃ passāmi – ‘jātiniraudhā jarāmaraṇaniraudhau’’’ti|

‘‘añ‍ñatraiva, āvusau musila, saddhāya añ‍ñatra ruciyā añ‍ñatra anussavā añ‍ñatra ākāraparivitak‍kā añ‍ñatra diṭṭhinijjhānakkhantiyā atthāyasmatau musilassa pac‍cattamaiva ñāṇaṃ – ‘bhavaniraudhā jātiniraudhauti…pai॰… upādānaniraudhā bhavaniraudhauti… taṇhāniraudhā upādānaniraudhauti… vaidanāniraudhā taṇhāniraudhauti… phassaniraudhā vaidanāniraudhauti… saḷāyatananiraudhā phassaniraudhauti… nāmarūpaniraudhā saḷāyatananiraudhauti… viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhā nāmarūpaniraudhauti … saṅkhāraniraudhā viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhauti… avij‍jāniraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau’’’ti? ‘‘añ‍ñatraiva, āvusau paviṭṭha, saddhāya añ‍ñatra ruciyā añ‍ñatra anussavā añ‍ñatra ākāraparivitak‍kā añ‍ñatra diṭṭhinijjhānakkhantiyā  ahamaitaṃ jānāmi ahamaitaṃ passāmi – ‘avij‍jāniraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau’’’ti|

‘‘añ‍ñatraiva, āvusau musila, saddhāya añ‍ñatra ruciyā añ‍ñatra anussavā añ‍ñatra ākāraparivitak‍kā añ‍ñatra diṭṭhinijjhānakkhantiyā atthāyasmatau musilassa pac‍cattamaiva ñāṇaṃ – ‘bhavaniraudhau nibbāna’’’nti? ‘‘añ‍ñatraiva, āvusau paviṭṭha, saddhāya añ‍ñatra ruciyā añ‍ñatra anussavā añ‍ñatra  ākāraparivitak‍kā añ‍ñatra diṭṭhinijjhānakkhantiyā ahamaitaṃ jānāmi ahamaitaṃ passāmi – ‘bhavaniraudhau nibbāna’’’nti|

‘‘tainahāyasmā musilau arahaṃ khīṇāsavau’’ti? aivaṃ vuttai, āyasmā musilau tuṇhī ahausi| atha khau āyasmā nāradau āyasmantaṃ paviṭṭhaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhāvusau paviṭṭha, ahaṃ aitaṃ pañhaṃ labhaiyyaṃ| maṃ aitaṃ pañhaṃ puccha| ahaṃ tai aitaṃ pañhaṃ byākarissāmī’’ti| ‘‘labhatāyasmā nāradau aitaṃ pañhaṃ| pucchāmahaṃ āyasmantaṃ nāradaṃ aitaṃ pañhaṃ| byākarautu ca mai āyasmā nāradau aitaṃ pañhaṃ’’|

‘‘añ‍ñatraiva, āvusau nārada, saddhāya añ‍ñatra ruciyā añ‍ñatra anussavā añ‍ñatra ākāraparivitak‍kā añ‍ñatra diṭṭhinijjhānakkhantiyā atthāyasmatau nāradassa pac‍cattamaiva ñāṇaṃ – ‘jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇa’’’nti? ‘‘añ‍ñatraiva, āvusau paviṭṭha, saddhāya añ‍ñatra ruciyā añ‍ñatra anussavā añ‍ñatra ākāraparivitak‍kā añ‍ñatra diṭṭhinijjhānakkhantiyā ahamaitaṃ  jānāmi ahamaitaṃ passāmi – ‘jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇa’’’nti|

‘‘añ‍ñatraiva, āvusau nārada, saddhāya añ‍ñatra ruciyā añ‍ñatra anussavā añ‍ñatra ākāraparivitak‍kā añ‍ñatra diṭṭhinijjhānakkhantiyā atthāyasmatau nāradassa pac‍cattamaiva ñāṇaṃ – bhavapac‍cayā jāti…pai॰… avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā’’ti? ‘‘añ‍ñatraiva, āvusau paviṭṭha, saddhāya añ‍ñatra ruciyā añ‍ñatra anussavā añ‍ñatra ākāraparivitak‍kā añ‍ñatra diṭṭhinijjhānakkhantiyā ahamaitaṃ jānāmi ahamaitaṃ passāmi – ‘avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā’’’ti|

‘‘añ‍ñatraiva, āvusau nārada, saddhāya añ‍ñatra ruciyā añ‍ñatra anussavā añ‍ñatra ākāraparivitak‍kā añ‍ñatra diṭṭhinijjhānakkhantiyā atthāyasmatau nāradassa pac‍cattamaiva ñāṇaṃ – ‘jātiniraudhā jarāmaraṇaniraudhau’’’ti? ‘‘añ‍ñatraiva, āvusau paviṭṭha, saddhāya añ‍ñatra ruciyā añ‍ñatra anussavā añ‍ñatra ākāraparivitak‍kā añ‍ñatra diṭṭhinijjhānakkhantiyā ahamaitaṃ jānāmi ahamaitaṃ passāmi – ‘jātiniraudhā jarāmaraṇaniraudhau’’’ti|

‘‘añ‍ñatraiva, āvusau nārada, saddhāya añ‍ñatra ruciyā añ‍ñatra anussavā añ‍ñatra ākāraparivitak‍kā añ‍ñatra diṭṭhinijjhānakkhantiyā atthāyasmatau nāradassa  pac‍cattamaiva ñāṇaṃ – ‘bhavaniraudhā jātiniraudhauti…pai॰… avij‍jāniraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau’’’ti? ‘‘añ‍ñatraiva, āvusau paviṭṭha, saddhāya añ‍ñatra  ruciyā añ‍ñatra anussavā añ‍ñatra ākāraparivitak‍kā añ‍ñatra diṭṭhinijjhānakkhantiyā ahamaitaṃ jānāmi ahamaitaṃ passāmi – ‘avij‍jāniraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau’’’ti|

‘‘añ‍ñatraiva, āvusau nārada, saddhāya añ‍ñatra ruciyā añ‍ñatra anussavā añ‍ñatra ākāraparivitak‍kā añ‍ñatra diṭṭhinijjhānakkhantiyā atthāyasmatau nāradassa pac‍cattamaiva ñāṇaṃ – ‘bhavaniraudhau nibbāna’’’nti? ‘‘añ‍ñatraiva, āvusau paviṭṭha, saddhāya añ‍ñatra ruciyā añ‍ñatra anussavā añ‍ñatra ākāraparivitak‍kā añ‍ñatra diṭṭhinijjhānakkhantiyā ahamaitaṃ jānāmi ahamaitaṃ passāmi – ‘bhavaniraudhau nibbāna’’’nti|

‘‘tainahāyasmā nāradau arahaṃ khīṇāsavau’’ti? ‘‘‘bhavaniraudhau  nibbāna’nti khau mai, āvusau, yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhaṃ, na camhi arahaṃ khīṇāsavau| saiyyathāpi, āvusau, kantāramaggai  udapānau| tatra naivassa raj‍ju na udakavārakau| atha purisau āgacchaiyya ghammābhitattau ghammaparaitau kilantau tasitau pipāsitau, sau taṃ udapānaṃ aulaukaiyya| tassa ‘udaka’nti hi khau ñāṇaṃ assa, na ca kāyaina phusitvā viharaiyya| aivamaiva khau, āvusau, ‘bhavaniraudhau nibbāna’nti yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhaṃ, na camhi arahaṃ khīṇāsavau’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai, āyasmā ānandau āyasmantaṃ paviṭṭhaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aivaṃvādī aivaṃvādiṃ (?) tvaṃ, āvusau paviṭṭha, āyasmantaṃ nāradaṃ kiṃ vadaisī’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃvādāhaṃ, āvusau ānanda, āyasmantaṃ nāradaṃ na kiñ‍ci vadāmi añ‍ñatra kalyāṇā añ‍ñatra kusalā’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. upayantisuttaṃ

69. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau…pai॰… ‘‘mahāsamuddau, bhikkhavai, upayantau mahānadiyau upayāpaiti, mahānadiyau upayantiyau kun‍nadiyau  upayāpainti, kun‍nadiyau upayantiyau mahāsaubbhai upayāpainti, mahāsaubbhā  upayantā kusaubbhai upayāpainti| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, avij‍jā upayantī saṅkhārai upayāpaiti, saṅkhārā upayantā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ upayāpainti, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ upayantaṃ nāmarūpaṃ upayāpaiti, nāmarūpaṃ upayantaṃ saḷāyatanaṃ upayāpaiti, saḷāyatanaṃ upayantaṃ phassaṃ upayāpaiti, phassau upayantau vaidanaṃ upayāpaiti, vaidanā upayantī taṇhaṃ upayāpaiti, taṇhā upayantī upādānaṃ upayāpaiti, upādānaṃ upayantaṃ bhavaṃ  upayāpaiti, bhavau upayantau jātiṃ upayāpaiti, jāti upayantī jarāmaraṇaṃ upayāpaiti|

‘‘mahāsamuddau, bhikkhavai, apayantau mahānadiyau apayāpaiti, mahānadiyau apayantiyau kun‍nadiyau apayāpainti, kun‍nadiyau apayantiyau mahāsaubbhai apayāpainti, mahāsaubbhā apayantā kusaubbhai apayāpainti| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, avij‍jā apayantī saṅkhārai apayāpaiti, saṅkhārā apayantā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ apayāpainti, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ apayantaṃ nāmarūpaṃ apayāpaiti, nāmarūpaṃ apayantaṃ saḷāyatanaṃ apayāpaiti, saḷāyatanaṃ apayantaṃ phassaṃ apayāpaiti, phassau apayantau vaidanaṃ apayāpaiti, vaidanā apayantī taṇhaṃ apayāpaiti, taṇhā apayantī upādānaṃ apayāpaiti, upādānaṃ  apayantaṃ bhavaṃ apayāpaiti, bhavau apayantau jātiṃ apayāpaiti, jāti apayantī jarāmaraṇaṃ apayāpaitī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. susimasuttaṃ

70. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai  kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā sak‍katau hauti garukatau mānitau pūjitau apacitau lābhī cīvara-piṇḍapāta-saināsana-gilānappac‍caya-bhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ| bhikkhusaṅghaupi sak‍katau hauti garukatau mānitau pūjitau apacitau lābhī cīvara-piṇḍapāta-saināsanagilānappac‍caya-bhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ| añ‍ñatitthiyā pana paribbājakā asak‍katā haunti agarukatā amānitā apūjitā anapacitā, na lābhinau cīvara-piṇḍapāta-saināsanagilānappac‍caya-bhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ|

taina  khau pana samayaina susimau susīmau (sī॰ ka॰) paribbājakau rājagahai paṭivasati mahatiyā paribbājakaparisāya saddhiṃ| atha  khau susimassa paribbājakassa parisā susimaṃ paribbājakaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘aihi tvaṃ, āvusau susima, samaṇai gautamai brahmacariyaṃ cara| tvaṃ dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇitvā amhai vācaiyyāsi vācaissasi (pī॰ ka॰)| taṃ mayaṃ dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇitvā gihīnaṃ bhāsissāma| aivaṃ mayampi sak‍katā bhavissāma garukatā mānitā pūjitā apacitā lābhinau cīvara-piṇḍapātasaināsana-gilānappac‍caya-bhaisaj‍japarikkhārāna’’nti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau susimau paribbājakau sakāya parisāya paṭissuṇitvā yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā ānandaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau susimau paribbājakau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘icchāmahaṃ, āvusau ānanda, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai brahmacariyaṃ caritu’’nti|

atha  khau āyasmā ānandau susimaṃ paribbājakaṃ ādāya yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ayaṃ, bhantai, susimau paribbājakau aivamāha – ‘icchāmahaṃ, āvusau ānanda, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai brahmacariyaṃ caritu’’nti| ‘‘tainahānanda, susimaṃ pabbājaithā’’ti| alattha khau susimau paribbājakau bhagavatau santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ, alattha upasampadaṃ|

taina khau pana samayaina sambahulaihi bhikkhūhi bhagavatau santikai añ‍ñā byākatā hauti – ‘‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānāmā’’ti| assausi khau āyasmā susimau – ‘‘sambahulaihi kira bhikkhūhi  bhagavatau santikai añ‍ñā byākatā – ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāmā’’ti| atha khau āyasmā susimau yaina tai bhikkhū tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā taihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā susimau tai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kirāyasmantaihi  bhagavatau santikai añ‍ñā byākatā – ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāmā’’ti? ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti|

‘‘api pana api nu (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) aivamuparipi tumhai āyasmantau aivaṃ jānantā aivaṃ passantā  anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhautha – aikaupi hutvā bahudhā hautha, bahudhāpi hutvā aikau hautha; āvibhāvaṃ, tiraubhāvaṃ, tiraukuṭṭaṃ tiraupākāraṃ tiraupabbataṃ asaj‍jamānā gacchatha, saiyyathāpi ākāsai; pathaviyāpi ummuj‍janimuj‍jaṃ karautha, saiyyathāpi udakai; udakaipi abhij‍jamānai gacchatha, saiyyathāpi pathaviyaṃ; ākāsaipi pal‍laṅkaina kamatha, saiyyathāpi pakkhī sakuṇau; imaipi candimasūriyai aivaṃmahiddhikai aivaṃmahānubhāvai pāṇinā parimasatha parimaj‍jatha, yāva brahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattaithā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, āvusau’’|

‘‘api pana tumhai āyasmantau aivaṃ jānantā aivaṃ passantā dibbāya sautadhātuyā visuddhāya atik‍kantamānusikāya ubhau saddai suṇātha dibbai ca mānusai ca yai dūrai santikai cā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, āvusau’’|

‘‘api pana tumhai āyasmantau aivaṃ jānantā aivaṃ passantā parasattānaṃ parapuggalānaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānātha – sarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ sarāgaṃ cittanti pajānātha; vītarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ vītarāgaṃ cittanti pajānātha; sadausaṃ vā cittaṃ sadausaṃ cittanti pajānātha; vītadausaṃ vā  cittaṃ vītadausaṃ cittanti pajānātha; samauhaṃ vā cittaṃ samauhaṃ  cittanti pajānātha; vītamauhaṃ vā cittaṃ vītamauhaṃ cittanti pajānātha; saṃkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ saṃkhittaṃ cittanti pajānātha; vikkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ vikkhittaṃ cittanti pajānātha; mahaggataṃ vā cittaṃ mahaggataṃ cittanti pajānātha; amahaggataṃ vā cittaṃ amahaggataṃ cittanti pajānātha; sauttaraṃ vā cittaṃ sauttaraṃ cittanti pajānātha; anuttaraṃ vā cittaṃ anuttaraṃ cittanti pajānātha; samāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ samāhitaṃ cittanti pajānātha; asamāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ asamāhitaṃ cittanti pajānātha; vimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ vimuttaṃ cittanti pajānātha; avimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ avimuttaṃ cittanti pajānāthā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, āvusau’’|

‘‘api pana tumhai āyasmantau aivaṃ jānantā aivaṃ passantā anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussaratha, saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau tissaupi jātiyau catassaupi jātiyau pañ‍capi jātiyau dasapi jātiyau vīsampi jātiyau tiṃsampi jātiyau cattārīsampi jātiyau pañ‍ñāsampi jātiyau jātisatampi jātisahassampi jātisatasahassampi, anaikaipi saṃvaṭṭakappai anaikaipi vivaṭṭakappai anaikaipi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭakappai – ‘amutrāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhapaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau amutra udapādiṃ; tatrāpāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhapaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau idhūpapan‍nau’ti| iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarathā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, āvusau’’|

‘‘api pana tumhai āyasmantau aivaṃ jānantā aivaṃ passantā dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passatha cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai  hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai, sugatai duggatai yathākammūpagai sattai pajānātha – ‘imai vata bhauntau sattā kāyaduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā  vacīduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā manauduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā, ariyānaṃ upavādakā micchādiṭṭhikā micchādiṭṭhikammasamādānā, tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapan‍nā; imai vā pana bhauntau sattā kāyasucaritaina saman‍nāgatā vacīsucaritaina saman‍nāgatā manausucaritaina saman‍nāgatā, ariyānaṃ anupavādakā sammādiṭṭhikā sammādiṭṭhikammasamādānā tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍nā’ti, iti dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passatha cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai  suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai, sugatai duggatai yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāthā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, āvusau’’|

‘‘api pana tumhai āyasmantau aivaṃ jānantā aivaṃ passantā yai tai santā vimaukkhā atik‍kamma rūpai āruppā, tai kāyaina phusitvā viharathā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, āvusau’’|

‘‘aittha  dāni āyasmantau idañ‍ca vaiyyākaraṇaṃ imaisañ‍ca dhammānaṃ asamāpatti; idaṃ nau, āvusau, katha’’nti? ‘‘pañ‍ñāvimuttā khau mayaṃ, āvusau susimā’’ti|

‘‘na khvāhaṃ imassa āyasmantānaṃ saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāmi| sādhu mai āyasmantau tathā bhāsantu yathāhaṃ imassa āyasmantānaṃ saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa  vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānaiyya’’nti| ‘‘ājānaiyyāsi  vā tvaṃ, āvusau susima, na vā tvaṃ ājānaiyyāsi atha khau pañ‍ñāvimuttā maya’’nti|

atha khau āyasmā susimau uṭṭhāyāsanā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā susimau yāvatakau taihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ ahausi kathāsal‍lāpau taṃ sabbaṃ bhagavatau āraucaisi| ‘‘pubbai khau, susima, dhammaṭṭhitiñāṇaṃ, pacchā nibbānai ñāṇa’’nti|

‘‘na khvāhaṃ, bhantai, imassa bhagavatā bhagavatau (pī॰) saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāmi| sādhu mai, bhantai, bhagavā tathā bhāsatu yathāhaṃ imassa bhagavatā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānaiyya’’nti| ‘‘ājānaiyyāsi vā tvaṃ, susima, na vā tvaṃ ājānaiyyāsi, atha khau dhammaṭṭhitiñāṇaṃ pubbai, pacchā nibbānai ñāṇaṃ’’|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, susima, rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘vaidanā nic‍cā vā anic‍cā vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍cā, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ, dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau  mai attā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘sañ‍ñā nic‍cā vā anic‍cā vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍cā, bhantai’’…pai॰… ‘‘saṅkhārā nic‍cā vā anic‍cā vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍cā, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ  dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘tasmātiha, susima, yaṃ kiñ‍ci rūpaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā auḷārikaṃ vā sukhumaṃ vā hīnaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā yaṃ dūrai santikai vā, sabbaṃ rūpaṃ naitaṃ mama naisauhamasmi na maisau attāti; aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| yā kāci vaidanā atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍nā ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā auḷārikā vā sukhumā vā hīnā vā paṇītā vā yā dūrai santikai vā, sabbā vaidanā naitaṃ mama naisauhamasmi na maisau attāti; aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| yā kāci sañ‍ñā…pai॰… yai kaici saṅkhārā atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍nā ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā auḷārikā vā sukhumā vā hīnā vā paṇītā vā yai dūrai santikai vā, sabbai saṅkhārā naitaṃ mama naisauhamasmi na  maisau attāti; aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| yaṃ kiñ‍ci viñ‍ñāṇaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā auḷārikaṃ vā sukhumaṃ vā hīnaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā yaṃ dūrai santikai vā, sabbaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ naitaṃ mama naisauhamasmi na maisau attāti; aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ|

‘‘aivaṃ passaṃ, susima, sutavā ariyasāvakau rūpasmimpi nibbindati, vaidanāyapi nibbindati, sañ‍ñāyapi nibbindati, saṅkhāraisupi nibbindati, viñ‍ñāṇasmimpi nibbindati| nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati, virāgā vimuc‍cati, vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāti|

‘‘‘jātipac‍cayā  jarāmaraṇa’nti, susima, passasī’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘‘bhavapac‍cayā jātī’ti, susima, passasī’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘‘upādānapac‍cayā bhavau’ti, susima, passasī’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘‘taṇhāpac‍cayā  upādāna’nti, susima, passasī’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhāti… phassapac‍cayā vaidanāti… saḷāyatanapac‍cayā phassauti… nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatananti… viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpanti… saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇanti… avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārāti, susima, passasī’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘‘jātiniraudhā  jarāmaraṇaniraudhau’ti, susima, passasī’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘‘bhavaniraudhā jātiniraudhau’ti susima, passasī’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘upādānaniraudhā bhavaniraudhauti… taṇhāniraudhā upādānaniraudhauti… vaidanāniraudhā taṇhāniraudhauti… phassaniraudhā vaidanāniraudhauti… saḷāyatananiraudhā phassaniraudhauti… nāmarūpaniraudhā saḷāyatananiraudhauti… viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhā nāmarūpaniraudhauti… saṅkhāraniraudhā viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhauti… avij‍jāniraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhauti, susima, passasī’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘api pana tvaṃ, susima, aivaṃ jānantau aivaṃ passantau anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhausi – aikaupi hutvā bahudhā hausi, bahudhāpi hutvā aikau hausi; āvibhāvaṃ, tiraubhāvaṃ, tiraukuṭṭaṃ tiraupākāraṃ tiraupabbataṃ asaj‍jamānau gacchasi, saiyyathāpi ākāsai; pathaviyāpi ummuj‍janimuj‍jaṃ karausi, saiyyathāpi udakai; udakaipi abhij‍jamānau gacchasi, saiyyathāpi pathaviyaṃ; ākāsaipi pal‍laṅkaina kamasi, saiyyathāpi pakkhī sakuṇau; imaipi candimasūriyai aivaṃmahiddhikai aivaṃmahānubhāvai pāṇinā parimasasi parimaj‍jasi, yāva brahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattaisī’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘api pana tvaṃ, susima, aivaṃ jānantau aivaṃ passantau dibbāya sautadhātuyā visuddhāya atik‍kantamānusikāya ubhau saddai suṇasi dibbai ca mānusai ca yai dūrai santikai cā’’ti? ‘‘nau  haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘api  pana tvaṃ, susima, aivaṃ jānantau aivaṃ passantau parasattānaṃ parapuggalānaṃ caitasā caitau  paric‍ca pajānāsi – sarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ sarāgaṃ cittanti pajānāsi…pai॰… vimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ vimuttaṃ cittanti pajānāsī’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘api pana tvaṃ, susima, aivaṃ jānantau aivaṃ passantau anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarasi, saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ…pai॰… iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarasī’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘api pana tvaṃ, susima, aivaṃ jānantau aivaṃ passantau dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passasi cavamānai…pai॰… yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāsī’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘api  pana tvaṃ, susima, aivaṃ jānantau aivaṃ passantau yai tai santā vimaukkhā atik‍kamma rūpai, āruppā tai kāyaina phusitvā viharasī’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘aittha dāni, susima, idañ‍ca vaiyyākaraṇaṃ imaisañ‍ca dhammānaṃ asamāpatti, idaṃ nau, susima, katha’’nti?

atha khau āyasmā susimau bhagavatau pādaisu sirasā nipatitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ac‍cayau maṃ, bhantai, ac‍cagamā yathābālaṃ yathāmūḷhaṃ yathāakusalaṃ, yvāhaṃ aivaṃ svākkhātai dhammavinayai dhammatthainakau pabbajitau| tassa mai, bhantai, bhagavā ac‍cayaṃ ac‍cayatau paṭiggaṇhātu āyatiṃ saṃvarāyā’’ti|

‘‘taggha  tvaṃ, susima, ac‍cayau ac‍cagamā yathābālaṃ yathāmūḷhaṃ yathāakusalaṃ, yau tvaṃ aivaṃ svākkhātai dhammavinayai dhammatthainakau pabbajitau| saiyyathāpi, susima, cauraṃ āgucāriṃ gahaitvā rañ‍ñau dassaiyyuṃ – ‘ayaṃ tai, daiva, caurau āgucārī, imassa yaṃ icchasi taṃ daṇḍaṃ paṇaihī’ti| tamainaṃ rājā aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘gacchatha, bhau, imaṃ purisaṃ daḷhāya raj‍juyā pacchābāhaṃ gāḷhabandhanaṃ bandhitvā khuramuṇḍaṃ karitvā kharassaraina paṇavaina rathiyāya rathiyaṃ siṅghāṭakaina siṅghāṭakaṃ parinaitvā dakkhiṇaina dvāraina nikkhāmaitvā dakkhiṇatau nagarassa sīsaṃ chindathā’ti| tamainaṃ rañ‍ñau purisā daḷhāya raj‍juyā pacchābāhaṃ gāḷhabandhanaṃ bandhitvā khuramuṇḍaṃ karitvā kharassaraina paṇavaina rathiyāya rathiyaṃ siṅghāṭakaina siṅghāṭakaṃ parinaitvā dakkhiṇaina dvāraina nikkhāmaitvā dakkhiṇatau nagarassa sīsaṃ chindaiyyuṃ| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, susima, api nu sau purisau tataunidānaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidiyaithā’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ khau sau, susima, purisau tataunidānaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidiyaitha paṭisaṃvaidiyaitha vā, na vā paṭisaṃvaidiyaitha (ka॰)| yā aivaṃ svākkhātai dhammavinayai dhammatthainakassa pabbaj‍jā, ayaṃ tatau dukkhavipākatarā ca kaṭukavipākatarā ca, api ca vinipātāya saṃvattati| yatau ca khau tvaṃ, susima, ac‍cayaṃ ac‍cayatau disvā yathādhammaṃ paṭikarausi taṃ tai mayaṃ paṭiggaṇhāma| vuddhi haisā, susima, ariyassa vinayai  yau ac‍cayaṃ ac‍cayatau disvā yathādhammaṃ  paṭikarauti, āyatiñ‍ca āyatiṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰) saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jatī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

mahāvaggau sattamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

dvai assutavatā vuttā, puttamaṃsaina cāparaṃ|

atthirāgau ca nagaraṃ, sammasaṃ naḷakalāpiyaṃ|

kausambī upayanti ca, dasamau susimaina cāti dasamau vuttau susīmaināti (sī॰)||

8. samaṇabrāhmaṇavaggau

1. jarāmaraṇasuttaṃ

71. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā…pai॰… ‘‘yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā jarāmaraṇaṃ nappajānanti, jarāmaraṇasamudayaṃ nappajānanti, jarāmaraṇaniraudhaṃ nappajānanti, jarāmaraṇaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ nappajānanti, na mai tai, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā samaṇaisu vā samaṇasammatā brāhmaṇaisu vā brāhmaṇasammatā, na ca pana tai āyasmantau sāmañ‍ñatthaṃ vā brahmañ‍ñatthaṃ vā diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharanti|

‘‘yai ca khau kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā jarāmaraṇaṃ pajānanti…pai॰… paṭipadaṃ pajānanti, tai khau mai, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā samaṇaisu caiva samaṇasammatā brāhmaṇaisu ca brāhmaṇasammatā, tai ca panāyasmantau sāmañ‍ñatthañ‍ca brahmañ‍ñatthañ‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharantī’’ti| (suttantau aikau)| paṭhamaṃ|

2-11. jātisuttādidasakaṃ

72. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati…pai॰… jātiṃ nappajānanti…pai॰…|

(3) bhavaṃ nappajānanti…pai॰…|

(4) upādānaṃ nappajānanti…pai॰…|

(5) taṇhaṃ nappajānanti…pai॰…|

(6) vaidanaṃ nappajānanti…pai॰…|

(7) phassaṃ  nappajānanti…pai॰…|

(8) saḷāyatanaṃ nappajānanti…pai॰…|

(9) nāmarūpaṃ  nappajānanti…pai॰…|

(10) viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nappajānanti…pai॰…|

(11) ‘‘saṅkhārai  nappajānanti, saṅkhārasamudayaṃ nappajānanti, saṅkhāraniraudhaṃ nappajānanti, saṅkhāraniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ nappajānanti…pai॰… saṅkhārai pajānanti…pai॰… sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharantī’’ti| aikādasamaṃ|

samaṇabrāhmaṇavaggau aṭṭhamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

pac‍cayaikādasa vuttā, catusac‍cavibhaj‍janā|

samaṇabrāhmaṇavaggau, nidānai bhavati aṭṭhamau||

vagguddānaṃ –

buddhau āhārau dasabalau, kaḷārau gahapatipañ‍camau|

dukkhavaggau mahāvaggau, aṭṭhamau samaṇabrāhmaṇauti||

9. antarapaiyyālaṃ

1. satthusuttaṃ

73. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati…pai॰… ‘‘jarāmaraṇaṃ, bhikkhavai, ajānatā apassatā yathābhūtaṃ jarāmaraṇai yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇāya satthā pariyaisitabbau; jarāmaraṇasamudayaṃ ajānatā apassatā yathābhūtaṃ jarāmaraṇasamudayai yathābhūtaṃ  ñāṇāya satthā pariyaisitabbau; jarāmaraṇaniraudhaṃ ajānatā apassatā yathābhūtaṃ jarāmaraṇaniraudhai yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇāya satthā pariyaisitabbau; jarāmaraṇaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ ajānatā apassatā yathābhūtaṃ jarāmaraṇaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇāya satthā pariyaisitabbau’’ti| (suttantau aikau)| paṭhamaṃ|

(sabbaisaṃ paiyyālau aivaṃ vitthāraitabbau)

2-11. dutiyasatthusuttādidasakaṃ

(2) jātiṃ, bhikkhavai, ajānatā apassatā yathābhūtaṃ…pai॰…|

(3) bhavaṃ, bhikkhavai, ajānatā apassatā yathābhūtaṃ…pai॰…|

(4) upādānaṃ, bhikkhavai, ajānatā apassatā yathābhūtaṃ…pai॰…|

(5) taṇhaṃ, bhikkhavai, ajānatā apassatā yathābhūtaṃ…pai॰…|

(6) vaidanaṃ, bhikkhavai, ajānatā apassatā yathābhūtaṃ…pai॰…|

(7) phassaṃ, bhikkhavai, ajānatā apassatā yathābhūtaṃ…pai॰…|

(8) saḷāyatanaṃ, bhikkhavai, ajānatā apassatā yathābhūtaṃ…pai॰…|

(9) nāmarūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, ajānatā apassatā yathābhūtaṃ…pai॰…|

(10) viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, bhikkhavai, ajānatā apassatā yathābhūtaṃ…pai॰…|

(11) ‘‘saṅkhārai, bhikkhavai, ajānatā apassatā yathābhūtaṃ saṅkhāraisu yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇāya satthā pariyaisitabbau; saṅkhārasamudayaṃ ajānatā apassatā yathābhūtaṃ saṅkhārasamudayai yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇāya satthā pariyaisitabbau; saṅkhāraniraudhaṃ ajānatā apassatā yathābhūtaṃ  saṅkhāraniraudhai yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇāya satthā pariyaisitabbau; saṅkhāraniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ ajānatā apassatā yathābhūtaṃ saṅkhāraniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇāya satthā pariyaisitabbau’’ti| aikādasamaṃ|

(sabbaisaṃ catusac‍cikaṃ kātabbaṃ)|

2-12. sikkhāsuttādipaiyyālaaikādasakaṃ

(2) ‘‘jarāmaraṇaṃ, bhikkhavai, ajānatā apassatā yathābhūtaṃ jarāmaraṇai yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇāya sikkhā karaṇīyā|

(paiyyālau| catusac‍cikaṃ kātabbaṃ)|

(3) jarāmaraṇaṃ, bhikkhavai, ajānatā…pai॰… yaugau karaṇīyau…pai॰…|

(4) jarāmaraṇaṃ, bhikkhavai, ajānatā…pai॰… chandau  karaṇīyau…pai॰…|

(5) jarāmaraṇaṃ, bhikkhavai, ajānatā…pai॰… ussauḷhī karaṇīyā…pai॰…|

(6) jarāmaraṇaṃ, bhikkhavai, ajānatā…pai॰… appaṭivānī karaṇīyā…pai॰…|

(7) jarāmaraṇaṃ, bhikkhavai, ajānatā…pai॰… ātappaṃ karaṇīyaṃ…pai॰…|

(8) jarāmaraṇaṃ, bhikkhavai, ajānatā…pai॰… vīriyaṃ karaṇīyaṃ…pai॰…|

(9) jarāmaraṇaṃ, bhikkhavai, ajānatā…pai॰… sātac‍caṃ karaṇīyaṃ…pai॰…|

(10) jarāmaraṇaṃ, bhikkhavai, ajānatā…pai॰… sati karaṇīyā…pai॰…|

(11) jarāmaraṇaṃ, bhikkhavai, ajānatā…pai॰… sampajañ‍ñaṃ karaṇīyaṃ…pai॰…|

(12) jarāmaraṇaṃ, bhikkhavai, ajānatā…pai॰… appamādau karaṇīyau…pai॰…|

antarapaiyyālau navamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

satthā sikkhā ca yaugau ca, chandau ussauḷhipañ‍camī|

appaṭivāni ātappaṃ, vīriyaṃ  sātac‍camuc‍cati|

sati ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca, appamādaina dvādasāti||

suttantā antarapaiyyālā niṭṭhitā|

parai  tai dvādasa haunti, suttā dvattiṃsa satāni|

catusac‍caina tai vuttā, paiyyālaantaramhi yaiti paiyyālā antaramhi yaiti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰)||

antarapaiyyālaisu uddānaṃ samattaṃ|

nidānasaṃyuttaṃ samattaṃ|



2. abhisamayasaṃyuttaṃ

1. nakhasikhāsuttaṃ

74. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau bhagavā parittaṃ nakhasikhāyaṃ paṃsuṃ āraupaitvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, katamaṃ nu khau bahutaraṃ, yau vāyaṃ yau cāyaṃ (sabbattha) dutiyasuttādīsu pana vāsaddauyaiva dissati mayā parittau nakhasikhāyaṃ paṃsu āraupitau, ayaṃ vā mahāpathavī’’ti?

‘‘aitadaiva, bhantai, bahutaraṃ, yadidaṃ mahāpathavī| appamattakau bhagavatā parittau nakhasikhāyaṃ paṃsu āraupitau| naiva satimaṃ kalaṃ upaiti na sahassimaṃ kalaṃ upaiti na satasahassimaṃ kalaṃ upaiti mahāpathaviṃ upanidhāya bhagavatā parittau nakhasikhāyaṃ paṃsu āraupitau’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakassa diṭṭhisampan‍nassa puggalassa abhisamaitāvinau aitadaiva bahutaraṃ dukkhaṃ yadidaṃ parikkhīṇaṃ pariyādiṇṇaṃ; appamattakaṃ avasiṭṭhaṃ| naiva satimaṃ kalaṃ upaiti na sahassimaṃ  kalaṃ upaiti na satasahassimaṃ kalaṃ upaiti purimaṃ dukkhakkhandhaṃ parikkhīṇaṃ pariyādiṇṇaṃ upanidhāya yadidaṃ sattakkhattuṃparamatā| aivaṃ mahatthiyau khau, bhikkhavai, dhammābhisamayau; aivaṃ mahatthiyau dhammacakkhupaṭilābhau’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. paukkharaṇīsuttaṃ

75. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, paukkharaṇī pañ‍ñāsayaujanāni āyāmaina pañ‍ñāsayaujanāni vitthāraina pañ‍ñāsayaujanāni  ubbaidhaina, puṇṇā udakassa samatittikā kākapaiyyā| tatau purisau kusaggaina udakaṃ uddharaiyya| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, katamaṃ nu khau bahutaraṃ, yaṃ vā kusaggaina udakaṃ ubbhataṃ yaṃ vā paukkharaṇiyā udaka’’nti?

‘‘aitadaiva, bhantai, bahutaraṃ, yadidaṃ paukkharaṇiyā udakaṃ| appamattakaṃ kusaggaina udakaṃ ubbhataṃ| naiva satimaṃ kalaṃ upaiti  na sahassimaṃ kalaṃ upaiti na satasahassimaṃ kalaṃ upaiti paukkharaṇiyā udakaṃ upanidhāya kusaggaina udakaṃ ubbhata’’nti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakassa diṭṭhisampan‍nassa puggalassa abhisamaitāvinau aitadaiva bahutaraṃ dukkhaṃ yadidaṃ parikkhīṇaṃ pariyādiṇṇaṃ; appamattakaṃ avasiṭṭhaṃ| naiva satimaṃ kalaṃ upaiti na sahassimaṃ kalaṃ upaiti na satasahassimaṃ kalaṃ upaiti purimaṃ dukkhakkhandhaṃ parikkhīṇaṃ pariyādiṇṇaṃ upanidhāya, yadidaṃ sattakkhattuṃparamatā| aivaṃ mahatthiyau khau, bhikkhavai, dhammābhisamayau; aivaṃ mahatthiyau dhammacakkhupaṭilābhau’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. sambhaij‍jaudakasuttaṃ

76. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yatthimā mahānadiyau saṃsandanti samainti, saiyyathidaṃ – gaṅgā yamunā aciravatī sarabhū mahī, tatau purisau dvai vā tīṇi vā udakaphusitāni uddharaiyya| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, katamaṃ nu khau bahutaraṃ, yāni vā dvai vā tīṇi vā udakaphusitāni ubbhatāni yaṃ vā sambhaij‍jaudaka’’nti?

‘‘aitadaiva, bhantai, bahutaraṃ yadidaṃ sambhaij‍jaudakaṃ; appamattakāni  dvai vā tīṇi vā udakaphusitāni ubbhatāni| naiva satimaṃ kalaṃ upainti na sahassimaṃ kalaṃ upainti na satasahassimaṃ kalaṃ upainti sambhaij‍jaudakaṃ upanidhāya dvai vā tīṇi vā udakaphusitāni ubbhatānī’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai…pai॰… dhammacakkhupaṭilābhau’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. dutiyasambhaij‍jaudakasuttaṃ

77. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yatthimā mahānadiyau saṃsandanti samainti, saiyyathidaṃ – gaṅgā yamunā aciravatī sarabhū mahī, taṃ udakaṃ parikkhayaṃ pariyādānaṃ gacchaiyya  ṭhapaitvā dvai vā tīṇi vā udakaphusitāni| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, katamaṃ nu khau bahutaraṃ, yaṃ vā sambhaij‍jaudakaṃ parikkhīṇaṃ pariyādiṇṇaṃ yāni vā dvai vā tīṇi vā udakaphusitāni avasiṭṭhānī’’ti?

‘‘aitadaiva, bhantai, bahutaraṃ sambhaij‍jaudakaṃ yadidaṃ parikkhīṇaṃ pariyādiṇṇaṃ; appamattakāni dvai vā tīṇi vā udakaphusitāni avasiṭṭhāni| naiva satimaṃ kalaṃ  upainti na sahassimaṃ kalaṃ upainti na satasahassimaṃ kalaṃ upainti sambhaij‍jaudakaṃ parikkhīṇaṃ pariyādiṇṇaṃ upanidhāya dvai vā tīṇi vā udakaphusitāni avasiṭṭhānī’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai…pai॰… dhammacakkhupaṭilābhau’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. pathavīsuttaṃ

78. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau  mahāpathaviyā satta kaulaṭṭhimattiyau guḷikā upanikkhipaiyya| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, katamaṃ nu khau bahutaraṃ, yā vā satta kaulaṭṭhimattiyau guḷikā upanikkhittā ayaṃ yā (syā॰ ka॰) vā mahāpathavī’’ti?

‘‘aitadaiva, bhantai, bahutaraṃ, yadidaṃ mahāpathavī; appamattikā satta kaulaṭṭhimattiyau guḷikā upanikkhittā| naiva satimaṃ kalaṃ upainti na sahassimaṃ kalaṃ upainti na satasahassimaṃ kalaṃ upainti mahāpathaviṃ upanidhāya satta kaulaṭṭhimattiyau guḷikā upanikkhittā’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai…pai॰… dhammacakkhupaṭilābhau’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dutiyapathavīsuttaṃ

79. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, mahāpathavī parikkhayaṃ pariyādānaṃ gacchaiyya, ṭhapaitvā satta kaulaṭṭhimattiyau guḷikā| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, katamaṃ nu khau bahutaraṃ, yaṃ vā mahāpathaviyā parikkhīṇaṃ pariyādiṇṇaṃ yā vā satta kaulaṭṭhimattiyau guḷikā avasiṭṭhā’’ti?

‘‘aitadaiva  bhantai, bahutaraṃ, mahāpathaviyā, yadidaṃ parikkhīṇaṃ pariyādiṇṇaṃ; appamattikā satta kaulaṭṭhimattiyau guḷikā avasiṭṭhā| naiva satimaṃ kalaṃ upainti na sahassimaṃ kalaṃ upainti na satasahassimaṃ kalaṃ upainti mahāpathaviyā parikkhīṇaṃ pariyādiṇṇaṃ upanidhāya satta kaulaṭṭhimattiyau guḷikā avasiṭṭhā’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai…pai॰… dhammacakkhupaṭilābhau’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. samuddasuttaṃ

80. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati…pai॰… ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau mahāsamuddatau dvai vā tīṇi vā udakaphusitāni uddharaiyya| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, katamaṃ nu khau bahutaraṃ, yāni vā dvai vā tīṇi vā udakaphusitāni ubbhatāni yaṃ vā mahāsamuddai udaka’’nti?

‘‘aitadaiva, bhantai, bahutaraṃ, yadidaṃ mahāsamuddai udakaṃ; appamattakāni dvai vā tīṇi vā udakaphusitāni ubbhatāni| naiva satimaṃ kalaṃ upainti na sahassimaṃ kalaṃ upainti na satasahassimaṃ kalaṃ upainti mahāsamuddai udakaṃ upanidhāya dvai vā tīṇi vā udakaphusitāni ubbhatānī’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai…pai॰… dhammacakkhupaṭilābhau’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. dutiyasamuddasuttaṃ

81. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, mahāsamuddau parikkhayaṃ pariyādānaṃ gacchaiyya, ṭhapaitvā dvai vā tīṇi vā udakaphusitāni| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, katamaṃ nu khau bahutaraṃ, yaṃ vā mahāsamuddai udakaṃ parikkhīṇaṃ pariyādin‍naṃ yāni vā dvai vā tīṇi vā udakaphusitāni avasiṭṭhānī’’ti?

‘‘aitadaiva, bhantai, bahutaraṃ mahāsamuddai udakaṃ, yadidaṃ parikkhīṇaṃ pariyādiṇṇaṃ; appamattakāni dvai vā tīṇi vā udakaphusitāni avasiṭṭhāni| naiva satimaṃ kalaṃ upainti na sahassimaṃ kalaṃ upainti na satasahassimaṃ kalaṃ upainti mahāsamuddai udakaṃ parikkhīṇaṃ pariyādiṇṇaṃ  upanidhāya dvai vā tīṇi vā udakaphusitāni avasiṭṭhānī’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva  khau bhikkhavai…pai॰… dhammacakkhupaṭilābhau’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. pabbatasuttaṃ

82. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau himavatau pabbatarājassa satta sāsapamattiyau pāsāṇasakkharā upanikkhipaiyya| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, katamaṃ nu khau bahutaraṃ, yā vā satta sāsapamattiyau pāsāṇasakkharā upanikkhittā yau vā himavā upanikkhittā, himavā vā (sī॰) pabbatarājā’’ti?

‘‘aitadaiva, bhantai, bahutaraṃ yadidaṃ himavā pabbatarājā; appamattikā satta sāsapamattiyau pāsāṇasakkharā  upanikkhittā| naiva satimaṃ kalaṃ upainti na sahassimaṃ kalaṃ upainti  na satasahassimaṃ kalaṃ upainti himavantaṃ pabbatarājānaṃ upanidhāya satta sāsapamattiyau pāsāṇasakkharā upanikkhittā’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau…pai॰… dhammacakkhupaṭilābhau’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. dutiyapabbatasuttaṃ

83. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, himavā pabbatarājā parikkhayaṃ pariyādānaṃ gacchaiyya, ṭhapaitvā satta sāsapamattiyau pāsāṇasakkharā| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, katamaṃ nu khau bahutaraṃ, yaṃ vā himavatau pabbatarājassa parikkhīṇaṃ pariyādiṇṇaṃ yā vā satta sāsapamattiyau pāsāṇasakkharā avasiṭṭhā’’ti?

‘‘aitadaiva, bhantai, bahutaraṃ himavatau pabbatarājassa yadidaṃ parikkhīṇaṃ pariyādiṇṇaṃ; appamattikā satta sāsapamattiyau pāsāṇasakkharā avasiṭṭhā| naiva satimaṃ  kalaṃ upainti na sahassimaṃ kalaṃ upainti na satasahassimaṃ kalaṃ upainti himavatau pabbatarājassa parikkhīṇaṃ pariyādiṇṇaṃ upanidhāya satta sāsapamattiyau pāsāṇasakkharā avasiṭṭhā’’ti|

‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakassa diṭṭhisampan‍nassa puggalassa abhisamaitāvinau aitadaiva  bahutaraṃ dukkhaṃ yadidaṃ parikkhīṇaṃ pariyādiṇṇaṃ; appamattakaṃ avasiṭṭhaṃ| naiva satimaṃ kalaṃ upaiti na sahassimaṃ kalaṃ upaiti na satasahassimaṃ kalaṃ upaiti purimaṃ dukkhakkhandhaṃ parikkhīṇaṃ pariyādiṇṇaṃ upanidhāya yadidaṃ sattakkhattuṃparamatā| aivaṃ mahatthiyau khau, bhikkhavai, dhammābhisamayau, aivaṃ mahatthiyau dhammacakkhupaṭilābhau’’ti| dasamaṃ|

11. tatiyapabbatasuttaṃ

84. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau sinairussa pabbatarājassa satta muggamattiyau pāsāṇasakkharā upanikkhipaiyya| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, katamaṃ nu khau bahutaraṃ, yā vā satta muggamattiyau pāsāṇasakkharā upanikkhittā yau vā sinairu upanikkhittā, sinairu vā (sī॰) pabbatarājā’’ti?

‘‘aitadaiva, bhantai, bahutaraṃ yadidaṃ sinairu pabbatarājā; appamattikā satta muggamattiyau pāsāṇasakkharā upanikkhittā| naiva satimaṃ kalaṃ upainti na sahassimaṃ  kalaṃ upainti na satasahassimaṃ kalaṃ upainti sinairuṃ pabbatarājānaṃ upanidhāya satta muggamattiyau pāsāṇasakkharā upanikkhittā’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakassa diṭṭhisampan‍nassa puggalassa  adhigamaṃ upanidhāya añ‍ñatitthiyasamaṇabrāhmaṇaparibbājakānaṃ adhigamau naiva satimaṃ kalaṃ upaiti na sahassimaṃ kalaṃ upaiti na satasahassimaṃ kalaṃ upaiti| aivaṃ mahādhigamau, bhikkhavai, diṭṭhisampan‍nau puggalau, aivaṃ mahābhiñ‍ñau’’ti| aikādasamaṃ|

abhisamayasaṃyuttaṃ samattaṃ|


tassuddānaṃ –

nakhasikhā paukkharaṇī, sambhaij‍jaudakai ca dvai|

dvai pathavī dvai samuddā, tayau ca pabbatūpamāti||


3. dhātusaṃyuttaṃ

1. nānattavaggau

1. dhātunānattasuttaṃ

85. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati…pai॰… ‘‘dhātunānattaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi| taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dhātunānattaṃ? cakkhudhātu rūpadhātu cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, sautadhātu saddadhātu sautaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, ghānadhātu gandhadhātu ghānaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, jivhādhātu rasadhātu jivhāviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, kāyadhātu phauṭṭhabbadhātu kāyaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, manaudhātu dhammadhātu manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dhātunānatta’’nti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. phassanānattasuttaṃ

86. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘dhātunānattaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati phassanānattaṃ| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dhātunānattaṃ? cakkhudhātu sautadhātu ghānadhātu jivhādhātu kāyadhātu manaudhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dhātunānattaṃ’’|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dhātunānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati phassanānattaṃ? cakkhudhātuṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati cakkhusamphassau| sautadhātuṃ paṭic‍ca… ghānadhātuṃ paṭic‍ca … jivhādhātuṃ  paṭic‍ca… kāyadhātuṃ paṭic‍ca… manaudhātuṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati manausamphassau| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, dhātunānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati phassanānatta’’nti| dutiyaṃ|

3. nauphassanānattasuttaṃ

87. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘dhātunānattaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati phassanānattaṃ, nau phassanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dhātunānattaṃ| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dhātunānattaṃ? cakkhudhātu…pai॰… manaudhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dhātunānattaṃ’’|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dhātunānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati phassanānattaṃ, nau phassanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dhātunānattaṃ? cakkhudhātuṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati cakkhusamphassau, nau cakkhusamphassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati cakkhudhātu…pai॰… manaudhātuṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati manausamphassau, nau manausamphassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati manaudhātu| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, dhātunānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati phassanānattaṃ, nau phassanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dhātunānatta’’nti| tatiyaṃ|

4. vaidanānānattasuttaṃ

88. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘dhātunānattaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati phassanānattaṃ, phassanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati vaidanānānattaṃ| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dhātunānattaṃ? cakkhudhātu …pai॰… manaudhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dhātunānattaṃ’’|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dhātunānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati phassanānattaṃ, phassanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati vaidanānānattaṃ? cakkhudhātuṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati cakkhusamphassau, cakkhusamphassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati cakkhusamphassajā vaidanā…pai॰… manaudhātuṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati  manausamphassau, manausamphassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati manausamphassajā vaidanā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, dhātunānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati phassanānattaṃ, phassanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati vaidanānānatta’’nti| catutthaṃ|

5. dutiyavaidanānānattasuttaṃ

89. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati…pai॰… ‘‘dhātunānattaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati phassanānattaṃ, phassanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati vaidanānānattaṃ, nau vaidanānānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati phassanānattaṃ, nau phassanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dhātunānattaṃ| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dhātunānattaṃ? cakkhudhātu…pai॰… manaudhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dhātunānattaṃ’’|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dhātunānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati phassanānattaṃ, phassanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati vaidanānānattaṃ, nau vaidanānānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati phassanānattaṃ, nau phassanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dhātunānattaṃ? cakkhudhātuṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati cakkhusamphassau, cakkhusamphassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati cakkhusamphassajā vaidanā, nau cakkhusamphassajaṃ vaidanaṃ paṭic‍ca  uppaj‍jati cakkhusamphassau, nau cakkhusamphassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati cakkhudhātu…pai॰… manaudhātuṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati manausamphassau, manausamphassaṃ  paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati manausamphassajā vaidanā, nau manausamphassajaṃ vaidanaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati manausamphassau, nau manausamphassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati manaudhātu| aivaṃ  khau, bhikkhavai, dhātunānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati phassanānattaṃ, phassanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati vaidanānānattaṃ, nau vaidanānānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati phassanānattaṃ, nau phassanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dhātunānatta’’nti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. bāhiradhātunānattasuttaṃ

90. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘dhātunānattaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi| taṃ suṇātha…pai॰… katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dhātunānattaṃ? rūpadhātu saddadhātu gandhadhātu rasadhātu phauṭṭhabbadhātu dhammadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dhātunānatta’’nti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. sañ‍ñānānattasuttaṃ

91. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca  – ‘‘dhātunānattaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sañ‍ñānānattaṃ, sañ‍ñānānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati saṅkappanānattaṃ, saṅkappanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati chandanānattaṃ, chandanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati pariḷāhanānattaṃ, pariḷāhanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati pariyaisanānānattaṃ| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dhātunānattaṃ? rūpadhātu…pai॰… dhammadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dhātunānattaṃ’’|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dhātunānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sañ‍ñānānattaṃ, sañ‍ñānānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati saṅkappanānattaṃ, saṅkappanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati chandanānattaṃ, chandanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati pariḷāhanānattaṃ, pariḷāhanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati pariyaisanānānattaṃ?

‘‘rūpadhātuṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati rūpasañ‍ñā, rūpasañ‍ñaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati rūpasaṅkappau, rūpasaṅkappaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati rūpacchandau, rūpacchandaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati rūpapariḷāhau, rūpapariḷāhaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati rūpapariyaisanā…pai॰… dhammadhātuṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dhammasañ‍ñā, dhammasañ‍ñaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dhammasaṅkappau, dhammasaṅkappaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dhammacchandau, dhammacchandaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dhammapariḷāhau, dhammapariḷāhaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dhammapariyaisanā|

‘‘aivaṃ, khau, bhikkhavai, dhātunānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sañ‍ñānānattaṃ, sañ‍ñānānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati saṅkappanānattaṃ, saṅkappanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati chandanānattaṃ, chandanānattaṃ  paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati pariḷāhanānattaṃ, pariḷāhanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati pariyaisanānānatta’’nti| sattamaṃ|

8. naupariyaisanānānattasuttaṃ

92. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘dhātunānattaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sañ‍ñānānattaṃ, sañ‍ñānānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati saṅkappanānattaṃ, saṅkappanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati chandanānattaṃ, chandanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati pariḷāhanānattaṃ, pariḷāhanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati pariyaisanānānattaṃ; nau pariyaisanānānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati pariḷāhanānattaṃ, nau pariḷāhanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati chandanānattaṃ, nau chandanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati saṅkappanānattaṃ, nau saṅkappanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sañ‍ñānānattaṃ, nau sañ‍ñānānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dhātunānattaṃ| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dhātunānattaṃ? rūpadhātu…pai॰… dhammadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dhātunānattaṃ’’|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dhātunānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sañ‍ñānānattaṃ, sañ‍ñānānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati…pai॰… pariyaisanānānattaṃ; nau pariyaisanānānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati pariḷāhanānattaṃ, nau pariḷāhanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati chandanānattaṃ, nau chandanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati saṅkappanānattaṃ, nau saṅkappanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sañ‍ñānānattaṃ, nau sañ‍ñānānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dhātunānattaṃ?

‘‘rūpadhātuṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati rūpasañ‍ñā…pai॰… dhammadhātuṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dhammasañ‍ñā, dhammasañ‍ñaṃ  paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati…pai॰… dhammapariyaisanā; nau dhammapariyaisanaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dhammapariḷāhau, nau dhammapariḷāhaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dhammacchandau, nau dhammacchandaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dhammasaṅkappau, nau dhammasaṅkappaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dhammasañ‍ñā, nau dhammasañ‍ñaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dhammadhātu|

‘‘aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, dhātunānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sañ‍ñānānattaṃ, sañ‍ñānānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati…pai॰… pariyaisanānānattaṃ; nau pariyaisanānānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati pariḷāhanānattaṃ, nau pariḷāhanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati chandanānattaṃ, nau chandanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati saṅkappanānattaṃ, nau saṅkappanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sañ‍ñānānattaṃ, nau sañ‍ñānānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dhātunānatta’’nti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. bāhiraphassanānattasuttaṃ

93. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati…pai॰… ‘‘dhātunānattaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sañ‍ñānānattaṃ, sañ‍ñānānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati saṅkappanānattaṃ, saṅkappanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati phassanānattaṃ, phassanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati vaidanānānattaṃ, vaidanānānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati chandanānattaṃ, chandanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati pariḷāhanānattaṃ, pariḷāhanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati pariyaisanānānattaṃ, pariyaisanānānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati lābhanānattaṃ| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dhātunānattaṃ? rūpadhātu…pai॰… dhammadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dhātunānattaṃ’’|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dhātunānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati  sañ‍ñānānattaṃ, sañ‍ñānānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati…pai॰… lābhanānattaṃ?

‘‘rūpadhātuṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati rūpasañ‍ñā, rūpasañ‍ñaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati rūpasaṅkappau, rūpasaṅkappaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati rūpasamphassau, rūpasamphassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati rūpasamphassajā vaidanā, rūpasamphassajaṃ vaidanaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati rūpacchandau, rūpacchandaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati rūpapariḷāhau, rūpapariḷāhaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati rūpapariyaisanā, rūpapariyaisanaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati rūpalābhau…pai॰… dhammadhātuṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dhammasañ‍ñā, dhammasañ‍ñaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dhammasaṅkappau, dhammasaṅkappaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dhammasamphassau, dhammasamphassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dhammasamphassajā vaidanā, dhammasamphassajaṃ vaidanaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dhammacchandau, dhammacchandaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dhammapariḷāhau, dhammapariḷāhaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dhammapariyaisanā, dhammapariyaisanaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dhammalābhau|

‘‘aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, dhātunānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sañ‍ñānānattaṃ, sañ‍ñānānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati…pai॰… pariyaisanānānattaṃ, pariyaisanānānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati lābhanānatta’’nti| navamaṃ|

10. dutiyabāhiraphassanānattasuttaṃ

94. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘dhātunānattaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sañ‍ñānānattaṃ, sañ‍ñānānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati saṅkappanānattaṃ, phassa… vaidanā… chanda… pariḷāha… pariyaisanānānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati lābhanānattaṃ; nau lābhanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati pariyaisanānānattaṃ, nau pariyaisanānānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati pariḷāhanānattaṃ, nau pariḷāhanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati…pai॰… chanda… vaidanā… phassa… saṅkappa… sañ‍ñānānattaṃ, nau sañ‍ñānānattaṃ paṭic‍ca  uppaj‍jati dhātunānattaṃ| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dhātunānattaṃ? rūpadhātu…pai॰… dhammadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dhātunānattaṃ’’|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dhātunānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sañ‍ñānānattaṃ, sañ‍ñānānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati saṅkappanānattaṃ? phassa… vaidanā… chanda… pariḷāha… pariyaisanā… lābha… nau lābhanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati pariyaisanānānattaṃ, nau pariyaisanānānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati pariḷāha… chanda… vaidanā… phassa… nau saṅkappanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sañ‍ñānānattaṃ, nau sañ‍ñānānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dhātunānattaṃ?

‘‘rūpadhātuṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati rūpasañ‍ñā…pai॰… dhammadhātuṃ  paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dhammasañ‍ñā, dhammasañ‍ñaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati…pai॰… dhammapariyaisanā, dhammapariyaisanaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dhammalābhau; nau dhammalābhaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dhammapariyaisanā, nau dhammapariyaisanaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dhammapariḷāhau, nau dhammapariḷāhaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dhammacchandau, nau dhammacchandaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dhammasamphassajā vaidanā, nau dhammasamphassajaṃ vaidanaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dhammasamphassau, nau dhammasamphassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dhammasaṅkappau, nau dhammasaṅkappaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dhammasañ‍ñā, nau dhammasañ‍ñaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dhammadhātu|

‘‘aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, dhātunānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sañ‍ñānānattaṃ, sañ‍ñānānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati…pai॰… saṅkappa… phassa… vaidanā… chanda… pariḷāha… pariyaisanā… lābha… nau lābhanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati pariyaisanānānattaṃ, nau pariyaisanānānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati pariḷāhanānattaṃ, nau pariḷāhanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati chandanānattaṃ, nau chandanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati vaidanānānattaṃ, nau vaidanānānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati phassanānattaṃ, nau phassanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati saṅkappanānattaṃ, nau saṅkappanānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sañ‍ñānānattaṃ, nau sañ‍ñānānattaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati dhātunānatta’’nti| dasamaṃ|

nānattavaggau paṭhamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

dhātuphassañ‍ca  nau caitaṃ, vaidanā aparai duvai|

aitaṃ ajjhattapañ‍cakaṃ, dhātusañ‍ñañ‍ca nau caitaṃ|

phassassa aparai duvai, aitaṃ bāhirapañ‍cakanti||

2. dutiyavaggau

1. sattadhātusuttaṃ

95. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati…pai॰… ‘‘sattimā, bhikkhavai, dhātuyau| katamā satta? ābhādhātu, subhadhātu, ākāsānañ‍cāyatanadhātu, viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanadhātu, ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanadhātu, naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanadhātu, sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhadhātu – imā khau, bhikkhavai, satta dhātuyau’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai, añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yā cāyaṃ, bhantai, ābhādhātu yā ca subhadhātu yā ca ākāsānañ‍cāyatanadhātu yā ca viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanadhātu yā ca ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanadhātu yā ca naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanadhātu yā ca sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhadhātu – imā nu khau, bhantai, dhātuyau kiṃ paṭic‍ca pañ‍ñāyantī’’ti?

‘‘yāyaṃ, bhikkhu, ābhādhātu – ayaṃ dhātu andhakāraṃ paṭic‍ca pañ‍ñāyati| yāyaṃ, bhikkhu, subhadhātu – ayaṃ dhātu asubhaṃ paṭic‍ca pañ‍ñāyati| yāyaṃ, bhikkhu, ākāsānañ‍cāyatanadhātu – ayaṃ dhātu rūpaṃ paṭic‍ca pañ‍ñāyati| yāyaṃ, bhikkhu, viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanadhātu – ayaṃ dhātu ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ paṭic‍ca pañ‍ñāyati| yāyaṃ, bhikkhu, ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanadhātu – ayaṃ dhātu viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ paṭic‍ca pañ‍ñāyati| yāyaṃ, bhikkhu, naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanadhātu – ayaṃ dhātu ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ paṭic‍ca pañ‍ñāyati| yāyaṃ, bhikkhu, sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhadhātu – ayaṃ dhātu niraudhaṃ paṭic‍ca pañ‍ñāyatī’’ti|

‘‘yā  cāyaṃ, bhantai, ābhādhātu yā ca subhadhātu yā ca ākāsānañ‍cāyatanadhātu yā ca viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanadhātu yā ca ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanadhātu yā ca naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanadhātu yā ca sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhadhātu – imā nu khau, bhantai, dhātuyau kathaṃ samāpatti pattabbā’’ti?

‘‘yā cāyaṃ, bhikkhu, ābhādhātu yā ca subhadhātu yā ca ākāsānañ‍cāyatanadhātu yā ca viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanadhātu yā ca  ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanadhātu – imā dhātuyau sañ‍ñāsamāpatti pattabbā| yāyaṃ, bhikkhu, naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanadhātu – ayaṃ dhātu saṅkhārāvasaisasamāpatti pattabbā| yāyaṃ, bhikkhu, sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhadhātu – ayaṃ dhātu niraudhasamāpatti pattabbā’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. sanidānasuttaṃ

96. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘sanidānaṃ, bhikkhavai, uppaj‍jati kāmavitak‍kau, nau anidānaṃ; sanidānaṃ uppaj‍jati byāpādavitak‍kau, nau anidānaṃ; sanidānaṃ uppaj‍jati vihiṃsāvitak‍kau, nau anidānaṃ’’|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sanidānaṃ uppaj‍jati kāmavitak‍kau, nau anidānaṃ; sanidānaṃ uppaj‍jati byāpādavitak‍kau, nau anidānaṃ; sanidānaṃ uppaj‍jati vihiṃsāvitak‍kau, nau anidānaṃ? kāmadhātuṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati kāmasañ‍ñā, kāmasañ‍ñaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati kāmasaṅkappau, kāmasaṅkappaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati kāmacchandau, kāmacchandaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati  kāmapariḷāhau, kāmapariḷāhaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati kāmapariyaisanā| kāmapariyaisanaṃ, bhikkhavai, pariyaisamānau assutavā puthuj‍janau tīhi ṭhānaihi micchā paṭipaj‍jati – kāyaina, vācāya, manasā|

‘‘byāpādadhātuṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati byāpādasañ‍ñā, byāpādasañ‍ñaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati byāpādasaṅkappau…pai॰… byāpādacchandau… byāpādapariḷāhau… byāpādapariyaisanā… byāpādapariyaisanaṃ, bhikkhavai, pariyaisamānau assutavā puthuj‍janau tīhi ṭhānaihi micchā paṭipaj‍jati – kāyaina, vācāya, manasā|

‘‘vihiṃsādhātuṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati vihiṃsāsañ‍ñā; vihiṃsāsañ‍ñaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati vihiṃsāsaṅkappau…pai॰… vihiṃsāchandau… vihiṃsāpariḷāhau… vihiṃsāpariyaisanā… vihiṃsāpariyaisanaṃ, bhikkhavai, pariyaisamānau assutavā puthuj‍janau tīhi ṭhānaihi micchā paṭipaj‍jati – kāyaina, vācāya, manasā|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau ādittaṃ tiṇuk‍kaṃ sukkhai tiṇadāyai nikkhipaiyya; nau cai hatthaihi ca pādaihi ca khippamaiva nibbāpaiyya| aivañhi, bhikkhavai, yai tiṇakaṭṭhanissitā pāṇā tai anayabyasanaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyuṃ| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yau hi kauci samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā uppan‍naṃ visamagataṃ sañ‍ñaṃ na khippamaiva pajahati vinaudaiti byantīkarauti anabhāvaṃ  gamaiti, sau diṭṭhai caiva dhammai dukkhaṃ viharati savighātaṃ saupāyāsaṃ sapariḷāhaṃ; kāyassa ca bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā duggati pāṭikaṅkhā|

‘‘sanidānaṃ, bhikkhavai, uppaj‍jati naikkhammavitak‍kau, nau anidānaṃ; sanidānaṃ  uppaj‍jati abyāpādavitak‍kau, nau anidānaṃ; sanidānaṃ uppaj‍jati avihiṃsāvitak‍kau, nau anidānaṃ|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sanidānaṃ uppaj‍jati naikkhammavitak‍kau, nau anidānaṃ; sanidānaṃ uppaj‍jati abyāpādavitak‍kau, nau anidānaṃ; sanidānaṃ uppaj‍jati avihiṃsāvitak‍kau, nau anidānaṃ? naikkhammadhātuṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati naikkhammasañ‍ñā, naikkhammasañ‍ñaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati naikkhammasaṅkappau, naikkhammasaṅkappaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati naikkhammacchandau, naikkhammacchandaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati naikkhammapariḷāhau, naikkhammapariḷāhaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati naikkhammapariyaisanā; naikkhammapariyaisanaṃ, bhikkhavai, pariyaisamānau sutavā ariyasāvakau tīhi ṭhānaihi sammā paṭipaj‍jati – kāyaina, vācāya, manasā|

‘‘abyāpādadhātuṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati abyāpādasañ‍ñā, abyāpādasañ‍ñaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati abyāpādasaṅkappau…pai॰… abyāpādacchandau… abyāpādapariḷāhau… abyāpādapariyaisanā, abyāpādapariyaisanaṃ, bhikkhavai, pariyaisamānau sutavā ariyasāvakau tīhi ṭhānaihi sammā paṭipaj‍jati – kāyaina, vācāya, manasā|

‘‘avihiṃsādhātuṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati avihiṃsāsañ‍ñā, avihiṃsāsañ‍ñaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati avihiṃsāsaṅkappau, avihiṃsāsaṅkappaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati avihiṃsāchandau, avihiṃsāchandaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati avihiṃsāpariḷāhau, avihiṃsāpariḷāhaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati avihiṃsāpariyaisanā; avihiṃsāpariyaisanaṃ, bhikkhavai, pariyaisamānau sutavā ariyasāvakau tīhi  ṭhānaihi sammā paṭipaj‍jati – kāyaina, vācāya, manasā|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau ādittaṃ tiṇuk‍kaṃ sukkhai tiṇadāyai nikkhipaiyya; tamainaṃ hatthaihi ca pādaihi ca khippamaiva nibbāpaiyya| aivañhi, bhikkhavai, yai tiṇakaṭṭhanissitā pāṇā tai na anayabyasanaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyuṃ| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yau hi kauci samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā uppan‍naṃ visamagataṃ sañ‍ñaṃ khippamaiva pajahati vinaudaiti byantīkarauti  anabhāvaṃ gamaiti, sau diṭṭhai caiva dhammai sukhaṃ viharati avighātaṃ anupāyāsaṃ apariḷāhaṃ; kāyassa ca bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugati pāṭikaṅkhā’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. giñ‍jakāvasathasuttaṃ

97. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā ñātikai viharati giñ‍jakāvasathai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘dhātuṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sañ‍ñā, uppaj‍jati diṭṭhi, uppaj‍jati vitak‍kau’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, āyasmā kac‍cānau saddhau kac‍cānau (ka॰) bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yāyaṃ, bhantai, diṭṭhi – ‘asammāsambuddhaisu sammāsambuddhā’ti, ayaṃ nu khau, bhantai, diṭṭhi kiṃ paṭic‍ca pañ‍ñāyatī’’ti?

‘‘mahati khau aisā, kac‍cāna, dhātu yadidaṃ avij‍jādhātu| hīnaṃ, kac‍cāna, dhātuṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati hīnā sañ‍ñā, hīnā diṭṭhi, hīnau vitak‍kau, hīnā caitanā, hīnā patthanā, hīnau paṇidhi, hīnau puggalau, hīnā vācā; hīnaṃ ācikkhati daisaiti pañ‍ñapaiti paṭṭhapaiti  vivarati vibhajati uttānīkarauti; hīnā tassa upapattīti vadāmi|

‘‘majjhimaṃ, kac‍cāna, dhātuṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati majjhimā sañ‍ñā, majjhimā diṭṭhi, majjhimau vitak‍kau, majjhimā caitanā, majjhimā patthanā, majjhimau paṇidhi, majjhimau puggalau, majjhimā vācā; majjhimaṃ ācikkhati daisaiti pañ‍ñapaiti paṭṭhapaiti vivarati vibhajati uttānīkarauti; majjhimā tassa upapattīti vadāmi|
