‘‘paṇītaṃ, kac‍cāna, dhātuṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati paṇītā sañ‍ñā, paṇītā diṭṭhi, paṇītau vitak‍kau, paṇītā caitanā, paṇītā patthanā, paṇītau paṇidhi, paṇītau puggalau, paṇītā vācā; paṇītaṃ ācikkhati daisaiti pañ‍ñapaiti paṭṭhapaiti vivarati vibhajati uttānīkarauti; paṇītā tassa upapattīti vadāmī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. hīnādhimuttikasuttaṃ

98. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati…pai॰… ‘‘dhātusauva dhātusau (sī॰ pī॰) ayañ‍ca paṭhamārambhavākyaiyaiva, na sabbattha| tīsu pana addhāsu ca upamāsaṃsandananigamanaṭṭhānai ca idaṃ pāṭhanānattaṃ natthi, bhikkhavai, sattā saṃsandanti samainti| hīnādhimuttikā hīnādhimuttikaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; kalyāṇādhimuttikā kalyāṇādhimuttikaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti’’|

‘‘atītampi khau khausaddau sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ pautthakaisu natthi, bhikkhavai, addhānaṃ dhātusauva īdisaisu ṭhānaisu pāṭhanānattaṃ natthi sattā saṃsandiṃsu samiṃsu| hīnādhimuttikā hīnādhimuttikaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandiṃsu samiṃsu; kalyāṇādhimuttikā kalyāṇādhimuttikaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandiṃsu samiṃsu|

‘‘anāgatampi khau khausaddau sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ pautthakaisu natthi, bhikkhavai, addhānaṃ dhātusauva īdisaisu ṭhānaisu pāṭhanānattaṃ natthi sattā saṃsandissanti samaissanti| hīnādhimuttikā hīnādhimuttikaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandissanti  samaissanti; kalyāṇādhimuttikā kalyāṇādhimuttikaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandissanti samaissanti|

‘‘aitarahipi  khau khausaddau sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ pautthakaisu natthi, bhikkhavai, pac‍cuppan‍naṃ addhānaṃ dhātusauva īdisaisu ṭhānaisu pāṭhanānattaṃ natthi sattā saṃsandanti samainti| hīnādhimuttikā hīnādhimuttikaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; kalyāṇādhimuttikā kalyāṇādhimuttikaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samaintī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. caṅkamasuttaṃ

99. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati gijjhakūṭai pabbatai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā sāriputtau sambahulaihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ bhagavatau avidūrai caṅkamati; āyasmāpi khau mahāmauggal‍lānau sambahulaihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ bhagavatau avidūrai caṅkamati; āyasmāpi khau mahākassapau sambahulaihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ bhagavatau avidūrai caṅkamati; āyasmāpi khau anuruddhau sambahulaihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ bhagavatau avidūrai caṅkamati; āyasmāpi khau puṇṇau mantāniputtau sambahulaihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ bhagavatau avidūrai caṅkamati; āyasmāpi khau upāli sambahulaihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ bhagavatau avidūrai caṅkamati; āyasmāpi khau ānandau sambahulaihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ bhagavatau avidūrai caṅkamati; daivadattaupi khau sambahulaihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ bhagavatau avidūrai caṅkamati|

atha  khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘passatha nau tumhai, bhikkhavai, sāriputtaṃ sambahulaihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ caṅkamanta’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘sabbai khau aitai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū mahāpañ‍ñā| passatha  nau tumhai, bhikkhavai, mauggal‍lānaṃ sambahulaihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ caṅkamanta’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘sabbai khau aitai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū mahiddhikā| passatha nau tumhai, bhikkhavai, kassapaṃ sambahulaihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ caṅkamanta’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘sabbai khau aitai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū dhutavādā| passatha nau tumhai, bhikkhavai, anuruddhaṃ sambahulaihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ caṅkamanta’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘sabbai khau aitai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū dibbacakkhukā| passatha nau tumhai, bhikkhavai, puṇṇaṃ mantāniputtaṃ sambahulaihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ caṅkamanta’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘sabbai khau aitai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū dhammakathikā| passatha nau tumhai, bhikkhavai, upāliṃ sambahulaihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ caṅkamanta’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘sabbai khau aitai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū vinayadharā| passatha nau tumhai, bhikkhavai, ānandaṃ sambahulaihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ caṅkamanta’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘sabbai khau aitai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū bahussutā| passatha nau tumhai, bhikkhavai, daivadattaṃ sambahulaihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ caṅkamanta’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘sabbai khau aitai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū pāpicchā’’|

‘‘dhātusauva, bhikkhavai, sattā saṃsandanti samainti| hīnādhimuttikā hīnādhimuttikaihi saddhiṃ  saṃsandanti samainti; kalyāṇādhimuttikā kalyāṇādhimuttikaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti| atītampi khau, bhikkhavai, addhānaṃ dhātusauva sattā saṃsandiṃsu samiṃsu| hīnādhimuttikā hīnādhimuttikaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandiṃsu samiṃsu; kalyāṇādhimuttikā kalyāṇādhimuttikaihi  saddhiṃ saṃsandiṃsu samiṃsu|

‘‘anāgatampi khau, bhikkhavai, addhānaṃ dhātusauva sattā saṃsandissanti samaissanti| hīnādhimuttikā hīnādhimuttikaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandissanti samaissanti; kalyāṇādhimuttikā kalyāṇādhimuttikaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandissanti samaissanti|

‘‘aitarahipi  khau, bhikkhavai, pac‍cuppan‍naṃ addhānaṃ dhātusauva sattā saṃsandanti samainti| hīnādhimuttikā hīnādhimuttikaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; kalyāṇādhimuttikā kalyāṇādhimuttikaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samaintī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. sagāthāsuttaṃ

100. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati…pai॰… ‘‘dhātusauva, bhikkhavai, sattā saṃsandanti samainti| hīnādhimuttikā hīnādhimuttikaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti| atītampi khau, bhikkhavai, addhānaṃ dhātusauva sattā saṃsandiṃsu samiṃsu| hīnādhimuttikā hīnādhimuttikaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandiṃsu samiṃsu’’|

‘‘anāgatampi khau, bhikkhavai, addhānaṃ dhātusauva sattā saṃsandissanti samaissanti| hīnādhimuttikā hīnādhimuttikaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandissanti samaissanti|

‘‘aitarahipi khau, bhikkhavai, pac‍cuppan‍naṃ addhānaṃ dhātusauva sattā saṃsandanti samainti| hīnādhimuttikā hīnādhimuttikaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, gūthau gūthaina saṃsandati samaiti; muttaṃ muttaina saṃsandati samaiti; khaiḷau khaiḷaina saṃsandati samaiti; pubbau pubbaina saṃsandati samaiti; lauhitaṃ lauhitaina saṃsandati samaiti; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, dhātusauva sabbatthapi aivamaiva dissati sattā saṃsandanti samainti| hīnādhimuttikā hīnādhimuttikaihi  saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti| atītampi khau addhānaṃ…pai॰… anāgatampi khau addhānaṃ…pai॰… aitarahipi khau pac‍cuppan‍naṃ addhānaṃ dhātusauva sattā saṃsandanti samainti| hīnādhimuttikā hīnādhimuttikaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti|

‘‘dhātusauva  bhikkhavai, sattā saṃsandanti samainti| kalyāṇādhimuttikā kalyāṇādhimuttikaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti| atītampi khau, bhikkhavai, addhānaṃ dhātusauva sattā saṃsandiṃsu samiṃsu| kalyāṇādhimuttikā kalyāṇādhimuttikaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandiṃsu samiṃsu|

‘‘anāgatampi khau, bhikkhavai, addhānaṃ…pai॰… aitarahipi khau, bhikkhavai, pac‍cuppan‍naṃ addhānaṃ dhātusauva sattā saṃsandanti samainti| kalyāṇādhimuttikā kalyāṇādhimuttikaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, khīraṃ khīraina saṃsandati samaiti; tailaṃ tailaina saṃsandati samaiti; sappi sappinā saṃsandati samaiti; madhu madhunā saṃsandati samaiti; phāṇitaṃ phāṇitaina saṃsandati samaiti; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, dhātusauva  sattā saṃsandanti samainti| kalyāṇādhimuttikā kalyāṇādhimuttikaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti| atītampi khau addhānaṃ… anāgatampi khau addhānaṃ… aitarahipi khau pac‍cuppan‍naṃ addhānaṃ dhātusauva sattā saṃsandanti samainti| kalyāṇādhimuttikā kalyāṇādhimuttikaihi saddhiṃ  saṃsandanti samaintī’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| idaṃ vatvāna sugatau athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

‘‘saṃsaggā vanathau jātau, asaṃsaggaina chij‍jati|

parittaṃ dārumāruyha, yathā sīdai mahaṇṇavai||

‘‘aivaṃ kusītamāgamma, sādhujīvipi sīdati|

tasmā taṃ parivaj‍jaiyya, kusītaṃ hīnavīriyaṃ||

‘‘pavivittaihi  ariyaihi, pahitattaihi jhāyīhi jhāyihi (sī॰), jhāyibhi (syā॰ kaṃ॰)|

nic‍caṃ āraddhavīriyaihi, paṇḍitaihi sahāvasai’’ti||

7. assaddhasaṃsandanasuttaṃ

101. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati…pai॰… ‘‘dhātusauva, bhikkhavai, sattā saṃsandanti samainti| assaddhā assaddhaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; ahirikā ahirikaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; anauttappinau anauttappīhi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; appassutā appassutaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; kusītā kusītaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; muṭṭhassatinau muṭṭhassatīhi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; duppañ‍ñā duppañ‍ñaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti’’|

‘‘atītampi khau, bhikkhavai, addhānaṃ dhātusauva sattā saṃsandiṃsu samiṃsu| assaddhā assaddhaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandiṃsu samiṃsu; ahirikā ahirikaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandiṃsu samiṃsu; anauttappinau anauttappīhi saddhiṃ saṃsandiṃsu samiṃsu; appassutā appassutaihi  saddhiṃ saṃsandiṃsu samiṃsu; kusītā kusītaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandiṃsu samiṃsu; muṭṭhassatinau muṭṭhassatīhi saddhiṃ saṃsandiṃsu samiṃsu; duppañ‍ñā duppañ‍ñaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandiṃsu samiṃsu|

‘‘anāgatampi  khau, bhikkhavai, addhānaṃ dhātusauva sattā saṃsandissanti samaissanti| assaddhā assaddhaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandissanti samaissanti; ahirikā ahirikaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandissanti samaissanti; anauttappinau anauttappīhi saddhiṃ…pai॰… appassutā  appassutaihi saddhiṃ…pai॰… kusītā kusītaihi saddhiṃ…pai॰… muṭṭhassatinau muṭṭhassatīhi saddhiṃ…pai॰… duppañ‍ñā duppañ‍ñaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandissanti samaissanti|

‘‘aitarahipi khau, bhikkhavai, pac‍cuppan‍naṃ addhānaṃ dhātusauva sattā saṃsandanti samainti| assaddhā assaddhaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; ahirikā ahirikaihi saddhiṃ…pai॰… anauttappinau anauttappīhi saddhiṃ…pai॰… appassutā appassutaihi saddhiṃ…pai॰…  kusītā kusītaihi saddhiṃ…pai॰… muṭṭhassatinau muṭṭhassatīhi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; duppañ‍ñā duppañ‍ñaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti|

‘‘dhātusauva, bhikkhavai, sattā saṃsandanti samainti| saddhā saddhaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; hirimanā hirimanaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; auttappinau auttappīhi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; bahussutā bahussutaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; āraddhavīriyā āraddhavīriyaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; upaṭṭhitassatinau upaṭṭhitassatīhi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; pañ‍ñavantau pañ‍ñavantaihi saddhiṃ  saṃsandanti samainti| atītampi khau, bhikkhavai, addhānaṃ…pai॰… anāgatampi khau, bhikkhavai…pai॰… aitarahipi khau, bhikkhavai, pac‍cuppan‍naṃ addhānaṃ dhātusauva sattā saṃsandanti samainti| saddhā saddhaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; pañ‍ñavantau pañ‍ñavantaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samaintī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. assaddhamūlakasuttaṃ

102. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘dhātusauva, bhikkhavai, sattā saṃsandanti samainti| assaddhā  assaddhaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; ahirikā ahirikaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; duppañ‍ñā duppañ‍ñaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; saddhā saddhaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; hirimanā hirimanaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; pañ‍ñavantau pañ‍ñavantaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti| atītampi khau, bhikkhavai, addhānaṃ dhātusauva sattā saṃsandiṃsu samiṃsu…pai॰…  anāgatampi khau, bhikkhavai, addhānaṃ dhātusauva sattā saṃsandissanti samaissanti…pai॰…|

‘‘aitarahipi  khau, bhikkhavai, pac‍cuppan‍naṃ addhānaṃ dhātusauva sattā saṃsandanti samainti| assaddhā assaddhaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; ahirikā ahirikaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti, duppañ‍ñā duppañ‍ñaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; saddhā saddhaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; hirimanā hirimanaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; pañ‍ñavantau pañ‍ñavantaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samaintīti| (1)

‘‘dhātusauva, bhikkhavai, sattā saṃsandanti samainti| assaddhā assaddhaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; anauttappinau anauttappīhi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; duppañ‍ñā duppañ‍ñaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; saddhā saddhaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; auttappinau auttappīhi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; pañ‍ñavantau pañ‍ñavantaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti…pai॰… paṭhamavārau viya vitthāraitabbau| (2)

‘‘dhātusauva, bhikkhavai…pai॰… assaddhā assaddhaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; appassutā appassutaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; duppañ‍ñā duppañ‍ñaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; saddhā saddhaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; bahussutā bahussutaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti, pañ‍ñavantau pañ‍ñavantaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti…pai॰…| (3)

‘‘dhātusauva, bhikkhavai…pai॰… assaddhā assaddhaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; kusītā kusītaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; duppañ‍ñā duppañ‍ñaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; saddhā saddhaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; āraddhavīriyā āraddhavīriyaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; pañ‍ñavantau pañ‍ñavantaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti…pai॰…| (4)

‘‘dhātusauva, bhikkhavai…pai॰… assaddhā assaddhaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; muṭṭhassatinau muṭṭhassatīhi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; duppañ‍ñā duppañ‍ñaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; saddhā saddhaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; upaṭṭhitassatinau upaṭṭhitassatīhi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; pañ‍ñavantau pañ‍ñavantaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samaintīti…pai॰…| aṭṭhamaṃ| (5)

9. ahirikamūlakasuttaṃ

103. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘dhātusauva…pai॰… ahirikā ahirikaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti  samainti, anauttappinau  anauttappīhi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti, duppañ‍ñā duppañ‍ñaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; hirimanā hirimanaihi saddhiṃ sasandanti samainti, auttappinau auttappīhi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti, pañ‍ñavantau pañ‍ñavantaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti…pai॰…| (1)

‘‘ahirikā ahirikaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti, appassutā appassutaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti, duppañ‍ñā duppañ‍ñaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; hirimanā hirimanaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti, bahussutā bahussutaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti, pañ‍ñavantau pañ‍ñavantaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti…pai॰…| (2)

‘‘ahirikā ahirikaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti, kusītā kusītaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti, duppañ‍ñā duppañ‍ñaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; hirimanā hirimanaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti, āraddhavīriyā āraddhavīriyaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti, pañ‍ñavantau pañ‍ñavantaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti…pai॰…| (3)

‘‘ahirikā ahirikaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti, muṭṭhassatinau muṭṭhassatīhi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti, duppañ‍ñā duppañ‍ñaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; hirimanā hirimanaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti, upaṭṭhitassatinau upaṭṭhitassatīhi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti, pañ‍ñavantau  pañ‍ñavantaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samaintīti…pai॰…| navamaṃ| (4)

10. anauttappamūlakasuttaṃ

104. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati…pai॰… ‘‘dhātusauva, bhikkhavai, sattā saṃsandanti samainti| anauttappinau anauttappīhi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; appassutā appassutaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; duppañ‍ñā duppañ‍ñaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; auttappinau auttappīhi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; bahussutā bahussutaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; pañ‍ñavantau pañ‍ñavantaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti…pai॰…| (1)

‘‘anauttappinau  anauttappīhi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; kusītā kusītaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; duppañ‍ñā duppañ‍ñaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; auttappinau auttappīhi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; āraddhavīriyā āraddhavīriyaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; pañ‍ñavantau pañ‍ñavantaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti…pai॰…| (2)

‘‘anauttappinau  anauttappīhi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; muṭṭhassatinau muṭṭhassatīhi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; duppañ‍ñā duppañ‍ñaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; auttappinau auttappīhi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; upaṭṭhitassatinau upaṭṭhitassatīhi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; pañ‍ñavantau pañ‍ñavantaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samaintīti…pai॰…| dasamaṃ| (3)

11. appassutamūlakasuttaṃ

105. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘dhātusauva, bhikkhavai, sattā saṃsandanti samainti| appassutā appassutaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti  samainti; kusītā kusītaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; duppañ‍ñā duppañ‍ñaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; bahussutā bahussutaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti  samainti; āraddhavīriyā āraddhavīriyaihi  saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; pañ‍ñavantau pañ‍ñavantaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti…pai॰…| (1)

‘‘appassutā appassutaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; muṭṭhassatinau muṭṭhassatīhi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; duppañ‍ñā duppañ‍ñaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; bahussutā bahussutaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; upaṭṭhitassatinau upaṭṭhitassatīhi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; pañ‍ñavantau pañ‍ñavantaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samaintīti…pai॰…| aikādasamaṃ| (2)

12. kusītamūlakasuttaṃ

106. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘dhātusauva, bhikkhavai, sattā saṃsandanti samainti| kusītā kusītaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; muṭṭhassatinau muṭṭhassatīhi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; duppañ‍ñā  duppañ‍ñaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; āraddhavīriyā āraddhavīriyaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; upaṭṭhitassatinau upaṭṭhitassatīhi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; pañ‍ñavantau pañ‍ñavantaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samaintīti…pai॰…| dvādasamaṃ|

dutiyau vaggau|

tassuddānaṃ –

sattimā  sanidānañ‍ca, giñ‍jakāvasathaina ca|

hīnādhimutti caṅkamaṃ, sagāthā assaddhasattamaṃ||

assaddhamūlakā  pañ‍ca, cattārau ahirikamūlakā|

anauttappamūlakā tīṇi, duvai appassutaina ca||

kusītaṃ aikakaṃ vuttaṃ, suttantā tīṇi pañ‍cakā|

bāvīsati vuttā suttā, dutiyau vaggau pavuc‍catīti||

3. kammapathavaggau

1. asamāhitasuttaṃ

107. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati…pai॰… ‘‘dhātusauva, bhikkhavai, sattā saṃsandanti samainti| assaddhā assaddhaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; ahirikā ahirikaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; anauttappinau anauttappīhi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; asamāhitā asamāhitaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; duppañ‍ñā duppañ‍ñaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti’’|

‘‘saddhā saddhaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; hirimanā hirimanaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; auttappinau auttappīhi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; samāhitā samāhitaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; pañ‍ñavantau pañ‍ñavantaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samaintī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. dussīlasuttaṃ

108. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘dhātusauva, bhikkhavai, sattā saṃsandanti samainti| assaddhā assaddhaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; ahirikā ahirikaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; anauttappinau anauttappīhi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; dussīlā dussīlaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; duppañ‍ñā duppañ‍ñaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti’’|

‘‘saddhā saddhaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; hirimanā hirimanaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; auttappinau auttappīhi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; sīlavantau  sīlavantaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti  samainti; pañ‍ñavantau pañ‍ñavantaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samaintī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. pañ‍casikkhāpadasuttaṃ

109. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘dhātusauva, bhikkhavai, sattā saṃsandanti samainti| pāṇātipātinau  pāṇātipātīhi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; adin‍nādāyinau adin‍nādāyīhi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; kāmaisumicchācārinau kāmaisumicchācārīhi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; musāvādinau musāvādīhi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; surāmairayamaj‍jappamādaṭṭhāyinau surāmairayamaj‍jappamādaṭṭhāyīhi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti’’|

‘‘pāṇātipātā paṭiviratā pāṇātipātā paṭivirataihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; adin‍nādānā paṭiviratā adin‍nādānā paṭivirataihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratā kāmaisumicchācārā paṭivirataihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; musāvādā paṭiviratā musāvādā paṭivirataihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; surāmairayamaj‍jappamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratā surāmairayamaj‍jappamādaṭṭhānā paṭivirataihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samaintī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. sattakammapathasuttaṃ

110. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘dhātusauva, bhikkhavai, sattā saṃsandanti samainti| pāṇātipātinau pāṇātipātīhi saddhiṃ  saṃsandanti samainti; adin‍nādāyinau adin‍nādāyīhi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; kāmaisumicchācārinau kāmaisumicchācārīhi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; musāvādinau musāvādīhi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; pisuṇavācā pisuṇavācaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; pharusavācā pharusavācaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; samphappalāpinau samphappalāpīhi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti’’|

‘‘pāṇātipātā paṭiviratā…pai॰… adin‍nādānā paṭiviratā… kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratā… musāvādā paṭiviratā… pisuṇāya vācāya paṭiviratā pisuṇāya vācāya paṭivirataihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; pharusāya vācāya paṭiviratā pharusāya vācāya paṭivirataihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; samphappalāpā paṭiviratā samphappalāpā paṭivirataihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samaintī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. dasakammapathasuttaṃ

111. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati…pai॰… ‘‘dhātusauva, bhikkhavai, sattā saṃsandanti samainti| pāṇātipātinau pāṇātipātīhi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; adin‍nādāyinau…pai॰… kāmaisumicchācārinau… musāvādinau… pisuṇavācā… pharusavācā… samphappalāpinau samphappalāpīhi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; abhijjhālunau abhijjhālūhi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; byāpan‍nacittā byāpan‍nacittaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; micchādiṭṭhikā micchādiṭṭhikaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti’’|

‘‘pāṇātipātā  paṭiviratā pāṇātipātā paṭivirataihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; adin‍nādānā paṭiviratā…pai॰… kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratā… musāvādā paṭiviratā… pisuṇāya vācāya… pharusāya vācāya… samphappalāpā paṭiviratā samphappalāpā paṭivirataihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; anabhijjhālunau anabhijjhālūhi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; abyāpan‍nacittā abyāpan‍nacittaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; sammādiṭṭhikā sammādiṭṭhikaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samaintī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. aṭṭhaṅgikasuttaṃ

112. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘dhātusauva, bhikkhavai, sattā saṃsandanti samainti| micchādiṭṭhikā micchādiṭṭhikaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; micchāsaṅkappā…pai॰… micchāvācā… micchākammantā… micchāājīvā… micchāvāyāmā… micchāsatinau … micchāsamādhinau micchāsamādhīhi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti| sammādiṭṭhikā sammādiṭṭhikaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; sammāsaṅkappā…pai॰… sammāvācā… sammākammantā… sammāājīvā… sammāvāyāmā… sammāsatinau… sammāsamādhinau sammāsamādhīhi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samaintī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. dasaṅgasuttaṃ

113. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘dhātusauva, bhikkhavai, sattā saṃsandanti samainti| micchādiṭṭhikā micchādiṭṭhikaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; micchāsaṅkappā…pai॰… micchāvācā… micchākammantā… micchāājīvā… micchāvāyāmā… micchāsatinau … micchāsamādhinau  micchāsamādhīhi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; micchāñāṇinau micchāñāṇīhi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; micchāvimuttinau micchāvimuttīhi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti’’|

‘‘sammādiṭṭhikā sammādiṭṭhikaihi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; sammāsaṅkappā…pai॰… sammāvācā… sammākammantā… sammāājīvā… sammāvāyāmā… sammāsatinau… sammāsamādhinau… sammāñāṇinau sammāñāṇīhi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samainti; sammāvimuttinau sammāvimuttīhi saddhiṃ saṃsandanti samaintī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

sattan‍naṃ suttantānaṃ uddānaṃ –

asamāhitaṃ  dussīlaṃ, pañ‍ca sikkhāpadāni ca|

satta kammapathā vuttā, dasakammapathaina ca|

chaṭṭhaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikau vuttau, dasaṅgaina ca sattamaṃ||

kammapathavaggau tatiyau|

4. catutthavaggau

1. catudhātusuttaṃ

114. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai…pai॰… ‘‘catassau imā, bhikkhavai, dhātuyau| katamā catassau? pathavīdhātu, āpaudhātu, taijaudhātu, vāyaudhātu – imā khau, bhikkhavai, catassau dhātuyau’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. pubbaisambaudhasuttaṃ

115. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘pubbaiva  mai, bhikkhavai, sambaudhā anabhisambuddhassa baudhisattassaiva satau aitadahausi – ‘kau nu khau pathavīdhātuyā assādau, kau ādīnavau, kiṃ nissaraṇaṃ; kau āpaudhātuyā assādau, kau ādīnavau, kiṃ nissaraṇaṃ; kau taijaudhātuyā  assādau, kau ādīnavau, kiṃ nissaraṇaṃ; kau vāyaudhātuyā assādau, kau ādīnavau, kiṃ nissaraṇa’’’nti?

‘‘tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi – ‘yaṃ khau pathavīdhātuṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ, ayaṃ pathavīdhātuyā assādau; yaṃ yā (sī॰) pathavīdhātu anic‍cā dukkhā vipariṇāmadhammā, ayaṃ pathavīdhātuyā ādīnavau; yau pathavīdhātuyā chandarāgavinayau chandarāgappahānaṃ, idaṃ pathavīdhātuyā nissaraṇaṃ| yaṃ āpaudhātuṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… yaṃ taijaudhātuṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… yaṃ vāyaudhātuṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ, ayaṃ vāyaudhātuyā assādau; yaṃ vāyaudhātu anic‍cā  dukkhā vipariṇāmadhammā, ayaṃ vāyaudhātuyā ādīnavau; yau vāyaudhātuyā chandarāgavinayau chandarāgappahānaṃ, idaṃ vāyaudhātuyā nissaraṇaṃ’’’|

‘‘yāvakīvañ‍cāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imāsaṃ catun‍naṃ dhātūnaṃ aivaṃ assādañ‍ca assādatau ādīnavañ‍ca ādīnavatau nissaraṇañ‍ca nissaraṇatau yathābhūtaṃ na abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, naiva tāvāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhauti abhisambuddhau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) pac‍cañ‍ñāsiṃ|

‘‘yatau ca khvāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imāsaṃ catun‍naṃ dhātūnaṃ aivaṃ assādañ‍ca assādatau ādīnavañ‍ca ādīnavatau nissaraṇañ‍ca nissaraṇatau yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, athāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhauti pac‍cañ‍ñāsiṃ| ñāṇañ‍ca  pana mai dassanaṃ udapādi – ‘akuppā mai vimutti caitauvimutti (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰), ayamantimā jāti, natthi dāni punabbhavau’’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. acariṃsuttaṃ

116. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘pathavīdhātuyāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, assādapariyaisanaṃ acariṃ, yau pathavīdhātuyā assādau tadajjhagamaṃ, yāvatā pathavīdhātuyā assādau pañ‍ñāya mai sau sudiṭṭhau| pathavīdhātuyāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, ādīnavapariyaisanaṃ acariṃ, yau pathavīdhātuyā ādīnavau tadajjhagamaṃ, yāvatā pathavīdhātuyā ādīnavau pañ‍ñāya mai sau sudiṭṭhau| pathavīdhātuyāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, nissaraṇapariyaisanaṃ acariṃ, yaṃ pathavīdhātuyā nissaraṇaṃ tadajjhagamaṃ, yāvatā pathavīdhātuyā nissaraṇaṃ pañ‍ñāya mai taṃ sudiṭṭhaṃ’’|

‘‘āpaudhātuyāhaṃ, bhikkhavai…pai॰… taijaudhātuyāhaṃ, bhikkhavai… vāyaudhātuyāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, assādapariyaisanaṃ  acariṃ, yau vāyaudhātuyā assādau tadajjhagamaṃ, yāvatā vāyaudhātuyā assādau pañ‍ñāya mai sau sudiṭṭhau| vāyaudhātuyāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, ādīnavapariyaisanaṃ acariṃ, yau vāyaudhātuyā ādīnavau tadajjhagamaṃ, yāvatā vāyaudhātuyā ādīnavau pañ‍ñāya mai sau  sudiṭṭhau| vāyaudhātuyāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, nissaraṇapariyaisanaṃ acariṃ, yaṃ vāyaudhātuyā nissaraṇaṃ tadajjhagamaṃ, yāvatā vāyaudhātuyā nissaraṇaṃ pañ‍ñāya mai taṃ sudiṭṭhaṃ|

‘‘yāvakīvañ‍cāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imāsaṃ catun‍naṃ dhātūnaṃ assādañ‍ca assādatau ādīnavañ‍ca ādīnavatau nissaraṇañ‍ca nissaraṇatau yathābhūtaṃ na abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, naiva  tāvāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhauti pac‍cañ‍ñāsiṃ|

‘‘yatau ca khvāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imāsaṃ catun‍naṃ dhātūnaṃ assādañ‍ca assādatau ādīnavañ‍ca ādīnavatau nissaraṇañ‍ca nissaraṇatau yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, athāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhauti pac‍cañ‍ñāsiṃ| ñāṇañ‍ca pana mai dassanaṃ udapādi – ‘akuppā mai vimutti, ayamantimā jāti, natthi dāni punabbhavau’’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. naucaidaṃsuttaṃ

117. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘nau caidaṃ, bhikkhavai, pathavīdhātuyā assādau abhavissa, nayidaṃ sattā pathavīdhātuyā sāraj‍jaiyyuṃ| yasmā  ca khau, bhikkhavai, atthi pathavīdhātuyā assādau, tasmā sattā pathavīdhātuyā sāraj‍janti| nau caidaṃ, bhikkhavai, pathavīdhātuyā ādīnavau abhavissa, nayidaṃ sattā pathavīdhātuyā nibbindaiyyuṃ| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhavai, atthi pathavīdhātuyā ādīnavau, tasmā sattā pathavīdhātuyā nibbindanti| nau caidaṃ, bhikkhavai, pathavīdhātuyā nissaraṇaṃ abhavissa, nayidaṃ sattā pathavīdhātuyā nissaraiyyuṃ| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhavai, atthi pathavīdhātuyā nissaraṇaṃ, tasmā sattā pathavīdhātuyā nissaranti’’|

‘‘nau caidaṃ, bhikkhavai, āpaudhātuyā assādau abhavissa…pai॰… nau caidaṃ, bhikkhavai, taijaudhātuyā…pai॰… nau caidaṃ, bhikkhavai, vāyaudhātuyā assādau abhavissa, nayidaṃ sattā vāyaudhātuyā sāraj‍jaiyyuṃ| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhavai, atthi vāyaudhātuyā assādau, tasmā sattā vāyaudhātuyā sāraj‍janti| nau  caidaṃ, bhikkhavai, vāyaudhātuyā ādīnavau abhavissa, nayidaṃ  sattā vāyaudhātuyā nibbindaiyyuṃ| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhavai, atthi vāyaudhātuyā ādīnavau, tasmā sattā vāyaudhātuyā nibbindanti| nau caidaṃ, bhikkhavai, vāyaudhātuyā nissaraṇaṃ abhavissa, nayidaṃ sattā vāyaudhātuyā nissaraiyyuṃ| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhavai, atthi vāyaudhātuyā nissaraṇaṃ, tasmā sattā vāyaudhātuyā nissaranti|

‘‘yāvakīvañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, sattā imāsaṃ catun‍naṃ dhātūnaṃ assādañ‍ca assādatau ādīnavañ‍ca ādīnavatau nissaraṇañ‍ca nissaraṇatau yathābhūtaṃ na abbhañ‍ñaṃsu, naiva tāvimai bhikkhavai, sattā sadaivakā laukā  samārakā sabrahmakā sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya nissaṭā visaṃyuttā vippamuttā vimariyādikataina caitasā vihariṃsu|

‘‘yatau ca khau, bhikkhavai, sattā imāsaṃ catun‍naṃ dhātūnaṃ assādañ‍ca assādatau ādīnavañ‍ca ādīnavatau nissaraṇañ‍ca nissaraṇatau yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñaṃsu, atha, bhikkhavai, sattā sadaivakā laukā samārakā sabrahmakā sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya nissaṭā visaṃyuttā vippamuttā vimariyādikataina caitasā viharantī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. aikantadukkhasuttaṃ

118. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati…pai॰… ‘‘pathavīdhātu cai ca (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) hidaṃ, bhikkhavai, aikantadukkhā abhavissa dukkhānupatitā dukkhāvak‍kantā anavak‍kantā sukhaina, nayidaṃ sattā pathavīdhātuyā sāraj‍jaiyyuṃ| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhavai, pathavīdhātu sukhā sukhānupatitā sukhāvak‍kantā anavak‍kantā dukkhaina, tasmā sattā pathavīdhātuyā sāraj‍janti’’|

‘‘āpaudhātu  cai hidaṃ, bhikkhavai…pai॰… taijaudhātu cai hidaṃ, bhikkhavai… vāyaudhātu cai hidaṃ, bhikkhavai, aikantadukkhā abhavissa dukkhānupatitā dukkhāvak‍kantā anavak‍kantā sukhaina, nayidaṃ sattā vāyaudhātuyā sāraj‍jaiyyuṃ| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhavai, vāyaudhātu sukhā sukhānupatitā sukhāvak‍kantā anavak‍kantā dukkhaina, tasmā sattā vāyaudhātuyā sāraj‍janti|

‘‘pathavīdhātu cai hidaṃ, bhikkhavai, aikantasukhā abhavissa sukhānupatitā sukhāvak‍kantā anavak‍kantā  dukkhaina, nayidaṃ sattā pathavīdhātuyā  nibbindaiyyuṃ| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhavai, pathavīdhātu dukkhā dukkhānupatitā dukkhāvak‍kantā anavak‍kantā sukhaina, tasmā sattā pathavīdhātuyā nibbindanti|

‘‘āpaudhātu cai hidaṃ, bhikkhavai…pai॰… taijaudhātu cai hidaṃ, bhikkhavai… vāyaudhātu cai hidaṃ, bhikkhavai, aikantasukhā abhavissa sukhānupatitā sukhāvak‍kantā anavak‍kantā dukkhaina, nayidaṃ sattā vāyaudhātuyā nibbindaiyyuṃ| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhavai, vāyaudhātu dukkhā dukkhānupatitā dukkhāvak‍kantā anavak‍kantā sukhaina, tasmā sattā vāyaudhātuyā nibbindantī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. abhinandasuttaṃ

119. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘yau, bhikkhavai, pathavīdhātuṃ abhinandati, dukkhaṃ sau abhinandati| yau dukkhaṃ abhinandati, aparimuttau sau dukkhasmāti vadāmi| yau āpaudhātuṃ abhinandati…pai॰… yau taijaudhātuṃ… yau vāyaudhātuṃ abhinandati, dukkhaṃ sau abhinandati| yau dukkhaṃ abhinandati, aparimuttau sau dukkhasmāti vadāmi’’|

‘‘yau  ca khau, bhikkhavai, pathavīdhātuṃ nābhinandati, dukkhaṃ sau nābhinandati| yau dukkhaṃ nābhinandati, parimuttau sau dukkhasmāti vadāmi| yau āpaudhātuṃ…pai॰… yau taijaudhātuṃ… yau vāyaudhātuṃ nābhinandati, dukkhaṃ sau nābhinandati| yau dukkhaṃ nābhinandati, parimuttau sau dukkhasmāti vadāmī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. uppādasuttaṃ

120. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘yau, bhikkhavai, pathavīdhātuyā  uppādau ṭhiti abhinibbatti pātubhāvau, dukkhassaisau uppādau raugānaṃ ṭhiti jarāmaraṇassa pātubhāvau| yau āpaudhātuyā…pai॰… yau taijaudhātuyā… yau vāyaudhātuyā uppādau ṭhiti abhinibbatti pātubhāvau, dukkhassaisau uppādau raugānaṃ ṭhiti jarāmaraṇassa pātubhāvau’’|

‘‘yau  ca khau, bhikkhavai, pathavīdhātuyā niraudhau vūpasamau atthaṅgamau, dukkhassaisau niraudhau raugānaṃ vūpasamau jarāmaraṇassa atthaṅgamau| yau āpaudhātuyā…pai॰… yau taijaudhātuyā… yau vāyaudhātuyā niraudhau vūpasamau atthaṅgamau, dukkhassaisau niraudhau raugānaṃ vūpasamau jarāmaraṇassa atthaṅgamau’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. samaṇabrāhmaṇasuttaṃ

121. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘catassau imā, bhikkhavai, dhātuyau| katamā catassau? pathavīdhātu, āpaudhātu, taijaudhātu, vāyaudhātu| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā imāsaṃ catun‍naṃ dhātūnaṃ assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ  nappajānanti, na mai tai, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā samaṇaisu vā samaṇasammatā brāhmaṇaisu vā brāhmaṇasammatā; na ca pana tai āyasmantau sāmañ‍ñatthaṃ vā brahmañ‍ñatthaṃ vā diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharanti’’|

‘‘yai ca khau kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā imāsaṃ catun‍naṃ dhātūnaṃ assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānanti, tai ca khau  mai, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā samaṇaisu caiva samaṇasammatā brāhmaṇaisu ca brāhmaṇasammatā; tai ca panāyasmantau sāmañ‍ñatthañ‍ca brahmañ‍ñatthañ‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharantī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. dutiyasamaṇabrāhmaṇasuttaṃ

122. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘catassau imā, bhikkhavai, dhātuyau| katamā catassau? pathavīdhātu, āpaudhātu, taijaudhātu, vāyaudhātu| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā imāsaṃ catun‍naṃ dhātūnaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ nappajānanti…pai॰… pajānanti…pai॰… sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharantī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. tatiyasamaṇabrāhmaṇasuttaṃ

123. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati…pai॰… ‘‘yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā pathavīdhātuṃ nappajānanti, pathavīdhātusamudayaṃ nappajānanti, pathavīdhātuniraudhaṃ nappajānanti, pathavīdhātuniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ nappajānanti…pai॰… āpaudhātuṃ  nappajānanti… taijaudhātuṃ nappajānanti… vāyaudhātuṃ nappajānanti, vāyaudhātusamudayaṃ nappajānanti, vāyaudhātuniraudhaṃ nappajānanti, vāyaudhātuniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ nappajānanti, na mai tai, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā samaṇaisu vā samaṇasammatā brāhmaṇaisu vā brāhmaṇasammatā; na ca pana tai āyasmantau sāmañ‍ñatthaṃ vā brahmañ‍ñatthaṃ vā diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharanti’’|

‘‘yai  ca khau kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā pathavīdhātuṃ pajānanti, pathavīdhātusamudayaṃ pajānanti, pathavīdhātuniraudhaṃ pajānanti, pathavīdhātuniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānanti… yai ca khau kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā…pai॰… āpaudhātuṃ pajānanti… taijaudhātuṃ pajānanti… vāyaudhātuṃ pajānanti, vāyaudhātusamudayaṃ pajānanti, vāyaudhātuniraudhaṃ pajānanti, vāyaudhātuniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānanti, tai  ca khau mai, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā samaṇaisu caiva samaṇasammatā brāhmaṇaisu ca brāhmaṇasammatā; tai ca panāyasmantau sāmañ‍ñatthañ‍ca brahmañ‍ñatthañ‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharantī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

catutthau vaggau|

tassuddānaṃ –

catassau pubbai acariṃ, naucaidañ‍ca dukkhaina ca|

abhinandañ‍ca uppādau, tayau samaṇabrāhmaṇāti||

dhātusaṃyuttaṃ samattaṃ|



4. anamataggasaṃyuttaṃ

1. paṭhamavaggau

1. tiṇakaṭṭhasuttaṃ

124. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘anamataggauyaṃ anamataggāyaṃ (pī॰ ka॰) bhikkhavai, saṃsārau| pubbā kauṭi na pañ‍ñāyati avij‍jānīvaraṇānaṃ sattānaṃ taṇhāsaṃyaujanānaṃ sandhāvataṃ saṃsarataṃ| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau yaṃ imasmiṃ jambudīpai tiṇakaṭṭhasākhāpalāsaṃ taṃ chaitvā tacchaitvā (bahūsu) aikajjhaṃ saṃharitvā caturaṅgulaṃ caturaṅgulaṃ ghaṭikaṃ katvā nikkhipaiyya – ‘ayaṃ mai mātā, tassā mai mātu ayaṃ mātā’ti, apariyādin‍nāva apariyādiṇṇāva (sī॰) bhikkhavai, tassa purisassa mātumātarau assu, atha imasmiṃ jambudīpai tiṇakaṭṭhasākhāpalāsaṃ parikkhayaṃ pariyādānaṃ gacchaiyya| taṃ kissa haitu? anamataggauyaṃ, bhikkhavai, saṃsārau| pubbā kauṭi na pañ‍ñāyati avij‍jānīvaraṇānaṃ sattānaṃ taṇhāsaṃyaujanānaṃ sandhāvataṃ saṃsarataṃ| aivaṃ dīgharattaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ pac‍canubhūtaṃ tibbaṃ pac‍canubhūtaṃ byasanaṃ pac‍canubhūtaṃ, kaṭasī kaṭasi (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) kaṭā chavā sayanti aitthāti kaṭasī vaḍḍhitā| yāvañ‍cidaṃ, bhikkhavai, alamaiva sabbasaṅkhāraisu nibbindituṃ alaṃ viraj‍jituṃ alaṃ vimuc‍citu’’nti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. pathavīsuttaṃ

125. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati…pai॰… ‘‘anamataggauyaṃ, bhikkhavai, saṃsārau| pubbā kauṭi na pañ‍ñāyati  avij‍jānīvaraṇānaṃ sattānaṃ taṇhāsaṃyaujanānaṃ sandhāvataṃ saṃsarataṃ| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau imaṃ mahāpathaviṃ kaulaṭṭhimattaṃ kaulaṭṭhimattaṃ mattikāguḷikaṃ karitvā nikkhipaiyya – ‘ayaṃ mai pitā, tassa mai pitu ayaṃ pitā’ti, apariyādin‍nāva bhikkhavai, tassa purisassa pitupitarau assu, athāyaṃ mahāpathavī parikkhayaṃ pariyādānaṃ gacchaiyya| taṃ kissa haitu? anamataggauyaṃ, bhikkhavai, saṃsārau| pubbā kauṭi na pañ‍ñāyati avij‍jānīvaraṇānaṃ sattānaṃ taṇhāsaṃyaujanānaṃ sandhāvataṃ saṃsarataṃ| aivaṃ dīgharattaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ pac‍canubhūtaṃ tibbaṃ pac‍canubhūtaṃ byasanaṃ pac‍canubhūtaṃ, kaṭasī vaḍḍhitā| yāvañ‍cidaṃ, bhikkhavai, alamaiva sabbasaṅkhāraisu nibbindituṃ, alaṃ viraj‍jituṃ, alaṃ vimuc‍citu’’nti| dutiyaṃ|

3. assusuttaṃ

126. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘anamataggauyaṃ, bhikkhavai, saṃsārau| pubbā kauṭi na pañ‍ñāyati avij‍jānīvaraṇānaṃ sattānaṃ taṇhāsaṃyaujanānaṃ sandhāvataṃ saṃsarataṃ| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, katamaṃ nu khau bahutaraṃ, yaṃ vā vau iminā dīghaina addhunā sandhāvataṃ saṃsarataṃ amanāpasampayaugā manāpavippayaugā kandantānaṃ raudantānaṃ rudantānaṃ (sī॰) assu passan‍naṃ passandaṃ (ka॰ sī॰), pasandaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰), pasan‍naṃ (pī॰ ka॰) paggharitaṃ, yaṃ vā catūsu mahāsamuddaisu udaka’’nti? ‘‘yathā khau mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāma, aitadaiva, bhantai, bahutaraṃ yaṃ nau iminā  dīghaina addhunā sandhāvataṃ saṃsarataṃ amanāpasampayaugā manāpavippayaugā kandantānaṃ raudantānaṃ assu passan‍naṃ paggharitaṃ, na tvaiva catūsu mahāsamuddaisu udaka’’nti|

‘‘sādhu  sādhu, bhikkhavai, sādhu khau mai tumhai, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānātha| aitadaiva, bhikkhavai, bahutaraṃ yaṃ vau iminā dīghaina addhunā sandhāvataṃ saṃsarataṃ amanāpasampayaugā manāpavippayaugā kandantānaṃ raudantānaṃ assu passan‍naṃ paggharitaṃ, na tvaiva catūsu mahāsamuddaisu udakaṃ| dīgharattaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, mātumaraṇaṃ pac‍canubhūtaṃ; taisaṃ vā mātumaraṇaṃ pac‍canubhauntānaṃ amanāpasampayaugā manāpavippayaugā kandantānaṃ raudantānaṃ assu passan‍naṃ paggharitaṃ, na tvaiva catūsu mahāsamuddaisu udakaṃ| dīgharattaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, pitumaraṇaṃ pac‍canubhūtaṃ …pai॰… bhātumaraṇaṃ pac‍canubhūtaṃ… bhaginimaraṇaṃ pac‍canubhūtaṃ… puttamaraṇaṃ pac‍canubhūtaṃ… dhītumaraṇaṃ pac‍canubhūtaṃ… ñātibyasanaṃ pac‍canubhūtaṃ… bhaugabyasanaṃ pac‍canubhūtaṃ| dīgharattaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, raugabyasanaṃ pac‍canubhūtaṃ, taisaṃ vau raugabyasanaṃ pac‍canubhauntānaṃ amanāpasampayaugā manāpavippayaugā kandantānaṃ raudantānaṃ assu passan‍naṃ paggharitaṃ, na tvaiva catūsu mahāsamuddaisu udakaṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? anamataggauyaṃ, bhikkhavai, saṃsārau  …pai॰… yāvañ‍cidaṃ, bhikkhavai, alamaiva sabbasaṅkhāraisu nibbindituṃ, alaṃ viraj‍jituṃ, alaṃ vimuc‍citu’’nti| tatiyaṃ|

4. khīrasuttaṃ

127. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘anamataggauyaṃ, bhikkhavai, saṃsārau| pubbā kauṭi na pañ‍ñāyati avij‍jānīvaraṇānaṃ sattānaṃ taṇhāsaṃyaujanānaṃ sandhāvataṃ saṃsarataṃ| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, katamaṃ nu khau bahutaraṃ, yaṃ vā vau iminā dīghaina addhunā sandhāvataṃ  saṃsarataṃ mātuthañ‍ñaṃ pītaṃ, yaṃ vā catūsu mahāsamuddaisu udaka’’nti? ‘‘yathā khau mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāma, aitadaiva, bhantai, bahutaraṃ yaṃ nau iminā dīghaina addhunā sandhāvataṃ saṃsarataṃ mātuthañ‍ñaṃ pītaṃ, na tvaiva catūsu mahāsamuddaisu udaka’’nti|

‘‘sādhu sādhu, bhikkhavai, sādhu khau mai tumhai, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānātha| aitadaiva, bhikkhavai, bahutaraṃ yaṃ vau iminā dīghaina addhunā sandhāvataṃ saṃsarataṃ mātuthañ‍ñaṃ pītaṃ, na tvaiva catūsu mahāsamuddaisu udakaṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? anamataggauyaṃ, bhikkhavai, saṃsārau…pai॰… alaṃ vimuc‍citu’’nti| catutthaṃ|

5. pabbatasuttaṃ

128. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ārāmai| atha khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kīvadīghau nu khau, bhantai, kappau’’ti? ‘‘dīghau khau, bhikkhu, kappau| sau na sukarau saṅkhātuṃ aittakāni vassāni iti vā, aittakāni vassasatāni iti vā, aittakāni vassasahassāni iti vā, aittakāni vassasatasahassāni iti vā’’ti|

‘‘sak‍kā pana, bhantai, upamaṃ kātu’’nti? ‘‘sak‍kā, bhikkhū’’ti bhagavā avauca| ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhu, mahāsailau pabbatau yaujanaṃ āyāmaina yaujanaṃ vitthāraina yaujanaṃ ubbaidhaina acchin‍nau asusirau aikagghanau| tamainaṃ purisau vassasatassa vassasatassa ac‍cayaina kāsikaina vatthaina  sakiṃ sakiṃ parimaj‍jaiyya| khippataraṃ khau sau, bhikkhu, mahāsailau pabbatau iminā upak‍kamaina parikkhayaṃ pariyādānaṃ gacchaiyya, na tvaiva kappau| aivaṃ dīghau, bhikkhu, kappau| aivaṃ dīghānaṃ khau, bhikkhu, kappānaṃ naikau kappau saṃsitau, naikaṃ kappasataṃ saṃsitaṃ, naikaṃ kappasahassaṃ saṃsitaṃ, naikaṃ kappasatasahassaṃ saṃsitaṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? anamataggauyaṃ, bhikkhu, saṃsārau| pubbā kauṭi…pai॰… yāvañ‍cidaṃ, bhikkhu, alamaiva sabbasaṅkhāraisu nibbindituṃ, alaṃ viraj‍jituṃ, alaṃ vimuc‍citu’’nti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. sāsapasuttaṃ

129. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati| atha khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu yaina bhagavā…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kīvadīghau, nu khau, bhantai, kappau’’ti? ‘‘dīghau khau, bhikkhu, kappau| sau na sukarau saṅkhātuṃ aittakāni vassāni iti vā…pai॰… aittakāni vassasatasahassāni iti vā’’ti|

‘‘sak‍kā pana, bhantai, upamaṃ kātu’’nti? ‘‘sak‍kā, bhikkhū’’ti bhagavā avauca| ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhu, āyasaṃ nagaraṃ yaujanaṃ āyāmaina yaujanaṃ vitthāraina yaujanaṃ ubbaidhaina, puṇṇaṃ sāsapānaṃ guḷikābaddhaṃ cūḷikābaddhaṃ (sī॰ pī॰)| tatau purisau vassasatassa vassasatassa ac‍cayaina aikamaikaṃ sāsapaṃ uddharaiyya| khippataraṃ khau sau, bhikkhu mahāsāsaparāsi iminā upak‍kamaina parikkhayaṃ pariyādānaṃ gacchaiyya, na tvaiva kappau| aivaṃ dīghau khau, bhikkhu, kappau| aivaṃ dīghānaṃ khau, bhikkhu, kappānaṃ naikau kappau saṃsitau, naikaṃ kappasataṃ saṃsitaṃ, naikaṃ kappasahassaṃ saṃsitaṃ, naikaṃ kappasatasahassaṃ saṃsitaṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? anamataggauyaṃ, bhikkhu, saṃsārau  …pai॰… alaṃ vimuc‍citu’’nti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. sāvakasuttaṃ

130. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati| atha  khau sambahulā bhikkhū yaina bhagavā…pai॰… aikamantaṃ  nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘kīvabahukā nu khau, bhantai, kappā abbhatītā atik‍kantā’’ti? ‘‘bahukā khau, bhikkhavai, kappā abbhatītā atik‍kantā| tai na sukarā saṅkhātuṃ – ‘aittakā kappā iti vā, aittakāni kappasatāni iti vā, aittakāni kappasahassāni iti vā, aittakāni kappasatasahassāni iti vā’’’ti|

‘‘sak‍kā pana, bhantai, upamaṃ kātu’’nti? ‘‘sak‍kā, bhikkhavai’’ti bhagavā avauca| ‘‘idhassu, bhikkhavai, cattārau sāvakā vassasatāyukā vassasatajīvinau| tai divasai divasai kappasatasahassaṃ kappasatasahassaṃ anussaraiyyuṃ| ananussaritāva bhikkhavai, taihi kappā assu, atha khau tai cattārau sāvakā vassasatāyukā vassasatajīvinau vassasatassa ac‍cayaina kālaṃ karaiyyuṃ| aivaṃ bahukā khau, bhikkhavai, kappā abbhatītā atik‍kantā| tai na sukarā saṅkhātuṃ – ‘aittakā kappā iti vā, aittakāni kappasatāni iti vā, aittakāni kappasahassāni iti vā, aittakāni kappasatasahassāni iti vā’ti| taṃ kissa haitu? anamataggauyaṃ, bhikkhavai, saṃsārau…pai॰… alaṃ vimuc‍citu’’nti| sattamaṃ|

8. gaṅgāsuttaṃ

131. rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai| atha khau añ‍ñatarau brāhmaṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā  bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kīvabahukā nu khau, bhau gautama, kappā abbhatītā atik‍kantā’’ti? ‘‘bahukā khau, brāhmaṇa, kappā abbhatītā atik‍kantā| tai na sukarā saṅkhātuṃ – ‘aittakā kappā iti vā, aittakāni kappasatāni iti vā, aittakāni kappasahassāni iti vā, aittakāni kappasatasahassāni iti vā’’’ti|

‘‘sak‍kā  pana, bhau gautama, upamaṃ kātu’’nti? ‘‘sak‍kā, brāhmaṇā’’ti bhagavā avauca| ‘‘saiyyathāpi, brāhmaṇa, yatau cāyaṃ gaṅgā nadī pabhavati yattha ca mahāsamuddaṃ appaiti, yā aitasmiṃ antarai vālikā sā na sukarā saṅkhātuṃ – ‘aittakā vālikā iti vā, aittakāni vālikasatāni iti vā, aittakāni vālikasahassāni iti vā, aittakāni vālikasatasahassāni  iti vā’ti| tatau bahutarā khau, brāhmaṇa, kappā abbhatītā atik‍kantā| tai na sukarā saṅkhātuṃ – ‘aittakā kappā iti  vā, aittakāni kappasatāni iti vā, aittakāni kappasahassāni iti vā, aittakāni kappasatasahassāni iti vā’ti| taṃ kissa haitu? anamataggauyaṃ, brāhmaṇa, saṃsārau| pubbā kauṭi na pañ‍ñāyati avij‍jānīvaraṇānaṃ sattānaṃ taṇhāsaṃyaujanānaṃ sandhāvataṃ saṃsarataṃ| aivaṃ dīgharattaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, dukkhaṃ pac‍canubhūtaṃ tibbaṃ pac‍canubhūtaṃ byasanaṃ pac‍canubhūtaṃ, kaṭasī vaḍḍhitā| yāvañ‍cidaṃ, brāhmaṇa, alamaiva sabbasaṅkhāraisu nibbindituṃ, alaṃ viraj‍jituṃ, alaṃ vimuc‍citu’’nti|

aivaṃ vuttai, sau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama…pai॰… upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. daṇḍasuttaṃ

132. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘anamataggauyaṃ, bhikkhavai, saṃsārau| pubbā kauṭi na pañ‍ñāyati avij‍jānīvaraṇānaṃ sattānaṃ taṇhāsaṃyaujanānaṃ sandhāvataṃ saṃsarataṃ| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, daṇḍau uparivaihāsaṃ khittau sakimpi mūlaina nipatati, sakimpi majjhaina nipatati, sakimpi antaina nipatati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, avij‍jānīvaraṇā  sattā taṇhāsaṃyaujanā sandhāvantā saṃsarantā sakimpi asmā laukā paraṃ laukaṃ gacchanti, sakimpi parasmā laukā imaṃ laukaṃ āgacchanti| taṃ kissa haitu? anamataggauyaṃ, bhikkhavai, saṃsārau…pai॰… alaṃ vimuc‍citu’’nti| navamaṃ|

10. puggalasuttaṃ

133. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati gijjhakūṭai pabbatai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘anamataggauyaṃ, bhikkhavai, saṃsārau…pai॰… aikapuggalassa, bhikkhavai, kappaṃ sandhāvatau saṃsaratau siyā aivaṃ mahā aṭṭhikaṅkalau aṭṭhipuñ‍jau aṭṭhirāsi yathāyaṃ vaipul‍lau pabbatau, sacai  saṃhārakau assa, sambhatañ‍ca na vinassaiyya| taṃ kissa haitu? anamataggauyaṃ, bhikkhavai, saṃsārau…pai॰… alaṃ vimuc‍citu’’nti|

idamavauca  bhagavā| idaṃ vatvāna sugatau athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

‘‘aikassaikaina kappaina, puggalassaṭṭhisañ‍cayau|

siyā pabbatasamau rāsi, iti vuttaṃ mahaisinā||

‘‘sau khau panāyaṃ akkhātau, vaipul‍lau pabbatau mahā|

uttarau gijjhakūṭassa, magadhānaṃ giribbajai||

‘‘yatau ca ariyasac‍cāni, sammappañ‍ñāya passati|

dukkhaṃ dukkhasamuppādaṃ, dukkhassa ca atik‍kamaṃ|

ariyaṃ caṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ, dukkhūpasamagāminaṃ||

‘‘sa sattakkhattuṃparamaṃ, sandhāvitvāna puggalau|

dukkhassantakarau  hauti, sabbasaṃyaujanakkhayā’’ti|| dasamaṃ|

paṭhamau vaggau|

tassuddānaṃ –

tiṇakaṭṭhañ‍ca pathavī, assu khīrañ‍ca pabbataṃ|

sāsapā sāvakā gaṅgā, daṇḍau ca puggalaina cāti||

2. dutiyavaggau

1. duggatasuttaṃ

134. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhu āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘anamataggauyaṃ, bhikkhavai, saṃsārau| pubbā kauṭi na pañ‍ñāyati avij‍jānīvaraṇānaṃ sattānaṃ taṇhāsaṃyaujanānaṃ sandhāvataṃ saṃsarataṃ| yaṃ, bhikkhavai, passaiyyātha duggataṃ durūpaitaṃ niṭṭhamaittha gantabbaṃ – ‘amhaihipi aivarūpaṃ pac‍canubhūtaṃ iminā dīghaina addhunā’ti| taṃ kissa haitu…pai॰… yāvañ‍cidaṃ, bhikkhavai, alamaiva sabbasaṅkhāraisu nibbindituṃ alaṃ viraj‍jituṃ alaṃ vimuc‍citu’’nti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. sukhitasuttaṃ

135. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati…pai॰… ‘‘anamataggauyaṃ, bhikkhavai, saṃsārau…pai॰… yaṃ, bhikkhavai, passaiyyātha sukhitaṃ susaj‍jitaṃ, niṭṭhamaittha  gantabbaṃ – ‘amhaihipi aivarūpaṃ pac‍canubhūtaṃ iminā dīghaina addhunā’ti| taṃ kissa haitu? anamataggauyaṃ, bhikkhavai, saṃsārau| pubbā kauṭi na pañ‍ñāyati…pai॰… alaṃ vimuc‍citu’’nti| dutiyaṃ|

3. tiṃsamattasuttaṃ

136. rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai| atha khau tiṃsamattā pāvaiyyakā pāṭhaiyyakā (katthaci) vinayapiṭakai mahāvaggai kathinakkhandhakaipi bhikkhū sabbai ārañ‍ñikā sabbai piṇḍapātikā sabbai paṃsukūlikā sabbai taicīvarikā sabbai sasaṃyaujanā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| atha khau bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘‘imai khau tiṃsamattā pāvaiyyakā bhikkhū sabbai ārañ‍ñikā sabbai piṇḍapātikā sabbai paṃsukūlikā  sabbai taicīvarikā sabbai sasaṃyaujanā| yaṃnūnāhaṃ imaisaṃ tathā dhammaṃ daisaiyyaṃ yathā naisaṃ imasmiṃyaiva āsanai anupādāya āsavaihi cittāni vimuc‍caiyyu’’nti| atha  khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘anamataggauyaṃ, bhikkhavai, saṃsārau| pubbā kauṭi na pañ‍ñāyati avij‍jānīvaraṇānaṃ sattānaṃ taṇhāsaṃyaujanānaṃ sandhāvataṃ saṃsarataṃ| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, katamaṃ nu khau bahutaraṃ, yaṃ vā vau iminā dīghaina addhunā sandhāvataṃ saṃsarataṃ sīsacchin‍nānaṃ lauhitaṃ passan‍naṃ paggharitaṃ, yaṃ vā catūsu mahāsamuddaisu udaka’’nti? ‘‘yathā khau mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāma, aitadaiva, bhantai, bahutaraṃ, yaṃ nau iminā dīghaina addhunā sandhāvataṃ saṃsarataṃ sīsacchin‍nānaṃ  lauhitaṃ passan‍naṃ paggharitaṃ, na tvaiva catūsu mahāsamuddaisu udaka’’nti|

‘‘sādhu sādhu, bhikkhavai, sādhu khau mai tumhai, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānātha| aitadaiva, bhikkhavai, bahutaraṃ, yaṃ vau iminā dīghaina addhunā sandhāvataṃ saṃsarataṃ sīsacchin‍nānaṃ lauhitaṃ passan‍naṃ paggharitaṃ, na tvaiva catūsu mahāsamuddaisu  udakaṃ| dīgharattaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, gun‍naṃ sataṃ gaubhūtānaṃ sīsacchin‍nānaṃ lauhitaṃ passan‍naṃ paggharitaṃ, na tvaiva catūsu mahāsamuddaisu udakaṃ| dīgharattaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, mahiṃsānaṃ mahisānaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) sataṃ mahiṃsabhūtānaṃ sīsacchin‍nānaṃ lauhitaṃ passan‍naṃ paggharitaṃ  …pai॰… dīgharattaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, urabbhānaṃ sataṃ urabbhabhūtānaṃ…pai॰… ajānaṃ sataṃ ajabhūtānaṃ… migānaṃ sataṃ migabhūtānaṃ… kuk‍kuṭānaṃ sataṃ kuk‍kuṭabhūtānaṃ… sūkarānaṃ sataṃ sūkarabhūtānaṃ… dīgharattaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, caurā gāmaghātāti gahaitvā sīsacchin‍nānaṃ lauhitaṃ passan‍naṃ paggharitaṃ| dīgharattaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, caurā pāripanthikāti gahaitvā sīsacchin‍nānaṃ lauhitaṃ passan‍naṃ paggharitaṃ| dīgharattaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, caurā pāradārikāti gahaitvā sīsacchin‍nānaṃ lauhitaṃ passan‍naṃ paggharitaṃ, na tvaiva catūsu mahāsamuddaisu udakaṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? anamataggauyaṃ, bhikkhavai, saṃsārau…pai॰… alaṃ vimuc‍citu’’nti|

‘‘idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti| imasmiñ‍ca  pana vaiyyākaraṇasmiṃ bhañ‍ñamānai tiṃsamattānaṃ pāvaiyyakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ anupādāya āsavaihi cittāni vimuc‍ciṃsū’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. mātusuttaṃ

137. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati…pai॰… ‘‘anamataggauyaṃ, bhikkhavai, saṃsārau…pai॰… na sau, bhikkhavai, sattau sulabharūpau yau namātābhūtapubbau iminā dīghaina addhunā| taṃ kissa haitu? anamataggauyaṃ, bhikkhavai, saṃsārau…pai॰… alaṃ vimuc‍citu’’nti| catutthaṃ|

5. pitusuttaṃ

138. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘anamataggauyaṃ, bhikkhavai, saṃsārau…pai॰… na sau, bhikkhavai, sattau sulabharūpau yau napitābhūtapubbau  …pai॰… alaṃ vimuc‍citu’’nti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. bhātusuttaṃ

139. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘na sau, bhikkhavai, sattau sulabharūpau yau nabhātābhūtapubbau…pai॰… alaṃ vimuc‍citu’’nti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. bhaginisuttaṃ

140. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati…pai॰… ‘‘na sau, bhikkhavai, sattau sulabharūpau yau nabhaginibhūtapubbau…pai॰… alaṃ vimuc‍citu’’nti| sattamaṃ|

8. puttasuttaṃ

141. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati…pai॰… ‘‘na sau, bhikkhavai, sattau sulabharūpau yau naputtabhūtapubbau…pai॰… alaṃ vimuc‍citu’’nti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. dhītusuttaṃ

142. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati…pai॰… ‘‘anamataggauyaṃ, bhikkhavai, saṃsārau| pubbā kauṭi na pañ‍ñāyati avij‍jānīvaraṇānaṃ sattānaṃ taṇhāsaṃyaujanānaṃ sandhāvataṃ saṃsarataṃ| na sau, bhikkhavai, sattau sulabharūpau yau na dhītābhūtapubbau iminā dīghaina addhunā| taṃ kissa haitu? anamataggauyaṃ, bhikkhavai, saṃsārau| pubbā kauṭi na pañ‍ñāyati avij‍jānīvaraṇānaṃ sattānaṃ taṇhāsaṃyaujanānaṃ sandhāvataṃ saṃsarataṃ| aivaṃ dīgharattaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ pac‍canubhūtaṃ tibbaṃ pac‍canubhūtaṃ byasanaṃ pac‍canubhūtaṃ, kaṭasī vaḍḍhitā| yāvañ‍cidaṃ, bhikkhavai, alamaiva sabbasaṅkhāraisu nibbindituṃ, alaṃ viraj‍jituṃ, alaṃ vimuc‍citu’’nti| navamaṃ|

10. vaipul‍lapabbatasuttaṃ

143. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati gijjhakūṭai pabbatai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘anamataggauyaṃ, bhikkhavai, saṃsārau| pubbā kauṭi na pañ‍ñāyati avij‍jānīvaraṇānaṃ sattānaṃ taṇhāsaṃyaujanānaṃ sandhāvataṃ saṃsarataṃ| bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, imassa vaipul‍lassa pabbatassa ‘pācīnavaṃsau’tvaiva samañ‍ñā udapādi| taina khau pana, bhikkhavai, samayaina manussānaṃ ‘tivarā’tvaiva samañ‍ñā udapādi| tivarānaṃ, bhikkhavai, manussānaṃ cattārīsa vassasahassāni āyuppamāṇaṃ ahausi| tivarā, bhikkhavai, manussā pācīnavaṃsaṃ pabbataṃ catūhaina ārauhanti, catūhaina aurauhanti| taina khau pana, bhikkhavai, samayaina kakusandhau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau laukai uppan‍nau hauti| kakusandhassa, bhikkhavai, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa vidhurasañ‍jīvaṃ nāma sāvakayugaṃ ahausi aggaṃ bhaddayugaṃ| passatha, bhikkhavai, sā caivimassa pabbatassa samañ‍ñā antarahitā, tai ca manussā kālaṅkatā, sau ca bhagavā parinibbutau| aivaṃ anic‍cā, bhikkhavai, saṅkhārā; aivaṃ addhuvā, bhikkhavai, saṅkhārā; aivaṃ anassāsikā, bhikkhavai, saṅkhārā| yāvañ‍cidaṃ, bhikkhavai, alamaiva sabbasaṅkhāraisu nibbindituṃ, alaṃ viraj‍jituṃ, alaṃ vimuc‍cituṃ|

‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, imassa vaipul‍lassa pabbatassa ‘vaṅkakau’tvaiva  samañ‍ñā udapādi| taina khau pana, bhikkhavai, samayaina manussānaṃ ‘rauhitassā’tvaiva samañ‍ñā udapādi| rauhitassānaṃ, bhikkhavai, manussānaṃ tiṃsavassasahassāni āyuppamāṇaṃ ahausi| rauhitassā, bhikkhavai, manussā vaṅkakaṃ pabbataṃ tīhaina ārauhanti, tīhaina aurauhanti| taina khau pana, bhikkhavai, samayaina kauṇāgamanau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau laukai uppan‍nau hauti| kauṇāgamanassa, bhikkhavai, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa bhiyyausuttaraṃ nāma sāvakayugaṃ ahausi aggaṃ bhaddayugaṃ| passatha, bhikkhavai, sā caivimassa pabbatassa samañ‍ñā antarahitā, tai ca manussā kālaṅkatā, sau ca bhagavā parinibbutau| aivaṃ  anic‍cā, bhikkhavai, saṅkhārā…pai॰… alaṃ vimuc‍cituṃ|

‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, imassa vaipul‍lassa pabbatassa ‘supassau’tvaiva suphassautvaiva (sī॰) samañ‍ñā udapādi| taina khau pana, bhikkhavai, samayaina manussānaṃ ‘suppiyā’tvaiva appiyātvaiva (sī॰) samañ‍ñā udapādi| suppiyānaṃ, bhikkhavai, manussānaṃ vīsativassasahassāni āyuppamāṇaṃ ahausi| suppiyā, bhikkhavai, manussā supassaṃ pabbataṃ dvīhaina ārauhanti, dvīhaina aurauhanti| taina khau pana, bhikkhavai, samayaina kassapau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau laukai uppan‍nau hauti| kassapassa, bhikkhavai, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa tissabhāradvājaṃ nāma sāvakayugaṃ ahausi aggaṃ bhaddayugaṃ| passatha, bhikkhavai, sā caivimassa pabbatassa samañ‍ñā antarahitā, tai ca manussā kālaṅkatā, sau ca bhagavā parinibbutau| aivaṃ anic‍cā, bhikkhavai, saṅkhārā; aivaṃ addhuvā, bhikkhavai, saṅkhārā…pai॰… alaṃ vimuc‍cituṃ|

‘‘aitarahi  khau pana, bhikkhavai, imassa vaipul‍lassa pabbatassa ‘vaipul‍lau’tvaiva samañ‍ñā udapādi| aitarahi khau pana, bhikkhavai, imaisaṃ manussānaṃ ‘māgadhakā’tvaiva samañ‍ñā udapādi| māgadhakānaṃ, bhikkhavai, manussānaṃ appakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ parittaṃ lahukaṃ lahusaṃ (sī॰); yau ciraṃ jīvati sau vassasataṃ appaṃ vā bhiyyau| māgadhakā, bhikkhavai, manussā vaipul‍laṃ pabbataṃ muhuttaina ārauhanti muhuttaina aurauhanti| aitarahi khau panāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau laukai uppan‍nau| mayhaṃ khau pana, bhikkhavai, sāriputtamauggal‍lānaṃ nāma sāvakayugaṃ aggaṃ bhaddayugaṃ| bhavissati, bhikkhavai, sau samayau yā ayañ‍caivimassa pabbatassa  samañ‍ñā antaradhāyissati, imai  ca manussā kālaṃ karissanti, ahañ‍ca parinibbāyissāmi| aivaṃ anic‍cā, bhikkhavai, saṅkhārā; aivaṃ addhuvā, bhikkhavai, saṅkhārā; aivaṃ anassāsikā, bhikkhavai, saṅkhārā| yāvañ‍cidaṃ, bhikkhavai, alamaiva sabbasaṅkhāraisu nibbindituṃ, alaṃ viraj‍jituṃ, alaṃ vimuc‍citu’’nti|

idamavauca bhagavā| idaṃ vatvāna sugatau athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

‘‘pācīnavaṃsau tivarānaṃ, rauhitassāna vaṅkakau|

suppiyānaṃ supassauti, māgadhānañ‍ca vaipul‍lau||

‘‘anic‍cā  vata saṅkhārā, uppādavayadhamminau|

uppaj‍jitvā nirujjhanti, taisaṃ vūpasamau sukhau’’ti|| dasamaṃ|

dutiyau vaggau|

tassuddānaṃ –

duggataṃ sukhitañ‍caiva, tiṃsa mātāpitaina ca|

bhātā bhaginī puttau ca, dhītā vaipul‍lapabbataṃ||

anamataggasaṃyuttaṃ samattaṃ|



5. kassapasaṃyuttaṃ

1. santuṭṭhasuttaṃ

144. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati…pai॰… ‘‘santuṭṭhāyaṃ santuṭṭhauyaṃ (sī॰), bhikkhavai, kassapau itarītaraina cīvaraina, itarītaracīvarasantuṭṭhiyā ca vaṇṇavādī; na ca cīvarahaitu anaisanaṃ appatirūpaṃ āpaj‍jati; aladdhā ca cīvaraṃ na paritassati; laddhā ca cīvaraṃ agadhitau agathitau (sī॰) amucchitau anajjhāpan‍nau ādīnavadassāvī nissaraṇapañ‍ñau paribhuñ‍jati’’|

‘‘santuṭṭhāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, kassapau itarītaraina piṇḍapātaina, itarītarapiṇḍapātasantuṭṭhiyā ca vaṇṇavādī; na ca piṇḍapātahaitu anaisanaṃ appatirūpaṃ āpaj‍jati; aladdhā ca piṇḍapātaṃ na paritassati; laddhā ca piṇḍapātaṃ agadhitau amucchitau anajjhāpan‍nau ādīnavadassāvī nissaraṇapañ‍ñau paribhuñ‍jati|

‘‘santuṭṭhāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, kassapau itarītaraina saināsanaina, itarītarasaināsanasantuṭṭhiyā ca vaṇṇavādī; na ca saināsanahaitu anaisanaṃ appatirūpaṃ āpaj‍jati; aladdhā ca saināsanaṃ na paritassati; laddhā ca saināsanaṃ agadhitau amucchitau anajjhāpan‍nau ādīnavadassāvī nissaraṇapañ‍ñau paribhuñ‍jati|

‘‘santuṭṭhāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, kassapau itarītaraina gilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraina, itarītaragilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārasantuṭṭhiyā ca vaṇṇavādī; na ca gilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārahaitu anaisanaṃ appatirūpaṃ āpaj‍jati; aladdhā ca gilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraṃ na paritassati; laddhā ca gilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraṃ agadhitau amucchitau  anajjhāpan‍nau ādīnavadassāvī nissaraṇapañ‍ñau paribhuñ‍jati|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘santuṭṭhā bhavissāma itarītaraina cīvaraina, itarītaracīvarasantuṭṭhiyā ca  vaṇṇavādinau; na ca cīvarahaitu anaisanaṃ appatirūpaṃ āpaj‍jissāma; aladdhā ca cīvaraṃ na ca paritassissāma; laddhā ca cīvaraṃ agadhitā amucchitā anajjhāpan‍nā ādīnavadassāvinau nissaraṇapañ‍ñā paribhuñ‍jissāma’’’| (aivaṃ sabbaṃ kātabbaṃ)|

‘‘‘santuṭṭhā  bhavissāma itarītaraina piṇḍapātaina…pai॰… santuṭṭhā bhavissāma itarītaraina saināsanaina…pai॰… santuṭṭhā bhavissāma itarītaraina gilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraina, itarītaragilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārasantuṭṭhiyā ca vaṇṇavādinau; na ca gilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārahaitu anaisanaṃ appatirūpaṃ āpaj‍jissāma aladdhā ca gilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraṃ na paritassissāma; laddhā ca gilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraṃ agadhitā amucchitā anajjhāpan‍nā ādīnavadassāvinau nissaraṇapañ‍ñā paribhuñ‍jissāmā’ti| aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabbaṃ| kassapaina vā hi vau, bhikkhavai, auvadissāmi yau vā panassa yau vā pana (sī॰), yau vā (pī॰) kassapasadisau, auvaditaihi ca pana vau tathattāya paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. anauttappīsuttaṃ

145. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā ca mahākassapau āyasmā ca sāriputtau bārāṇasiyaṃ viharanti isipatanai migadāyai| atha  khau āyasmā sāriputtau sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaināyasmā mahākassapau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā mahākassapaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā sāriputtau āyasmantaṃ mahākassapaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘vuc‍cati hidaṃ, āvusau kassapa, anātāpī anauttappī abhabbau sambaudhāya abhabbau nibbānāya abhabbau anuttarassa yaugakkhaimassa adhigamāya; ātāpī ca khau auttappī  bhabbau sambaudhāya bhabbau nibbānāya bhabbau anuttarassa yaugakkhaimassa adhigamāyā’’ti|

‘‘kittāvatā nu khau, āvusau, anātāpī hauti anauttappī abhabbau sambaudhāya abhabbau nibbānāya abhabbau anuttarassa yaugakkhaimassa adhigamāya; kittāvatā ca panāvusau, ātāpī  hauti auttappī bhabbau sambaudhāya bhabbau nibbānāya bhabbau anuttarassa yaugakkhaimassa adhigamāyā’’ti? ‘‘idhāvusau, bhikkhu ‘anuppan‍nā mai pāpakā akusalā dhammā uppaj‍jamānā anatthāya saṃvattaiyyu’nti  na ātappaṃ karauti, ‘uppan‍nā mai pāpakā akusalā dhammā appahīyamānā anatthāya saṃvattaiyyu’nti na ātappaṃ karauti, ‘anuppan‍nā mai kusalā dhammānuppaj‍jamānā anatthāya saṃvattaiyyu’nti na ātappaṃ karauti, ‘uppan‍nā mai kusalā dhammā nirujjhamānā anatthāya saṃvattaiyyu’nti na  ātappaṃ karauti| aivaṃ khau, āvusau, anātāpī hauti’’|

‘‘kathañ‍cāvusau, anauttappī hauti? idhāvusau, bhikkhu ‘anuppan‍nā mai pāpakā akusalā dhammā uppaj‍jamānā anatthāya saṃvattaiyyu’nti na auttappati, ‘uppan‍nā mai pāpakā akusalā dhammā appahīyamānā anatthāya saṃvattaiyyu’nti na auttappati, ‘anuppan‍nā mai kusalā dhammānuppaj‍jamānā anatthāya saṃvattaiyyu’nti na auttappati, ‘uppan‍nā mai kusalā dhammā nirujjhamānā anatthāya saṃvattaiyyu’nti na auttappati| aivaṃ  khau, āvusau, anauttappī hauti| aivaṃ khau, āvusau, anātāpī anauttappī abhabbau sambaudhāya abhabbau nibbānāya abhabbau anuttarassa yaugakkhaimassa adhigamāya|

‘‘kathañ‍cāvusau, ātāpī hauti? idhāvusau, bhikkhu ‘anuppan‍nā mai pāpakā akusalā dhammā uppaj‍jamānā anatthāya saṃvattaiyyu’nti ātappaṃ karauti, ‘uppan‍nā mai pāpakā akusalā dhammā appahīyamānā anatthāya saṃvattaiyyu’nti ātappaṃ karauti, anuppan‍nā mai kusalā dhammā…pai॰… ātappaṃ karauti| aivaṃ khau, āvusau, ātāpī hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍cāvusau, auttappī hauti? idhāvusau, bhikkhu ‘anuppan‍nā  mai pāpakā akusalā dhammā uppaj‍jamānā anatthāya saṃvattaiyyu’nti auttappati, ‘uppan‍nā mai pāpakā akusalā dhammā appahīyamānā anatthāya saṃvattaiyyu’nti auttappati, ‘anuppan‍nā mai kusalā dhammā anuppaj‍jamānā anatthāya saṃvattaiyyu’nti auttappati, ‘uppan‍nā mai kusalā dhammā nirujjhamānā anatthāya saṃvattaiyyu’nti auttappati| aivaṃ khau, āvusau, auttappī hauti| aivaṃ khau, āvusau, ātāpī auttappī bhabbau sambaudhāya bhabbau nibbānāya bhabbau anuttarassa yaugakkhaimassa adhigamāyā’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. candūpamasuttaṃ

146. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati…pai॰… ‘‘candūpamā, bhikkhavai, kulāni upasaṅkamatha – apakassaiva  kāyaṃ, apakassa cittaṃ, nic‍canavakā kulaisu appagabbhā appagabbā (ka॰)| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau jarudapānaṃ vā aulaukaiyya pabbatavisamaṃ vā nadīviduggaṃ vā – apakassaiva kāyaṃ, apakassa cittaṃ; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, candūpamā kulāni upasaṅkamatha – apakassaiva kāyaṃ, apakassa cittaṃ, nic‍canavakā kulaisu appagabbhā’’|

‘‘kassapau, bhikkhavai, candūpamau kulāni upasaṅkamati – apakassaiva kāyaṃ, apakassa cittaṃ, nic‍canavakau kulaisu appagabbhau| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, kathaṃrūpau bhikkhu arahati kulāni upasaṅkamitu’’nti? ‘‘bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā bhagavaṃnaittikā bhagavaṃpaṭisaraṇā| sādhu vata, bhantai, bhagavantaṃyaiva paṭibhātu aitassa bhāsitassa atthau| bhagavatau sutvā bhikkhū dhāraissantī’’ti|

atha khau  bhagavā ākāsai pāṇiṃ cālaisi| ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, ayaṃ ākāsai pāṇi na saj‍jati na gayhati na bajjhati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yassa kassaci bhikkhunau kulāni upasaṅkamatau kulaisu cittaṃ na saj‍jati na gayhati na bajjhati – ‘labhantu lābhakāmā, puñ‍ñakāmā karauntu puñ‍ñānī’ti; yathāsakaina lābhaina attamanau hauti sumanau, aivaṃ paraisaṃ lābhaina attamanau hauti sumanau; aivarūpau khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu arahati kulāni upasaṅkamituṃ|

‘‘kassapassa, bhikkhavai, kulāni upasaṅkamatau kulaisu cittaṃ na saj‍jati na gayhati na bajjhati – ‘labhantu lābhakāmā, puñ‍ñakāmā karauntu puñ‍ñānī’ti; yathāsakaina lābhaina attamanau hauti sumanau; aivaṃ paraisaṃ lābhaina attamanau hauti sumanau|

‘‘taṃ  kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, kathaṃrūpassa bhikkhunau aparisuddhā dhammadaisanā hauti, kathaṃrūpassa bhikkhunau parisuddhā dhammadaisanā hautī’’ti? ‘‘bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā bhagavaṃnaittikā bhagavaṃpaṭisaraṇā| sādhu vata, bhantai, bhagavantaṃyaiva paṭibhātu aitassa bhāsitassa atthau| bhagavatau sutvā bhikkhū dhāraissantī’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘yau hi kauci, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃcittau paraisaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti – ‘ahau vata mai dhammaṃ suṇaiyyuṃ, sutvā ca pana dhammaṃ pasīdaiyyuṃ, pasan‍nā ca  mai pasan‍nākāraṃ  karaiyyu’nti; aivarūpassa khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau aparisuddhā dhammadaisanā hauti|

‘‘yau ca khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃcittau paraisaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti – ‘svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau sandiṭṭhikau akālikau aihipassikau aupanaiyyikau pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhīti viñ‍ñūhi (?)| ahau, vata mai dhammaṃ suṇaiyyuṃ, sutvā ca pana dhammaṃ ājānaiyyuṃ, ājānitvā ca pana tathattāya paṭipaj‍jaiyyu’nti| iti dhammasudhammataṃ paṭic‍ca paraisaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti, kāruñ‍ñaṃ paṭic‍ca anuddayaṃ anudayaṃ (bahūsu) dvittakāraṇaṃ pana gavaisitabbaṃ paṭic‍ca anukampaṃ upādāya paraisaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti| aivarūpassa khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau parisuddhā dhammadaisanā hauti|

‘‘kassapau, bhikkhavai, aivaṃcittau paraisaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti – ‘svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau sandiṭṭhikau akālikau aihipassikau aupanaiyyikau pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhīti| ahau, vata  mai dhammaṃ suṇaiyyuṃ, sutvā ca pana dhammaṃ ājānaiyyuṃ, ājānitvā ca pana tathattāya paṭipaj‍jaiyyu’nti| iti dhammasudhammataṃ paṭic‍ca paraisaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti, kāruñ‍ñaṃ paṭic‍ca anuddayaṃ paṭic‍ca anukampaṃ upādāya paraisaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti| kassapaina vā hi vau, bhikkhavai, auvadissāmi yau vā panassa kassapasadisau, auvaditaihi ca pana vau tathattāya paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti| tatiyaṃ|

4. kulūpakasuttaṃ

147. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, kathaṃrūpau bhikkhu  arahati kulūpakau hautuṃ, kathaṃrūpau bhikkhu na arahati kulūpakau hautu’’nti? bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā…pai॰… bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘yau  hi kauci, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃcittau kulāni upasaṅkamati – ‘daintuyaiva mai, mā nādaṃsu; bahukañ‍ñaiva mai daintu, mā thaukaṃ; paṇītañ‍ñaiva mai daintu, mā lūkhaṃ; sīghañ‍ñaiva mai daintu, mā dandhaṃ; sak‍kac‍cañ‍ñaiva mai daintu, mā asak‍kac‍ca’nti| tassa cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau aivaṃcittassa kulāni upasaṅkamatau na dainti, taina bhikkhu sandīyati; sau tataunidānaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidayati| thaukaṃ dainti, nau bahukaṃ…pai॰… lūkhaṃ dainti, nau paṇītaṃ… dandhaṃ dainti, nau sīghaṃ, taina bhikkhu sandīyati; sau tataunidānaṃ  dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidayati| asak‍kac‍caṃ dainti, nau sak‍kac‍caṃ; taina bhikkhu sandīyati; sau  tataunidānaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidayati| aivarūpau khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na arahati kūlūpakau hautuṃ|

‘‘yau ca khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃcittau kulāni upasaṅkamati – ‘taṃ kutaittha labbhā parakulaisu – daintuyaiva mai, mā nādaṃsu; bahukañ‍ñaiva mai daintu, mā thaukaṃ; paṇītañ‍ñaiva mai daintu, mā lūkhaṃ; dīghañ‍ñaiva mai daintu, mā dandhaṃ; sak‍kac‍cañ‍ñaiva mai daintu, mā asak‍kac‍ca’nti| tassa cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau aivaṃcittassa kulāni upasaṅkamatau na dainti; taina bhikkhu na sandīyati; sau na tataunidānaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ  paṭisaṃvaidayati| thaukaṃ dainti, nau bahukaṃ; taina bhikkhu na sandīyati; sau na tataunidānaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidayati| lūkhaṃ dainti, nau paṇītaṃ; taina bhikkhu na sandīyati; sau na tataunidānaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidayati| dandhaṃ dainti, nau sīghaṃ; taina bhikkhu na sandīyati; sau na tataunidānaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidayati| asak‍kac‍caṃ dainti, nau sak‍kac‍caṃ; taina bhikkhu na sandīyati; sau na tataunidānaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidayati| aivarūpau khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu arahati kulūpakau hautuṃ|

‘‘kassapau, bhikkhavai, aivaṃcittau kulāni upasaṅkamati – ‘taṃ kutaittha labbhā parakulaisu – daintuyaiva mai, mā nādaṃsu; bahukañ‍ñaiva mai daintu, mā thaukaṃ; paṇītañ‍ñaiva mai daintu, mā lūkhaṃ; sīghañ‍ñaiva mai daintu, mā dandhaṃ; sak‍kac‍cañ‍ñaiva mai daintu, mā asak‍kac‍ca’nti| tassa cai, bhikkhavai, kassapassa aivaṃcittassa kulāni upasaṅkamatau na dainti; taina kassapau na sandīyati; sau na tataunidānaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidayati| thaukaṃ dainti, nau bahukaṃ; taina kassapau na sandīyati; sau na tataunidānaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidayati| lūkhaṃ dainti, nau paṇītaṃ; taina kassapau na sandīyati; sau na tataunidānaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidayati| dandhaṃ dainti, nau sīghaṃ; taina kassapau na sandīyati; sau na tataunidānaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidayati| asak‍kac‍caṃ dainti, nau sak‍kac‍caṃ; taina kassapau na sandīyati; sau na tataunidānaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidayati| kassapaina vā hi vau, bhikkhavai, auvadissāmi yau vā panassa kassapasadisau| auvaditaihi ca pana vau tathattāya paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti| catutthaṃ|

5. jiṇṇasuttaṃ

148. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ…pai॰… rājagahai vaiḷuvanai| atha khau āyasmā mahākassapau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ mahākassapaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘jiṇṇausi dāni tvaṃ, kassapa, garukāni ca tai imāni sāṇāni paṃsukūlāni nibbasanāni| tasmātiha tvaṃ, kassapa, gahapatāni gahapatikāni (sī॰) caiva cīvarāni dhāraihi, nimantanāni ca bhuñ‍jāhi, mama ca santikai viharāhī’’ti|

‘‘ahaṃ khau, bhantai, dīgharattaṃ ārañ‍ñikau caiva ārañ‍ñikattassa ca vaṇṇavādī, piṇḍapātikau caiva piṇḍapātikattassa ca vaṇṇavādī, paṃsukūlikau caiva paṃsukūlikattassa ca vaṇṇavādī, taicīvarikau caiva taicīvarikattassa ca vaṇṇavādī, appicchau caiva appicchatāya ca vaṇṇavādī, santuṭṭhau caiva santuṭṭhiyā ca vaṇṇavādī, pavivittau caiva pavivaikassa ca vaṇṇavādī, asaṃsaṭṭhau caiva asaṃsaggassa ca vaṇṇavādī, āraddhavīriyau caiva vīriyārambhassa vīriyārabbhassa (ka॰) ca vaṇṇavādī’’ti|

‘‘kiṃ kaṃ (ka॰) pana tvaṃ, kassapa, atthavasaṃ sampassamānau dīgharattaṃ ārañ‍ñikau caiva ārañ‍ñikattassa ca vaṇṇavādī, piṇḍapātikau caiva…pai॰… paṃsukūlikau caiva… taicīvarikau caiva… appicchau caiva… santuṭṭhau caiva… pavivittau caiva… asaṃsaṭṭhau caiva… āraddhavīriyau caiva vīriyārambhassa ca vaṇṇavādī’’ti?

‘‘dvai  khvāhaṃ, bhantai, atthavasai sampassamānau dīgharattaṃ ārañ‍ñikau caiva ārañ‍ñikattassa ca vaṇṇavādī, piṇḍapātikau  caiva…pai॰… paṃsukūlikau caiva… taicīvarikau caiva… appicchau caiva… santuṭṭhau caiva… pavivittau caiva… asaṃsaṭṭhau caiva… āraddhavīriyau caiva vīriyārambhassa ca vaṇṇavādī| attanau ca diṭṭhadhammasukhavihāraṃ sampassamānau, pacchimañ‍ca janataṃ anukampamānau – ‘appaiva nāma pacchimā janatā diṭṭhānugatiṃ āpaj‍jaiyyuṃ’ āpaj‍jaiyya (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰)| ‘yai kira tai ahaisuṃ buddhānubuddhasāvakā tai dīgharattaṃ ārañ‍ñikā caiva ahaisuṃ ārañ‍ñikattassa ca vaṇṇavādinau…pai॰… piṇḍapātikā caiva ahaisuṃ  …pai॰… paṃsukūlikā caiva ahaisuṃ… taicīvarikā caiva ahaisuṃ… appicchā caiva ahaisuṃ… santuṭṭhā caiva ahaisuṃ… pavivittā caiva ahaisuṃ… asaṃsaṭṭhā caiva ahaisuṃ… āraddhavīriyā caiva ahaisuṃ vīriyārambhassa ca vaṇṇavādinau’ti| tai tathattāya paṭipaj‍jissanti, taisaṃ taṃ bhavissati dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya|

‘‘imai khvāhaṃ, bhantai, dvai atthavasai sampassamānau dīgharattaṃ ārañ‍ñikau caiva ārañ‍ñikattassa ca vaṇṇavādī, piṇḍapātikau caiva…pai॰… paṃsukūlikau caiva… taicīvarikau caiva… appicchau caiva… santuṭṭhau caiva… pavivittau caiva… asaṃsaṭṭhau caiva… āraddhavīriyau caiva vīriyārambhassa ca vaṇṇavādī’’ti|

‘‘sādhu sādhu, kassapa| bahujanahitāya kira tvaṃ, kassapa, paṭipan‍nau  bahujanasukhāya laukānukampāya atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussānaṃ| tasmātiha tvaṃ, kassapa, sāṇāni caiva paṃsukūlāni dhāraihi nibbasanāni, piṇḍāya ca carāhi, arañ‍ñai ca viharāhī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. auvādasuttaṃ

149. rājagahai vaiḷuvanai| atha khau āyasmā mahākassapau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ mahākassapaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘auvada, kassapa, bhikkhū; karauhi, kassapa, bhikkhūnaṃ dhammiṃ kathaṃ| ahaṃ vā, kassapa, bhikkhū auvadaiyyaṃ tvaṃ vā; ahaṃ vā bhikkhūnaṃ dhammiṃ kathaṃ karaiyyaṃ tvaṃ vā’’ti|

‘‘dubbacā  khau, bhantai, aitarahi bhikkhū, dauvacassakaraṇaihi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatā, akkhamā, appadakkhiṇaggāhinau anusāsaniṃ| idhāhaṃ, bhantai, addasaṃ bhaṇḍañ‍ca bhaṇḍuñ‍ca (sī॰) nāma bhikkhuṃ ānandassa saddhivihāriṃ abhijikañ‍ca ābhiñ‍jikañ‍ca (sī॰ ka॰), ābhij‍jikañ‍ca (syā॰ kaṃ॰) nāma bhikkhuṃ anuruddhassa saddhivihāriṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ sutaina ac‍cāvadantai – ‘aihi, bhikkhu, kau bahutaraṃ bhāsissati, kau sundarataraṃ bhāsissati, kau cirataraṃ bhāsissatī’’’ti|

atha  khau bhagavā añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘aihi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, mama vacanaina bhaṇḍañ‍ca bhikkhuṃ ānandassa saddhivihāriṃ abhijikañ‍ca bhikkhuṃ anuruddhassa saddhivihāriṃ āmantaihi – ‘satthā āyasmantai āmantaitī’’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau sau bhikkhu bhagavatau paṭissutvā yaina tai bhikkhū tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā tai bhikkhū  aitadavauca – ‘‘satthā āyasmantai āmantaitī’’ti|

‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū tassa bhikkhunau paṭissutvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nai khau tai bhikkhū bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ sutaina ac‍cāvadatha – ‘aihi, bhikkhu, kau bahutaraṃ bhāsissati, kau sundarataraṃ bhāsissati, kau cirataraṃ bhāsissatī’’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘kiṃ nu khau mai tumhai, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānātha – ‘aitha tumhai, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ sutaina ac‍cāvadatha – aihi, bhikkhu, kau bahutaraṃ bhāsissati, kau sundarataraṃ bhāsissati, kau cirataraṃ bhāsissatī’’’ti? ‘‘nau  haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘nau cai kira mai tumhai, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānātha, atha kiṃ carahi tumhai, maughapurisā, kiṃ jānantā kiṃ passantā aivaṃ svākkhātai dhammavinayai pabbajitā samānā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ sutaina ac‍cāvadatha – ‘aihi, bhikkhu, kau bahutaraṃ bhāsissati, kau sundarataraṃ bhāsissati, kau cirataraṃ bhāsissatī’’’ti|

atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pādaisu sirasā nipatitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘ac‍cayau nau, bhantai, ac‍cagamā, yathābālai yathāmūḷhai yathāakusalai yathā bālai yathā mūḷhai yathā akusalai (pī॰), yathābālaṃ yathāmūḷhaṃ yathāakusalaṃ (?), yai mayaṃ aivaṃ svākkhātai dhammavinayai pabbajitā samānā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ sutaina ac‍cāvadimha – ‘aihi, bhikkhu, kau bahutaraṃ bhāsissati, kau sundarataraṃ bhāsissati, kau cirataraṃ bhāsissatī’ti| taisaṃ  nau, bhantai, bhagavā ac‍cayaṃ ac‍cayatau paṭiggaṇhātu āyatiṃ saṃvarāyā’’ti|

‘‘taggha tumhai, bhikkhavai, ac‍cayau ac‍cagamā yathābālai yathāmūḷhai yathāakusalai, yai tumhai aivaṃ svākkhātai dhammavinayai pabbajitā samānā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ sutaina ac‍cāvadittha – ‘aihi, bhikkhu, kau bahutaraṃ bhāsissati, kau  sundarataraṃ bhāsissati, kau cirataraṃ bhāsissatī’ti| yatau ca khau tumhai, bhikkhavai, ac‍cayaṃ ac‍cayatau disvā yathādhammaṃ paṭikarautha, taṃ vau mayaṃ mayaṃ ac‍cayaṃ (sī॰) paṭiggaṇhāma| vuddhi haisā, bhikkhavai, ariyassa vinayai yau ac‍cayaṃ ac‍cayatau disvā yathādhammaṃ paṭikarauti āyatiñ‍ca saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jatī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. dutiyaauvādasuttaṃ

150. rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai sāvatthi, tatra-aitadavauca (sī॰)| atha khau āyasmā mahākassapau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ mahākassapaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘auvada, kassapa, bhikkhū; karauhi, kassapa, bhikkhūnaṃ dhammiṃ kathaṃ| ahaṃ vā, kassapa, bhikkhū auvadaiyyaṃ tvaṃ vā; ahaṃ vā bhikkhūnaṃ dhammiṃ kathaṃ karaiyyaṃ tvaṃ vā’’ti|

‘‘dubbacā khau, bhantai, aitarahi bhikkhū, dauvacassakaraṇaihi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatā akkhamā appadakkhiṇaggāhinau anusāsaniṃ| yassa kassaci, bhantai, saddhā natthi kusalaisu dhammaisu, hirī hiri (sabbattha) natthi kusalaisu dhammaisu, auttappaṃ natthi kusalaisu dhammaisu, vīriyaṃ natthi kusalaisu dhammaisu, pañ‍ñā natthi kusalaisu dhammaisu, tassa yā  ratti vā divasau vā āgacchati āgacchanti (sī॰), hāniyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalaisu dhammaisu, nau vuddhi|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhantai, kāḷapakkhai candassa yā ratti vā divasau vā āgacchati, hāyataiva vaṇṇaina, hāyati maṇḍalaina, hāyati ābhāya, hāyati ārauhapariṇāhaina| aivamaiva khau, bhantai, yassa kassaci saddhā natthi kusalaisu dhammaisu…pai॰… hirī natthi… auttappaṃ natthi … vīriyaṃ natthi… pañ‍ñā natthi… kusalaisu dhammaisu tassa yā ratti vā divasau vā āgacchati, hāniyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalaisu dhammaisu, nau vuddhi|

‘‘‘assaddhau purisapuggalau’ti, bhantai, parihānamaitaṃ; ‘ahirikau purisapuggalau’ti, bhantai, parihānamaitaṃ; ‘anauttappī purisapuggalau’ti, bhantai, parihānamaitaṃ; ‘kusītau purisapuggalau’ti, bhantai, parihānamaitaṃ; ‘duppañ‍ñau purisapuggalau’ti, bhantai, parihānamaitaṃ; ‘kaudhanau  purisapuggalau’ti, bhantai, parihānamaitaṃ; ‘upanāhī purisapuggalau’ti, bhantai, parihānamaitaṃ; ‘na santi bhikkhū auvādakā’ti, bhantai, parihānamaitaṃ|

‘‘yassa kassaci, bhantai, saddhā atthi kusalaisu dhammaisu, hirī atthi kusalaisu dhammaisu, auttappaṃ atthi kusalaisu dhammaisu, vīriyaṃ atthi kusalaisu dhammaisu, pañ‍ñā atthi kusalaisu dhammaisu, tassa yā ratti vā divasau vā āgacchati, vuddhiyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalaisu dhammaisu, nau parihāni|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhantai, juṇhapakkhai candassa yā ratti vā divasau vā āgacchati, vaḍḍhataiva vaṇṇaina, vaḍḍhati maṇḍalaina, vaḍḍhati ābhāya, vaḍḍhati ārauhapariṇāhaina| aivamaiva  khau, bhantai, yassa kassaci saddhā atthi kusalaisu dhammaisu… hirī atthi…pai॰… auttappaṃ atthi… vīriyaṃ atthi… pañ‍ñā atthi kusalaisu dhammaisu tassa yā ratti vā divasau vā āgacchati, vuddhiyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalaisu dhammaisu, nau parihāni|

‘‘‘saddhau purisapuggalau’ti, bhantai, aparihānamaitaṃ; ‘hirimā purisapuggalau’ti, bhantai, aparihānamaitaṃ; ‘auttappī purisapuggalau’ti, bhantai, aparihānamaitaṃ; ‘āraddhavīriyau purisapuggalau’ti, bhantai, aparihānamaitaṃ; ‘pañ‍ñavā purisapuggalau’ti, bhantai, aparihānamaitaṃ; ‘ak‍kaudhanau purisapuggalau’ti, bhantai, aparihānamaitaṃ; ‘anupanāhī purisapuggalau’ti, bhantai, aparihānamaitaṃ; ‘santi bhikkhū auvādakā’ti, bhantai, aparihānamaita’’nti|

‘‘sādhu sādhu, kassapa| yassa kassaci, kassapa, saddhā natthi kusalaisu dhammaisu…pai॰… hirī  natthi… auttappaṃ natthi… vīriyaṃ natthi… pañ‍ñā natthi kusalaisu dhammaisu tassa yā ratti vā divasau vā āgacchati, hāniyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalaisu dhammaisu, nau vuddhi|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, kassapa, kāḷapakkhai candassa yā ratti vā divasau vā āgacchati, hāyataiva vaṇṇaina…pai॰… hāyati ārauhapariṇāhaina| aivamaiva khau, kassapa, yassa kassaci saddhā natthi kusalaisu dhammaisu…pai॰… hirī natthi… auttappaṃ natthi… vīriyaṃ natthi… pañ‍ñā natthi kusalaisu dhammaisu tassa yā ratti vā divasau vā āgacchati, hāniyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalaisu dhammaisu, nau vuddhi| ‘assaddhau  purisapuggalau’ti, kassapa, parihānamaitaṃ; ahirikau…pai॰… anauttappī… kusītau… duppañ‍ñau… kaudhanau… ‘upanāhī purisapuggalau’ti, kassapa, parihānamaitaṃ; ‘na santi bhikkhū auvādakā’ti, kassapa, parihānamaitaṃ|

‘‘yassa kassaci, kassapa, saddhā atthi kusalaisu dhammaisu…pai॰… hirī atthi… auttappaṃ atthi… vīriyaṃ atthi… pañ‍ñā atthi kusalaisu dhammaisu tassa yā ratti vā divasau vā āgacchati, vuddhiyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalaisu dhammaisu, nau parihāni|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, kassapa, juṇhapakkhai candassa yā ratti vā divasau  vā āgacchati, vaḍḍhataiva vaṇṇaina, vaḍḍhati maṇḍalaina, vaḍḍhati ābhāya, vaḍḍhati ārauhapariṇāhaina| aivamaiva khau, kassapa, yassa kassaci saddhā atthi kusalaisu dhammaisu hirī atthi… auttappaṃ atthi… vīriyaṃ atthi… pañ‍ñā atthi kusalaisu dhammaisu tassa yā ratti vā divasau vā āgacchati, vuddhiyaiva pāṭikaṅkhā kusalaisu dhammaisu, nau parihāni|

‘‘‘saddhau purisapuggalau’ti, kassapa, aparihānamaitaṃ; hirimā…pai॰… auttappī… āraddhavīriyau… pañ‍ñavā… ak‍kaudhanau… ‘anupanāhī purisapuggalau’ti, kassapa, aparihānamaitaṃ; ‘santi bhikkhū auvādakā’ti, kassapa, aparihānamaita’’nti| sattamaṃ|

8. tatiyaauvādasuttaṃ

151. rājagahai kalandakanivāpai sāvatthi, ārāmai (sī॰)| atha khau āyasmā mahākassapau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ  abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ mahākassapaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘auvada, kassapa, bhikkhū; karauhi, kassapa, bhikkhūnaṃ dhammiṃ kathaṃ| ahaṃ vā, kassapa, bhikkhūnaṃ auvadaiyyaṃ tvaṃ vā; ahaṃ vā bhikkhūnaṃ dhammiṃ kathaṃ karaiyyaṃ tvaṃ vā’’ti|

‘‘dubbacā khau, bhantai, aitarahi bhikkhū, dauvacassakaraṇaihi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatā, akkhamā, appadakkhiṇaggāhinau anusāsanī’’nti| ‘‘tathā hi pana, kassapa, pubbai thairā bhikkhū ārañ‍ñikā caiva ahaisuṃ ārañ‍ñikattassa ca vaṇṇavādinau, piṇḍapātikā caiva ahaisuṃ piṇḍapātikattassa ca vaṇṇavādinau, paṃsukūlikā caiva ahaisuṃ paṃsukūlikattassa ca vaṇṇavādinau, taicīvarikā caiva ahaisuṃ taicīvarikattassa ca vaṇṇavādinau, appicchā caiva ahaisuṃ appicchatāya ca vaṇṇavādinau, santuṭṭhā caiva ahaisuṃ santuṭṭhiyā ca vaṇṇavādinau, pavivittā caiva ahaisuṃ pavivaikassa ca vaṇṇavādinau, asaṃsaṭṭhā caiva ahaisuṃ  asaṃsaggassa ca vaṇṇavādinau, āraddhavīriyā caiva ahaisuṃ vīriyārambhassa ca vaṇṇavādinau|
