‘‘tatra yau hauti bhikkhu ārañ‍ñikau caiva ārañ‍ñikattassa ca vaṇṇavādī, piṇḍapātikau caiva piṇḍapātikattassa ca vaṇṇavādī, paṃsukūlikau caiva paṃsukūlikattassa ca vaṇṇavādī, taicīvarikau caiva taicīvarikattassa ca vaṇṇavādī, appicchau caiva appicchatāya ca vaṇṇavādī, santuṭṭhau caiva santuṭṭhiyā ca vaṇṇavādī, pavivittau caiva pavivaikassa ca vaṇṇavādī, asaṃsaṭṭhau  caiva asaṃsaggassa ca vaṇṇavādī, āraddhavīriyau caiva vīriyārambhassa ca vaṇṇavādī, taṃ thairā bhikkhū āsanaina nimantainti – ‘aihi, bhikkhu, kau nāmāyaṃ bhikkhu, bhaddakau vatāyaṃ bhikkhu, sikkhākāmau vatāyaṃ bhikkhu; aihi, bhikkhu, idaṃ āsanaṃ nisīdāhī’’’ti|

‘‘tatra, kassapa, navānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘yau kira sau hauti bhikkhu ārañ‍ñikau caiva ārañ‍ñikattassa ca vaṇṇavādī, piṇḍapātikau caiva…pai॰… paṃsukūlikau caiva… taicīvarikau caiva… appicchau caiva… santuṭṭhau caiva… pavivittau caiva… asaṃsaṭṭhau caiva… āraddhavīriyau caiva vīriyārambhassa ca vaṇṇavādī, taṃ thairā bhikkhū āsanaina nimantainti – aihi, bhikkhu, kau nāmāyaṃ bhikkhu, bhaddakau vatāyaṃ bhikkhu, sikkhākāmau vatāyaṃ  bhikkhu; aihi, bhikkhu, idaṃ āsanaṃ nisīdāhī’ti| tai tathattāya paṭipaj‍janti; taisaṃ taṃ hauti dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya|

‘‘aitarahi pana, kassapa, thairā bhikkhū na caiva ārañ‍ñikā na ca ārañ‍ñikattassa vaṇṇavādinau, na caiva piṇḍapātikā na ca piṇḍapātikattassa vaṇṇavādinau, na caiva paṃsukūlikā na ca paṃsukūlikattassa vaṇṇavādinau, na caiva taicīvarikā na ca taicīvarikattassa vaṇṇavādinau, na caiva appicchā na ca appicchatāya vaṇṇavādinau, na caiva santuṭṭhā na ca santuṭṭhiyā vaṇṇavādinau, na caiva pavivittā na ca pavivaikassa vaṇṇavādinau, na caiva asaṃsaṭṭhā na ca asaṃsaggassa vaṇṇavādinau, na caiva āraddhavīriyā na  ca vīriyārambhassa vaṇṇavādinau|

‘‘tatra  yau hauti bhikkhu ñātau yasassī lābhī cīvara-piṇḍapāta-saināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ taṃ thairā bhikkhū āsanaina nimantainti – ‘aihi, bhikkhu, kau nāmāyaṃ bhikkhu, bhaddakau vatāyaṃ bhikkhu, sabrahmacārikāmau vatāyaṃ bhikkhu; aihi, bhikkhu, idaṃ āsanaṃ nisīdāhī’’’ti|

‘‘tatra, kassapa, navānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘yau kira sau hauti bhikkhu ñātau yasassī lābhī cīvara-piṇḍapāta-saināsana-gilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ taṃ thairā bhikkhū āsanaina nimantainti – aihi, bhikkhu, kau nāmāyaṃ bhikkhu, bhaddakau vatāyaṃ bhikkhu, sabrahmacārikāmau vatāyaṃ bhikkhu; aihi, bhikkhu, idaṃ āsanaṃ nisīdāhī’ti| tai tathattāya paṭipaj‍janti| taisaṃ taṃ hauti dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya| yañhi taṃ, kassapa, sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘upaddutā brahmacārī brahmacārūpaddavaina abhipatthanā abhibhavanā (sī॰) brahmacārī brahmacāriabhipatthanainā’ti brahmacāriabhibhavanaināti (sī॰), aitarahi taṃ, kassapa, sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘upaddutā brahmacārī brahmacārūpaddavaina abhipatthanā brahmacārī brahmacāriabhipatthanainā’’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. jhānābhiñ‍ñasuttaṃ

152. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhāmi vivic‍caiva kāmaihi  vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| kassapaupi, bhikkhavai, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhati vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati’’|

‘‘ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhāmi vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| kassapaupi, bhikkhavai, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhati vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati|

‘‘ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhāmi pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca viharāmi satau ca sampajānau sukhañ‍ca kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaimi, yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti – ‘upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārī’ti tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| kassapaupi, bhikkhavai, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhati pītiyā  ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca viharati satau ca sampajānau, sukhañ‍ca kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaiti, yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti – ‘upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārī’ti tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati|

‘‘ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhāmi sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| kassapaupi, bhikkhavai, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhati sukhassa ca pahānā …pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati|

‘‘ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhāmi sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā anantau ākāsauti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| kassapaupi, bhikkhavai, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhati sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā…pai॰… ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati|

‘‘ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhāmi sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma anantaṃ  viñ‍ñāṇanti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| kassapaupi, bhikkhavai, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhati sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇanti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati|

‘‘ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhāmi sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘natthi kiñ‍cī’ti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| kassapaupi, bhikkhavai, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhati…pai॰… ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati|

‘‘ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhāmi sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| kassapaupi, bhikkhavai, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhati…pai॰… naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati|

‘‘ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhāmi sabbasau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| kassapaupi, bhikkhavai…pai॰… sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati|

‘‘ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhāmi anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhaumi  – aikaupi hutvā bahudhā haumi, bahudhāpi hutvā aikau haumi; āvibhāvaṃ, tiraubhāvaṃ, tiraukuṭṭaṃ, tiraupākāraṃ, tiraupabbataṃ, asaj‍jamānau gacchāmi, saiyyathāpi ākāsai; pathaviyāpi ummuj‍janimuj‍jaṃ karaumi, saiyyathāpi udakai; udakaipi abhij‍jamānai gacchāmi, saiyyathāpi pathaviyaṃ; ākāsaipi pal‍laṅkaina kamāmi, saiyyathāpi pakkhī sakuṇau; imaipi candimasūriyai aivaṃmahiddhikai aivaṃmahānubhāvai pāṇinā parimasāmi parimaj‍jāmi; yāva brahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattaimi| kassapaupi, bhikkhavai, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhati anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhauti…pai॰… yāva brahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattaiti|

‘‘ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhāmi dibbāya sautadhātuyā visuddhāya atik‍kantamānusikāya ubhau saddai suṇāmi, dibbai ca mānusai ca, yai dūrai santikai ca| kassapaupi, bhikkhavai, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhati dibbāya sautadhātuyā…pai॰… dūrai santikai ca|

‘‘ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhāmi parasattānaṃ parapuggalānaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāmi – sarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ sarāgaṃ cittanti pajānāmi, vītarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ vītarāgaṃ cittanti pajānāmi, sadausaṃ vā cittaṃ…pai॰… vītadausaṃ vā cittaṃ… samauhaṃ vā cittaṃ… vītamauhaṃ vā cittaṃ… saṃkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ… vikkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ… mahaggataṃ vā cittaṃ… amahaggataṃ vā cittaṃ… sauttaraṃ vā cittaṃ… anuttaraṃ vā cittaṃ… samāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ… asamāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ… vimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ… avimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ avimuttaṃ cittanti pajānāmi| kassapaupi, bhikkhavai, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhati parasattānaṃ  parapuggalānaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāti – sarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ sarāgaṃ cittanti pajānāti…pai॰… avimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ avimuttaṃ cittanti pajānāti|

‘‘ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhāmi anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarāmi, saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau tissaupi jātiyau catassaupi jātiyau pañ‍capi jātiyau dasapi jātiyau vīsampi jātiyau tiṃsampi jātiyau cattālīsampi jātiyau pañ‍ñāsampi jātiyau jātisatampi jātisahassampi jātisatasahassampi, anaikaipi saṃvaṭṭakappai anaikaipi vivaṭṭakappai anaikaipi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭakappai – ‘amutrāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau amutra udapādiṃ; tatrāpāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau idhūpapan‍nau’ti| iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarāmi| kassapaupi, bhikkhavai, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhati anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati, saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ…pai॰… iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati|

‘‘ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhāmi dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passāmi cavamānai  upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai, sugatai duggatai yathākammūpagai sattai  pajānāmi – ‘imai vata, bhauntau, sattā kāyaduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā vacīduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā manauduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā ariyānaṃ upavādakā micchādiṭṭhikā micchādiṭṭhikammasamādānā; tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapan‍nā, imai vā pana, bhauntau, sattā kāyasucaritaina saman‍nāgatā vacīsucaritaina  saman‍nāgatā manausucaritaina saman‍nāgatā ariyānaṃ anupavādakā sammādiṭṭhikā sammādiṭṭhikammasamādānā; tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍nā’ti| iti dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passāmi cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai, sugatai duggatai yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāmi| kassapaupi, bhikkhavai, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhati dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passati cavamānai…pai॰… yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti|

‘‘ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| kassapaupi, bhikkhavai, āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharatī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. upassayasuttaṃ

153. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā mahākassapau sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau āyasmā  ānandau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā  pattacīvaramādāya yaināyasmā  mahākassapau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ mahākassapaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘āyāma, bhantai kassapa, yaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhunupassayau tainupasaṅkamissāmā’’ti| ‘‘gaccha tvaṃ, āvusau ānanda, bahukic‍cau tvaṃ bahukaraṇīyau’’ti| dutiyampi khau āyasmā ānandau āyasmantaṃ mahākassapaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘āyāma, bhantai kassapa, yaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhunupassayau tainupasaṅkamissāmā’’ti| ‘‘gaccha tvaṃ, āvusau ānanda, bahukic‍cau tvaṃ bahukaraṇīyau’’ti| tatiyampi khau āyasmā ānandau āyasmantaṃ mahākassapaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘āyāma, bhantai kassapa, yaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhunupassayau tainupasaṅkamissāmā’’ti|

atha khau āyasmā mahākassapau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya āyasmatā ānandaina pacchāsamaṇaina yaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhunupassayau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| atha khau sambahulā bhikkhuniyau yaināyasmā mahākassapau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā  āyasmantaṃ mahākassapaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tā bhikkhuniyau āyasmā mahākassapau dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisi samādapaisi samuttaijaisi sampahaṃsaisi| atha khau āyasmā mahākassapau tā bhikkhuniyau dhammiyā kathāya sandassaitvā samādapaitvā samuttaijaitvā sampahaṃsaitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi|

atha  khau thul‍latissā bhikkhunī anattamanā anattamanavācaṃ nicchāraisi – ‘‘kiṃ pana ayyau mahākassapau, ayyassa ānandassa vaidaihamuninau sammukhā dhammaṃ bhāsitabbaṃ mañ‍ñati? saiyyathāpi nāma sūcivāṇijakau sūcikārassa  santikai sūciṃ vik‍kaitabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyya; aivamaiva ayyau mahākassapau ayyassa ānandassa vaidaihamuninau sammukhā dhammaṃ bhāsitabbaṃ mañ‍ñatī’’ti|

assausi khau āyasmā mahākassapau thul‍latissāya bhikkhuniyā imaṃ vācaṃ bhāsamānāya| atha khau āyasmā mahākassapau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau, āvusau ānanda, ahaṃ sūcivāṇijakau, tvaṃ sūcikārau; udāhu ahaṃ sūcikārau, tvaṃ sūcivāṇijakau’’ti? ‘‘khama, bhantai kassapa, bālau mātugāmau’’ti| ‘‘āgamaihi tvaṃ, āvusau ānanda, mā tai saṅghau uttari upaparikkhi’’|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, āvusau ānanda, api nu tvaṃ bhagavatau sammukhā bhikkhusaṅghai upanītau – ‘ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhāmi vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| ānandaupi, bhikkhavai, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhati vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharatī’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘ahaṃ khau, āvusau, bhagavatau sammukhā bhikkhusaṅghai upanītau – ‘ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhāmi vivic‍caiva kāmaihi  vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| kassapaupi, bhikkhavai, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhati vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharatī’ti…pai॰…| (navan‍naṃ anupubbavihārasamāpattīnaṃ pañ‍can‍nañ‍ca abhiñ‍ñānaṃ aivaṃ vitthārau vaiditabbau|)

‘‘taṃ  kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, āvusau ānanda, api nu tvaṃ bhagavatau sammukhā bhikkhusaṅghai upanītau – ‘ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| ānandaupi, bhikkhavai, āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharatī’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘ahaṃ khau, āvusau, bhagavatau sammukhā bhikkhusaṅghai upanītau – ‘ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| kassapaupi, bhikkhavai, āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharatī’’’ti|

‘‘sattaratanaṃ vā, āvusau, nāgaṃ aḍḍhaṭṭhamaratanaṃ vā tālapattikāya chādaitabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyya, yau mai cha abhiñ‍ñā chādaitabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyyā’’ti|

cavittha ca pana thul‍latissā bhikkhunī brahmacariyamhāti| dasamaṃ|

11. cīvarasuttaṃ

154. aikaṃ  samayaṃ āyasmā mahākassapau rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā ānandau dakkhiṇagirismiṃ cārikaṃ carati mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ|

taina khau pana samayaina āyasmatau ānandassa tiṃsamattā saddhivihārinau bhikkhū sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattā bhavanti yaibhuyyaina kumārabhūtā| atha  khau āyasmā ānandau dakkhiṇagirismiṃ yathābhirantaṃ cārikaṃ caritvā yaina rājagahaṃ vaiḷuvanaṃ kalandakanivāpau yaināyasmā mahākassapau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ mahākassapaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āyasmā mahākassapau  aitadavauca – ‘‘kati nu khau, āvusau ānanda, atthavasai paṭic‍ca bhagavatā kulaisu tikabhaujanaṃ pañ‍ñatta’’nti?

‘‘tayau khau, bhantai kassapa, atthavasai paṭic‍ca bhagavatā kulaisu tikabhaujanaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ – dummaṅkūnaṃ puggalānaṃ niggahāya paisalānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ phāsuvihārāya, mā pāpicchā pakkhaṃ nissāya saṅghaṃ bhindaiyyuṃ vinayapiṭakai cūḷavaggai saṃghabhaidakakkhandhakai vajirabuddhiyaṃ añ‍ñathā sambandhau dassitau, kulānuddayatāya ca| imai khau, bhantai kassapa, tayau atthavasai paṭic‍ca bhagavatā kulaisu tikabhaujanaṃ pañ‍ñatta’’nti|

‘‘atha kiñ‍carahi tvaṃ, āvusau ānanda, imaihi navaihi bhikkhūhi indriyaisu aguttadvāraihi bhaujanai amattañ‍ñūhi jāgariyaṃ ananuyuttaihi saddhiṃ cārikaṃ carasi? sassaghātaṃ mañ‍ñai carasi, kulūpaghātaṃ mañ‍ñai carasi| auluj‍jati ul‍luj‍jati (sī॰ aṭṭhakathāsu ca) khau tai, āvusau ānanda, parisā; paluj‍janti khau  tai, āvusau, navappāyā| na vāyaṃ kumārakau mattamañ‍ñāsī’’ti|

‘‘api mai, bhantai kassapa, sirasmiṃ palitāni jātāni| atha ca pana mayaṃ aj‍jāpi āyasmatau mahākassapassa kumārakavādā na muc‍cāmā’’ti| ‘‘tathā  hi pana tvaṃ, āvusau ānanda, imaihi navaihi bhikkhūhi indriyaisu aguttadvāraihi bhaujanai amattañ‍ñūhi jāgariyaṃ ananuyuttaihi saddhiṃ cārikaṃ carasi, sassaghātaṃ mañ‍ñai carasi, kulūpaghātaṃ mañ‍ñai carasi| auluj‍jati  khau tai, āvusau ānanda, parisā; paluj‍janti khau tai, āvusau, navappāyā| paluj‍jati khau tai āvusau ānanda parisā (ka॰ sī॰) na vāyaṃ kumārakau mattamañ‍ñāsī’’ti|

assausi khau thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī – ‘‘ayyaina kira mahākassapaina ayyau ānandau vaidaihamuni kumārakavādaina apasāditau’’ti|

atha khau thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī anattamanā anattamanavācaṃ nicchāraisi – ‘‘kiṃ pana ayyau mahākassapau añ‍ñatitthiyapubbau samānau ayyaṃ ānandaṃ vaidaihamuniṃ kumārakavādaina apasādaitabbaṃ mañ‍ñatī’’ti! assausi khau āyasmā mahākassapau thul‍lanandāya bhikkhuniyā imaṃ vācaṃ bhāsamānāya|

atha  khau āyasmā mahākassapau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘tagghāvusau ānanda, thul‍lanandāya bhikkhuniyā sahasā appaṭisaṅkhā vācā bhāsitā| yatvāhaṃ, āvusau, kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau, nābhijānāmi añ‍ñaṃ satthāraṃ uddisitā uddisituṃ (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰), añ‍ñatra taina bhagavatā arahatā  sammāsambuddhaina| pubbai mai, āvusau, agārikabhūtassa satau aitadahausi – ‘sambādhau gharāvāsau rajāpathau rajaupathau (sī॰), abbhaukāsau pabbaj‍jā| nayidaṃ sukaraṃ agāraṃ ajjhāvasatā aikantaparipuṇṇaṃ aikantaparisuddhaṃ saṅkhalikhitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carituṃ| yaṃnūnāhaṃ kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajaiyya’nti| sau khvāhaṃ, āvusau, aparaina samayaina paṭapilautikānaṃ  saṅghāṭiṃ kāraitvā karitvā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) yai laukai arahantau tai uddissa kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajiṃ|

sau aivaṃ pabbajitau samānau addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nau addasaṃ bhagavantaṃ antarā ca rājagahaṃ antarā ca nāḷandaṃ bahuputtai caitiyai nisin‍naṃ| disvāna mai aitadahausi – ‘satthārañ‍ca vatāhaṃ passaiyyaṃ, bhagavantamaiva passaiyyaṃ; sugatañ‍ca vatāhaṃ passaiyyaṃ, bhagavantamaiva passaiyyaṃ; sammāsambuddhañ‍ca vatāhaṃ passaiyyaṃ; bhagavantamaiva passaiyya’nti| sau khvāhaṃ, āvusau, tatthaiva bhagavatau pādaisu sirasā nipatitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘satthā mai, bhantai, bhagavā, sāvakauhamasmi; satthā mai, bhantai, bhagavā, sāvakauhamasmī’ti| aivaṃ vuttai maṃ, āvusau, bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘yau khau, kassapa, aivaṃ sabbacaitasā saman‍nāgataṃ sāvakaṃ ajānañ‍ñaiva vadaiyya jānāmīti, apassañ‍ñaiva vadaiyya passāmīti, muddhāpi tassa vipataiyya| ahaṃ khau pana, kassapa, jānañ‍ñaiva vadāmi jānāmīti, passañ‍ñaiva  vadāmi passāmī’ti|

tasmātiha tai, kassapa, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘tibbaṃ mai hirauttappaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ bhavissati thairaisu navaisu majjhimaisū’ti| aivañhi tai, kassapa, sikkhitabbaṃ|

tasmātiha tai, kassapa, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci dhammaṃ suṇissāmi kusalūpasaṃhitaṃ sabbaṃ taṃ aṭṭhiṃ katvā manasi karitvā sabbacaitasā saman‍nāharitvā auhitasautau dhammaṃ suṇissāmī’ti| aivañhi tai, kassapa, sikkhitabbaṃ|

tasmātiha  tai, kassapa, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘sātasahagatā ca mai kāyagatāsati na vijahissatī’ti| aivañhi tai, kassapa, sikkhitabbanti|

‘‘atha khau maṃ, āvusau, bhagavā iminā auvādaina auvaditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi| sattāhamaiva  khvāhaṃ, āvusau, saraṇau sāṇau (sī॰) raṭṭhapiṇḍaṃ bhuñ‍jiṃ’’| aṭṭhamiyā añ‍ñā udapādi|

‘‘atha khau, āvusau, bhagavā maggā auk‍kamma yaina añ‍ñataraṃ rukkhamūlaṃ tainupasaṅkami| atha khvāhaṃ, āvusau, paṭapilautikānaṃ saṅghāṭiṃ catugguṇaṃ pañ‍ñapaitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘idha, bhantai, bhagavā nisīdatu, yaṃ mamassa dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāyā’ti| nisīdi khau, āvusau, bhagavā pañ‍ñattai āsanai| nisaj‍ja khau maṃ, āvusau, bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘mudukā khau tyāyaṃ, kassapa, paṭapilautikānaṃ saṅghāṭī’ti| ‘paṭiggaṇhātu mai, bhantai, bhagavā paṭapilautikānaṃ saṅghāṭiṃ anukampaṃ upādāyā’ti| ‘dhāraissasi pana mai tvaṃ, kassapa, sāṇāni paṃsukūlāni nibbasanānī’ti| ‘dhāraissāmahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau sāṇāni paṃsukūlāni nibbasanānī’ti| ‘‘sau  khvāhaṃ, āvusau, paṭapilautikānaṃ saṅghāṭiṃ bhagavatau pādāsiṃ| ahaṃ pana bhagavatau sāṇāni paṃsukūlāni nibbasanāni paṭipaj‍jiṃ’’|

‘‘yañhi taṃ, āvusau, sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘bhagavatau puttau aurasau mukhatau jātau dhammajau dhammanimmitau dhammadāyādau, paṭiggahitāni  paṭiggahaitā (sī॰) sāṇāni paṃsukūlāni nibbasanānī’ti, mamaṃ taṃ sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘bhagavatau puttau aurasau mukhatau jātau dhammajau dhammanimmitau dhammadāyādau, paṭiggahitāni sāṇāni paṃsukūlāni nibbasanānī’’’ti|

‘‘ahaṃ khau, āvusau, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhāmi vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ  pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| ahaṃ khau, āvusau, yāvadai ākaṅkhāmi…pai॰… (navan‍naṃ anupubbavihārasamāpattinaṃ pañ‍can‍nañ‍ca abhiñ‍ñānaṃ aivaṃ vitthārau vaiditabbau)|

‘‘ahaṃ khau, āvusau, āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharāmi; sattaratanaṃ vā, āvusau, nāgaṃ aḍḍhaṭṭhamaratanaṃ vā tālapattikāya chādaitabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyya, yau mai cha abhiñ‍ñā chādaitabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyyā’’ti|

cavittha ca pana thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī brahmacariyamhāti| aikādasamaṃ|

12. paraṃmaraṇasuttaṃ

155. aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā ca mahākassapau āyasmā ca sāriputtau  bārāṇasiyaṃ viharanti isipatanai migadāyai| atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaināyasmā mahākassapau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā mahākassapaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā sāriputtau āyasmantaṃ mahākassapaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau, āvusau kassapa, hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti? ‘‘abyākataṃ khau aitaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatā – ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ panāvusau, na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti? ‘‘aivampi khau, āvusau, abyākataṃ bhagavatā – ‘na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ  nu khau, āvusau, hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti? ‘‘abyākataṃ khau aitaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatā – ‘hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ panāvusau, naiva hauti, na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti? ‘‘aivampi khau, āvusau, abyākataṃ bhagavatā – ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau  paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti| ‘‘kasmā caitaṃ, āvusau, abyākataṃ bhagavatā’’ti? ‘‘na haitaṃ, āvusau, atthasaṃhitaṃ nādibrahmacariyakaṃ na nibbidāya na virāgāya na niraudhāya na upasamāya na abhiñ‍ñāya na sambaudhāya na nibbānāya saṃvattati| tasmā taṃ abyākataṃ bhagavatā’’ti|

‘‘atha kiñ‍carahāvusau, byākataṃ bhagavatā’’ti? ‘‘idaṃ ‘dukkha’nti khau, āvusau, byākataṃ bhagavatā; ayaṃ ‘dukkhasamudayau’ti byākataṃ bhagavatā; ayaṃ ‘dukkhaniraudhau’ti byākataṃ bhagavatā; ayaṃ ‘dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti byākataṃ bhagavatā’’ti| ‘‘kasmā caitaṃ, āvusau, byākataṃ bhagavatā’’ti? ‘‘aitañhi, āvusau, atthasaṃhitaṃ aitaṃ ādibrahmacariyakaṃ aitaṃ nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya upasamāya abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati| tasmā taṃ byākataṃ bhagavatā’’ti| dvādasamaṃ|

13. saddhammappatirūpakasuttaṃ

156. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau āyasmā mahākassapau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ  nisin‍nau khau āyasmā mahākassapau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu kau pac‍cayau, yaina pubbai appatarāni caiva sikkhāpadāni ahaisuṃ bahutarā ca bhikkhū añ‍ñāya saṇṭhahiṃsu? kau pana, bhantai, haitu kau pac‍cayau, yainaitarahi bahutarāni caiva sikkhāpadāni appatarā ca bhikkhū añ‍ñāya saṇṭhahantī’’ti? ‘‘aivañ‍caitaṃ, kassapa, hauti sattaisu hāyamānaisu saddhammai antaradhāyamānai, bahutarāni caiva sikkhāpadāni haunti appatarā ca bhikkhū añ‍ñāya saṇṭhahanti| na tāva, kassapa, saddhammassa antaradhānaṃ hauti yāva na saddhammappatirūpakaṃ laukai uppaj‍jati| yatau ca khau, kassapa, saddhammappatirūpakaṃ laukai uppaj‍jati, atha saddhammassa antaradhānaṃ hauti’’|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, kassapa, na tāva jātarūpassa antaradhānaṃ  hauti yāva na jātarūpappatirūpakaṃ laukai uppaj‍jati| yatau ca khau, kassapa, jātarūpappatirūpakaṃ laukai uppaj‍jati, atha jātarūpassa antaradhānaṃ hauti| aivamaiva khau, kassapa, na tāva saddhammassa antaradhānaṃ hauti yāva  na saddhammappatirūpakaṃ laukai uppaj‍jati| yatau ca khau, kassapa, saddhammappatirūpakaṃ laukai uppaj‍jati, atha saddhammassa antaradhānaṃ hauti|

‘‘na khau, kassapa, pathavīdhātu saddhammaṃ antaradhāpaiti, na āpaudhātu saddhammaṃ antaradhāpaiti, na taijaudhātu saddhammaṃ antaradhāpaiti, na vāyaudhātu saddhammaṃ antaradhāpaiti; atha khau idhaiva tai uppaj‍janti maughapurisā yai imaṃ saddhammaṃ antaradhāpainti| saiyyathāpi, kassapa, nāvā ādikainaiva aupilavati; na khau, kassapa, aivaṃ saddhammassa antaradhānaṃ hauti|

‘‘pañ‍ca khaumai, kassapa, auk‍kamaniyā dhammā saddhammassa sammausāya antaradhānāya saṃvattanti| katamai pañ‍ca? idha, kassapa, bhikkhū bhikkhuniyau upāsakā upāsikāyau satthari agāravā viharanti appatissā, dhammai agāravā viharanti appatissā, saṅghai agāravā viharanti appatissā, sikkhāya  agāravā viharanti appatissā, samādhismiṃ agāravā viharanti appatissā – imai khau, kassapa, pañ‍ca auk‍kamaniyā dhammā saddhammassa sammausāya antaradhānāya saṃvattanti|

‘‘pañ‍ca khaumai, kassapa, dhammā saddhammassa ṭhitiyā asammausāya  anantaradhānāya saṃvattanti| katamai pañ‍ca? idha, kassapa, bhikkhū bhikkhuniyau upāsakā upāsikāyau satthari sagāravā viharanti sappatissā, dhammai sagāravā viharanti sappatissā, saṅghai sagāravā viharanti sappatissā, sikkhāya sagāravā viharanti sappatissā, samādhismiṃ sagāravā viharanti sappatissā – imai khau, kassapa, pañ‍ca dhammā saddhammassa ṭhitiyā asammausāya anantaradhānāya saṃvattantī’’ti| tairasamaṃ|

kassapasaṃyuttaṃ samattaṃ|


tassuddānaṃ –

santuṭṭhañ‍ca anauttappī, candūpamaṃ kulūpakaṃ|

jiṇṇaṃ tayau ca auvādā, jhānābhiñ‍ñā upassayaṃ|

cīvaraṃ paraṃmaraṇaṃ, saddhammappatirūpakanti||


6. lābhasak‍kārasaṃyuttaṃ

1. paṭhamavaggau

1. dāruṇasuttaṃ

157. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā  aitadavauca –

‘‘dāruṇau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukau kaṭukau pharusau antarāyikau anuttarassa yaugakkhaimassa adhigamāya| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘uppan‍naṃ lābhasak‍kārasilaukaṃ pajahissāma, na ca nau uppan‍nau lābhasak‍kārasilaukau cittaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassatī’ti| aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. baḷisasuttaṃ

158. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘dāruṇau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukau kaṭukau pharusau antarāyikau anuttarassa yaugakkhaimassa adhigamāya| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, bāḷisikau āmisagataṃ baḷisaṃ gambhīrai udakarahadai pakkhipaiyya| tamainaṃ añ‍ñatarau āmisacakkhu macchau gilaiyya| aivañhi sau, bhikkhavai, macchau gilabaḷisau bāḷisikassa anayaṃ āpan‍nau byasanaṃ āpan‍nau yathākāmakaraṇīyau bāḷisikassa’’|

‘‘bāḷisikauti khau, bhikkhavai, mārassaitaṃ pāpimatau adhivacanaṃ| baḷisanti khau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| yau hi kauci, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu uppan‍naṃ lābhasak‍kārasilaukaṃ assādaiti nikāmaiti, ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu gilabaḷisau mārassa anayaṃ āpan‍nau byasanaṃ āpan‍nau yathākāmakaraṇīyau pāpimatau| aivaṃ dāruṇau khau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukau kaṭukau pharusau antarāyikau anuttarassa yaugakkhaimassa adhigamāya| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘uppan‍naṃ lābhasak‍kārasilaukaṃ pajahissāma, na ca nau uppan‍nau lābhasak‍kārasilaukau cittaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassatī’ti| aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| dutiyaṃ|

3. kummasuttaṃ

159. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘dāruṇau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukau…pai॰… adhigamāya| bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatarasmiṃ udakarahadai mahākummakulaṃ ciranivāsi ahausi| atha khau, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatarau kummau añ‍ñataraṃ kummaṃ aitadavauca – ‘mā khau tvaṃ, tāta kumma, aitaṃ padaisaṃ agamāsī’ti| agamāsi khau, bhikkhavai, sau kummau taṃ padaisaṃ| tamainaṃ luddau papatāya vijjhi| atha khau, bhikkhavai, sau kummau yaina sau kummau tainupasaṅkami| addasā khau, bhikkhavai, sau kummau taṃ kummaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna taṃ kummaṃ aitadavauca – ‘kac‍ci tvaṃ, tāta kumma, na taṃ padaisaṃ agamāsī’ti? ‘agamāsiṃ khvāhaṃ, tāta kumma, taṃ padaisa’nti| ‘kac‍ci  panāsi, tāta kumma, akkhatau anupahatau’ti? ‘akkhatau khaumhi, tāta kumma, anupahatau, atthi ca mai idaṃ suttakaṃ piṭṭhitau piṭṭhitau anubandha’nti| ‘tagghasi, tāta kumma, khatau, taggha upahatau| aitaina hi tai, tāta kumma, suttakaina pitarau ca pitāmahā ca anayaṃ āpan‍nā byasanaṃ āpan‍nā| gaccha dāni tvaṃ, tāta kumma, na dāni tvaṃ amhāka’’’nti|

‘‘luddauti khau, bhikkhavai, mārassaitaṃ pāpimatau adhivacanaṃ| papatāti khau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| suttakanti khau, bhikkhavai, nandirāgassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| yau hi kauci, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu uppan‍naṃ lābhasak‍kārasilaukaṃ assādaiti nikāmaiti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu giddhau papatāya bhikkhu papatāya (syā॰ kaṃ॰), bhikkhu viddhau papatāya (?) anayaṃ āpan‍nau byasanaṃ āpan‍nau yathākāmakaraṇīyau  pāpimatau| aivaṃ dāruṇau khau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukau…pai॰… aivañhi  vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| tatiyaṃ|

4. dīghalaumikasuttaṃ

160. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati…pai॰… ‘‘dāruṇau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukau…pai॰… adhigamāya| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, dīghalaumikā aiḷakā kaṇṭakagahanaṃ pavisaiyya| sā tatra tatra saj‍jaiyya, tatra tatra gayhaiyya gacchaiyya (sī॰), gaṇhaiyya (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰), tatra tatra bajjhaiyya, tatra tatra anayabyasanaṃ āpaj‍jaiyya| ‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau bhikkhu lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina abhibhūtau pariyādiṇṇacittau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā  piṇḍāya pavisati| sau tatra tatra saj‍jati, tatra tatra gayhati, tatra tatra bajjhati, tatra tatra anayabyasanaṃ āpaj‍jati| aivaṃ dāruṇau khau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukau…pai॰… aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’’nti| catutthaṃ|

5. mīḷhakasuttaṃ

161. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘dāruṇau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukau…pai॰… adhigamāya| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, mīḷhakā gūthādī gūthapūrā puṇṇā gūthassa| puratau cassa mahāgūthapuñ‍jau| sā taina añ‍ñā mīḷhakā atimañ‍ñaiyya – ‘ahamhi gūthādī gūthapūrā puṇṇā gūthassa, puratau ca myāyaṃ mahāgūthapuñ‍jau’ti| aivamaiva  khau, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau bhikkhu lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina abhibhūtau pariyādiṇṇacittau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya pavisati| sau tattha bhuttāvī ca hauti yāvadatthau, nimantitau ca svātanāya, piṇḍapātau cassa pūrau| sau ārāmaṃ gantvā bhikkhugaṇassa majjhai vikatthati – ‘bhuttāvī camhi yāvadatthau, nimantitau camhi svātanāya, piṇḍapātau ca myāyaṃ pūrau, lābhī camhi cīvara-piṇḍapāta-saināsana-gilānappac‍caya-bhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ, imai panañ‍ñai bhikkhū appapuñ‍ñā appaisakkhā na lābhinau cīvara-piṇḍapātasaināsana-gilānappac‍caya-bhaisaj‍ja-parikkhārāna’nti| sau taina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina abhibhūtau pariyādiṇṇacittau añ‍ñai paisalai bhikkhū atimañ‍ñati| tañhi tassa, bhikkhavai, maughapurisassa  hauti dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya| aivaṃ dāruṇau khau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukau…pai॰… aivañhi vau bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. asanisuttaṃ

162. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati…pai॰… ‘‘dāruṇau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukau…pai॰… adhigamāya| upari tatiyavaggai tatiyacatutthasuttaisu ‘‘mā ca khau tvaṃ tāta saikhaṃ… anupāpuṇātū’’ti āgataṃ| taina nayaina idhāpi atthau gahaitabbau| aittha hi kiṃ saddaina paṭikkhaipatthaupi sak‍kā ñātuṃ, yathā ‘‘sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya kamuddisaiyya’’nti| tasmā kaṃ… āgacchatūti aittha kamapi… mā āgacchatūti ca, kaṃ saikhaṃ… anupāpuṇātūti aittha kamapi saikhaṃ… mā pāpuṇātūti ca atthau vaiditabbau| aṭṭhakathāṭīkāsu ca ayamaivatthau ñāpitau kaṃ, bhikkhavai, asanivicak‍kaṃ āgacchatu upari tatiyavaggai tariyacatutthasuttaisu ‘‘mā ca khau tvaṃ tāta saikhaṃ… anupāpuṇātū’’ti āgataṃ| taina nayaina idhāpi atthau gahaitabbau| aittha hi kiṃ saddaina paṭikkhaipatthaupi sak‍kā ñātuṃ, yathā ‘‘sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya kamuddisaiyya’’nti| tasmā kaṃ… āgacchatūti aittha kamapi… mā āgacchatūtica, taṃ saikhaṃ… anupāpuṇātūti aittha kamapi saikhaṃ… mā pāpuṇātūti ca atthau vaiditabbau| aṭṭhakathāṭīkāsu ca ayamaivatthau ñāpitau, saikhaṃ asanivicak‍kaṃ, taṃ saikhaṃ (pī॰ ka॰), asanivicak‍kaṃ, saikhaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰), asanivicak‍kaṃ āgacchatu, kaṃ saikhaṃ (?) appattamānasaṃ lābhasak‍kārasilaukau anupāpuṇātu’’ anupāpuṇāti (pī॰ ka॰)|

‘‘asanivicak‍kanti khau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| aivaṃ dāruṇau khau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukau…pai॰… aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. diddhasuttaṃ

163. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘dāruṇau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukau…pai॰… adhigamāya| kaṃ, bhikkhavai, diddhagataina visal‍laina sal‍laina diṭṭhigataina visal‍laina (ka॰ sī॰), diṭṭhigataina sal‍laina (syā॰ kaṃ॰), diṭṭhigataina visal‍laina sal‍laina (ka॰), diṭṭhagataina visal‍laina sal‍laina (pī॰) vijjhatu, saikhaṃ vijjhatu, taṃ saikhaṃ (sī॰), vijjhati, taṃ saikhaṃ (pī॰ ka॰) appattamānasaṃ lābhasak‍kārasilaukau anupāpuṇātu’’ anupāpuṇāti (pī॰ ka॰)|

‘‘sal‍lanti khau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| aivaṃ dāruṇau khau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukau…pai॰… aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| sattamaṃ|

8. siṅgālasuttaṃ

164. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘dāruṇau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukau…pai॰… adhigamāya| assuttha nau tumhai, bhikkhavai, rattiyā pac‍cūsasamayaṃ  jarasiṅgālassa siṅgālassa (ka॰), jarasigālassa (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) vassamānassā’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aisau khau, bhikkhavai, jarasiṅgālau uk‍kaṇḍakaina  uk‍kaṇḍakaina (sī॰), uk‍kaṇṇakaina (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) nāma raugajātaina phuṭṭhau naiva bilagatau ramati, na rukkhamūlagatau ramati, na ajjhaukāsagatau ramati; yaina  yaina gacchati, yattha yattha tiṭṭhati, yattha yattha nisīdati, yattha yattha nipaj‍jati; tattha tattha anayabyasanaṃ āpaj‍jati| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau bhikkhu lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina abhibhūtau pariyādiṇṇacittau naiva suñ‍ñāgāragatau ramati, na rukkhamūlagatau ramati, na ajjhaukāsagatau ramati; yaina yaina gacchati, yattha yattha tiṭṭhati, yattha yattha nisīdati, yattha yattha nipaj‍jati; tattha tattha anayabyasanaṃ āpaj‍jati| aivaṃ  dāruṇau khau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukau…pai॰… aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. vairambhasuttaṃ

165. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘dāruṇau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukau…pai॰… adhigamāya| upari, bhikkhavai, ākāsai vairambhā vairambā (sī॰ pī॰) nāma vātā vāyanti| tattha yau pakkhī gacchati tamainaṃ vairambhā vātā khipanti| tassa vairambhavātakkhittassa añ‍ñainaiva pādā gacchanti, añ‍ñaina pakkhā gacchanti, añ‍ñaina sīsaṃ gacchati, añ‍ñaina kāyau gacchati| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau bhikkhu lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina abhibhūtau pariyādiṇṇacittau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya pavisati arakkhitainaiva kāyaina arakkhitāya vācāya arakkhitaina cittaina, anupaṭṭhitāya satiyā, asaṃvutaihi indriyaihi| sau tattha  passati mātugāmaṃ dun‍nivatthaṃ vā duppārutaṃ vā| tassa mātugāmaṃ disvā dun‍nivatthaṃ vā duppārutaṃ vā rāgau cittaṃ anuddhaṃsaiti| sau rāgānuddhaṃsitaina cittaina sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattati| tassa añ‍ñai cīvaraṃ haranti, añ‍ñai pattaṃ haranti, añ‍ñai nisīdanaṃ haranti, añ‍ñai sūcigharaṃ haranti, vairambhavātakkhittassaiva sakuṇassa| aivaṃ dāruṇau khau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukau…pai॰… aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| navamaṃ|

10. sagāthakasuttaṃ

166. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati…pai॰… ‘‘dāruṇau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukau…pai॰… adhigamāya| idhāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aikac‍caṃ puggalaṃ passāmi sak‍kāraina  abhibhūtaṃ pariyādiṇṇacittaṃ, kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapan‍naṃ| idha panāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aikac‍caṃ puggalaṃ passāmi asak‍kāraina abhibhūtaṃ pariyādiṇṇacittaṃ, kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapan‍naṃ| idha panāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aikac‍caṃ puggalaṃ passāmi sak‍kāraina ca asak‍kāraina ca tadubhayaina abhibhūtaṃ pariyādiṇṇacittaṃ, kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapan‍naṃ| aivaṃ dāruṇau khau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukau…pai॰… aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti|

idamavauca bhagavā| idaṃ vatvāna sugatau athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

‘‘yassa  sak‍kariyamānassa, asak‍kāraina cūbhayaṃ|

samādhi na vikampati, appamāṇavihārinau appamādavihārinau (pī॰ ka॰) appamāṇauti haittha phalasamādhi, na sati||

‘‘taṃ jhāyinaṃ sātatikaṃ, sukhumaṃ diṭṭhivipassakaṃ|

upādānakkhayārāmaṃ, āhu sappurisau itī’’ti|| dasamaṃ|

paṭhamau vaggau|

tassuddānaṃ –

dāruṇau baḷisaṃ kummaṃ, dīghalaumi ca mīḷhakaṃ|

asani diddhaṃ siṅgālaṃ, vairambhaina sagāthakanti||

2. dutiyavaggau

1. suvaṇṇapātisuttaṃ

167. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati…pai॰… ‘‘dāruṇau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukau…pai॰… adhigamāya| idhāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aikac‍caṃ puggalaṃ aivaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāmi – ‘na cāyamāyasmā  suvaṇṇapātiyāpi rūpiyacuṇṇaparipūrāya haitu sampajānamusā bhāsaiyyā’ti| tamainaṃ passāmi aparaina samayaina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina abhibhūtaṃ pariyādiṇṇacittaṃ sampajānamusā bhāsantaṃ| aivaṃ dāruṇau khau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukau…pai॰… aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. rūpiyapātisuttaṃ

168. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘dāruṇau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukau…pai॰… idhāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aikac‍caṃ puggalaṃ aivaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāmi – ‘na cāyamāyasmā rūpiyapātiyāpi suvaṇṇacuṇṇaparipūrāya haitu sampajānamusā bhāsaiyyā’ti| tamainaṃ passāmi aparaina samayaina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina abhibhūtaṃ pariyādiṇṇacittaṃ sampajānamusā bhāsantaṃ| aivaṃ dāruṇau khau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukau…pai॰… aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| dutiyaṃ|

3-10. suvaṇṇanikkhasuttādiaṭṭhakaṃ

169. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘idhāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aikac‍caṃ puggalaṃ aivaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāmi – ‘na cāyamāyasmā suvaṇṇanikkhassāpi  haitu…pai॰… suvaṇṇanikkhasatassāpi haitu… siṅgīnikkhassāpi haitu… siṅgīnikkhasatassāpi haitu… pathaviyāpi jātarūpaparipūrāya haitu… āmisakiñ‍cikkhahaitupi… jīvitahaitupi… janapadakalyāṇiyāpi haitu sampajānamusā bhāsaiyyā’ti| tamainaṃ passāmi aparaina samayaina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina abhibhūtaṃ pariyādiṇṇacittaṃ sampajānamusā bhāsantaṃ| aivaṃ dāruṇau khau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukau…pai॰… aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| dasamaṃ|

dutiyau vaggau|

tassuddānaṃ –

dvai  pāti dvai suvaṇṇā ca, siṅgīhi aparai duvai|

pathavī kiñ‍cikkhajīvitaṃ, janapadakalyāṇiyā dasāti||

3. tatiyavaggau

1. mātugāmasuttaṃ

170. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati…pai॰… ‘‘dāruṇau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukau…pai॰… na  tassa, bhikkhavai, mātugāmau aikau aikassa cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhati yassa lābhasak‍kārasilaukau cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhati| aivaṃ dāruṇau khau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukau …pai॰… aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. kalyāṇīsuttaṃ

171. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘dāruṇau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukau…pai॰… na tassa, bhikkhavai, janapadakalyāṇī aikā aikassa cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhati yassa lābhasak‍kārasilaukau cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhati| aivaṃ dāruṇau khau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukau…pai॰… aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| dutiyaṃ|

3. aikaputtakasuttaṃ

172. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘dāruṇau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukau…pai॰… saddhā, bhikkhavai, upāsikā aikaputtakaṃ piyaṃ manāpaṃ aivaṃ sammā āyācamānā āyācaiyya – ‘tādisau, tāta, bhavāhi yādisau cittau ca gahapati hatthakau ca āḷavakau’ti| aisā, bhikkhavai, tulā aitaṃ pamāṇaṃ mama sāvakānaṃ upāsakānaṃ, yadidaṃ cittau  ca gahapati hatthakau ca āḷavakau| sacai khau tvaṃ, tāta, agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajasi; tādisau, tāta, bhavāhi yādisā sāriputtamauggal‍lānāti| aisā, bhikkhavai, tulā aitaṃ pamāṇaṃ mama sāvakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, yadidaṃ sāriputtamauggalānā| mā ca khau tvaṃ, tāta, saikhaṃ appattamānasaṃ lābhasak‍kārasilaukau anupāpuṇātūti| tañ‍cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuṃ  saikhaṃ appattamānasaṃ lābhasak‍kārasilaukau anupāpuṇāti, sau tassa hauti antarāyāya| aivaṃ dāruṇau khau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukau…pai॰… aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| tatiyaṃ|

4. aikadhītusuttaṃ

173. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘dāruṇau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukau…pai॰… saddhā bhikkhavai upāsikā aikaṃ dhītaraṃ piyaṃ manāpaṃ aivaṃ sammā āyācamānā āyācaiyya – ‘tādisā, ayyai, bhavāhi yādisā khuj‍juttarā ca upāsikā vaiḷukaṇḍakiyā vaiḷukaṇḍakī (sī॰ chak‍kaṅguttaraipi) ca nandamātā’ti| aisā, bhikkhavai, tulā aitaṃ pamāṇaṃ mama sāvikānaṃ upāsikānaṃ, yadidaṃ khuj‍juttarā ca upāsikā vaiḷukaṇḍakiyā ca nandamātā| sacai khau tvaṃ, ayyai, agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajasi; tādisā, ayyai, bhavāhi yādisā khaimā ca bhikkhunī uppalavaṇṇā cāti| aisā, bhikkhavai, tulā aitaṃ pamāṇaṃ mama sāvikānaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ, yadidaṃ khaimā  ca bhikkhunī uppalavaṇṇā ca| mā ca khau tvaṃ, ayyai, saikhaṃ appattamānasaṃ lābhasak‍kārasilaukau anupāpuṇātūti| taṃ cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniṃ saikhaṃ appattamānasaṃ lābhasak‍kārasilaukau anupāpuṇāti, sau tassā hauti antarāyāya| aivaṃ dāruṇau khau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukau…pai॰… aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| catutthaṃ|

5. samaṇabrāhmaṇasuttaṃ

174. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘yai  hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā lābhasak‍kārasilaukassa assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ nappajānanti, na mai tai, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā samaṇaisu vā samaṇasammatā brāhmaṇaisu vā brāhmaṇasammatā, na ca pana tai āyasmantā sāmañ‍ñatthaṃ vā brahmañ‍ñatthaṃ vā diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā  sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharanti| yai ca khau kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā lābhasak‍kārasilaukassa assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca  nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānanti, tai ca khau mai, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā samaṇaisu caiva samaṇasammatā brāhmaṇaisu ca brāhmaṇasammatā, tai ca panāyasmantau sāmañ‍ñatthañ‍ca brahmañ‍ñatthañ‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharantī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dutiyasamaṇabrāhmaṇasuttaṃ

175. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā lābhasak‍kārasilaukassa samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ nappajānanti…pai॰… pajānanti…pai॰… sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharantī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. tatiyasamaṇabrāhmaṇasuttaṃ

176. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā  vā lābhasak‍kārasilaukaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānanti, lābhasak‍kārasilaukasamudayaṃ nappajānanti, lābhasak‍kārasilaukaniraudhaṃ nappajānanti, lābhasak‍kārasilaukaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ nappajānanti …pai॰… pajānanti…pai॰… sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharantī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. chavisuttaṃ

177. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘dāruṇau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukau| lābhasak‍kārasilaukau, bhikkhavai, chaviṃ chindati, chaviṃ chaitvā cammaṃ chindati, cammaṃ chaitvā maṃsaṃ chindati, maṃsaṃ chaitvā nhāruṃ chindati, nhāruṃ chaitvā aṭṭhiṃ chindati, aṭṭhiṃ chaitvā aṭṭhimiñ‍jaṃ  āhac‍ca tiṭṭhati| aivaṃ dāruṇau khau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukau…pai॰… aivañhi vau bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. raj‍jusuttaṃ

178. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘dāruṇau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukau| lābhasak‍kārasilaukau, bhikkhavai, chaviṃ chindati, chaviṃ chaitvā cammaṃ chindati, cammaṃ chaitvā maṃsaṃ chindati, maṃsaṃ chaitvā nhāruṃ chindati, nhāruṃ chaitvā aṭṭhiṃ chindati, aṭṭhiṃ chaitvā aṭṭhimiñ‍jaṃ āhac‍ca tiṭṭhati’’|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, balavā purisau daḷhāya vāḷaraj‍juyā jaṅghaṃ vaiṭhaitvā ghaṃsaiyya| sā chaviṃ chindaiyya, chaviṃ chaitvā cammaṃ chindaiyya, cammaṃ chaitvā maṃsaṃ chindaiyya, maṃsaṃ chaitvā nhāruṃ chindaiyya, nhāruṃ chaitvā aṭṭhiṃ chindaiyya, aṭṭhiṃ chaitvā aṭṭhimiñ‍jaṃ āhac‍ca tiṭṭhaiyya| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukau  chaviṃ chindati, chaviṃ chaitvā cammaṃ chindati, cammaṃ chaitvā maṃsaṃ chindati, maṃsaṃ chaitvā nhāruṃ chindati, nhāruṃ chaitvā aṭṭhiṃ chindati, aṭṭhiṃ chaitvā aṭṭhiṃmiñ‍jaṃ āhac‍ca tiṭṭhati| aivaṃ dāruṇau khau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukau…pai॰… aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| navamaṃ|

10. bhikkhusuttaṃ

179. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘yaupi  sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu arahaṃ khīṇāsavau tassapāhaṃ lābhasak‍kārasilaukau antarāyāya vadāmī’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kissa pana, bhantai, khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau lābhasak‍kārasilaukau antarāyāyā’’ti? ‘‘yā hissa sā, ānanda, akuppā caitauvimutti nāhaṃ tassā lābhasak‍kārasilaukaṃ antarāyāya vadāmi| yai ca khvassa, ānanda, appamattassa ātāpinau pahitattassa viharatau diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārā adhigatā taisāhamassa lābhasak‍kārasilaukaṃ antarāyāya vadāmi| aivaṃ dāruṇau khau, ānanda, lābhasak‍kārasilaukau kaṭukau pharusau antarāyikau anuttarassa yaugakkhaimassa adhigamāya| tasmātihānanda, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘uppan‍naṃ  lābhasak‍kārasilaukaṃ pajahissāma, na ca nau uppan‍nau lābhasak‍kārasilaukau cittaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassatī’ti| aivañhi vau, ānanda, sikkhitabba’’nti| dasamaṃ|

tatiyau vaggau|

tassuddānaṃ –

mātugāmau  ca kalyāṇī, puttau ca aikadhītu ca|

samaṇabrāhmaṇā tīṇi, chavi raj‍ju ca bhikkhunāti||

4. catutthavaggau

1. bhindisuttaṃ

180. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati…pai॰… ‘‘dāruṇau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukau| lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina  abhibhūtau pariyādiṇṇacittau, bhikkhavai, daivadattau saṅghaṃ bhindi| aivaṃ dāruṇau khau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukau…pai॰… sikkhitabba’’nti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. kusalamūlasuttaṃ

181. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘dāruṇau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukau| lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina abhibhūtassa pariyādiṇṇacittassa, bhikkhavai, daivadattassa kusalamūlaṃ samucchaidamagamā| aivaṃ dāruṇau khau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukau…pai॰… sikkhitabba’’nti| dutiyaṃ|

3. kusaladhammasuttaṃ

182. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati…pai॰… ‘‘dāruṇau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukau| lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina abhibhūtassa pariyādiṇṇacittassa, bhikkhavai, daivadattassa kusalau dhammau samucchaidamagamā| aivaṃ dāruṇau khau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukau…pai॰… sikkhitabba’’nti| tatiyaṃ|

4. suk‍kadhammasuttaṃ

183. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘dāruṇau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukau| lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina abhibhūtassa pariyādiṇṇacittassa, bhikkhavai, daivadattassa suk‍kau dhammau samucchaidamagamā| aivaṃ dāruṇau khau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukau…pai॰… sikkhitabba’’nti| catutthaṃ|

5. acirapak‍kantasuttaṃ

184. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati gijjhakūṭai pabbatai acirapak‍kantai daivadattai| tatra khau bhagavā daivadattaṃ ārabbha bhikkhū āmantaisi  – ‘‘attavadhāya, bhikkhavai, daivadattassa lābhasak‍kārasilaukau udapādi, parābhavāya daivadattassa lābhasak‍kārasilaukau udapādi’’|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, kadalī attavadhāya phalaṃ daiti, parābhavāya phalaṃ daiti; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, attavadhāya daivadattassa lābhasak‍kārasilaukau udapādi, parābhavāya daivadattassa lābhasak‍kārasilaukau udapādi|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, vaiḷu attavadhāya phalaṃ daiti, parābhavāya phalaṃ daiti; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, attavadhāya daivadattassa lābhasak‍kārasilaukau udapādi, parābhavāya daivadattassa lābhasak‍kārasilaukau udapādi|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, naḷau attavadhāya phalaṃ daiti, parābhavāya phalaṃ daiti; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, attavadhāya daivadattassa lābhasak‍kārasilaukau udapādi, parābhavāya daivadattassa lābhasak‍kārasilaukau udapādi|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, assatarī attavadhāya gabbhaṃ gaṇhāti, parābhavāya gabbhaṃ gaṇhāti; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, attavadhāya daivadattassa lābhasak‍kārasilaukau udapādi, parābhavāya daivadattassa lābhasak‍kārasilaukau  udapādi| aivaṃ dāruṇau khau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukau| aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti|

idamavauca bhagavā| idaṃ vatvāna sugatau athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

‘‘phalaṃ vai kadaliṃ hanti, phalaṃ vaiḷuṃ phalaṃ naḷaṃ|

sak‍kārau kāpurisaṃ hanti, gabbhau assatariṃ yathāti’’|| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. pañ‍carathasatasuttaṃ

185. rājagahai  viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina daivadattassa ajātasattukumārau pañ‍cahi rathasataihi sāyaṃ pātaṃ  upaṭṭhānaṃ gacchati, pañ‍ca ca thālipākasatāni bhattābhihārau abhiharīyati| atha khau sambahulā bhikkhū yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘daivadattassa, bhantai, ajātasattukumārau pañ‍cahi rathasataihi sāyaṃ pātaṃ upaṭṭhānaṃ gacchati, pañ‍ca ca thālipākasatāni bhattābhihārau abhiharīyatī’’ti| ‘‘mā, bhikkhavai, daivadattassa lābhasak‍kārasilaukaṃ pihayittha| yāvakīvañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, daivadattassa ajātasattukumārau pañ‍cahi rathasataihi sāyaṃ pātaṃ upaṭṭhānaṃ gamissati, pañ‍ca ca thālipākasatāni bhattābhihārau āharīyissati, hāniyaiva, bhikkhavai, daivadattassa pāṭikaṅkhā kusalaisu dhammaisu, nau vuddhi’’|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, caṇḍassa kuk‍kurassa nāsāya pittaṃ bhindaiyyuṃ, aivañhi sau, bhikkhavai, kuk‍kurau bhiyyausaumattāya caṇḍatarau assa; aivamaiva, bhikkhavai, yāvakīvañ‍ca daivadattassa ajātasattukumārau pañ‍cahi rathasataihi sāyaṃ pātaṃ upaṭṭhānaṃ gamissati, pañ‍ca ca thālipākasatāni bhattābhihārau āharīyissati, hāniyaiva, bhikkhavai, daivadattassa pāṭikaṅkhā kusalaisu dhammaisu, nau vuddhi| aivaṃ dāruṇau khau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukau…pai॰… aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. mātusuttaṃ

186. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘dāruṇau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukau kaṭukau pharusau antarāyikau anuttarassa yaugakkhaimassa adhigamāya| idhāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aikac‍caṃ puggalaṃ aivaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāmi – ‘na cāyamāyasmā mātupi haitu sampajānamusā bhāsaiyyā’ti| tamainaṃ passāmi aparaina samayaina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina abhibhūtaṃ pariyādiṇṇacittaṃ sampajānamusā bhāsantaṃ| aivaṃ dāruṇau khau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukau kaṭukau pharusau antarāyikau anuttarassa yaugakkhaimassa adhigamāya| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘uppan‍naṃ lābhasak‍kārasilaukaṃ pajahissāma| na ca nau uppan‍nau lābhasak‍kārasilaukau cittaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassatī’ti| aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| sattamaṃ|

8-13. pitusuttādichak‍kaṃ

187. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati…pai॰… ‘‘dāruṇau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukau kaṭukau pharusau antarāyikau anuttarassa yaugakkhaimassa adhigamāya| idhāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aikac‍caṃ puggalaṃ aivaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāmi – ‘na cāyamāyasmā pitupi haitu…pai॰… bhātupi haitu… bhaginiyāpi haitu… puttassapi haitu… dhītuyāpi haitu… pajāpatiyāpi haitu sampajānamusā bhāsaiyyā’ti| tamainaṃ passāmi aparaina samayaina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina abhibhūtaṃ pariyādiṇṇacittaṃ sampajānamusā bhāsantaṃ| aivaṃ dāruṇau khau, bhikkhavai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukau kaṭukau pharusau antarāyikau anuttarassa yaugakkhaimassa adhigamāya| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘uppan‍naṃ  lābhasak‍kārasilaukaṃ pajahissāma, na ca nau uppan‍nau lābhasak‍kārasilaukau  cittaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassatī’ti| aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| tairasamaṃ|

catutthau vaggau|

tassuddānaṃ –

bhindi mūlaṃ duvai dhammā, pak‍kantaṃ ratha mātari|

pitā bhātā ca bhaginī, puttau dhītā pajāpatīti||

lābhasak‍kārasaṃyuttaṃ samattaṃ|



7. rāhulasaṃyuttaṃ

1. paṭhamavaggau

1. cakkhusuttaṃ

188. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau āyasmā rāhulau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā rāhulau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhu mai, bhantai, bhagavā saṃkhittaina dhammaṃ daisaitu, yamahaṃ bhagavatau dhammaṃ sutvā aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharaiyya’’nti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, rāhula, cakkhuṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ  panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘sautaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’…pai॰…| ‘‘ghānaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’…pai॰… ‘‘jivhā nic‍cā vā anic‍cā vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍cā, bhantai’’…pai॰… ‘‘kāyau nic‍cau vā anic‍cau vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍cau, bhantai’’  …pai॰… ‘‘manau nic‍cau vā anic‍cau vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍cau, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘aivaṃ passaṃ, rāhula, sutavā ariyasāvakau cakkhusmimpi nibbindati  …pai॰… sautasmimpi nibbindati… ghānasmimpi nibbindati… jivhāyapi nibbindati… kāyasmimpi nibbindati… manasmimpi nibbindati; nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati; virāgā vimuc‍cati; vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānātī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. rūpasuttaṃ

189. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati…pai॰… ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, rāhula, rūpā nic‍cā vā anic‍cā vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍cā, bhantai’’…pai॰… saddā… gandhā… rasā… phauṭṭhabbā… dhammā nic‍cā vā anic‍cā vāti? ‘‘anic‍cā, bhantai’’…pai॰… ‘‘aivaṃ passaṃ, rāhula, sutavā ariyasāvakau rūpaisupi nibbindati… saddaisupi nibbindati… gandhaisupi nibbindati… rasaisupi nibbindati… phauṭṭhabbaisupi nibbindati… dhammaisupi nibbindati; nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati…pai॰… pajānātī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. viñ‍ñāṇasuttaṃ

190. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati…pai॰… ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, rāhula, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’…pai॰… ‘‘sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’  …pai॰… ‘‘aivaṃ passaṃ, rāhula, sutavā ariyasāvakau cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇasmimpi nibbindati…pai॰… sautaviñ‍ñāṇasmimpi nibbindati… ghānaviñ‍ñāṇasmimpi nibbindati… jivhāviñ‍ñāṇasmimpi nibbindati… kāyaviñ‍ñāṇasmimpi nibbindati… manauviñ‍ñāṇasmimpi nibbindati; nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati…pai॰… pajānātī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. samphassasuttaṃ

191. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, rāhula, cakkhusamphassau nic‍cau vā anic‍cau vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍cau, bhantai’’…pai॰… ‘‘sautasamphassau…pai॰… ghānasamphassau… jivhāsamphassau… kāyasamphassau… manausamphassau nic‍cau vā anic‍cau vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍cau, bhantai’’…pai॰… ‘‘aivaṃ passaṃ, rāhula, sutavā ariyasāvakau cakkhusamphassasmimpi nibbindati…pai॰… sautasamphassasmimpi nibbindati… ghānasamphassasmimpi nibbindati… jivhāsamphassasmimpi nibbindati… kāyasamphassasmimpi nibbindati… manausamphassasmimpi nibbindati; nibbindaṃ  viraj‍jati…pai॰… pajānātī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. vaidanāsuttaṃ

192. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati…pai॰… ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, rāhula, cakkhusamphassajā  vaidanā nic‍cā vā anic‍cā vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍cā, bhantai’’…pai॰… ‘‘sautasamphassajā vaidanā…pai॰… ghānasamphassajā vaidanā… jivhāsamphassajā vaidanā… kāyasamphassajā vaidanā… manausamphassajā vaidanā nic‍cā vā anic‍cā vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍cā, bhantai’’…pai॰… ‘‘aivaṃ passaṃ, rāhula, sutavā ariyasāvakau cakkhusamphassajāya vaidanāyapi nibbindati…pai॰… sauta… ghāna… jivhā… kāya… manausamphassajāya vaidanāyapi nibbindati…pai॰… pajānātī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. sañ‍ñāsuttaṃ

193. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, rāhula, rūpasañ‍ñā nic‍cā vā anic‍cā vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍cā, bhantai’’…pai॰… ‘‘saddasañ‍ñā…pai॰… gandhasañ‍ñā… rasasañ‍ñā… phauṭṭhabbasañ‍ñā… dhammasañ‍ñā nic‍cā vā anic‍cā vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍cā, bhantai’’…pai॰… ‘‘aivaṃ passaṃ, rāhula, sutavā ariyasāvakau rūpasañ‍ñāyapi nibbindati…pai॰… saddasañ‍ñāyapi nibbindati… gandhasañ‍ñāyapi nibbindati… rasasañ‍ñāyapi nibbindati… phauṭṭhabbasañ‍ñāyapi nibbindati… dhammasañ‍ñāyapi nibbindati…pai॰… pajānātī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. sañ‍caitanāsuttaṃ

194. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati…pai॰… ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, rāhula, rūpasañ‍caitanā nic‍cā vā anic‍cā vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍cā, bhantai’’…pai॰… ‘‘saddasañ‍caitanā…pai॰… gandhasañ‍caitanā… rasasañ‍caitanā … phauṭṭhabbasañ‍caitanā… dhammasañ‍caitanā nic‍cā vā anic‍cā vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍cā, bhantai’’…pai॰… ‘‘aivaṃ passaṃ, rāhula, sutavā ariyasāvakau rūpasañ‍caitanāyapi nibbindati…pai॰… saddasañ‍caitanāyapi nibbindati… gandhasañ‍caitanāyapi nibbindati… rasasañ‍caitanāyapi nibbindati… phauṭṭhabbasañ‍caitanāyapi  nibbindati… dhammasañ‍caitanāyapi nibbindati…pai॰… pajānātī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. taṇhāsuttaṃ

195. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, rāhula, rūpataṇhā nic‍cā vā anic‍cā vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍cā, bhantai’’…pai॰… ‘‘saddataṇhā…pai॰… gandhataṇhā… rasataṇhā… phauṭṭhabbataṇhā… dhammataṇhā nic‍cā vā anic‍cā vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍cā, bhantai’’…pai॰… ‘‘aivaṃ passaṃ, rāhula, sutavā ariyasāvakau rūpataṇhāyapi nibbindati…pai॰… saddataṇhāyapi nibbindati… gandhataṇhāyapi nibbindati… rasataṇhāyapi nibbindati… phauṭṭhabbataṇhāya nibbindati… dhammataṇhāyapi nibbindati  …pai॰… pajānātī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. dhātusuttaṃ

196. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati…pai॰… ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, rāhula, pathavīdhātu nic‍cā vā anic‍cā vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍cā, bhantai’’…pai॰… ‘‘āpaudhātu…pai॰… taijaudhātu… vāyaudhātu… ākāsadhātu… viñ‍ñāṇadhātu nic‍cā vā anic‍cā vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍cā, bhantai’’…pai॰… ‘‘aivaṃ passaṃ, rāhula, sutavā ariyasāvakau pathavīdhātuyāpi nibbindati…pai॰… āpaudhātuyāpi nibbindati… taijaudhātuyāpi nibbindati… vāyaudhātuyāpi  nibbindati… ākāsadhātuyāpi nibbindati… viñ‍ñāṇadhātuyāpi nibbindati…pai॰… pajānātī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. khandhasuttaṃ

197. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, rāhula, rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’…pai॰… ‘‘vaidanā…pai॰… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’…pai॰… ‘‘aivaṃ passaṃ, rāhula, sutavā ariyasāvakau rūpasmimpi nibbindati…pai॰… vaidanāyapi nibbindati… sañ‍ñāyapi nibbindati… saṅkhāraisupi nibbindati… viñ‍ñāṇasmimpi nibbindati; nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati; virāgā vimuc‍cati; vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānātī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

paṭhamau vaggau|

tassuddānaṃ –

cakkhu rūpañ‍ca viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, samphassau vaidanāya ca|

sañ‍ñā sañ‍caitanā taṇhā, dhātu khandhaina tai dasāti||

2. dutiyavaggau

1. cakkhusuttaṃ

198. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati| atha  khau āyasmā rāhulau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ rāhulaṃ  bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, rāhula, cakkhuṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ, dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘sautaṃ…pai॰… ghānaṃ… jivhā… kāyau… manau nic‍cau vā anic‍cau vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍cau, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ, dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aivaṃ passaṃ, rāhula, sutavā ariyasāvakau cakkhusmimpi nibbindati…pai॰… sautasmimpi nibbindati… ghānasmimpi nibbindati … jivhāyapi nibbindati… kāyasmimpi nibbindati… manasmimpi nibbindati; nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati; virāgā vimuc‍cati; vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā  jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānātī’’ti| aitaina paiyyālaina dasa suttantā kātabbā| paṭhamaṃ|

2-10. rūpādisuttanavakaṃ

199. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, rāhula, rūpā  nic‍cā vā anic‍cā vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍cā, bhantai’’…pai॰… saddā… gandhā… rasā… phauṭṭhabbā… dhammā…|

‘‘cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…|

‘‘cakkhusamphassau…pai॰… sautasamphassau… ghānasamphassau… jivhāsamphassau… kāyasamphassau… manausamphassau…|

‘‘cakkhusamphassajā vaidanā…pai॰… sautasamphassajā vaidanā… ghānasamphassajā vaidanā… jivhāsamphassajā vaidanā… kāyasamphassajā vaidanā… manausamphassajā vaidanā…|

‘‘rūpasañ‍ñā…pai॰… saddasañ‍ñā… gandhasañ‍ñā… rasasañ‍ñā… phauṭṭhabbasañ‍ñā… dhammasañ‍ñā…|

‘‘rūpasañ‍caitanā…pai॰… saddasañ‍caitanā… gandhasañ‍caitanā… rasasañ‍caitanā… phauṭṭhabbasañ‍caitanā… dhammasañ‍caitanā…|

‘‘rūpataṇhā  …pai॰… saddataṇhā… gandhataṇhā… rasataṇhā… phauṭṭhabbataṇhā… dhammataṇhā…|

‘‘pathavīdhātu…pai॰… āpaudhātu… taijaudhātu… vāyaudhātu… ākāsadhātu … viñ‍ñāṇadhātu…|

‘‘rūpaṃ …pai॰… vaidanā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? anic‍caṃ, bhantai…pai॰… aivaṃ passaṃ rāhula…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātīti| dasamaṃ|

11. anusayasuttaṃ

200. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati| atha khau āyasmā rāhulau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā rāhulau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kathaṃ nu khau, bhantai, jānatau kathaṃ passatau imasmiñ‍ca saviñ‍ñāṇakai kāyai bahiddhā ca sabbanimittaisu ahaṅkāramamaṅkāramānānusayā na hauntī’’ti? ‘‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci, rāhula, rūpaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā auḷārikaṃ vā sukhumaṃ vā hīnaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā yaṃ dūrai santikai vā, sabbaṃ rūpaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passati| yā kāci vaidanā…pai॰… yā kāci sañ‍ñā… yai kaici saṅkhārā… yaṃ kiñ‍ci viñ‍ñāṇaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā auḷārikaṃ vā sukhumaṃ vā hīnaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā yaṃ dūrai santikai vā, sabbaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passati| aivaṃ khau, rāhula, jānatau aivaṃ passatau imasmiñ‍ca saviñ‍ñāṇakai kāyai bahiddhā ca sabbanimittaisu ahaṅkāramamaṅkāramānānusayā  na hauntī’’ti| aikādasamaṃ|

12. apagatasuttaṃ

201. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau āyasmā rāhulau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā rāhulau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kathaṃ nu khau, bhantai, jānatau kathaṃ passatau imasmiṃ ca saviñ‍ñāṇakai kāyai  bahiddhā ca sabbanimittaisu ahaṅkāramamaṅkāramānāpagataṃ mānasaṃ hauti vidhā samatik‍kantaṃ santaṃ suvimutta’’nti? ‘‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci, rāhula, rūpaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā auḷārikaṃ vā sukhumaṃ vā hīnaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā yaṃ dūrai santikai vā sabbaṃ rūpaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya disvā anupādā vimuttau hauti’’|

‘‘yā kāci vaidanā…pai॰… yā kāci sañ‍ñā… yai kaici saṅkhārā… yaṃ kiñ‍ci viñ‍ñāṇaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā auḷārikaṃ vā sukhumaṃ vā hīnaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā yaṃ dūrai santikai vā, sabbaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya disvā anupādā vimuttau hauti| aivaṃ khau, rāhula, jānatau aivaṃ passatau imasmiñ‍ca saviñ‍ñāṇakai kāyai bahiddhā ca sabbanimittaisu ahaṅkāramamaṅkāramānāpagataṃ mānasaṃ hauti  vidhā samatik‍kantaṃ santaṃ suvimutta’’nti| dvādasamaṃ|

dutiyau vaggau|

tassuddānaṃ –

cakkhu  rūpañ‍ca viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, samphassau vaidanāya ca|

sañ‍ñā sañ‍caitanā taṇhā, dhātu khandhaina tai dasa|

anusayaṃ apagatañ‍caiva, vaggau taina pavuc‍catīti||

rāhulasaṃyuttaṃ samattaṃ|



8. lakkhaṇasaṃyuttaṃ

1. paṭhamavaggau

1. aṭṭhisuttaṃ

202. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā ca lakkhaṇau āyasmā ca mahāmauggal‍lānau mahāmauggalānau (ka॰) gijjhakūṭai pabbatai viharanti| atha khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaināyasmā lakkhaṇau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ lakkhaṇaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘āyāmāvusau aihi āvusau (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) lakkhaṇa, rājagahaṃ piṇḍāya pavisissāmā’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau āyasmā lakkhaṇau āyasmatau mahāmauggal‍lānassa pac‍cassausi| atha khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau gijjhakūṭā pabbatā aurauhantau añ‍ñatarasmiṃ padaisai sitaṃ pātvākāsi| atha khau āyasmā lakkhaṇau āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, āvusau mauggal‍lāna, haitu kau pac‍cayau sitassa pātukammāyā’’ti? ‘‘akālau khau, āvusau lakkhaṇa, aitassa pañhassa| bhagavatau maṃ santikai aitaṃ pañhaṃ pucchā’’ti|

atha  khau āyasmā ca lakkhaṇau āyasmā ca mahāmauggal‍lānau  rājagahai piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā lakkhaṇau āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idhāyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau gijjhakūṭā pabbatā aurauhantau añ‍ñatarasmiṃ padaisai sitaṃ pātvākāsi| kau nu khau, āvusau mauggal‍lāna, haitu kau pac‍cayau sitassa pātukammāyā’’ti?

‘‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau, gijjhakūṭā pabbatā aurauhantau addasaṃ aṭṭhikasaṅkhalikaṃ vaihāsaṃ gacchantiṃ| tamainaṃ gijjhāpi kākāpi kulalāpi anupatitvā  anupatitvā phāsuḷantarikāhi vitudainti vitacchainti virājainti vitudainti (sī॰), vitacchainti vibhajainti (pī॰ ka॰)| sā sudaṃ aṭṭassaraṃ karauti| tassa mayhaṃ, āvusau, aitadahausi – ‘acchariyaṃ vata, bhau, abbhutaṃ vata, bhau! aivarūpaupi nāma sattau bhavissati! aivarūpaupi nāma yakkhau bhavissati! aivarūpaupi nāma attabhāvapaṭilābhau bhavissatī’’’ti!!

atha khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘cakkhubhūtā vata, bhikkhavai, sāvakā viharanti; ñāṇabhūtā vata, bhikkhavai, sāvakā viharanti, yatra hi nāma sāvakau aivarūpaṃ ñassati vā dakkhati vā sakkhiṃ vā karissati| pubbaiva mai sau, bhikkhavai, sattau diṭṭhau ahausi, api cāhaṃ na byākāsiṃ| ahañ‍caitaṃ ahamaivaitaṃ (sī॰) byākaraiyyaṃ, parai ca mai parai mai (sī॰) na saddahaiyyuṃ| yai mai na saddahaiyyuṃ, taisaṃ taṃ assa dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya| aisau, bhikkhavai, sattau imasmiṃyaiva rājagahai gaughātakau  ahausi| sau tassa kammassa vipākaina bahūni vassāni bahūni vassasatāni bahūni vassasahassāni bahūni vassasatasahassāni nirayai pac‍citvā tassaiva kammassa vipākāvasaisaina  aivarūpaṃ attabhāvapaṭilābhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidayatī’’ti| (sabbaisaṃ suttantānaṃ aisaiva paiyyālau)| paṭhamaṃ|

2. paisisuttaṃ

203. ‘‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau, gijjhakūṭā pabbatā aurauhantau addasaṃ maṃsapaisiṃ vaihāsaṃ gacchantiṃ| tamainaṃ gijjhāpi kākāpi kulalāpi anupatitvā anupatitvā vitacchainti virājainti virājainti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰), vibhajainti (pī॰ ka॰)| sā sudaṃ aṭṭassaraṃ karauti…pai॰… aisau, bhikkhavai, sattau imasmiṃyaiva rājagahai gaughātakau ahausi…pai॰…| dutiyaṃ|

3. piṇḍasuttaṃ

204. ‘‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau, gijjhakūṭā pabbatā aurauhantau addasaṃ maṃsapiṇḍaṃ vaihāsaṃ gacchantaṃ| tamainaṃ gijjhāpi kākāpi kulalāpi anupatitvā anupatitvā vitacchainti virājainti| sā sudaṃ aṭṭassaraṃ karauti…pai॰… aisau, bhikkhavai, sattau imasmiṃyaiva rājagahai sākuṇikau ahausi…pai॰…| tatiyaṃ|

4. nicchavisuttaṃ

205. ‘‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau, gijjhakūṭā pabbatā aurauhantau addasaṃ nicchaviṃ purisaṃ vaihāsaṃ gacchantaṃ| tamainaṃ gijjhāpi kākāpi kulalāpi anupatitvā anupatitvā vitacchainti virājainti| sau sudaṃ aṭṭassaraṃ karauti…pai॰… aisau, bhikkhavai, sattau imasmiṃyaiva rājagahai aurabbhikau ahausi…pai॰…| catutthaṃ|

5. asilaumasuttaṃ

206. ‘‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau, gijjhakūṭā pabbatā aurauhantau addasaṃ asilaumaṃ purisaṃ vaihāsaṃ gacchantaṃ| tassa tai asī uppatitvā uppatitvā tassaiva kāyai nipatanti| sau sudaṃ aṭṭassaraṃ karauti…pai॰… aisau, bhikkhavai, sattau imasmiṃyaiva rājagahai sūkarikau ahausi…pai॰…| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. sattisuttaṃ

207. ‘‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau, gijjhakūṭā pabbatā aurauhantau addasaṃ sattilaumaṃ purisaṃ vaihāsaṃ gacchantaṃ| tassa tā sattiyau uppatitvā uppatitvā tassaiva kāyai nipatanti| sau sudaṃ aṭṭassaraṃ karauti…pai॰… aisau, bhikkhavai, sattau imasmiṃyaiva rājagahai māgavikau ahausi…pai॰…| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. usulaumasuttaṃ

208. ‘‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau, gijjhakūṭā pabbatā aurauhantau addasaṃ usulaumaṃ purisaṃ vaihāsaṃ gacchantaṃ| tassa tai usū uppatitvā uppatitvā tassaiva kāyai nipatanti| sau sudaṃ aṭṭassaraṃ  karauti…pai॰… aisau, bhikkhavai, sattau imasmiṃyaiva rājagahai kāraṇikau ahausi…pai॰…| sattamaṃ|

8. sūcilaumasuttaṃ

209. ‘‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau, gijjhakūṭā pabbatā aurauhantau addasaṃ sūcilaumaṃ purisaṃ vaihāsaṃ gacchantaṃ| tassa tā sūciyau uppatitvā uppatitvā tassaiva kāyai nipatanti| sau sudaṃ aṭṭassaraṃ karauti…pai॰… aisau, bhikkhavai, sattau imasmiṃyaiva rājagahai sūtau sārathikau (ka॰ vinayaipi) ahausi…pai॰…| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. dutiyasūcilaumasuttaṃ

210. ‘‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau, gijjhakūṭā pabbatā aurauhantau addasaṃ sūcilaumaṃ purisaṃ vaihāsaṃ gacchantaṃ| tassa  tā sūciyau sīsai pavisitvā mukhatau nikkhamanti; mukhai pavisitvā uratau nikkhamanti; urai pavisitvā udaratau nikkhamanti; udarai pavisitvā ūrūhi nikkhamanti; ūrūsu pavisitvā  jaṅghāhi nikkhamanti; jaṅghāsu pavisitvā pādaihi nikkhamanti; sau sudaṃ aṭṭassaraṃ karauti…pai॰… aisau, bhikkhavai, sattau imasmiṃyaiva rājagahai sūcakau ahausi…pai॰…| navamaṃ|

10. kumbhaṇḍasuttaṃ

211. ‘‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau, gijjhakūṭā pabbatā aurauhantau addasaṃ kumbhaṇḍaṃ purisaṃ vaihāsaṃ gacchantaṃ| sau gacchantaupi taiva aṇḍai khandhai āraupaitvā gacchati| nisīdantaupi taisvaiva aṇḍaisu nisīdati| tamainaṃ gijjhāpi kākāpi kulalāpi anupatitvā anupatitvā vitacchainti virājainti| sau sudaṃ aṭṭassaraṃ karauti…pai॰… aisau, bhikkhavai, sattau imasmiṃyaiva rājagahai gāmakūṭakau ahausi…pai॰…| dasamaṃ|

paṭhamau vaggau|

tassuddānaṃ  –

aṭṭhi paisi ubhau gāvaghātakā,

piṇḍau sākuṇiyau nicchavaurabbhi|

asi sūkarikau sattimāgavi,

usu kāraṇikau sūci sārathi|

yau ca sibbiyati sūcakau hi sau,

aṇḍabhāri ahu gāmakūṭakauti||

2. dutiyavaggau

1. sasīsakasuttaṃ

212. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ rājagahai vaiḷuvanai| ‘‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau, gijjhakūṭā pabbatā aurauhantau addasaṃ purisaṃ gūthakūpai sasīsakaṃ nimuggaṃ…pai॰… aisau, bhikkhavai, sattau imasmiṃyaiva rājagahai pāradārikau ahausi…pai॰…| paṭhamaṃ|

2. gūthakhādasuttaṃ

213. ‘‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau, gijjhakūṭā pabbatā aurauhantau addasaṃ purisaṃ gūthakūpai nimuggaṃ ubhauhi hatthaihi gūthaṃ khādantaṃ…pai॰… aisau, bhikkhavai, sattau imasmiṃyaiva rājagahai duṭṭhabrāhmaṇau ahausi| sau kassapassa sammāsambuddhassa pāvacanai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ bhattaina nimantaitvā dauṇiyau dauṇiyā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) gūthassa pūrāpaitvā aitadavauca – ahau bhauntau, yāvadatthaṃ bhuñ‍jantu caiva harantu cāti…pai॰…| dutiyaṃ|

3. nicchavitthisuttaṃ

214. ‘‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau, gijjhakūṭā pabbatā aurauhantau addasaṃ nicchaviṃ itthiṃ vaihāsaṃ gacchantiṃ| tamainaṃ gijjhāpi kākāpi kulalāpi anupatitvā anupatitvā vitacchainti virājainti| sā sudaṃ aṭṭassaraṃ karauti…pai॰… aisā, bhikkhavai, itthī imasmiṃyaiva rājagahai aticārinī ahausi…pai॰…| tatiyaṃ|

4. maṅgulitthisuttaṃ

215. ‘‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau, gijjhakūṭā pabbatā aurauhantau addasaṃ itthiṃ duggandhaṃ maṅguliṃ vaihāsaṃ gacchantiṃ| tamainaṃ gijjhāpi kākāpi kulalāpi  anupatitvā anupatitvā vitacchainti virājainti| sā sudaṃ aṭṭassaraṃ karauti…pai॰… aisā, bhikkhavai, itthī imasmiṃyaiva rājagahai ikkhaṇikā ahausi…pai॰…| catutthaṃ|

5. aukilinīsuttaṃ

216. ‘‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau, gijjhakūṭā pabbatā aurauhantau addasaṃ itthiṃ uppak‍kaṃ aukiliniṃ aukiriniṃ vaihāsaṃ gacchantiṃ| sā sudaṃ aṭṭassaraṃ karauti…pai॰… aisā, bhikkhavai, itthī kaliṅgassa rañ‍ñau aggamahaisī ahausi| sā issāpakatā sapattiṃ aṅgārakaṭāhaina aukiri…pai॰…| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. asīsakasuttaṃ

217. ‘‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau, gijjhakūṭā pabbatā aurauhantau addasaṃ asīsakaṃ kabandhaṃ kavandhaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) vaihāsaṃ gacchantaṃ| tassa urai akkhīni caiva haunti mukhañ‍ca| tamainaṃ gijjhāpi kākāpi kulalāpi anupatitvā anupatitvā vitacchainti virājainti| sau sudaṃ aṭṭassaraṃ karauti…pai॰… aisau, bhikkhavai, sattau imasmiṃyaiva rājagahai hārikau nāma cauraghātakau ahausi…pai॰…| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. pāpabhikkhusuttaṃ

218. ‘‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau, gijjhakūṭā pabbatā aurauhantau addasaṃ bhikkhuṃ vaihāsaṃ gacchantaṃ| tassa saṅghāṭipi ādittā sampaj‍jalitā sajautibhūtā sañ‍jautibhūtā (syā॰ kaṃ॰), pattaupi ādittau sampaj‍jalitau sajautibhūtau, kāyabandhanampi  ādittaṃ sampaj‍jalitaṃ sajautibhūtaṃ, kāyaupi ādittau sampaj‍jalitau sajautibhūtau| sau sudaṃ aṭṭassaraṃ karauti…pai॰… aisau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kassapassa sammāsambuddhassa pāvacanai pāpabhikkhu ahausi…pai॰…| sattamaṃ|

8. pāpabhikkhunīsuttaṃ

219. ‘‘addasaṃ  bhikkhuniṃ vaihāsaṃ gacchantiṃ| tassā saṅghāṭipi ādittā…pai॰… pāpabhikkhunī ahausi…pai॰…| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. pāpasikkhamānasuttaṃ

220. ‘‘addasaṃ  sikkhamānaṃ vaihāsaṃ gacchantiṃ| tassā saṅghāṭipi ādittā…pai॰… pāpasikkhamānā ahausi…pai॰…| navamaṃ|

10. pāpasāmaṇairasuttaṃ

221. ‘‘addasaṃ sāmaṇairaṃ vaihāsaṃ gacchantaṃ| tassa saṅghāṭipi ādittā…pai॰… pāpasāmaṇairau ahausi…pai॰…| dasamaṃ|

11. pāpasāmaṇairīsuttaṃ

222. ‘‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau, gijjhakūṭā pabbatā aurauhantau addasaṃ sāmaṇairiṃ vaihāsaṃ gacchantiṃ| tassā saṅghāṭipi ādittā sampaj‍jalitā sajautibhūtā, pattaupi ādittau sampaj‍jalitau  sajautibhūtau, kāyabandhanampi ādittaṃ sampaj‍jalitaṃ sajautibhūtaṃ, kāyaupi ādittau sampaj‍jalitau sajautibhūtau| sā sudaṃ aṭṭassaraṃ karauti| tassa mayhaṃ, āvusau, aitadahausi – ‘acchariyaṃ vata, bhau, abbhutaṃ vata, bhau! aivarūpaupi nāma sattau bhavissati! aivarūpaupi nāma yakkhau bhavissati! aivarūpaupi nāma attabhāvapaṭilābhau bhavissatī’’’ti!!

atha khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘cakkhubhūtā vata, bhikkhavai, sāvakā viharanti; ñāṇabhūtā vata, bhikkhavai, sāvakā viharanti, yatra hi nāma sāvakau aivarūpaṃ ñassati vā dakkhati vā sakkhiṃ vā karissati| pubbaiva  mai sā, bhikkhavai, sāmaṇairī diṭṭhā ahausi| api cāhaṃ na byākāsiṃ| ahañ‍caitaṃ byākaraiyyaṃ, parai ca mai na saddahaiyyuṃ| yai mai na saddahaiyyuṃ, taisaṃ taṃ assa dīgharattaṃ ahitāya  dukkhāya| aisā, bhikkhavai, sāmaṇairī kassapassa sammāsambuddhassa pāvacanai pāpasāmaṇairī ahausi| sā tassa kammassa vipākaina bahūni vassāni bahūni vassasatāni bahūni vassasahassāni bahūni vassasatasahassāni nirayai pac‍citvā tassaiva kammassa vipākāvasaisaina aivarūpaṃ attabhāvapaṭilābhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidayatī’’ti| aikādasamaṃ|

dutiyau vaggau|

tassuddānaṃ –

kūpai  nimuggau hi sau pāradārikau|

gūthakhādi ahu duṭṭhabrāhmaṇau||

nicchavitthi aticārinī ahu|

maṅgulitthi ahu ikkhaṇitthikā||

aukilini sapattaṅgāraukiri|

sīsacchin‍nau ahu cauraghātakau||

bhikkhu  bhikkhunī sikkhamānā|

sāmaṇairau atha sāmaṇairikā||

kassapassa vinayasmiṃ pabbaj‍jaṃ|

pāpakammaṃ kariṃsu tāvadaiti||

lakkhaṇasaṃyuttaṃ samattaṃ|



9. aupammasaṃyuttaṃ

1. kūṭasuttaṃ

223. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra  khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, kūṭāgārassa yā kāci gaupānasiyau sabbā tā kūṭaṅgamā kūṭasamausaraṇā kūṭasamugghātā sabbā tā samugghātaṃ gacchanti; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yai kaici akusalā dhammā sabbai tai avij‍jāmūlakā avij‍jāsamausaraṇā avij‍jāsamugghātā, sabbai tai samugghātaṃ gacchanti| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘appamattā viharissāmā’ti| aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. nakhasikhasuttaṃ

224. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati| atha khau bhagavā parittaṃ nakhasikhāyaṃ paṃsuṃ āraupaitvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha bhikkhavai, katamaṃ nu khau bahutaraṃ, yau vāyaṃ yau cāyaṃ (bahūsu) mayā parittau nakhasikhāyaṃ paṃsu āraupitau ayaṃ vā yā cāyaṃ (syā॰ ka॰) mahāpathavī’’ti? ‘‘aitadaiva, bhantai, bahutaraṃ yadidaṃ mahāpathavī| appamattakauyaṃ bhagavatā parittau nakhasikhāyaṃ paṃsu āraupitau| saṅkhampi na upaiti upanidhimpi na upaiti kalabhāgampi na upaiti mahāpathaviṃ upanidhāya bhagavatā parittau nakhasikhāyaṃ paṃsu āraupitau’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, appakā tai sattā yai manussaisu pac‍cājāyanti; atha khau aitaiyaiva bahutarā sattā yai añ‍ñatra manussaihi pac‍cājāyanti| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ  – ‘appamattā viharissāmā’ti| aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| dutiyaṃ|

3. kulasuttaṃ

225. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati…pai॰… ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yāni kānici kulāni bahutthikāni appapurisāni tāni suppadhaṃsiyāni haunti cauraihi kumbhatthainakaihi; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yassa kassaci bhikkhunau maittācaitauvimutti abhāvitā abahulīkatā sau suppadhaṃsiyau hauti amanussaihi| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yāni kānici kulāni appitthikāni bahupurisāni tāni duppadhaṃsiyāni haunti cauraihi kumbhatthainakaihi, aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yassa kassaci bhikkhunau maittācaitauvimutti bhāvitā bahulīkatā sau duppadhaṃsiyau hauti amanussaihi| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘maittā nau caitauvimutti bhāvitā bhavissati bahulīkatā yānīkatā vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā’ti| aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| tatiyaṃ|

4. aukkhāsuttaṃ

226. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘yau, bhikkhavai, pubbaṇhasamayaṃ aukkhāsataṃ dānaṃ dadaiyya, yau majjhanhikasamayaṃ aukkhāsataṃ dānaṃ dadaiyya, yau sāyanhasamayaṃ aukkhāsataṃ dānaṃ dadaiyya, yau vā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ antamasau gadduhanamattampi maittacittaṃ bhāvaiyya, yau vā majjhanhikasamayaṃ antamasau gadduhanamattampi maittacittaṃ bhāvaiyya, yau  vā sāyanhasamayaṃ antamasau gadduhanamattampi maittacittaṃ bhāvaiyya, idaṃ tatau mahapphalataraṃ| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘maittā nau caitauvimutti bhāvitā bhavissati bahulīkatā yānīkatā vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā’ti| aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| catutthaṃ|

5. sattisuttaṃ

227. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati…pai॰… ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, satti tiṇhaphalā| atha purisau āgacchaiyya – ‘ahaṃ imaṃ sattiṃ tiṇhaphalaṃ pāṇinā vā muṭṭhinā vā paṭilaiṇissāmi paṭikauṭṭissāmi paṭivaṭṭaissāmī’ti| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, bhabbau nu khau sau purisau amuṃ sattiṃ tiṇhaphalaṃ pāṇinā vā muṭṭhinā vā paṭilaiṇaituṃ paṭikauṭṭaituṃ paṭivaṭṭaitu’’nti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kissa haitu’’? ‘‘asu hi, bhantai, satti tiṇhaphalā na sukarā pāṇinā vā muṭṭhinā vā paṭilaiṇaituṃ paṭikauṭṭaituṃ paṭivaṭṭaituṃ| yāvadaiva ca pana sau purisau kilamathassa vighātassa bhāgī assā’’ti|

‘‘aivamaiva  khau, bhikkhavai, yassa kassaci bhikkhunau maittācaitauvimutti bhāvitā bahulīkatā yānīkatā vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā, tassa cai amanussau cittaṃ khipitabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyya; atha khau svaiva amanussau kilamathassa vighātassa bhāgī assa| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘maittā nau caitauvimutti bhāvitā bhavissati bahulīkatā yānīkatā vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā  paricitā susamāraddhā’ti| aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dhanuggahasuttaṃ

228. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, cattārau daḷhadhammā dhanuggahā  susikkhitā katahatthā katūpāsanā catuddisā ṭhitā assu| atha purisau āgacchaiyya – ‘ahaṃ imaisaṃ catun‍naṃ daḷhadhammānaṃ dhanuggahānaṃ susikkhitānaṃ katahatthānaṃ katūpāsanānaṃ catuddisā kaṇḍai khittai appatiṭṭhitai pathaviyaṃ gahaitvā āharissāmī’ti| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, ‘javanau purisau paramaina javaina saman‍nāgatau’ti alaṃ vacanāyā’’ti?

‘‘aikassa caipi, bhantai, daḷhadhammassa dhanuggahassa susikkhitassa katahatthassa katūpāsanassa kaṇḍaṃ khittaṃ appatiṭṭhitaṃ pathaviyaṃ gahaitvā āharaiyya – ‘javanau purisau paramaina javaina saman‍nāgatau’ti alaṃ vacanāya, kau pana vādau catun‍naṃ daḷhadhammānaṃ dhanuggahānaṃ susikkhitānaṃ katahatthānaṃ katūpāsanāna’’nti?

‘‘yathā ca, bhikkhavai, tassa purisassa javau, yathā ca candimasūriyānaṃ javau, tatau sīghatarau| yathā ca, bhikkhavai, tassa purisassa javau yathā ca candimasūriyānaṃ javau yathā ca yā daivatā candimasūriyānaṃ puratau dhāvanti tāsaṃ daivatānaṃ javau, ( ) (tatau sīghatarau| yathā ca bhikkhavai tassa purisassa javau, yathā ca candimasuriyānaṃ javau, yathā ca yā daivatā candimasuriyānaṃ puratau dhāvanti, tāsaṃ daivatānaṃ javau,) (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) tatau sīghataraṃ āyusaṅkhārā  khiyanti| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘appamattā viharissāmā’ti| aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. āṇisuttaṃ

229. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati…pai॰… ‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, dasārahānaṃ ānakau āṇakau (sī॰) nāma mudiṅgau ahausi| tassa dasārahā ānakai ghaṭitai añ‍ñaṃ āṇiṃ audahiṃsu| ahu  khau sau, bhikkhavai, samayau yaṃ ānakassa mudiṅgassa paurāṇaṃ paukkharaphalakaṃ antaradhāyi| āṇisaṅghāṭauva avasissi| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhavissanti bhikkhū anāgatamaddhānaṃ, yai tai suttantā tathāgatabhāsitā gambhīrā gambhīratthā laukuttarā suñ‍ñatappaṭisaṃyuttā, taisu bhañ‍ñamānaisu na sussūsissanti na sautaṃ audahissanti na añ‍ñā cittaṃ upaṭṭhāpaissanti na ca tai dhammai uggahaitabbaṃ pariyāpuṇitabbaṃ mañ‍ñissanti’’|

‘‘yai pana tai suttantā kavikatā kāvaiyyā cittakkharā cittabyañ‍janā bāhirakā sāvakabhāsitā, taisu bhañ‍ñamānaisu sussūsissanti, sautaṃ audahissanti, añ‍ñā cittaṃ upaṭṭhāpaissanti, tai ca dhammai uggahaitabbaṃ pariyāpuṇitabbaṃ mañ‍ñissanti| aivamaitaisaṃ, bhikkhavai, suttantānaṃ tathāgatabhāsitānaṃ gambhīrānaṃ gambhīratthānaṃ laukuttarānaṃ suñ‍ñatappaṭisaṃyuttānaṃ antaradhānaṃ bhavissati| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘yai tai suttantā tathāgatabhāsitā gambhīrā gambhīratthā laukuttarā suñ‍ñatappaṭisaṃyuttā, taisu bhañ‍ñamānaisu sussūsissāma, sautaṃ audahissāma, añ‍ñā cittaṃ upaṭṭhāpaissāma, tai ca dhammai uggahaitabbaṃ pariyāpuṇitabbaṃ mañ‍ñissāmā’ti| aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| sattamaṃ|

8. kaliṅgarasuttaṃ
