230. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘kaliṅgarūpadhānā, bhikkhavai, aitarahi licchavī viharanti  appamattā ātāpinau upāsanasmiṃ| taisaṃ rājā māgadhau ajātasattu vaidaihiputtau na labhati autāraṃ na labhati ārammaṇaṃ| bhavissanti, bhikkhavai, anāgatamaddhānaṃ licchavī sukhumālā sukumālā (sī॰), sukhumā (ka॰) mudutalunahatthapādā mudutalāhatthapādā (syā॰ kaṃ॰) tai mudukāsu saiyyāsu tūlabimbauhanāsu tūlabimbauhanāsu (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), tūlabimbauhanādīsu (sī॰), tūlabibbauhanādīsu (ka॰) yāvasūriyuggamanā saiyyaṃ kappissanti| taisaṃ rājā māgadhau ajātasattu vaidaihiputtau lacchati autāraṃ lacchati ārammaṇaṃ|

‘‘kaliṅgarūpadhānā, bhikkhavai, aitarahi bhikkhū viharanti appamattā ātāpinau padhānasmiṃ| taisaṃ mārau pāpimā na labhati autāraṃ na labhati ārammaṇaṃ| bhavissanti, bhikkhavai, anāgatamaddhānaṃ bhikkhū sukhumā mudutalunahatthapādā| tai mudukāsu saiyyāsu tūlabimbauhanāsu yāvasūriyuggamanā saiyyaṃ kappissanti| taisaṃ mārau pāpimā lacchati autāraṃ lacchati ārammaṇaṃ| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ  sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘kaliṅgarūpadhānā viharissāma appamattā ātāpinau padhānasmi’nti| aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. nāgasuttaṃ

231. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau navau bhikkhu ativailaṃ kulāni upasaṅkamati| tamainaṃ bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘māyasmā ativailaṃ kulāni upasaṅkamī’’ti| sau bhikkhu bhikkhūhi vuc‍camānau aivamāha – ‘‘imai hi nāma thairā bhikkhū kulāni upasaṅkamitabbaṃ mañ‍ñissanti, kimaṅgaṃ kimaṅga (sī॰) panāha’’nti?

atha khau sambahulā bhikkhū yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ  nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘idha, bhantai, añ‍ñatarau navau bhikkhu ativailaṃ kulāni upasaṅkamati| tamainaṃ bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘māyasmā ativailaṃ kulāni upasaṅkamī’ti| sau bhikkhu bhikkhūhi vuc‍camānau aivamāha – ‘imai hi nāma thairā bhikkhū kulāni upasaṅkamitabbaṃ mañ‍ñissanti, kimaṅgaṃ panāha’’’nti|

‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, arañ‍ñāyatanai mahāsarasī| taṃ nāgā upanissāya viharanti| tai taṃ sarasiṃ augāhaitvā sauṇḍāya bhisamuḷālaṃ abbuhaitvā abbhūhaitvā (ka॰), abbāhitvā (mahāva॰ 278) suvikkhālitaṃ vikkhālaitvā akaddamaṃ saṅkhāditvā saṅkharitvā (pī॰ ka॰) ajjhauharanti| taisaṃ taṃ vaṇṇāya caiva hauti balāya ca, na ca tataunidānaṃ maraṇaṃ vā nigacchanti maraṇamattaṃ vā dukkhaṃ| taisaṃyaiva  khau pana, bhikkhavai, mahānāgānaṃ anusikkhamānā taruṇā bhiṅkacchāpā taṃ sarasiṃ augāhaitvā sauṇḍāya bhisamuḷālaṃ abbuhaitvā na suvikkhālitaṃ vikkhālaitvā sakaddamaṃ asaṅkhāditvā ajjhauharanti| taisaṃ taṃ naiva vaṇṇāya hauti na balāya| tataunidānaṃ maraṇaṃ vā nigacchanti maraṇamattaṃ vā dukkhaṃ|

‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, idha thairā bhikkhū pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya pavisanti| tai tattha dhammaṃ bhāsanti| taisaṃ gihī pasan‍nākāraṃ karaunti| tai taṃ lābhaṃ agadhitā amucchitā anajjhaupan‍nā anajjhāpan‍nā (sabbattha) ma॰ ni॰ 1 pāsarāsisuttavaṇṇanā aulaukaitabbā ādīnavadassāvinau nissaraṇapañ‍ñā paribhuñ‍janti| taisaṃ taṃ vaṇṇāya caiva hauti balāya ca, na ca tataunidānaṃ maraṇaṃ vā nigacchanti maraṇamattaṃ vā dukkhaṃ| taisaṃyaiva khau pana, bhikkhavai, thairānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ anusikkhamānā navā bhikkhū pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya pavisanti| tai tattha dhammaṃ bhāsanti| taisaṃ gihī pasan‍nākāraṃ  karaunti| tai taṃ lābhaṃ gadhitā mucchitā ajjhaupan‍nā anādīnavadassāvinau anissaraṇapañ‍ñā paribhuñ‍janti| taisaṃ taṃ naiva vaṇṇāya hauti na balāya, tai tataunidānaṃ maraṇaṃ vā nigacchanti maraṇamattaṃ vā dukkhaṃ| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘agadhitā amucchitā anajjhaupan‍nā ādīnavadassāvinau nissaraṇapañ‍ñā taṃ lābhaṃ paribhuñ‍jissāmā’ti| aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| navamaṃ|

10. biḷārasuttaṃ

232. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu ativailaṃ kulaisu cārittaṃ āpaj‍jati| tamainaṃ bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘māyasmā ativailaṃ kulaisu cārittaṃ āpaj‍jī’’ti| sau bhikkhu bhikkhūhi vuc‍camānau na viramati| atha khau sambahulā bhikkhū yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘idha, bhantai, añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu ativailaṃ  kulaisu cārittaṃ āpaj‍jati| tamainaṃ bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘māyasmā ativailaṃ kulaisu cārittaṃ āpaj‍jī’ti| sau bhikkhu bhikkhūhi vuc‍camānau na viramatī’’ti|

‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, biḷārau sandhisamalasaṅkaṭīrai ṭhitau ahausi mudumūsiṃ maggayamānau – ‘yadāyaṃ mudumūsi gaucarāya pak‍kamissati, tatthaiva naṃ gahaitvā khādissāmī’ti| atha khau sau, bhikkhavai, mudumūsi gaucarāya pak‍kāmi| tamainaṃ biḷārau gahaitvā sahasā saṅkhāditvā saṅkharitvā (pī॰ ka॰), maṃsaṃ khāditvā (syā॰ kaṃ॰), asaṃkhāditvā (katthaci) ajjhauhari| tassa sau mudumūsi antampi khādi, antaguṇampi khādi| sau tataunidānaṃ  maraṇampi nigacchi maraṇamattampi dukkhaṃ|

‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau bhikkhu pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya pavisati arakkhitainaiva kāyaina arakkhitāya vācāya arakkhitaina cittaina, anupaṭṭhitāya  satiyā, asaṃvutaihi indriyaihi| sau tattha passati mātugāmaṃ dun‍nivatthaṃ vā duppārutaṃ vā| tassa mātugāmaṃ disvā dun‍nivatthaṃ vā duppārutaṃ vā rāgau cittaṃ anuddhaṃsaiti| sau rāgānuddhaṃsaina cittaina maraṇaṃ vā nigacchati maraṇamattaṃ vā dukkhaṃ| maraṇañhaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyassa vinayai yau sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattati| maraṇamattañhaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ yadidaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ saṃkiliṭṭhaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| yathārūpāya āpattiyā vuṭṭhānaṃ pañ‍ñāyati| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘rakkhitainaiva kāyaina rakkhitāya vācāya rakkhitaina cittaina, upaṭṭhitāya satiyā, saṃvutaihi indriyaihi gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya pavisissāmā’ti| aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| dasamaṃ|

11. siṅgālasuttaṃ

233. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘assuttha nau tumhai, bhikkhavai, rattiyā pac‍cūsasamayaṃ jarasiṅgālassa vassamānassā’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aisau khau, bhikkhavai, jarasiṅgālau uk‍kaṇḍakaina nāma raugajātaina phuṭṭhau| sau yaina  yaina icchati taina taina gacchati; yattha yattha icchati tattha tattha tiṭṭhati; yattha yattha icchati tattha tattha nisīdati; yattha yattha icchati  tattha tattha nipaj‍jati; sītakaupi naṃ vātau upavāyati| sādhu khvassa, bhikkhavai, yaṃ idhaikac‍cau sakyaputtiyapaṭiñ‍ñau aivarūpampi attabhāvapaṭilābhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidiyaitha| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ  – ‘appamattā viharissāmā’ti| aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| aikādasamaṃ|

12. dutiyasiṅgālasuttaṃ

234. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… ‘‘assuttha nau tumhai, bhikkhavai, rattiyā pac‍cūsasamayaṃ jarasiṅgālassa vassamānassā’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘siyā khau, bhikkhavai, tasmiṃ jarasiṅgālai yā kāci katañ‍ñutā katavaiditā, na tvaiva idhaikac‍cai sakyaputtiyapaṭiñ‍ñai siyā yā kāci katañ‍ñutā katavaiditā| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘katañ‍ñunau bhavissāma katavaidinau; na ca nau na ca nauti idaṃ sī॰ pī॰ pautthakaisu natthi amhaisu appakampi kataṃ nassissatī’ti mā nassissatīti (sī॰ pī॰), vinassissatīti (syā॰ kaṃ॰)| aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| dvādasamaṃ|

aupammasaṃyuttaṃ samattaṃ|


tassuddānaṃ –

kūṭaṃ nakhasikhaṃ kulaṃ, aukkhā satti dhanuggahau|

āṇi kaliṅgarau nāgau, biḷārau dvai siṅgālakāti||


10. bhikkhusaṃyuttaṃ

1. kaulitasuttaṃ

235. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘āvusau bhikkhavai’’ti| ‘‘āvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau mahāmauggal‍lānassa pac‍cassausuṃ|

āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau aitadavauca – ‘‘idha mayhaṃ, āvusau, rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘ariyau tuṇhībhāvau, ariyau tuṇhībhāvauti vuc‍cati| katamau nu khau ariyau tuṇhībhāvau’ti? tassa mayhaṃ āvusau, aitadahausi – ‘idha bhikkhu vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati ariyau tuṇhībhāvau’ti| sau khvāhaṃ, āvusau, vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja vihariṃ| tassa mayhaṃ, āvusau, iminā vihāraina viharatau vitak‍kasahagatā sañ‍ñā manasikārā samudācaranti’’|

‘‘atha khau maṃ, āvusau, bhagavā iddhiyā upasaṅkamitvā aitadavauca – ‘mauggal‍lāna, mauggal‍lāna, mā, brāhmaṇa, ariyaṃ tuṇhībhāvaṃ pamādau, ariyai tuṇhībhāvai cittaṃ saṇṭhapaihi, ariyai tuṇhībhāvai cittaṃ aikaudibhāvaṃ karauhi, ariyai tuṇhībhāvai cittaṃ  samādahā’ti| sau khvāhaṃ, āvusau, aparaina samayaina vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| yañhi  taṃ, āvusau, sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘satthārā anuggahitau sāvakau mahābhiñ‍ñataṃ pattau’ti, mamaṃ taṃ sammā vadamānau vadaiyya – ‘satthārā anuggahitau sāvakau mahābhiñ‍ñataṃ pattau’’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. upatissasuttaṃ

236. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati| tatra khau āyasmā sāriputtau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘āvusau bhikkhavai’’ti| ‘‘āvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau sāriputtassa pac‍cassausuṃ| āyasmā sāriputtau aitadavauca –

‘‘idha mayhaṃ, āvusau, rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘atthi nu khau taṃ kiñ‍ci laukasmiṃ yassa mai vipariṇāmañ‍ñathābhāvā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā’ti? tassa mayhaṃ, āvusau, aitadahausi – ‘natthi khau taṃ kiñ‍ci laukasmiṃ yassa mai vipariṇāmañ‍ñathābhāvā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā’’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai, āyasmā ānandau āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘satthupi khau tai, āvusau sāriputta, vipariṇāmañ‍ñathābhāvā nuppaj‍jaiyyuṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā’’ti? ‘‘satthupi khau mai, āvusau, vipariṇāmañ‍ñathābhāvā nuppaj‍jaiyyuṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā, api ca mai aivamassa – ‘mahaisakkhau vata, bhau, satthā  antarahitau mahiddhikau mahānubhāvau| sacai hi bhagavā ciraṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ tiṭṭhaiyya tadassa bahujanahitāya bahujanasukhāya laukānukampāya atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussāna’nti| tathā  hi panāyasmatau sāriputtassa dīgharattaṃ ahaṅkāramamaṅkāramānānusayā susamūhatā| tasmā āyasmatau sāriputtassa satthupi vipariṇāmañ‍ñathābhāvā nuppaj‍jaiyyuṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. ghaṭasuttaṃ

237. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā ca sāriputtau āyasmā ca mahāmauggal‍lānau  rājagahai viharanti vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai aikavihārai| atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaināyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā mahāmauggal‍lānaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ  nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā sāriputtau āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘vippasan‍nāni khau tai, āvusau mauggal‍lāna, indriyāni; parisuddhau mukhavaṇṇau pariyaudātau santaina nūnāyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau aj‍ja vihāraina vihāsī’’ti| ‘‘auḷārikaina khvāhaṃ, āvusau, aj‍ja vihāraina vihāsiṃ| api ca, mai ahausi dhammī kathā’’ti| ‘‘kaina saddhiṃ panāyasmatau mahāmauggal‍lānassa ahausi dhammī kathā’’ti? ‘‘bhagavatā  khau mai, āvusau, saddhiṃ ahausi dhammī kathā’’ti| ‘‘dūrai khau, āvusau, bhagavā aitarahi sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| kiṃ nu khau, āyasmā, mahāmauggal‍lānau bhagavantaṃ iddhiyā upasaṅkami; udāhu bhagavā āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ iddhiyā upasaṅkamī’’ti? ‘‘na  khvāhaṃ, āvusau, bhagavantaṃ iddhiyā upasaṅkamiṃ; napi maṃ bhagavā iddhiyā upasaṅkami| api ca, mai yāvatā bhagavā aittāvatā dibbacakkhu visujjhi dibbā ca sautadhātu| bhagavataupi yāvatāhaṃ aittāvatā dibbacakkhu visujjhi dibbā ca sautadhātū’’ti| ‘‘yathākathaṃ panāyasmatau mahāmauggal‍lānassa bhagavatā saddhiṃ ahausi dhammī kathā’’ti?

‘‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau, bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘āraddhavīriyau āraddhavīriyauti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, āraddhavīriyau hautī’ti? aivaṃ vuttai, maṃ, āvusau, bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘idha, mauggal‍lāna, bhikkhu āraddhavīriyau viharati – kāmaṃ tacau ca nhāru ca aṭṭhī ca avasissatu, sarīrai upasussatu maṃsalauhitaṃ, yaṃ taṃ purisathāmaina purisavīriyaina purisaparak‍kamaina pattabbaṃ na taṃ apāpuṇitvā vīriyassa saṇṭhānaṃ bhavissatīti| aivaṃ khau, mauggal‍lāna, āraddhavīriyau hautī’ti| aivaṃ khau mai, āvusau, bhagavatā saddhiṃ ahausi dhammī kathā’’ti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, āvusau, himavatau pabbatarājassa parittā pāsāṇasakkharā  yāvadaiva upanikkhaipanamattāya; aivamaiva khau mayaṃ āyasmatau mahāmauggal‍lānassa yāvadaiva upanikkhaipanamattāya| āyasmā hi mahāmauggal‍lānau mahiddhikau mahānubhāvau ākaṅkhamānau kappaṃ tiṭṭhaiyyā’’ti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, āvusau, mahatiyā lauṇaghaṭāya parittā lauṇasakkharāya yāvadaiva upanikkhaipanamattāya; aivamaiva khau mayaṃ āyasmatau sāriputtassa yāvadaiva upanikkhaipanamattāya| āyasmā  hi sāriputtau bhagavatā anaikapariyāyaina thaumitau vaṇṇitau pasatthau –

‘‘sāriputtauva pañ‍ñāya, sīlaina upasamaina ca|

yaupi pāraṅgatau bhikkhu, aitāvaparamau siyā’’ti||

itiha tai ubhau mahānāgā añ‍ñamañ‍ñassa subhāsitaṃ sulapitaṃ samanumaudiṃsūti| tatiyaṃ|

4. navasuttaṃ

238. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau navau bhikkhu pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau vihāraṃ pavisitvā appaussuk‍kau tuṇhībhūtau saṅkasāyati, na bhikkhūnaṃ vaiyyāvac‍caṃ karauti cīvarakārasamayai| atha khau sambahulā bhikkhū yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘idha, bhantai, añ‍ñatarau navau bhikkhu pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau vihāraṃ pavisitvā appaussuk‍kau tuṇhībhūtau saṅkasāyati, na bhikkhūnaṃ vaiyyāvac‍caṃ karauti cīvarakārasamayai’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘aihi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, mama vacanaina taṃ bhikkhuṃ āmantaihi ‘satthā taṃ, āvusau, āmantaitī’’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ bhantai’’ti khau sau bhikkhu bhagavatau paṭissutvā yaina sau bhikkhu tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘satthā taṃ, āvusau, āmantaitī’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau sau bhikkhu tassa bhikkhunau paṭissutvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ  nisin‍naṃ khau taṃ bhikkhuṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, bhikkhu, pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau vihāraṃ pavisitvā appaussuk‍kau tuṇhībhūtau saṅkasāyasi, na bhikkhūnaṃ vaiyyāvac‍caṃ karausi cīvarakārasamayai’’ti? ‘‘ahampi khau, bhantai, sakaṃ kic‍caṃ karaumī’’ti|

atha  khau bhagavā tassa bhikkhunau caitasā caitauparivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘mā khau tumhai, bhikkhavai, aitassa bhikkhunau ujjhāyittha| aisau khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, yassa catthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti, tadanuttaraṃ brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā  sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharatī’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| idaṃ vatvāna sugatau athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

‘‘nayidaṃ sithilamārabbha, nayidaṃ appaina thāmasā|

nibbānaṃ adhigantabbaṃ, sabbadukkhappamaucanaṃ||

‘‘ayañ‍ca daharau bhikkhu, ayamuttamapurisau|

dhāraiti antimaṃ daihaṃ, jaitvā māraṃ savāhini’’nti|| catutthaṃ|

5. sujātasuttaṃ

239. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati| atha khau āyasmā sujātau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami| addasā khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ sujātaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘ubhayainaivāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, kulaputtau saubhati – yañ‍ca  abhirūpau dassanīyau pāsādikau paramāya vaṇṇapaukkharatāya saman‍nāgatau, yassa catthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍janti tadanuttaraṃ brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharatī’’ti| idamavauca bhagavā…pai॰… satthā –

‘‘saubhati  vatāyaṃ bhikkhu, ujubhūtaina caitasā|

vippayuttau visaṃyuttau, anupādāya nibbutau|

dhāraiti antimaṃ daihaṃ, jaitvā māraṃ savāhini’’nti|| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. lakuṇḍakabhaddiyasuttaṃ

240. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati| atha khau āyasmā lakuṇḍakabhaddiyau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami| addasā khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ lakuṇḍakabhaddiyaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘passatha nau tumhai, bhikkhavai, aitaṃ bhikkhuṃ āgacchantaṃ dubbaṇṇaṃ duddasikaṃ aukauṭimakaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ paribhūtarūpa’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aisau khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu mahiddhikau mahānubhāvau, na ca sā samāpatti sulabharūpā yā taina bhikkhunā asamāpan‍napubbā| yassa catthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti, tadanuttaraṃ brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharatī’’ti| idamavauca bhagavā…pai॰… satthā –

‘‘haṃsā kauñ‍cā mayūrā ca, hatthayau pasadā migā|

sabbai sīhassa bhāyanti, natthi kāyasmiṃ tulyatā||

‘‘aivamaiva manussaisu, daharau caipi pañ‍ñavā|

sau hi tattha mahā hauti, naiva bālau sarīravā’’ti|| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. visākhasuttaṃ

241. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā visākhau pañ‍cālaputtau upaṭṭhānasālāyaṃ bhikkhū dhammiyā kathāya sandassaiti samādapaiti samuttaijaiti sampahaṃsaiti, pauriyā vācāya vissaṭṭhāya  anailagalāya atthassa viñ‍ñāpaniyā pariyāpan‍nāya anissitāya|

atha khau bhagavā sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaina upaṭṭhānasālā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā  pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| nisaj‍ja khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhikkhavai, upaṭṭhānasālāyaṃ bhikkhū dhammiyā kathāya sandassaiti samādapaiti samuttaijaiti sampahaṃsaiti pauriyā vācāya vissaṭṭhāya anailagalāya atthassa viñ‍ñāpaniyā pariyāpan‍nāya anissitāyā’’ti? ‘‘āyasmā, bhantai, visākhau  pañ‍cālaputtau upaṭṭhānasālāyaṃ bhikkhū dhammiyā kathāya sandassaiti samādapaiti samuttaijaiti sampahaṃsaiti, pauriyā vācāya vissaṭṭhāya anailagalāya atthassa viñ‍ñāpaniyā pariyāpan‍nāya anissitāyā’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ visākhaṃ pañ‍cālaputtaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘sādhu sādhu, visākha, sādhu khau tvaṃ, visākha, bhikkhū dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisi…pai॰… atthassa viñ‍ñāpaniyā pariyāpan‍nāya anissitāyā’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| idaṃ vatvāna sugatau athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

‘‘nābhāsamānaṃ jānanti, missaṃ bālaihi paṇḍitaṃ|

bhāsamānañ‍ca jānanti, daisaintaṃ amataṃ padaṃ||

‘‘bhāsayai jautayai dhammaṃ, paggaṇhai isinaṃ dhajaṃ|

subhāsitadhajā isayau, dhammau hi isinaṃ dhajau’’ti|| sattamaṃ|

8. nandasuttaṃ

242. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati| atha khau āyasmā nandau bhagavatau mātucchāputtau ākauṭitapac‍cākauṭitāni cīvarāni pārupitvā akkhīni añ‍jaitvā acchaṃ pattaṃ gahaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ nandaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘na khau tai taṃ, nanda, patirūpaṃ kulaputtassa saddhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitassa, yaṃ tvaṃ ākauṭitapac‍cākauṭitāni cīvarāni pārupaiyyāsi, akkhīni ca añ‍jaiyyāsi, acchañ‍ca pattaṃ dhāraiyyāsi| aitaṃ khau tai, nanda, patirūpaṃ kulaputtassa saddhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitassa, yaṃ tvaṃ ārañ‍ñikau  ca assasi, piṇḍapātikau ca paṃsukulikau ca kāmaisu ca anapaikkhau viharaiyyāsī’’ti| idamavauca bhagavā…pai॰… satthā –

‘‘kadāhaṃ nandaṃ passaiyyaṃ, ārañ‍ñaṃ paṃsukūlikaṃ|

añ‍ñātuñchaina yāpaintaṃ, kāmaisu anapaikkhina’’nti||

atha  khau āyasmā nandau aparaina samayaina ārañ‍ñikau ca piṇḍapātikau ca paṃsukūlikau ca kāmaisu ca anapaikkhau vihāsīti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. tissasuttaṃ

243. sāvatthiyaṃ viharati| atha  khau āyasmā tissau bhagavatau pitucchāputtau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi dukkhī dummanau assūni pavattayamānau| atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ tissaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau tvaṃ, tissa, aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau dukkhī dummanau assūni pavattayamānau’’ti? ‘‘tathā hi pana maṃ, bhantai, bhikkhū samantā vācāsan‍nitaudakaina vācāya san‍nitaudakaina (ka॰) sañ‍jambharimakaṃsū’’ti sañ‍jabbharimakaṃsūti (?)| ‘‘tathāhi pana tvaṃ, tissa, vattā nau ca vacanakkhamau; na khau tai taṃ, tissa, patirūpaṃ kulaputtassa saddhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitassa, yaṃ tvaṃ vattā nau ca vacanakkhamau| aitaṃ khau tai, tissa, patirūpaṃ kulaputtassa saddhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitassa – ‘yaṃ tvaṃ vattā ca assa vacanakkhamau cā’’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| idaṃ vatvāna sugatau athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

‘‘kiṃ nu kujjhasi mā kujjhi, ak‍kaudhau tissa tai varaṃ|

kaudhamānamakkhavinayatthañhi, tissa brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’’ti|| navamaṃ|

10. thairanāmakasuttaṃ

244. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu thairanāmakau aikavihārī caiva hauti aikavihārassa ca vaṇṇavādī| sau aikau gāmaṃ piṇḍāya pavisati aikau paṭik‍kamati aikau rahau nisīdati aikau caṅkamaṃ adhiṭṭhāti| atha khau sambahulā bhikkhū yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu;  upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau  tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘idha, bhantai, añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu thairanāmakau aikavihārī aikavihārassa ca vaṇṇavādī’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘aihi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, mama vacanaina thairaṃ bhikkhuṃ āmantaihi – ‘satthā taṃ, āvusau thaira, āmantaitī’’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau sau bhikkhu bhagavatau paṭissutvā yaināyasmā thairau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ thairaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘satthā taṃ, āvusau thaira, āmantaitī’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau āyasmā thairau tassa bhikkhunau paṭissutvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ thairaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, thaira, aikavihārī aikavihārassa ca vaṇṇavādī’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana tvaṃ, thaira, aikavihārī aikavihārassa ca vaṇṇavādī’’ti? ‘‘idhāhaṃ, bhantai, aikau gāmaṃ piṇḍāya pavisāmi aikau paṭik‍kamāmi aikau rahau nisīdāmi aikau caṅkamaṃ adhiṭṭhāmi| aivaṃ khvāhaṃ, bhantai, aikavihārī aikavihārassa ca vaṇṇavādī’’ti|

‘‘atthaisau, thaira, aikavihārau naisau natthīti vadāmi| api ca, thaira, yathā aikavihārau vitthāraina paripuṇṇau hauti taṃ suṇāhi, sādhukaṃ manasi karauhi; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau…pai॰…| ‘‘kathañ‍ca, thaira, aikavihārau vitthāraina paripuṇṇau hauti| idha, thaira, yaṃ atītaṃ taṃ pahīnaṃ, yaṃ anāgataṃ taṃ paṭinissaṭṭhaṃ, pac‍cuppan‍naisu ca attabhāvapaṭilābhaisu chandarāgau suppaṭivinītau| aivaṃ khau, thaira, aikavihārau vitthāraina paripuṇṇau hautī’’ti|

idamavauca  bhagavā| idaṃ vatvāna sugatau athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

‘‘sabbābhibhuṃ  sabbaviduṃ sumaidhaṃ,

sabbaisu dhammaisu anūpalittaṃ|

sabbañ‍jahaṃ taṇhākkhayai vimuttaṃ,

tamahaṃ naraṃ aikavihārīti brūmī’’ti|| dasamaṃ|

11. mahākappinasuttaṃ

245. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati| atha khau āyasmā mahākappinau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami| addasā khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ mahākappinaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘passatha nau tumhai bhikkhavai, aitaṃ bhikkhuṃ āgacchantaṃ audātakaṃ tanukaṃ tuṅganāsika’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aisau khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu mahiddhikau mahānubhāvau| na ca sā samāpatti sulabharūpā yā taina bhikkhunā asamāpan‍napubbā| yassa catthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti tadanuttaraṃ brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ  diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharatī’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| idaṃ vatvāna sugatau athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

‘‘khattiyau saiṭṭhau janaitasmiṃ, yai gauttapaṭisārinau|

vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau, sau saiṭṭhau daivamānusai||

‘‘divā tapati ādic‍cau, rattimābhāti candimā|

san‍naddhau khattiyau tapati, jhāyī tapati brāhmaṇau|

atha sabbamahaurattiṃ atha sabbamahaurattaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰), buddhau tapati taijasā’’ti|| aikādasamaṃ|

12. sahāyakasuttaṃ

246. sāvatthiyaṃ  viharati| atha khau dvai bhikkhū sahāyakā āyasmatau mahākappinassa saddhivihārinau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu| addasā khau bhagavā tai bhikkhū dūratauva  āgacchantai| disvāna bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘passatha nau tumhai, bhikkhavai, aitai bhikkhū sahāyakai āgacchantai kappinassa saddhivihārinau’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aitai khau tai bhikkhū mahiddhikā mahānubhāvā| na ca sā samāpatti sulabharūpā, yā taihi bhikkhūhi asamāpan‍napubbā| yassa catthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti tadanuttaraṃ brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā  upasampaj‍ja viharantī’’ti|

idamavauca  bhagavā| idaṃ vatvāna sugatau athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

‘‘sahāyā vatimai bhikkhū, cirarattaṃ samaitikā|

samaiti naisaṃ saddhammau, dhammai buddhappavaiditai||

‘‘suvinītā kappinaina, dhammai ariyappavaiditai|

dhārainti antimaṃ daihaṃ, jaitvā māraṃ savāhini’’nti|| dvādasamaṃ|

bhikkhusaṃyuttaṃ samattaṃ|


tassuddānaṃ –

kaulitau upatissau ca, ghaṭau cāpi pavuc‍cati|

navau  sujātau bhaddi ca, visākhau nandau tissau ca|

thairanāmau ca kappinau, sahāyaina ca dvādasāti||

nidānavaggau dutiyau|

tassuddānaṃ –

nidānābhisamayadhātu, anamataggaina kassapaṃ|

sak‍kārarāhulalakkhaṇau, aupamma-bhikkhunā vaggau||

dutiyau taina pavuc‍catīti|

nidānavaggasaṃyuttapāḷi niṭṭhitā|
